### [1] [Counterexamples as Feedback for Agent Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02892)
*Sidhesh Badrinarayan,Adithya Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 A-CEGIS 框架,将反例作为反馈信号,用于评估智能体在自然语言到正则表达式合成任务中的多轮自我修正能力。该框架通过确定性验证器提供紧凑的假正例和假负例作为反馈,显著提升了任务成功率并揭示了隐藏集成功与鲁棒性之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮代码生成指标(如 pass@1)低估了已部署智能体的核心特性:在接收到具体反馈后能否修复错误产物。现有评估方法无法衡量修复效率、修订过程中的稳定性,以及表观已解决的任务在更严格测试下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 A-CEGIS 评估框架,采用反例引导的迭代合成(CEGIS)模式:智能体在每一轮提出正则表达式,确定性 oracle 使用 re.fullmatch 语义检查,通过解析驱动的采样从金标正则生成正例,通过突变和随机采样生成负例。当候选失败时,反馈中包含最多两个假负例和两个假例,并按紧凑性排序。引入硬化协议:在隐藏集成功后,针对描述标记、正则字面量和现有示例构建目标探针池,搜索金标与候选不一致的字符串。设计了完整的轨迹指标体系,包括均值首次成功轮数(MTT)、平均倒数排名(MRR)、修复效率比(RER,固定事件数除以回归事件数)和结构稳定性(归一化编辑相似度)。

Result: 在 30 个 NL-RX-Turk 任务上:四轮预算内诊断式 A-CEGIS 达到 90% 通过率,而零样本生成仅 17%、通用自我修正仅 27%、仅错误反馈仅 23%。完整诊断运行(最多 7 轮 + 硬化)在隐藏集上达到 100% 通过率,均值首次成功轮数为 2.67,RER 为 2.61(311 次修复 vs 99 次回归)。硬化后鲁棒通过率为 76.7%,揭示了 7 个隐藏集通过但探针失败的案例,主要失败模式包括边界欠规范、通配符过度泛化(如 .*)和字符类边界混淆(如 \w 范围过宽)。MRR 为 0.477,表明大多数任务在少数轮次内收敛,众数为第 2 轮。代表性修复案例显示模型倾向于用宽松结构(如 .*)捕捉主体,在硬化阶段被收紧。

Conclusion: 反例是智能体自我修正的强大反馈信号。相同的运行在不同测量层次上呈现不同面貌:隐藏集测试显示完全解决,轨迹指标显示高效且建设性的修复,硬化测试则揭示约四分之一隐藏集通过的解决方案仍存在潜在语义错误。A-CEGIS 使这些层次在统一循环中可见,表明修正质量应作为轨迹而非单一数字进行评估。该框架可推广到任何具备三个条件的场景:紧凑的形式化产物、确定性 oracle 和可向模型呈现的反例,包括 SQL 查询、模式、配置策略等。

Abstract: Single-turn code-generation metrics understate a central property of deployed agents: whether they can repair a wrong artifact after receiving concrete feedback. This paper presents A-CEGIS, a lightweight framework that uses counterexamples as feedback for evaluating multi-turn refinement in natural-language-to-regex synthesis. An agent proposes a regex, a deterministic oracle checks it under full-match semantics, and compact false-positive or false-negative witnesses guide the next turn. On 30 NL-RX-Turk tasks, diagnostic counterexample feedback solves 90\% of tasks within a four-turn ablation budget, compared with 17% for zero-shot generation, 27% for generic self-correction, and 23% for error-only feedback. In a full diagnostic run with hardening, all tasks are solved on the hidden set by the final turn, with mean time-to-success of 2.7 turns and robust success of 77% after targeted probing. These results show that A-CEGIS measures how efficiently an agent improves across turns while adding a practical robustness check beyond the original held-out cases.

</details>


### [2] [Listen to the Latents: Self-Correcting Speech Recognition in Large Audio Language Models Through Hidden-State Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02940)
*Chan-Jan Hsu,Jaeyeon Kim,Chao-Han Huck Yang,Shinji Watanabe,Hung-yi Lee,Carlos Busso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid Search，一种基于隐状态交互特征的自校正解码算法，用于改善基于热初始化LLM的ASR模型对语义依赖型token（尤其是命名实体）的识别能力，无需额外训练或参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-ASR集成方法（logit融合与热初始化）各有优劣，但如何有效结合两者尚不明确。此外，热初始化后ASR-LLM是否仍能利用其原始base LLM提升推理性能也未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 提取ASR-LLM与base LLM在语音条件与文本条件下的配对隐藏状态，计算层级的余弦相似度（AngleFeat）和相对范数比（MagFeat）作为交互特征；2. 通过YODAS数据集上的高斯密度估计，发现命名实体token在中间层（10-28层）呈现出AngleFeat和MagFeat显著偏高的分布特征；3. 提出目标token选择器T(·)，基于密度比阈值τ识别高语义依赖token；4. 设计Hybrid Search解码算法：在目标token处切换到beam search（B=5），在非目标token处保持greedy search，当所有beam在非目标token处收敛后回退；5. 采用分段logit融合公式（式8），仅对目标token融合LLM的对数概率（α=0.2）。

Result: 在Open ASR Leaderboard和Common Voice上：1. Hybrid Search for Decoding在greedy基础上恢复了beam search WER改进的25%和NE-ER改进的96.4%，计算开销仅为1.4×greedy；2. Hybrid Search for Error Correction在beam search基础上进一步将平均NE-ER从18.29%降至17.69%（TED-LIUM和SPGISpeech分别提升12.7%和8.8%），同时保持相当的WER；3. 统计显著性极高（NE-ER: p=3.56×10⁻³⁸, WER: p=9.19×10⁻⁵）；4. 朴素的重打分和late fusion会显著退化WER（如late fusion从6.36%升至9.11%）；5. 隐状态交互特征在Phi-4-Multimodal和Granite-4.0-1B-Speech两个模型上均观察到一致的分布偏移。

Conclusion: 热初始化后的ASR-LLM在适应过程中存在部分语义知识遗忘，但其原始base LLM的残存知识在推理时仍可被有效利用。基于隐藏状态交互特征（余弦相似度与相对范数比）的目标性自校正策略能够在不增加参数或训练的前提下显著改善命名实体识别，同时维持整体WER，表明选择性LLM纠正确比全局融合更有效。

Abstract: Recent automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems increasingly integrate large language models (LLMs) to leverage their semantic knowledge, either externally through logit fusion or internally through warm initialization. However, how to effectively combine these two strategies remains underexplored. In this work, we refine warm-initialized LLM-based ASR models by leveraging their own pre-adaptation base LLMs, focusing on LoRA-adapted settings where the base LLM is preserved. To achieve this, we propose Hybrid Search, a targeted correction strategy motivated by two observations. First, interaction features that characterize the relationship between LLM-based ASR hidden states and base-LLM hidden states provide informative signals about a token's degree of semantic dependence. Second, selectively refining targeted tokens with high semantic dependence improves ASR performance far beyond naive global LLM-correction methods including rescoring and late fusion. Our analysis suggests that, even after semantic knowledge transfer through warm initialization, LLM-based ASR models can still leverage their base LLM to further improve inference-time performance.

</details>


### [3] [Judging LLM-as-a-Judge: Concerning Rubric Artifacts in LLM-based Automated Text Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02942)
*Anshul Bagaria,Sowmya S Sundaram,Gokul S Krishnan,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于评分标准的LLM评判系统存在严重缺陷：仅通过评分标准文本就能预测评判结果，且评判模型对答案或评分标准的反事实修改反应迟钝，质疑了当前基于评分标准的自动评估方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge评估流程的可靠性受到广泛关注，现有研究已发现位置效应、冗长偏好等偏见。然而，基于评分标准的评估隐含一个核心假设：评分标准仅作为评估指令，而非独立的信息源。本文旨在探究这一前提是否成立。

Method: 1. 提出"评分标准探针"实验：训练分类器仅基于评分标准文本预测评判结果标签；3. 在HealthBench和ResearchRubrics两个基准上进行实验，使用PubMedBERT等分类器；2. 进行反事实扰动实验：分别翻转候选答案和评分标准，检验评判模型的反应灵敏度；4. 通过UMAP可视化、TF-IDF权重分析、BERTopic主题建模等手段分析评分标准的语义结构。

Result: 1. 仅基于评分标准的分类器在多个评判模型和数据集上准确率显著超过0.5的随机水平，准确率达到0.826-0.900；2. 输出扰动实验中，评判模型仅在37.7%的情况下正确翻转判断；3. 评分标准扰动实验中，期望翻转率仅为16.8%（Gemma）和32.2%（LLaMA）；4. UMAP可视化显示不同难度的评分标准在嵌入空间中具有可分离性。

Conclusion: 评分标准文本本身就编码了与评判结果相关的可恢复信号，使得评判标签可独立于候选响应进行部分预测。评判模型对答案或评分标准的反事实修改表现出显著的判断不一致性。本文呼吁在基于LLM的自动文本评估中，应对评分标准设计进行系统性验证和更严格的方法学审查。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge pipelines are increasingly used to evaluate AI-generated text, based on the assumption that judgments arise from reasoning over candidate responses with respect to a rubric. We show that this assumption warrants further scrutiny. Classifiers trained only on rubric text, without access to any evaluated response, achieve nontrivial predictive performance on judge outputs. This suggests that rubric formulations encode recoverable evaluative signals, allowing scores to be partially anticipated independently of model outputs. Finally, counterfactual perturbations reveal that judges often fail to reliably update their decisions when either the candidate response or the rubric criterion is reversed. Our findings raise concerns about the reliability of rubric-based LLM evaluation and highlight the need for further methodological study of automated evaluation via LLMs.

</details>


### [4] [SHELF: A Synthetic Harness for Multi-Task Bibliographic Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03047)
*Michael J. Bommarito II*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHELF（Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness）是一个用于图书馆书目工作多任务基准测试的合成数据生成与评估系统。它基于美国国会图书馆词表生成62,899篇受控文档，支持分类、聚类、检索、配对分类和指令检索五类任务，比较了稀疏方法（TF、TF-IDF、BM25）与多种编码器，并对比了零样本解码器在主题分类上的表现。结果显示主题分类达0.8887，而体裁形式分类仅0.2605，部分聚类与配对任务接近随机；模型排名在SHELF与自然语料（Gutenberg、LCSHBench）间可迁移，但绝对分数不能预测生产环境精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆和档案馆需要在大规模馆藏上完成主题、体裁、受众、语域等书目任务，但现有通用基准（如MTEB）未系统覆盖书目工作；同时，人工编目数据存在标签噪声、特征共变等问题，无法独立评估各书目维度。因此需要一个受控、可复用、能区分各书目维度的基准，以辅助方法选型与诊断。

Method: SHELF是一个Python系统，其方法分为四个核心部分。1.数据生成：输入为带标签的分类法（21个LCC大类、133个细粒度体裁形式等）、写作规范（含主题、受众、语域、地理、长度）和生成预算，调用25个LLM（来自11家机构）在受控规范下独立采样各书目维度，产出合成文档，并记录写作模型、提示风格、温度、核采样概率等元数据。2.任务构建：将文档转为五种任务——分类（macro-F1）、聚类（ARI）、检索（nDCG@10）、配对分类（AUC）和指令检索（nDCG@10），覆盖六种书目维度。3.模型评估：对比TF、TF-IDF+SVD、BM25等稀疏方法，以及BERT/RoBERTa/DistilBERT/MiniLM/MPNet/BGE/E5/GTE/GTR-T5/Instructor/OGBert等编码器；分类任务额外对比四个零样本解码器（Qwen3.5-0.8B、Gemma-4-E2B、Qwen3.5-2B、GPT-5.6-luna）。4.诊断与外部验证：实施标签词遮蔽、sham控制、组内文档自举等诊断实验，并将SHELF与Gutenberg、LCSHBench语料进行跨语料迁移测试（训练-测试矩阵+模型排名相关性）。

Result: 在分类上，EmbeddingGemma-300M以macro-F1 0.8887领跑21类主题分类，Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B以0.8823次之；TF-IDF在主题任务以0.8686排名第三，在语域任务以0.6358领先，体裁形式分类仅0.2605。检索方面，Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B在主题检索达0.7104 nDCG@10，体裁形式检索仅0.1173；BM25在三个检索任务中均处末位（23-25/25）。聚类上，仅主题聚类有判别力（最佳0.5152），地理聚类最佳仅0.0068。配对分类中，"相同主题"AUC达0.8474，但"相同受众"和"相同语域"接近随机。指令检索锚定主题的指令被部分遵循（0.5496），锚定体裁/受众的指令几乎无效（≤0.0764）。零样本解码器主题分类最佳为GPT-5.6-luna的0.5860。跨语料迁移显示SHELF与Gutenberg/LCSHBench的模型排名Spearman相关为0.870/0.765，但SHELF训练→Gutenberg测试的绝对分数从0.8796骤降至0.3193。标签词遮蔽使SHELF分数下降0.0337但几乎不影响模型排序。TF-IDF处理速度达2,270.9 docs/s，为各方法之首。

Conclusion: SHELF填补了通用嵌入基准中书目任务的空白，其受控合成数据支持书目维度的独立诊断。结果证实主题分类接近饱和而体裁形式、语域、受众、地理等任务仍具挑战；稀疏方法在受控场景中仍具竞争力，TF-IDF兼具速度与精度优势；模型排名在SHELF与自然语料间可迁移，但SHELF绝对分数不能预测生产编目精度。该系统支持训练截止后重新生成未见文档，可作为未来抗污染基准。局限包括：无人类编目基线、缺乏前沿大规模编码器评测、池化语料的拆分保证仅适用于后期规范支持子集、计时结果为单次测量且硬件依赖。

Abstract: Libraries and archives manage large collections with limited staff and computing budgets, yet common benchmarks do not systematically test their bibliographic work. They need to know which methods work for their tasks and what those methods require to run. SHELF, the Synthetic Harness for Evaluating LLM Fitness, addresses this gap. It is a Python system that turns labelled taxonomies, writing specifications, and a generation budget into controlled benchmark data and evaluation tasks. This first release contains 62,899 model-written documents based on Library of Congress vocabularies, with tasks for classification, clustering, retrieval, pair classification, and instruction retrieval. We compare TF, TF-IDF, BM25, popular encoders, and, on subject classification only, zero-shot decoders; each method appears only on tasks that support it. Subject classification reaches 0.8887, while genre-form classification reaches only 0.2605, and several pair and clustering tasks remain near chance. Sparse methods remain competitive on classification, while TF-IDF is the fastest measured arm in the subject timing experiment. SHELF also varies bibliographic facets independently and can generate new, verifiably unseen documents after a model's training cutoff. Comparisons with LCSHBench and Project Gutenberg show that model rankings transfer more reliably than absolute scores, but SHELF scores do not estimate accuracy on production catalogue data. We release all source code and data under permissive licenses on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [5] [TabScope: Question-Adaptive Scope Selection for Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03395)
*Yuxiang Wang,Junhao Gan,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TabScope提出了一种问题自适应的表格问答框架，根据问题类型动态选择局部化子表推理或全表推理，并引入长表基准SLQA和子表评估集WTQ-SubTab。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在表格问答任务中，随表格规模增大而性能下降，但这种退化在不同问题类型间并不均匀：定位敏感型问题受无关内容干扰大，而需要广泛证据的问题反而受益于全表推理。因此核心问题是：何时该对表格进行定位，何时该保留全表进行推理。

Method: 框架包含三个组件：(1) 基于LLM的问题类型分类器配合固定策略π，选择局部化或全表推理模式；(2) 操作感知的分解器（包含操作感知检索、K次采样证据聚合、基于支持度与紧凑性的子表精炼三个步骤）构建问题特定子表；(3) 答案模型根据选定输入生成回答。策略π通过验证集离线分析得出，并将表格大小纳入考量（如频率计数问题在大表中转为偏好定位）。同时构建SLQA长表基准和WTQ-SubTab银标准子表评估集。

Result: 在WikiTQ和SLQA上，TabScope分别取得82.2%和68.1%的平均准确率，优于全表CoT和所有分解方法基线。操作感知分解在WTQ-SubTab上六个指标均最优，相对DATER在行精确匹配上提升9.24点。消融实验证实各组件均有贡献，移除范围选择导致WikiTQ下降4.3点。问题类型分析显示：查找、排序、局部推理、差值计数偏好定位；而通用计数、比较问题偏好全表。

Conclusion: 长表问答不仅需要解决如何定位的问题，还需决定是否定位。通过问题自适应范围选择与操作感知分解结合，TabScope在减少无关干扰的同时保留了必要的上下文信息，验证了表格范围应作为问题依赖的决策而非固定预处理步骤。局限在于当前评估限于英文基准和两种LLM，范围选择策略基于预定义问题类型分类法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on table question answering, yet their accuracy often degrades as table size increases. We find that this degradation is not uniform across question types. Localization-sensitive questions are particularly affected by irrelevant table content, while questions requiring broader evidence may still benefit from full-table reasoning. Based on this observation, we propose a question-adaptive framework that dynamically selects between localized and full-table reasoning. The framework constructs question-specific sub-tables through operation-aware table decomposition and uses the predicted question type to determine the appropriate reasoning mode. We further introduce silver reference sub-tables for evaluating evidence selection and construct SLQA, a benchmark based on real-world long tables. Experiments on WikiTQ and SLQA show that localization is particularly effective for lookup and local reasoning questions, while adaptive selection between localized and full-table reasoning achieves the best overall performance. These results highlight that long-table QA requires deciding not only how to localize, but also when to localize. Our code and datasets will be made available upon publication of the paper.

</details>


### [6] [When Users Don't Ask: Benchmarking Context-Driven Memory Retrieval in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03467)
*Wen-Yu Chang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 LoCoMo-Conv，一个面向长对话场景的对话式记忆评估基准。它基于 LoCoMo 数据集构建了四种会话查询类型（dialog、implicit、counterfactual、composed），在五种记忆系统上评估检索召回率与端到端响应质量，发现对话式框架暴露了 QA 式评估未发现的检索缺口，并提出"隐性锚定"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆基准主要通过 QA 式探针评估记忆，而非在实际对话场景中使用。当 LLM 作为长时对话代理部署时，需要评估它们能否在自然的、第一人称的对话环境中调用记忆，而非仅在显式提问时表现良好。现有基准在自然对话使用情境下评估不足，需要一个新的对话式记忆评估框架。

Method: 基于 LoCoMo 数据集构建 LoCoMo-Conv，将每个 QA 问题改写为四种第一人称会话查询类型：(1) Dialog：直接对话式重写；(2) Implicit：不显式提问的情境话语；(3) Counterfactual：包含错误前提需助理纠正；(4) Composed：组合多个源 QA 需综合多记忆。查询改写由 GPT-5.4-mini 完成。评估五种记忆系统（AnchorMem、A-MEM、mem0、Memora、NaiveRAG），统一嵌入模型为 all-MiniLM-L6-v2，主干 LLM 为 gemma-4-31B-it。从两个维度评估：检索召回率（top-K=10）与风格特定的 LLM 判断（GPT-5.4-mini 为主判断）。还引入多面查询改写（multi-facet query rewriting），通过 RRF 融合改善检索。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 对话式框架暴露了 QA 式评估掩盖的大量检索缺口，尤其在 implicit 和 composed 查询上；多面查询改写对原始轮次记忆有效，但对 mem0/Memora 等抽象记忆无效。(2) 强检索不完全转化为响应质量——mem0 检索最佳但响应生成较弱（dialog 仅 0.366 vs AnchorMem 0.598），表明有损压缩保留了语义匹配但丢失了生成所需的细节。(3) 发现"隐性锚定"（silent grounding）现象：implicit 查询中，记忆改善了上下文锚定但未显式呈现事实，Oracle 在事实使用=0 的情况下仍比无记忆版本在忠实性上领先 +55.1 分。结论指向基于推理的记忆语义扩展优于有损压缩。释放 supportive_memory 辅助标注层。

Conclusion: LoCoMo-Conv 表明对话式框架暴露了记忆系统评估中被忽视的挑战。严格的基于事实的评估指标低估了隐性锚定的价值。研究建议未来对话记忆系统应通过语义扩展而非有损压缩来丰富记忆表示，同时保留响应锚定所需的细节。查询时扩展和记忆时扩展在功能上存在重叠。released supportive_memory 标注层为超越显式事实的会话支持提供了额外资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increas- ingly deployed as long-horizon conversational agents, motivating growing interest in mem- ory systems. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate memory through QA-style probing rather than in-situ conversational usage. We introduce LOCOMO-CONV, a conversa- tional memory benchmark derived from Lo- CoMo with four query styles: dialog, implicit, counterfactual, and composed. Across five rep- resentative memory systems, we evaluate both retrieval recall and end-to-end response qual- ity. Our experiments show that conversational framing exposes substantial retrieval gaps over- looked by QA benchmarks, especially on im- plicit and composed queries, which multi-facet query rewriting narrows for raw-turn mem- ory but not abstractive memory. We further find that strong retrieval does not fully trans- late into response quality, and that implicit queries exhibit silent grounding, where mem- ory improves contextual grounding without ex- plicitly surfacing the gold fact. These results point to reasoning-based memory elaboration as a promising direction, and we release aux- iliary supportive_memory annotations captur- ing conversationally useful context beyond the original gold evidence.

</details>


### [7] [Pattern Over-Generalization of Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03487)
*Junsik Kim,Kangil Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PogRE模型，利用密集线性变换与复合操作解决知识图谱嵌入中的"模式过度泛化"问题，即现有KGE模型仅从单一模式实例就将其普遍化到所有相关实例，导致错误预测。PogRE通过QR分解启发的参数化方法，在理论保证模式随观察实体数增加而渐进式普遍化的同时，在三个标准基准数据集上实现SOTA链接预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGE模型（如TransE、RotatE、PairRE、CompoundE）虽然能有效捕捉对称/反对称、逆、组合等推理模式，但由于其模式条件仅依赖于关系嵌入（与实体无关），存在"模式过度泛化"问题——模型从单一实例中将仅局部有效的模式普遍泛化到全图所有实例，导致错误预测。这一问题在低频模式（实际为局部模式）被高频体实例影响时尤为严重。

Method: 方法名为PogRE（Pattern Over-Generalization Robust Embedding），核心设计包括：(1) 评分函数 f_r(h,t)=||L_r h_r - t_r||，其中L_r为关系特定的线性变换；(2) L_r通过QR分解启发的参数化方法分解为关系特定正交矩阵Q_r（由k个Householder反射近似）与跨关系共享的上三角矩阵R；(3) 对R施加谱归一化以限制Lipschitz常数为1；(4) 通过齐次坐标将仿射映射与线性变换统一为(d+1)×(d+1)的单一线性矩阵A_r；(5) 采用自对抗负采样训练。理论上证明：随着观察到的线性独立实体数增加，模式约束空间维度增加；当观察到d+1个线性独立实体时，模式获得完全普遍泛化的保证。

Result: 在WN18RR、FB15k-237、YAGO3-10三个基准数据集以及ogbl-biokg、ogbl-wikikg2大规模KG上，PogRE均取得SOTA链接预测性能。WN18RR上MRR达0.506（超越DualE的次优0.492），FB15k-237上MRR达0.369，YAGO3-10上MRR达0.556。消融实验表明各组件（QR分解、共享上三角矩阵、谱归一化、线性变换L_r）均对性能有贡献。OG比率（衡量过度泛化程度）显著低于基线，表明有效缓解了过度泛化问题。实体嵌入经验上呈线性独立，验证理论假设的合理性。

Conclusion: PogRE通过密集线性变换与复合操作的结合，有效解决了知识图谱嵌入中的模式过度泛化问题。理论分析保证了模式随观察实体数量渐进普遍化的特性：少量观察→局部模式，充分观察→普遍模式。在多个基准数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。局限性在于：(1) 不利用模式语义信息，对低频普遍模式和高频局部模式的失败案例处理不佳；(2) 仅适用于直推式设置，无法处理归纳式场景中的未见实体和关系。未来工作将探索结合模式语义进行泛化以及扩展至归纳式设置。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) demonstrates its effectiveness for predicting missing links in knowledge graphs (KGs) by projecting entities and relations into a low-dimensional vector space. It is crucial for KGE models to effectively capture inference patterns (patterns) inherent in KGs, such as symmetry/antisymmetry, inversion and composition. Although recent KGE models exhibit strong capabilities in modeling such diverse patterns, they suffer from inherent limitations stemming from pattern over-generalization, where embeddings learned from only a single pattern instance inevitably generalize that pattern to all related instances, i.e., generalize the pattern universally. To address this issue, we propose PogRE (Pattern Over-Generalization Robust Embedding), a simple but effective method that utilizes dense linear transformations and compound operations for relation representation. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that a dense linear transformation allows a pattern to become progressively universal as more triples are observed in the pattern. Furthermore, after observing d+1 linearly independent entities (d+1 denotes the dimension of entity), the linear transformation guarantees universal generalization of the pattern across all related instances. Experimental results on three standard benchmark datasets show that PogRE outperforms existing state-of-the-art KGE models in link prediction. Moreover, our empirical results indicate that PogRE effectively addresses the negative impact of over-generalization.

</details>


### [8] [Building and Evaluating Fixed-Voice Thai TTS from Synthetic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03502)
*Kunat Pipatanakul,Potsawee Manakul,Warit Sirichotedumrong,Sittipong Sripaisarnmongkol,Pakorn Nathong,Phatrasek Jirabovonvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大型零样本语音克隆教师模型蒸馏为紧凑固定语音学生模型的泰语TTS方法。该方法利用15秒语音参考，通过合成语音生成、质量过滤和重采样构建训练语料，训练出82M参数的Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge模型，支持设备端泰英双语推理，无需参考音频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言TTS部署面临两难：大型零样本语音克隆模型推理成本高，固定语音系统又需要大量特定说话人语料。泰语还面临词边界模糊、 lexical tone、姓名/借词数字化、泰英代码转换等独特挑战。论文探索第三条路径：以大型语音克隆模型作为可编程数据源，用短参考音频生成合成语料训练紧凑学生模型。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 文本准备与verbalization（LLM将数字/英文片段改写为发音导向文本）；(2) 用OmniVoice教师模型结合12个种子参考音频合成语音，经CTC音素验证、停顿位置、语速、时长四重质量过滤，对失败样本最多重渲染4次以保留困难文本覆盖；(3) 基于Kokoro/StyleTTS2 82M骨架训练固定语音学生，集成TLTK泰语G2P前端与Misaki英文前端实现双语路由。评估框架包含CER、挑战集关键词准确率（1531项5类）、韵律停顿准确率、说话人相似度、语速五个维度。

Result: 最终模型Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge（82M参数）：挑战集关键词准确率68.2%（达Gemini 3.1的85.5%），泰语停顿精确率91.4%，超过教师OmniVoice（89.9%）；泰语CER 3.7%、英文CER 1.1%；停顿位置错误率（PPER 6.7%）和词内停顿率（1.4%）在三者中最低。最佳K=118采样揭示教师有15.1百分点的可恢复空间，从72.8%升至87.9%。伊善方言实验中，15秒参考可迁移说话人特征（余弦相似度0.842 vs 教师0.854），方言CER从6.6%降至5.5%。预训练初始化使代码切换准确率从22.8%跃升至65.5%。

Conclusion: 紧凑TTS模型在低资源泰语场景下可与大型专有模型竞争；停顿和关键词准确率作为语料构建和模型评估的有效信号，但仍依赖基于规则的分词和音素转换启发式方法，存在局限性；泰语上下文依赖性强，结合神经与规则方法是未来有前景的方向。论文开源了模型与评估框架以促进泰语TTS发展。

Abstract: In low-resource settings, deploying TTS typically requires choosing between a large voice-cloning model with costly inference or a compact fixed-voice system that requires a speaker-specific corpus. We study a third route: using a large voice-cloning model as a programmable data source to turn a short voice reference (e.g., 15 seconds) into a compact fixed-voice student trained entirely on synthetic speech. This setting makes pipeline design consequential: teacher errors become training targets, while filtering failed generations can reduce coverage of difficult texts. Thai further introduces challenges from ambiguous word boundaries, lexical tone, names and loanwords, numeric verbalization, and Thai-English code-switching. We study how text preparation, synthetic generation, quality filtering, rejection sampling, and frontend choices affect the resulting student, and where teacher limitations remain. We evaluate CER, Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy, Prosody Pause Accuracy, speaker similarity, and speaking rate. The resulting 82M-parameter model, Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge, enables on-device Thai TTS without reference audio. It achieves 68.2% Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy (85.5% of Gemini 3.1) and 91.4% pause precision, outperforming its OmniVoice teacher (89.9%) and reaching 94.8% of Gemini 3.1. It also achieves the lowest pause-placement error and intra-word pause rates among the three systems, and 3.7% and 1.1% CER on Thai and English, respectively. We open-source the model and evaluation framework for Thai TTS development.

</details>


### [9] [How Far Can Synthetic Data Take Thai OCR?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03595)
*Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据在泰语OCR中的迁移效果，通过可控文档重建管线解耦"真实性"的多个因素（源域、页面上下文、字体多样性、二维布局、手写字形），发现字体多样性、二维结构和真实手写字形对迁移有益，而非文本上下文影响有限；并基于此构建了Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero模型，在不使用真实泰语文档OCR标签的情况下，将PaddleOCR-VL-1.6在印刷页面的中位CER从6.64%降至1.24%，手写页面从74.87%降至20.55%，且在所有5个评估集上超越Typhoon OCR v1 7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰语具有独特的字形系统，无法从英语或中文OCR迁移；现有泰语OCR模型虽有效但未厘清合成监督的具体贡献。合成OCR迁移在多种语言上已展示潜力，但先前工作同时变化多个生成因素，无法区分布局、非文本上下文、字体或训练粒度的各自贡献。本文旨在通过可控实验回答"合成数据能将泰语OCR推进多远"这一核心问题。

Method: 1. **可控文档重建管线**：将源文档中的文本像素擦除/修复后，用HarfBuzz排版OCR标签并按约束渲染到原区域，独立控制源域（域内/域外）、非文本上下文、字体分布、二维布局和手写字形来源；2. **训练设置**：使用Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct进行页面级和裁剪级训练对比；3. **消融实验**：系统性地移除各属性（非文本上下文→字体多样性→二维布局），观察对Heldout/Handwriting/Easy Handwriting三个测试集CER的影响；4. **源域对比**：对比域外合成（英语DocLayNet等翻译为泰语）与域内合成（重建真实泰语页面）；5. **手写渲染对比**：对比手写字体渲染与从iApp等构建的真实字形库（5953实例）渲染；6. **最终模型**：基于研究发现扩展训练数据至45723张合成页面，适配PaddleOCR-VL-1.6（0.9B参数）得到Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero。

Result: **关键消融发现**：① 移除非文本上下文影响不一致（最大1.62点变化）；② 移除字体多样性使手写集CER上升6.70-13.37点；③ 进一步扁平化布局继续恶化手写识别。**源域-训练粒度交互**：页级训练下，域内重建在Heldout上达到1.82%中位CER（接近真实监督的1.31%），手写集从43.99%降至36.27%；但裁剪级训练下域内重建在Heldout上反而从5.52%恶化至15.59%。**手写渲染**：真实字形实例在手写集上进一步降低CER至37.91%/30.66%（页级）和39.97%/35.66%（裁剪级），优于纯手写字体。**最终模型**：Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero在5个评估集（Heldout/Handwriting/Easy Handwriting/ThaiOCRBench/SEA-DocBench）上均超越其基座和7B Typhoon OCR，但落后于2B Typhoon OCR 1.5和Gemini 3.7 Flash。

Conclusion: 合成文档重建可在不使用真实泰语OCR标签的情况下产出具有竞争力的泰语OCR模型；字体多样性、二维结构和真实手写字形是提升迁移的关键因素，而非文本页面上下文贡献有限；源域匹配的价值取决于训练粒度（页级受益、裁剪级反而受损）。合成监督在典型印刷页面上接近真实监督，但仍存在严重失败案例和手写变体覆盖不足的问题。该方法为零资源/低资源语言的OCR合成训练提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: We investigate what makes synthetic OCR supervision transfer to real Thai documents and use the resulting insights to build Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero, a Thai OCR model adapted without OCR labels from real Thai document pages. Synthetic data provide exact labels at scale, but "realism" conflates source domain, page context, typography, spatial structure, and glyph variation. We disentangle these factors with a controlled document-reconstruction pipeline and evaluate each variant under page- and crop-level training on printed and handwritten Thai documents. Non-text context has little consistent effect, whereas typeface diversity, two-dimensional structure, and real handwriting glyphs improve transfer; moreover, source-domain matching depends on training granularity, with in-domain reconstruction approaching real printed supervision under page-level training (1.82% versus 1.31% median character error rate) but underperforming out-of-domain reconstruction under crop-level training (15.59% versus 5.52%). Guided by these findings, we adapt the 0.9B-parameter PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 into Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero using 45,723 synthetic pages: relative to its base checkpoint, it reduces median character error rate from 6.64% to 1.24% on printed pages and from 74.87% to 20.55% on handwriting and outperforms Typhoon OCR v1 7B on all five evaluation sets, showing that synthetic-only training can be competitive.

</details>


### [10] [Remember and Reweight: Enhancing Multi-Agent Debate with Experience Memory and Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03619)
*Xuanfa Jin,Zhijian Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出R²-MAD框架，通过经验记忆同时干预多智能体辩论中"共享误解"问题的两个根源——有偏的概念先验和放大的同伴偏差，以缓解多数智能体初始错误时辩论反而放大错误的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多智能体辩论（MAD）存在"共享误解"漏洞：当多数智能体初始收敛于错误答案时，辩论过程倾向于放大而非纠正错误。现有的MAD方法主要处理同伴偏差（peer skew），但忽略了智能体固有的有偏概念先验（concept prior），缺乏同时干预两个失效模式的机制。

Method: R²-MAD为每个智能体配备从历史辩论中积累的经验记忆，包含两个互补机制：(1) 辩论状态感知的检索策略——根据当前共识水平动态调整检索策略，高共识时检索多样化对比性经验，低共识时检索积极结果经验；(2) 记忆驱动的置信度加权——利用检索到的经验估计每个同伴的可靠性，生成置信度权重以调节同伴影响（通过sigmoid函数映射，并在prompt层面标注高/低置信度）。理论方面，将现有潜在概念分解框架扩展以纳入记忆修正和置信度加权，证明记忆作为先验的似然比修正，且置信度加权将多数主导的收敛速率从O(m)减缓至O(αm)。

Result: 在MATH500、MMLU-Pro Economics/Engineering、TruthfulQA四个基准上，使用Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-3-4B三个模型进行实验，R²-MAD在所有模型的平均准确率上均达到最优，优于CoT、Self-Consistency、MAD和MAD-M²基线。在Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-4o-mini上同样保持优势。消融实验证实记忆检索和置信度加权均有独立贡献。在"共享误解"子集（标准MAD第0轮多数智能体均答错的情况）上，R²-MAD提升最大。检索策略消融表明辩论状态感知策略优于随机、仅相似度、仅正向、仅多样性和固定λ等替代方案。置信度加权有效降低了正确→错误的翻转率并提高了错误→正确的恢复率。计算开销为MAD的约1.5–2倍，属于有界常数因子。

Conclusion: R²-MAD通过经验记忆同时校准概念先验和调节同伴偏差，有效缓解了多智能体辩论中的共享误解问题。实验证明两个组件互补且均有贡献，在共享误解场景下增益尤为显著。该框架依赖于可获得的结果信号进行记忆构建，且记忆库需在使用前审计以避免系统性错误经验的负面影响。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by having multiple agents iteratively refine their responses through discussion. However, MAD suffers from a critical vulnerability known as shared misconception: when a majority of agents initially converge on an incorrect answer, the debate process tends to amplify rather than correct the error. Existing methods primarily address peer skew but leave the agents' inherently biased concept priors unaddressed. To mitigate this systematic weakness, we propose R$^2$-MAD (Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate), a framework that equips agents with an experience memory accumulated from past debates. R$^2$-MAD intervenes on both failure modes through two complementary mechanisms: A debate-state-aware retrieval policy dynamically calibrates the concept prior by retrieving relevant historical evidence based on the current consensus level. Then these retrieved experiences provide a basis for estimating per-agent reliability, yielding confidence weights to modulate peer influence. Experiments on various benchmarks show that R$^2$-MAD achieves consistent improvements over existing single-agent and MAD baselines.

</details>


### [11] [</think> Doesn't Stop Reasoning: Analysis of Spurious CoT Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03633)
*Seunghee Koh,Sungjae Choi,Minchan Kwon,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了大型推理模型（LRMs）在思维链（CoT）早退方法中，通过注入结束思考标记（EoT, </think>）来触发推理到回答阶段转换时出现的“虚假CoT终止”现象。作者发现注入的EoT并不总是引发干净的转换，模型可能在回答阶段继续生成推理类内容，直到再次生成EoT。作者提出“退出标记注意力偏置”（EAB）方法，通过增加对注入EoT的注意力来减少这种现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费早退方法通过在中间位置注入EoT标记来缩短推理链，但作者观察到注入EoT后模型可能不会进入干净的回答阶段，而是在回答阶段继续推理类生成，直到再次生成EoT，这削弱了早退的效率。论文旨在表征这一现象并探究其机制。

Method: 1. 定义EoT再生率（ERR）量化再生现象；2. 将回答阶段按再生EoT分割为pre-regen和post-regen两段进行行为分析；3. 提出退出标记注意力偏置（EAB），通过在pre-softmax注意力logit上添加常数α来调节回答阶段对注入EoT的注意力；4. 在4个LRM（R1-Distill-1.5B/14B、Qwen3-14B、QwQ-32B）、5个基准（GSM8K、MATH-500、AMC、AIME、GPQA）和2种早退方法（DEER、DynaSoR）上进行实验。

Result: 1. EoT再生与早退压缩率正相关，压缩90-100%时ERR可达约40%；2. 再生EoT前的pre-regen段呈现推理类行为（包含Wait标记和boxed表达式），且长度与早退节省的推理token数强相关（ρ=0.67）；3. EAB的正α值减少ERR和回答长度，R1-Distill-14B上ERR降低超13个百分点；4. EAB效应具有token特异性（仅偏置EoT有效，偏置相邻标记反而加剧问题）和样本选择性（主要作用于有虚假终止的样本）；5. 控制实验支持EAB的因果作用。

Conclusion: 外部匹配显式think-block格式（如插入EoT标记）并不保证预期的推理到回答转换，因为插入的分隔符可能未充分参与模型的内部转换机制。注入的EoT是否有效取决于后续生成对其的关注程度，注意力不足是虚假CoT终止的一个原因。这一发现揭示了通过外部格式匹配控制LRMs的局限性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large reasoning models (LRMs) on complex tasks but often produces long, redundant traces. Recent training-free early-exit methods shorten these traces by choosing an intermediate point to stop reasoning. We study one such strategy that injects an end-of-think token (EoT, </think>) at this point to trigger the reasoning-to-answering transition, and find that the injected EoT does not always induce a clean answering phase. Answering-phase generation can continue before the model regenerates another EoT, with the span preceding this regenerated EoT scaling with the reasoning tokens saved by early exit and exhibiting continued reasoning behavior. We call this spurious CoT termination, where reasoning-like generation continues into the answering phase. We hypothesize that insufficient attention to the injected EoT contributes to spurious CoT termination and probe this hypothesis with Exit-token Attention Biasing (EAB). Across four LRMs, five benchmarks, and two early-exit methods, increasing attention to the injected EoT reduces spurious CoT termination and answering-phase length. These results reveal a limitation of controlling LRMs by externally matching their explicit think-block format. Inserting the EoT token conforms to this format but does not by itself guarantee the intended reasoning-to-answering transition. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [Enhancing Financial Question Answering: A Novel Benchmark Dataset of Banks' financial statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03654)
*Arianna Miola,Bruno Spaccavento,Lorenzo Silotto,Marco Bianchetti,Luca Cagliero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FinRAG-QA，一个针对银行财务报告跨机构问答的新型基准数据集，包含 999 个问题、209 份年报和 Pillar 3 报告，并系统性地分析了多阶段 RAG 管道中各组件的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务问答基准主要集中于美国单一机构文档分析，难以应对银行财务报告的复杂性、长度、技术语言及跨司法管辖区的不一致性。

Method: 构建跨欧洲与美国 24 家主要银行（2019-2023 年）的 FinRAG-QA 基准数据集；采用多阶段 RAG 管道（上下文块增强 + 检索优化嵌入模型 + 推理优化生成器），并通过消融实验评估各组件贡献。

Result: 上下文块增强与检索优化嵌入模型使 NDCG@10 从 0.322 提升至 0.710；推理优化生成器在检索正确前提下将答案准确率从 44.6% 提升至 79.0%（+34.4 个百分点），但生成延迟约为原来的 20 倍；交叉编码器重排序在首阶段排序较强时反而降低检索性能；单一最高排序块在生成阶段优于更大上下文。

Conclusion: FinRAG-QA 为跨机构、长篇财务文档问答提供了更具挑战性的评测平台；研究揭示了 RAG 管道中检索与生成阶段的权衡，并为组件选择（如重排序使用、上下文大小）提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The comparative analysis of banks' financial statements poses significant challenges for automated question answering systems due to their complexity, substantial length, technical language, and inhomogeneity of both textual and numerical content across different jurisdictions and institutions. We introduce FinRAG-QA, a novel benchmark dataset for financial question answering, which comprises 999 practitioner-curated questions on 10 standardised indicators, grounded in 209 annual and Pillar 3 reports from 24 major European and U.S. banks spanning 2019-2023. Unlike prior financial QA benchmarks, which centre on U.S. filings and single-institution analysis, FinRAG-QA targets cross-institutional retrieval over documents averaging 198k words, longer than any existing financial QA resource. On this benchmark we evaluate a multi-stage RAG pipeline and isolate the contribution of each component. Contextual chunk enrichment combined with a retrieval-optimised embedding model raises NDCG@10 from 0.322 to 0.710; conditional on the ground truth being retrieved, a reasoning-optimised generator raises answer accuracy from 44.6% to 79.0% (+34.4 percentage points), at roughly 20x the generation latency. We further show that cross-encoder reranking degrades retrieval when the first-stage ranking is already strong, and that a single top-ranked chunk outperforms larger contexts at generation time. Experiments were run in late 2024-early 2025 with the models available at that time.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond BLEU: A Case for Redefining Sign Language Translation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03734)
*Oline Ranum,Edward Fish,Simon Hadfield,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地批判了 BLEU-4 作为手语翻译（SLT）评测标准的局限性，提出一种基于开放权重 LLM 的问答（QA）评估协议来衡量"显著内容保留度"。该协议对释义更鲁棒、对语义反转更敏感、几乎不受目标端规律利用的影响，并在六个 SOTA SLT 模型上揭示出与 BLEU-4 截然不同的排名格局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLEU-4 在 SLT 这一多模态、低资源任务中并非可靠的评测指标。口语化 n-gram 精度指标容易被模型利用目标语言先验和虚假相关性来刷分，无法真实反映模型对视觉手语信号的理解能力。当前 SLT 基准（PHOENIX14T、CSL-Daily）数据规模小、语言范围窄，使这一问题被进一步放大。

Method: 1. **输入扰动分析**：通过空间掩码（手、脸）、时间帧打乱、高斯噪声注入，定量分析 BLEU-4 对视觉信号的依赖程度。
2. **词性归因分析**：使用 spaCy 对 gloss 和 text 做 POS 标注，分析 BLEU-4 的得分分别归属于哪些词类。
3. **QA 评估协议设计**：以开源 LLM（qwen2.5-32b）作为统一教师模型，从参考译文中提取内容单元 → 生成多项选择题（含"未提及"哨兵选项）→ 经三轮质量门控（round-trip、世界知识探测、歧义检查）→ 候选译文仅基于自身作答。
4. **口语语言基准验证**：在 WMT19/21（释义不变性 SNR）、OpusParcus（人工分级相关性）、PAWS-X（语义反转 AUC）、WMT 历年系统排序（Kendall 一致性）上验证协议有效性。
5. **SLT 应用**：在六个 SOTA 模型（GFSLT-VLP、FLA-LLM、CiCo、SignCL、C2RL、TwoStream-SingleStream）上对比 BLEU-4 与 QA，分析其对训练-测试重叠的敏感度。

Result: **BLEU-4 的根本缺陷**：
- 在掩码双手/面部、打乱帧序后仍维持 53%–86% 的基线分数，远高于纯噪声下限（≤5%）；其中 7.3±0.9 点的"无信号"得分已接近六个系统间 2.5 点的总差距。
- 约 37% 的得分来自功能词（介词、助词），这些词在 gloss 中几乎不存在，无法由视觉信号预测。
- 在训练-测试相似度高的句子上 BLEU-4 加速上升（Phoenix-2014T 上相似度敏感度达 23.5%/点），而 QA 几乎保持平稳（3.6%/点）。

**QA 协议的验证结果**：
- 释义不变性 SNR：QA 在 WMT19 上 12.5 vs BLEU-4 2.1；WMT21 上 23.0 vs 3.1（约 6–7 倍优势）。
- 语义反转 AUC：QA 在 PAWS-X 上 0.83 vs BLEU-4 0.61（七语种全部领先）。
- 与人工分级相关性：在 OpusParcus 上 QA 的 Spearman ρ 是 BLEU-4 的约两倍。
- 训练-测试重叠下：在 Phoenix-2014T 最不像训练集的十分之一测试句上，QA 保留 57% 的分数，BLEU-4 仅保留 12%。

**SLT 重排结果**：
- PHOENIX14T：五个 gloss-free 系统在 QA 上彼此无法区分（差距仅 3.6 点，置信区间全部重叠）；gloss-supervised 的 SingleStream 领先 9.3 点，而 BLEU-4 将其排在第二位。
- CSL-Daily：QA 得分跨度（11.6%–61.6%）远大于 BLEU-4 跨度，揭示 gloss-free 模型在该数据集上存在大量"零内容恢复"实例。
- 单系统保留的内容最多约 2/3，最少仅 1/10。

Conclusion: BLEU-4 在 SLT 上的差异并不构成手语理解进步的证据。提出的 QA 评估协议具有释义不变性强、对语义反转敏感、几乎不受目标端规律影响、与人工判断更一致、单卡 2–3 GPU 分钟/系统的高效性等优点。它重塑了 SLT 领域的性能图景：在 PHOENIX14T 上五个 gloss-free 系统实质上不可区分，gloss 监督能带来真实但被 BLEU-4 掩盖的显著优势。建议领域转向以"显著内容保留"为核心的评估范式。

Abstract: BLEU-4 is the standard metric for evaluating sign language translation (SLT), but spoken-language metrics may not adequately reflect sign language proficiency. The multimodal, low-resource context of SLT allows models to exploit spurious correlations and spoken-language priors, rather than learning stronger sign representations. In this paper, we evaluate the relationship between spatio-temporal understanding and BLEU-4 across six SLT models on Phoenix-2014T and CSL-Daily, showing that gains in BLEU-4 are not on their own evidence of better sign language understanding. This work introduces an alternative inspired by language-learning assessment, using an open-weight-LLM QA protocol that measures salient content preservation. It aligns more closely with human rankings and is six to seven times more paraphrase-invariant than BLEU-4. Applied to SLT, this protocol targets content transfer, is more robust to train-test overlap, and gives a different picture of the field: the five gloss-free systems are largely within noise of one another on Phoenix-2014T, while the gloss-supervised system stands 9.3 points higher, a gap invisible to BLEU-4.

</details>


### [14] [IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03781)
*Saikat Mondal,Mamta,Deeksha Varshney,Oana Cocarascu,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndicSafeEval 是一个针对印度语言的多语言说服式越狱评估基准，通过结合 6 种人类说服策略与 10 类安全风险，在印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语上构建 7,200 个对抗提示，用于黑盒评估开源大模型的多语言安全鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 安全评估以英语为中心，对低资源、文化多样的语言（如印度语言）中的对齐失效理解不足；缺乏结合本土语言与说服策略的多语言越狱基准。

Method: 三阶段构建数据集：(1) 用 GPT-5.1 生成六类说服策略（逻辑诉求、权威背书、错误表征、锚定、启动、确认偏误）的少样本示例；(2) 基于 Shen et al. 2024 的 300 条种子有害查询，通过少样本提示生成 1,800 条英文对抗提示；(3) 使用 Google Translate 翻译为四种印度语言，并通过 LaBSE 嵌入余弦相似度及人工评估验证语义一致性，最终形成 9,000 条多语言提示。采用 Gemini-2.5-Flash 作为 5 点制 LLM 评判器，指标包括 ASR、SRR、HLR、PCR。

Result: 五种开源模型（Sarvam-M、Gemma3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B、Llama-3-Nanda-10B-Chat）在所有语言上均存在显著越狱漏洞（平均 ASR 38.85%-77.68%）；权威背书与逻辑诉求最有效（平均 ASR >69%），确认偏误最弱；印地语因模型训练暴露多而最脆弱；治理决策、政治游说、物理伤害等政策类与技术类类别比仇恨言论更易被攻破；强制英语回复使 ASR 从 68.89% 升至 82.22%；Llama-3.3-70B 与 GPT-4o-mini 在物理伤害类别上仍达 ~46% ASR，表明漏洞随模型规模仍持续存在。

Conclusion: LLM 安全性能高度依赖语言与说服框架，印地语等高资源印度语言反而更脆弱，部分语言 ASR 较低反映语言能力弱而非对齐强；现有英语中心评估严重低估实际部署风险，亟需多语言、说服感知的对齐与评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at this https URL . Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [15] [Investigating the Ability of Large Language Models to Analyze Recipes for Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03967)
*Revathy Venkataramanan,Aditya Luthra,Venkatesan Nadimuthu,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种大语言模型（LLM）判断食谱是否适合糖尿病患者的能力。研究构建了包含7607个食谱的基准数据集，并设计了三种不同层次的提示策略（直接查询、上下文引导、示例上下文），揭示了LLM在医疗知识检索、概念理解和演绎推理三大核心挑战上的表现差异。结果表明Mistral-7B和Llama3.1-70B表现最优，且具备基于膳食指南进行推理能力的模型整体性能更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病影响着全球约8.3亿人，饮食管理是控制病情的关键手段。尽管已有大量糖尿病膳食指南（如MayoClinic、CDC、NIDDK发布），但这些指南内容繁杂，难以记忆和日常应用。现有AI饮食工具多专注于通用餐食规划，缺乏针对糖尿病的专项分析能力，且LLM在饮食建议的可靠性方面存在争议。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究LLM能否准确判断给定食谱对糖尿病患者的适用性。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：从MayoClinic、Diabetes UK、Diabetes Hub收集3807个适合糖尿病的食谱；基于MayoClinic指南中的关键词（如ribs、sausage、shortening、soda等）从Recipe1M数据集中筛选3800个不适合的食谱，构建平衡数据集。
2. 模型选择：测试四类共八个LLM，包括Mistral 7B/12B、Gemma2 2B/9B/27B、Llama 3.1 8B/70B、Llama 3.2 2B、ChatGPT-3.5。
3. 三种提示策略：直接查询提示（Prompt-1，测试内部知识检索）、上下文引导提示（Prompt-2，提供膳食指南概念）、示例上下文提示（Prompt-3，提供具体食物实例）。
4. 提出稳定度指标S = 1 - |Precision - Recall| 衡量模型精度与召回率的平衡。
5. 使用Gemma2 27B提取模型推理中使用的膳食指南关键词，分析推理能力与性能的相关性。

Result: 1. 整体表现：Mistral 7B在Prompt-1上表现最佳（准确率0.84），Llama3.1-70B在Prompt-2和Prompt-3上表现最佳（准确率0.83和0.85）。
2. 保守性偏差：大多数模型（尤其Gemma2系列）呈现高精度-低召回率特征，表明模型倾向于将食谱预测为"不适合"，以避免假阳性带来的健康风险。
3. ChatGPT异常：在Prompt-2中出现内部知识与外部知识冲突，召回率骤降至0.09。
4. 推理-性能相关性：模型推理中使用的膳食指南关键词越多，F1分数和稳定度越高。Mistral 7B产生的关键词最多（Prompt-2约1200个，Prompt-3约1008个）。
5. 概念理解差异：模型对健康碳水化合物、纤维、添加糖等概念理解较好，但对反式脂肪、深油炸食品、超加工食品等需结合烹饪方法分析的概念理解较弱。
6. 模型规模非决定性：Mistral 7B（较小模型）反而优于部分更大模型，表明对外部知识的对齐能力比参数规模更重要。

Conclusion: 本研究证实LLM具备一定评估糖尿病食谱适用性的能力，但仍面临三大核心挑战：（1）医疗知识检索——通用模型难以从庞大嵌入空间中精准检索完整指南，外部知识显式提供时性能更佳；（2）概念理解——对部分抽象概念（如反式脂肪、深油炸食品）理解不足，需结合烹饪方法分析；（3）演绎推理——具备基于膳食指南进行推理能力的模型表现显著更优。Mistral 7B和Llama 3.1-70B综合表现最佳。研究的临床和伦理意义在于：LLM在医疗饮食建议中应保持谨慎态度，需与临床医生建议结合使用，且必须经过领域专家验证。未来工作将聚焦于评估LLM对食材和烹饪方法进行组合推理的细粒度能力。

Abstract: Several studies have evaluated the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for meal planning, yielding positive outcomes. These models can process natural language inputs and leverage learned knowledge from their pretraining to generate meal plans. In this work, we investigate the ability of LLMs to analyze the suitability of given recipes for diabetes. The primary challenge for LLMs is to retrieve relevant dietary guidelines for diabetes, decompose recipes into ingredients and cooking methods, and apply these guidelines to determine the recipe's suitability. To study these challenges, we employ three kinds of prompts namely, (i) Direct Query Prompt (ii) Context-Guided Prompt, and (iii) Exemplary Context Prompt that incorporate different levels of diabetes dietary guidelines from medical sources. We introduce a benchmark dataset curated for this investigation consisting of 7607 recipes that include 3807 recipes suitable for diabetes and 3800 recipes not suitable for diabetes. Our results demonstrate that most LLMs are cautious in predicting recipes as suitable to prevent detrimental outcomes. Further, the models that can reason using the dietary guidelines performed better in predicting the suitability of recipes for diabetes. Overall, Mistral-7B and Llama 70B showed superior performance to their counterparts.

</details>


### [16] [Representational alignment yields generalizable safety in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04022)
*Lingyu Li,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前大语言模型(LLM)对齐方法主要优化可观察的响应，但在面对改写的有害意图时仍然脆弱。作者提出基于原型理论的表征相似性优化(Representational Similarity Optimization)方法，通过直接将LLM的潜在表征与人类道德判断的分类结构对齐，显著提升了模型在对抗性评估中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐方法(如RLHF等)主要优化输出响应层面的行为，但在对抗性提示或陌生形式的恶意意图面前，模型仍然脆弱。研究者受认知科学中原型理论(Prototype Theory)的启发，探究LLM是否真正"理解"了人类的道德概念结构，而非仅仅在表面上模仿合规响应。

Method: 1. 在23个LLM上探测其内部表征是否编码了基于原型理论的道德概念分类结构。2. 提出**表征相似性优化(Representational Similarity Optimization)**：使用25万条人类道德标注数据，直接在潜在表征空间中约束LLM的表征几何结构(如表征相似性矩阵)与人类道德判断的分类结构对齐，而不直接监督生成的响应文本。3. 在匹配实验下与标准行为对齐方法(在响应层面做监督)进行对比。

Result: 1. 当前LLM的道德概念分类结构与人类原型式分类仅存在**弱保持**：模型常常无法区分对立的道德类别，也无法保留类别内的细粒度典型性差异，这一缺陷在不同参数规模和不同对齐阶段均存在。2. 在匹配数据量下，标准行为对齐虽提升了显式判断准确率，但基本未改变内部分类结构，反而在对抗评估中脆弱性增加。3. 重组道德分类结构(表征相似性优化)虽然在显式判断上提升较温和，但**在多种模型规模、多个基准和多种攻击策略下持续提升对抗鲁棒性**。

Conclusion: 研究为"基于原型的分类有助于行为适应性"这一观点提供了功能性证据，表明将这种表征原理迁移到LLM中，可在对抗条件下产生可泛化的安全性。这暗示真正的安全对齐应关注模型内部的表征结构，而不仅仅是输出行为的表层合规。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) is essential for their safe deployment. Current alignment methods mainly optimize observable responses, yet models remain vulnerable when the same harmful intent is recast in unfamiliar or adversarial forms that humans can easily recognize. Prototype theory offers an account of this adaptability. Human concepts are represented around central cases, and new instances are categorized according to their graded typicality relative to these prototypes. Here we show that such categorization of moral concepts is weakly preserved in current LLMs. Across 23 LLMs, models often failed to distinguish opposed moral categories or preserve fine-grained typicality within each category. These deficits persist across parameter sizes and alignment stages. We developed representational similarity optimization, which directly aligns the latent representations in LLMs with the categorization expressed in human moral judgements, without supervising generated responses. In matched experiments using the same 251,334 moral annotations, standard behavioral alignment learned the intended moral judgements at the response level while leaving the categorization structure largely unchanged and increasing vulnerability across adversarial evaluations. Reorganizing moral categorization produced more modest gains in explicit judgements but consistently improved adversarial robustness across model scales on diverse benchmarks and attack strategies. Our findings provide functional support for the view that prototype-based categorization contributes to behavioral adaptability. They also show that transferring this representational principle to LLMs yields generalizable safety under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [17] [Translation as a Decision Space: A Multi-Agent Perspective on Low-Resource Dialect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04048)
*Hasan Alkhder,Mohammad Abboush,Igor Tchappi,Ahmet Zengin,Amro Najjar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经机器翻译（NMT）的输出重新定义为结构化决策空间，通过多个自主翻译智能体（agent）探索土耳其语-叙利亚阿拉伯语翻译的多样化路径，将不同路径间的分歧视为可解释的"行为信号"而非错误，为低资源方言翻译提供新的可解释性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMT系统对单一输入仅产生单一输出，掩盖了多语言解码中隐含的替代决策路径。在低资源方言场景中，多个语言学上合法的翻译可能在词汇真实性、语域（register）和结构稳定性上存在差异，这种不透明性成为突出问题。因此需要一种能够揭示和利用翻译决策空间多样性的方法。

Method: 将翻译建模为共享多语言主干上的多个自主智能体所探索的结构化决策空间，设计三种翻译智能体：(1) 零样本直接翻译（zero-shot direct translation）；(2) 通过轻量化微调实现的方言稳定化翻译（dialect-stabilized translation）；(3) 通过英语进行的中介翻译（pivot translation）。在5,000句对话句子上进行评估，方言稳定化训练使用来自电视对话和MADAR-Turk资源的额外5,000个土耳其语-叙利亚句对。评估指标不是传统性能指标，而是使用方言标记频率、与标准阿拉伯语的词汇接近度和结构方差来量化结构化行为位移（structured behavioral displacement）。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 轻量化稳定化使方言标记使用率近乎翻倍，从0.2266提升至0.4988，同时显著降低了结构不稳定性；(2) 中介翻译引入归一化压力（normalization pressure）和可测量的压缩效应；(3) 零样本翻译表现出最高的决策方差。这些结果表明不同翻译路径之间的差异能够揭示多语言模型中潜在的决策灵活性。

Conclusion: 翻译智能体之间的分歧（divergence）揭示了多语言模型内部潜在的决策灵活性，本文为低资源方言生成提供了一个有原则的可解释性框架。核心贡献在于将翻译从单一输出任务重新定义为可探索的结构化决策空间，以智能体行为差异作为可解释信号。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) systems typically produce a single output per input, obscuring the alternative decision trajectories implicitly available within multilingual decoding. This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [18] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了"先OPD后RL"的顺序训练方案与现有联合优化方法，发现在逻辑与数学推理任务上，简单分阶段训练持续优于所有联合优化基线，并从pass@k行为、学习动力学和参数更新三个角度给出了机理分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR提供稀疏但与真实任务对齐的奖励信号，OPD提供来自教师的密集token级监督，二者各有优劣。已有工作（KDRL、SRPO、HDPO、TRRD、RLSD等）尝试在单步内融合两种信号，但这些联合优化方法是否最优尚不明确，且缺乏系统性的理解。

Method: 1) 在统一的token级策略梯度框架下，将现有OPD-RLVR组合方法归为两类范式：加权加性（weighted-additive）和教师调制（teacher-modulated）；2) 提出"OPD-then-RL"两阶段顺序方案——先做OPD至切换点S（默认step 60），再做纯GRPO；3) 从pass@k分析、学习动力学（KL分解为交叉熵与熵、top-K重叠与共享质量）以及参数更新的符号冲突率（SCR）三个角度进行机理分析；4) 研究切换时机准则与OPD vs SFT冷启动比较。实验使用Qwen3-1.7B-Base/0.6B-Base作为学生，Qwen3-8B作为教师，跨Qwen与OLMo两个模型族验证。

Result: 在K&K/Zebra/Countdown逻辑推理及MATH-500/AMC23/AIME24/AIME25数学推理上，OPD-then-RL在pass@1平均均达到最优（逻辑80.6、数学31.8），对纯OPD、纯RL及所有联合基线（KDRL、SRPO、HDPO、RLSD、TRRD、KDRL-Annealing）均有一致优势，逻辑推理上对部分基线领先高达26.7 pass@1点；配对bootstrap检验显示对9个数学基线中6个显著领先，其余3个为统计持平。机理分析表明：OPD扩展教师支持解的覆盖（提升pass@k），RL在该支持内锐化（提升pass@1）；联合优化导致参数更新与OPD产生符号冲突（KDRL top-100% SCR达15.02%，TRRD为6.58%，而OPD-then-RL仅3.38%），损害覆盖扩展。切换点实验显示OPD验证分数决定后续RL上限；OPD作为RL冷启动全面优于SFT（+5.9 p@1），且RL后的增益（+7.2 avg p@32）也更大。

Conclusion: 两阶段顺序训练（OPD-then-RL）通过将两种信号解耦为互补阶段，使OPD负责能力边界扩展、RL负责边界内锐化，从而避免联合优化中的信号相互干扰。这为结合密集教师监督与稀疏可验证奖励的后训练流水线提供了一条简洁而强力的基线方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to this http URL provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [19] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练中LLM的知识获取机制，提出"辅助视图"(auxiliary views，即同一知识的概念性重构)是有益学习的关键因素。通过控制实验证明：将token预算从文档复述分配给辅助视图能显著改善学习效果（包括事实记忆），且效果随模型规模增大而增强。此外，该效果不依赖于教师模型强度，并涉及FFN层的特定层级偏置和参数压缩模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已认识到数据去重、过滤、覆盖度、质量和多样性对预训练的重要性，但鲜少关注"知识应以何种形式在自然语言中表征"。本文旨在探讨在复杂领域知识（如生物医学、法律）中，如何表征知识以促进LLM更有效的学习，并解释"数据多样性"为何有效的底层机制。

Method: 使用OLMo-2系列模型（1B/7B/13B/32B）和Qwen-2.5-7B进行控制实验。数据集涵盖36份文档（计算机科学论文、法律意见、医学病例报告，均在OLMo-2预训练截止日期之后）。为每份文档合成三种"辅助视图"：教科书章节、Stack Exchange问答、博客文章。同时生成49个释义作为对照。设计了事实性探针（factual probes, 6435个）和推理探针（inference probes, 430个）。在固定token预算下比较三种训练条件：Source（仅原文）、Para. 9（原文+9个释义）、Para. 9 + Aux.（原文+9个释义+辅助视图）。通过FFN权重与基模型的逐通道余弦距离、相对范数变化和Gini系数分析机制层面的差异。

Result: （1）重复对知识获取必要，释义仅在小批量下有帮助，大批量时其优势消失；（2）将token从文档重复分配给辅助视图可改善学习，反直觉地提升事实记忆；（3）辅助视图效果与教师模型强度无关（r=-0.14），仅与生成文本量正相关（r=+0.62）；（4）上下文知识和先决知识在存在知识缺口时有互补作用；（5）机制上，辅助视图使FFN变化在中间和最终层更大、上中部（16-24层）更小，体现"压缩"效应和层级偏置，且此特征仅在≥7B模型中出现。

Conclusion: 概念性多元的知识表征（辅助视图），而非单纯的语言学释义，是预训练数据多样性的关键因素。更大模型通过参数效率更高的编码方式和层级偏置的再分配，更善于整合多元视图。实践中建议：补充先决知识、在数据稀缺时使用释义、采用合成辅助视图增强。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [20] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ESPO提出三阶段框架（诊断-生成-选择）解决进化式提示优化中的"提示膨胀"问题，通过结构化错误聚类、多策略候选生成和Bootstrap稳定性选择，在7个NLP基准上以更短提示（缩短47%）实现比GEPA更高的准确率（+3.76 pp），并跨模型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式提示优化方法（如GEPA）存在"提示膨胀"问题：每次迭代追加规则与例外，导致提示最长可达3倍却未带来准确率提升。作者将此归因于三个结构性缺陷：(1) 不完整的错误观测（每次仅随机抽样3个错误，需约15轮才能覆盖所有错误模式）；(2) 有限的搜索多样性（单一变异算子产生偏见锁定）；(3) 不可靠的选择（在小验证集上的点估计易受多重检验问题影响，选中噪声驱动的冗长候选）。

Method: ESPO将提示优化重构为结构化统计估计问题，分为三个原则性阶段：(1) 诊断阶段：一次性收集所有训练错误，通过反射LLM聚类为3–7个结构化错误模式φ；(2) 生成阶段：使用K=4种互补策略独立生成候选——诊断修订S1、合并S2、剪枝S3、事实注入S4，再经两轮交叉授粉与定向优化，候选池上限N=10；(3) 选择阶段：通过B=20次Bootstrap重采样进行稳定性选择，平局时偏好更短提示。理论分析将测试时泛化差距分解为偏置下界（受多样性影响）、探索增益（受K影响，σ·Φ⁻¹(1−1/K)）和选择误差（受B控制，O(√(ln K/(n_val·B))））三项，各对应一个阶段。GEPA被证明是K=1, B=1, m=3的退化情形。

Result: 在7个公开NLP基准（Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, PUPA）上，从故意设计的弱提示出发，使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为学生模型：ESPO平均准确率达74.67%，相比GEPA（70.91%）提升+3.76 pp（成对t检验显著，α=0.05），在所有7个数据集上均匹配或超过GEPA，提示长度缩短47%（1004 vs 1878字符），推理延迟更低。跨模型实验（Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5）显示ESPO在每个学生模型上均获得最佳平均准确率，Qwen3 GSM8K提升最显著（15.00%→91.40%，比GEPA高+56 pp）。约束版GEPA实验表明仅控制长度并不能提升准确率（+0.09%）。消融实验验证三个组件均为必需：仅增加多样性而无Bootstrap选择反而损害性能（−1.20%）。超参数敏感性实验显示在默认值附近均稳健（变化在5%以内）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过三阶段分解为进化式提示优化中的提示膨胀与准确率停滞问题提供了统一解决方案；理论保证（偏置下界、探索增益、选择精度的三个引理）解释了各组件的必要性。实验证明ESPO在准确率、提示简洁性和推理速度三方面同时优于GEPA，跨模型泛化良好，对反射模型选择具有鲁棒性。局限性：诊断阶段假设错误标签可用（限制了开放式生成场景的扩展）；理论假设的独立性在实践中部分违反（策略间Jaccard相似度0.62、Pearson相关0.48）；覆盖范围限于7个NLP基准，工具使用、长上下文、代码生成、多轮对话未涉及。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [21] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"通过训练编译"方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的本地神经函数（LoRA 适配器+小型解释器），以分钟级编译换取运行时零远程调用的高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多文本函数难以用规则实现，但调用大模型又成本高、延迟大、依赖外部服务。需要一种折中方案：用大模型构建一次，得到可重复使用的本地小模型函数。

Method: 两阶段流程：(1) 教师大模型根据自然语言规范合成任务特定的输入-输出示例数据集；(2) 用这些示例对共享冻结的小型解释器（Qwen3-0.6B）进行 LoRA 微调，生成可独立运行、可版本化的程序包。结合流式合成与训练重叠、作业队列调度和缓存复用实现分钟级交互式编译。

Result: 在 FuzzyBench-Hard 基准上语义准确率达 83.6%（PAW 快速编译器为 22.4%），编译耗时约 50.9 秒。部署了多网站助手（30 个编译程序）、语言控制的 3D 化身（44 例中 43 例正确）和英-Claudish 双向翻译服务（11 天内完成 10 万次翻译）。

Conclusion: "通过训练编译"在 PAW 的速度-精度权衡曲线上增加了高精度一端，将适配编译转化为类似软件构建步骤的离线过程，使大语言模型成为工具构建者而非运行时依赖。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


### [22] [CROCODIL: Cross-Model Code Editing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03894)
*Linghan Zhong,Aditya Thimmaiah,Jayanth Srinivasa,Milos Gligoric,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在编辑其他LLM生成的代码时存在过度编辑问题，并提出CROCODIL后训练框架，通过相似度奖励和执行奖励的组合，使用GRPO优化来减少编辑量同时保持功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发团队常同时使用多个LLM助手，不同模型有不同的编码风格偏好。当一个模型编辑另一个模型生成的代码时，是否会因为风格差异而产生过度编辑？研究发现确实存在这一问题，且提示工程无法有效解决。

Method: 1) 构建基于GitHub Rust crates的PR语料库，包含多种上下文项；2) 使用5个LLM（Qwen3.5 35B A3B、GPT-OSS 20B、Olmo3 7B、Olmo3.1 32B、Haiku 4.5）分别作为实现者和编辑器，通过Levenshtein编辑距离量化自编辑与跨编辑差异；3) 提出CROCODIL框架，使用相似度奖励（惩罚大改动，基于开发者原始PR编辑规模归一化）和执行奖励（构建成功+测试通过率）的乘积，通过GRPO在Olmo3 7B上用LoRA进行后训练。

Result: 研究发现4个开源权重LLM在16个模型配对中有14对存在自编辑小于跨编辑的现象。CROCODIL将Olmo3 7B在所有实现者代码上的编辑距离减半，同时在除自身外的所有实现者上提升了构建和测试通过率。提示工程方法无法持续降低编辑量或提升编辑成功率。

Conclusion: LLM确实会对其他LLM生成的代码进行过度编辑；CROCODIL通过强化学习框架有效减少了过度编辑，同时保持甚至提升了功能正确性，证明了训练信号比提示工程更有效。局限性在于仅研究了Rust语言、函数级别范围和有限的模型池。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous tools for code generation and editing. However, development teams often use multiple LLM assistants. Different developers may prefer different models, and individual developers may switch between models across different coding sessions. Because of this, the edits any one model makes are frequently applied to foreign code originally generated by another model. These LLMs are often trained on different datasets, and as a result have different stylistic preferences. Do LLMs behave differently when they edit foreign code originally written by a different LLM with a different coding style? We find that models tend to make more, and often excessive, edits on foreign code. We introduce CROCODIL (Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs), a post-training framework for reducing excessive edits while preserving functional correctness. CROCODIL's similarity reward penalizes large changes, while its execution reward scores build and test success. We use the product of these two rewards to encourage the policy to decrease the edit size without decreasing the edit task success rate. CROCODIL is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [BharatGather: A Culturally-Informed Benchmark Dataset for Misinformation and Fake News Detection in Indian Public Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02895)
*Parth Bramhecha,Smit Deshmukh,Sairaj Bodhale,Adwait Borate,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BharatGather数据集，这是一个针对印度大规模公共事件（如宗教节日、政治集会、文化活动）中虚假信息检测的基准数据集。该数据集包含14,646条记录，通过三层采集管道构建：来自五家印度事实核查平台的网络爬虫数据、YouTube新闻视频转录文本以及使用LLM（Qwen3-32B）生成的合成对抗样本。研究使用bert-base-uncased模型进行二分类实验，结果表明冻结特征提取方法准确率仅为77.3%，而微调后可达96.1%。数据集填补了现有研究中缺乏文化语境敏感、面向印度特定事件的虚假信息检测资源的空白，为开发区域适配的虚假信息识别系统提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度大型公共活动（宗教节日、政治集会等）是虚假信息快速传播的高风险场景，可能引发公共安全和社会凝聚力的严重问题。然而，现有的虚假信息检测基准数据集大多基于通用领域或西方语境，缺乏对印度社会文化背景（如宗教、政治敏感性）的捕捉能力，无法有效支持区域性部署。因此，亟需一个针对印度特定公共事件生态、具有文化情境感知能力的数据集来推动该领域研究。

Method: 数据集构建采用三层混合采集管道：(1) Tier I - 通过定制爬虫从AltNews、BoomLive、Factly、The Quint、NewsMeter五家印度事实核查机构抓取10,691条记录，对非结构化来源使用Qwen3-32B进行语义提取；(2) Tier II - 从BBC News、NDTV等认证YouTube新闻频道提取360条转录文本；(3) Tier III - 仅基于高完整性种子记录，使用Qwen3-32B生成3,595条对抗性合成样本（含真实变体和虚假变体，采用差异化的提示工程和词汇重叠质量门控）。所有记录统一为包含10个属性的标准化模式。实验部分采用bert-base-uncased模型，对比冻结特征提取与微调两种范式，使用加权交叉熵损失和验证集macro F1进行模型选择。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) BERT冻结特征提取配置下测试准确率为77.3%，macro F1为0.769，模型倾向于将多数样本预测为"真实"类，对少数类（虚假信息）召回率极低；(2) BERT部分微调配置下测试准确率显著提升至96.1%，macro F1和weighted F1均达到0.960，两个类别表现均衡（虚假信息类F1=0.95，真实类F1=0.97）；(3) 验证集与测试集的macro F1差距约1.5个百分点，表明模型具有良好的内部效度。混淆矩阵显示微调模型对879条虚假信息样本正确识别844条，对1,199条真实样本正确识别1,153条。

Conclusion: BharatGather数据集是首个针对印度大规模公共事件虚假信息分类的文化情境感知基准，填补了该领域事件特定数据集的空白。实验证明合成对抗样本对表面词汇分析具有挑战性，验证了领域特定微调的必要性。然而，数据集存在以下局限性：(1) 编辑覆盖偏差导致地方性事件代表性不足；(2) LLM介导的语义提取可能引入自动化近似误差；(3) 跨频道转录文本存在主题重叠风险；(4) 仅覆盖英语内容，缺乏印地语及地方语言多语言记录；(5) 时间快照性质限制了对演变中新叙事的适应性。未来研究方向应包括多语言编码器集成、检索增强验证框架以及增强类间语义邻近性以构建更具挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Large-scale public events, such as religious festivals, political rallies, and cultural gatherings, are increasingly vulnerable to the rapid dissemination of misinformation, posing substantial risks to public safety and social cohesion. While automated fake news detection has seen significant methodological progress, existing benchmarks frequently fail to capture the socio-cultural nuances and event-specific dynamics characteristic of the Indian context. This paper introduces BharatGather, a curated, multi-source dataset specifically engineered for binary misinformation classification within the ecosystem of Indian mass gatherings. The corpus comprises 14,646 records constructed through a hybrid pipeline involving systematic web scraping of prominent fact-checking platforms, multimedia transcript extraction, and Large Language Model (LLM)-mediated synthetic augmentation to ensure narrative diversity. By providing a resource tailored to the unique complexities of event-aware misinformation in India, this work facilitates the development of culturally informed detection systems and establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating their performance in high-stakes public environments.

</details>


### [24] [Unifying Conformal Language Tasks with In-Context Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03005)
*Xiao Shi Huang,Chen-Yuan Lin,Bruce Kuwahara,Kin Kwan Leung,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conformal Relevance 框架，使用上下文学习（ICL）示例筛选与集成策略构建评分函数，统一了多种 NLP 内容选择任务（如摘要、抽取式 QA、PII 检测等）下的共形预测（conformal prediction）召回式覆盖保证。通过四种机制多样的 ICL 示例选择策略（anchor_dpp、pattern_dpp、bm25、random）构成的 Ens4 集成，在七个跨领域数据集上均显著优于手工提示基线，最高可减少约 50% 的保留文本长度。同时理论分析了 K=2 时集成的互补性条件（Comp > S_max - S_min）及 O(1/K) 的边际收益递减界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NLP 内容选择任务（如摘要、抽取式 QA）依赖手工设计的 LLM 提示作为共形预测的评分函数，以同时满足覆盖率和简洁性约束。但手动提示工程劳动密集、任务特定且脆弱，难以扩展到不同相关性定义的任务。因此需要一种通用、自动化的相关性评分框架，能够跨任务提供共形覆盖保证的同时提升简洁性。

Method: 提出 Conformal Relevance 框架，将召回式共形校准与基于 ICL 的相关性评分函数相结合。用 ICL 示例筛选替代手工任务描述，构建四种机制多样的评分器：anchor_dpp（基于语义嵌入的锚点+DPP 多样性选择）、pattern_dpp（DPP 在相关性方向向量上的选择）、bm25（词法检索）和 random（均匀随机，作为正则化器）。这些评分器的均值得分作为单一共形评分函数（Ens4）。理论上证明：均值集成保持 1-α 覆盖保证；K=2 时集成优于个体的充分条件为互补性 Comp > S_max - S_min；边际收益以 O(1/K) 递减。

Result: 在五个领域（金融、百科、医学、通用 QA、法律）的七个数据集上，单一 Ens4 配置均优于任务特定的 ICL0 手工提示基线（MAP 提升 +0.090 至 +0.170，相对提升 12%–59%）以及最优单策略 oracle（最高 +0.091）。在不同 α、β 值下，Ens4 在同等覆盖率下过滤更多无关内容（最高约 50% 长度缩减）。覆盖率经验上保持在目标 1 个百分点以内。集成大小 K=4 在所有数据集上一致最佳，但边际收益递减，与 O(1/K) 理论界一致。互补性条件经验满足时 99% 出现实际优势。跨模型（Llama3-8B、Qwen3-8B、GPT-5.6-terra）验证稳健。温度多样性和监督分类器控制实验均弱于 ICL 多样性，确认检索诱导的互补性是关键机制。

Conclusion: Conformal Relevance 框架成功统一了多种 NLP 内容选择任务，无需手工提示工程即可提供共形覆盖保证。ICL 示例筛选策略的均值集成通过检索诱导的互补性显著提升评分函数质量，从而在固定覆盖率下获得更简洁的预测集。理论分析准确刻画了集成优势和边际收益递减，为实际部署提供了原则性指导。未来工作可探索自适应聚合方案、分级相关性标签的共形风险控制，以及向生成任务的扩展。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks, such as summarization and extractive question answering, reduce to retrieving relevant content from documents under two constraints: coverage, retaining enough pertinent information to achieve some goal, and conciseness, removing as much irrelevant information as possible. Conformal prediction methods have been used to guarantee coverage, and must be optimized for conciseness through design of a score function. State-of-the-art scoring functions use hand-engineered LLM prompts asking the model to rate the importance of content, but manual prompt engineering is labor-intensive and task-specific. We introduce the Conformal Relevance framework which uses in-context learning example curation and ensembling to create a score function which maintains coverage while improving conciseness with minimal manual input. We demonstrate this framework's application on seven NLP tasks, and also theoretically study the impact of diversity for ensembled conformal scores, giving a complementarity condition that characterizes when ensembling improves worst-case sentence scores, and a saturation bound on ensemble improvement.

</details>


### [25] [MemoryLACE: Memory Lifecycle-Aware Consolidation and Evidence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03201)
*Meriem Yacoubi,Pia Schmidt,Nenad Petrovic,Ahmed Frikha,Martin Kirchhoff,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemLACE是一个轻量级的LLM长期记忆框架，通过显式建模文本证据的生命周期关系（合并、取代、矛盾）而非构建全局知识图谱，在BEAM和StructMemEval上取得了最佳同骨干性能，同时比Hindsight基线快2.99倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本记忆系统检索效率高但记忆间关系隐式，而结构化方法（图、层次、反思）计算成本大。核心问题：如何在不构建全局结构的前提下，让智能体保留证据演化并联合检索相关记忆。

Method: 两层架构：(1) Stage I 记忆构建与合并：原子记忆提取 + 候选选择（语义+实体+一跳生命周期邻居）+ 交叉编码器重排序 + 单决策合并（merge/update/contradict/no_action）+ 生命周期状态更新 + 局部批量演化修复遗漏关系。(2) Stage II 证据检索：混合检索（RRF融合语义/关键词/结构化/主题）+ 生命周期扩展（超代链+矛盾一跳）+ 证据单元分组 + 链感知重排序 + 上下文打包 + 生命周期顺序生成。

Result: BEAM 100K子集：Qwen3.5-9B骨干下MemLACE总体51.9%，超过Hindsight(50.3%)、LIGHT(42.1%)、SimpleMem(30.7%)；Qwen3.5-4B下45.4%为最佳4B系统。StructMemEval：MemLACE 52.08% vs Mem-Agent 35.00%，完美解决所有14个状态追踪和10个树推理场景。运行时间比Hindsight减少66.6%（2.99×加速）。消融：生命周期扩展(-5.16)和时间感知(-4.97)是最大贡献者。

Conclusion: 显式建模文本证据的局部生命周期足以显著改善长期记忆推理，无需全面知识图谱或全局反思。证据演化而非全局结构是长期记忆的主要挑战。未来工作可通过专门的聚合、计数和数值推理算子扩展此框架。

Abstract: Long-term LLM agents must preserve information across interactions while distinguishing repeated evidence, historical states, updates, and unresolved contradictions. Existing textual memory systems retrieve semantically relevant memories efficiently but often leave these relationships implicit, whereas richer structured approaches model them through global graphs, hierarchical abstractions, or reflection at greater complexity. We introduce MemoryLACE (MemLACE), a lightweight memory framework that explicitly models the lifecycle of textual evidence through sparse merge, supersession, and contradiction relations while preserving atomic natural-language memories and their provenance. Rather than retrieving memories independently, MemLACE reconstructs relation-aware evidence units that expose current, historical, supporting, and conflicting evidence for downstream reasoning. Across BEAM and StructMemEval, using open-weight and proprietary LLM backbones, MemLACE achieves the highest overall performance in same-backbone comparisons while reducing end-to-end runtime on BEAM by 66.6% relative to Hindsight, the strongest reported reflective-memory baseline. Ablation studies identify lifecycle expansion and temporal awareness as the principal contributors to these gains. Together, the results demonstrate that explicitly modeling the local lifecycle of textual evidence is sufficient to substantially improve long-term memory reasoning without requiring comprehensive knowledge graphs or global reflection.

</details>


### [26] [SWIM: Student Writing Simulation via Proficiency-Conditioned Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03215)
*Heejin Do,Jakub Kontak,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIM任务，将学生写作模拟定义为以熟练度为条件的作文生成，系统比较了提示工程（rubric-grounded prompting）、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（GRPO+PAR）三种方法，发现显式监督显著优于纯提示方法，但低熟练度写作的语言形式真实性仍是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM学生模拟研究主要关注短回答、辅导对话或行为预测，写作作为最能体现学生多维度能力（内容、组织、词汇、语法等）的行为却鲜有探索。本文旨在探究语言模型能否真实模拟不同熟练度学生的长文写作，并填补这一研究空白。

Method: SWIM将任务形式化为"给定写作提示和目标熟练度profile，生成匹配的作文"。三种方法层层递进：(1) Rubric-grounded prompting，包括Contrastive Trait Prompting (CTP)和Score-level Rubric-Lookup Prompting (SRLP)，结合few-shot；(2) SFT在真实(作文, 分数)对上微调Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B；(3) GRPO+PAR（Proficiency Alignment Reward），利用冻结的ArTS多维度AES模型作为评估器，奖励预测分数与目标profile在所有维度上的trait-normalized accuracy。评估采用五折交叉验证，以QWK衡量profile对齐程度。

Result: 在ASAP/ASAP++上：Claude Sonnet最佳提示配置平均trait-level QWK仅0.577，GPT 5.4仅0.422，Qwen提示几乎为零。SFT大幅提升Qwen2.5-7B至0.474、Qwen3-4B至0.428。GRPO+PAR进一步提升至0.618和0.674（最佳）。所有方法在content-oriented traits（Content、Prompt Adherence、Language、Narrativity）上对齐远好于form-oriented traits（Organization、Word Choice、Sentence Fluency、Conventions）。提示方法倾向于"理想化高熟练度学生"，平均归一化分0.74且25%满分；SFT（0.57）和GRPO（0.53）更接近人类分布（0.58）。低熟练度时，提示通过过度的大小写和拼写错误"表面破坏"实现，训练方法能恢复句法结构但语法错误仍偏少。未发现记忆化或生成崩溃。

Conclusion: 显式监督（尤其是SFT+GRPO+PAR）能显著强化profile对齐，但profile对齐≠行为真实性。指令调优LLM的流畅性先验是根本瓶颈：模型能很好生成高熟练度写作，但对低熟练度学生的真实语言错误、不流畅性和结构弱点仍然难以忠实再现。未来需要超越分数对齐，专注于proficiency-calibrated的语言形式忠实模拟。

Abstract: Writing proficiency manifests in how students develop content, organize ideas, choose words, and use language. Despite growing interest in LLM-based student simulation, whether LLMs can reproduce such multidimensional variation in extended writing remains largely unexplored. In this work, we explore if language models can realistically simulate student writing, and introduce SWIM, a task that formulates Student Writing sIMulation as proficiency-conditioned essay generation. We evaluate prompting, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) methods for writing simulation using automated essay scoring as a measure of profile alignment. Extensive experiments reveal that prompting provides limited proficiency control, even for strong proprietary LLMs with rubric-grounded strategies. In particular, while models can adjust content-oriented traits, they struggle to reproduce the lexical, grammatical, and organizational variation in different proficiency levels. SFT substantially improves alignment, while RL with the proposed proficiency-alignment reward yields further gains across all writing traits and essay prompts. Our findings suggest that explicit supervision enables substantially stronger profile alignment than prompting alone, while authentic low-proficiency writing remains challenging to reproduce.

</details>


### [27] [Counterfactual Fairness Audits of Multi-Step Clinical LLM Agents Require a Measured Per-Action Instability Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03221)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu,Manpreet Singh,Deepak Parashar,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出临床LLM智能体的反事实公平性审计存在严重的测量噪声问题，必须在每个动作上报告"不稳定底限"（instability floor），否则无法解读人口学对比所得到的翻转率差异。论文同时发布了FairMedAgent评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床AI公平性审计中，反事实翻转率（counterfactual flip rate）是衡量智能体是否因人口学标签差异而对临床相同患者做出不同决策的标准工具。但作者指出，仅报告翻转率是不可解读的，因为智能体自身的解码不确定性会带来不可忽视的"基线噪声"，从而淹没真实的不公平信号。

Method: 方法包括：（1）在16个合成临床情景上对同一参考条件运行10次重复（仅解码变化），得到每动作的不稳定底限；（2）在第二个模型上重复实验以验证底限的普适性；（3）通过多数投票聚合（majority vote）测试底限的可减少性，并进行零模拟（null simulation）分解残差；（4）提出FairMedAgent评估框架：六阶段智能体轨迹（分诊→医嘱→环境→再评估→处方→文档），含可接受动作带（acceptable-action band）、PROGRESS-Plus对齐的人口学条件（含LEP和交叉性轴）、三个控制臂（identical re-render、sham-attribute、rare-token）、混淆配对登记表；（5）使用约束性wild cluster bootstrap + Webb六点权重 + McNemar精确检验 + Benjamini-Yekutieli多重比较校正。

Result: 主要结果：（1）池化不稳定底限为8.7%（374/4320次比较），按动作差异达8倍——ICU升级2.2% vs 受控物质警示17.9%；（2）第二个模型池化底限6.7%，六个动作排名Spearman相关0.94（精确p=0.017），表明底限并非单系统产物；（3）五次多数投票仅去除39%的底限（从8.7%降至5.3%），曲线快速趋于平坦，零模拟将残差归因于每格率异质性；（4）小样本（4个情景）底限为0.136，置信区间宽且两个动作显示零翻转——证明小样本底限估计不可靠；（5）试点中所有人口学对比均落在底限范围内，无可声明的不公平结果。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个结论：（1）任何反事实公平性估计若不附带按动作测量的不稳定底限，不能作为差异的证据；（2）"域内翻转率"（within-range flip rate）将人口学敏感性与临床错误分离，但需要临床医生裁定可接受动作带（本研究尚未完成）；（3）智能体轨迹使跨步传播的差异可识别，但需要固定相位顺序和强制上游干预；论文同时明确局限：合成情景、粗粒度人口学描述符、控制臂无法完全消除表层扰动的替代解释、且无法审计"指南本身存在的人口学偏差"。作者发布FairMedAgent作为pip可安装包，强调"零是结果"——测量基础设施不应以发现差异为价值前提。

Abstract: Counterfactual audits are the standard tool for checking whether a clinical agent treats demographically distinct but clinically identical patients differently. They report a flip rate: how often an action changes when only the patient descriptor changes. We show that this quantity is uninterpretable on its own. Re-running an identical condition ten times over sixteen vignettes (same narrative, same descriptor string, nothing varied) moved a clinical agent's action in 8.7% of outcome-vignette cells, and instability was heterogeneous across actions by a factor of eight, from 0.022 for ICU escalation to 0.179 for controlled-substance caution. No demographic contrast in our data was distinguishable from that floor. A second model gives a pooled floor of 6.7% and ranks the six actions almost identically (Spearman 0.94, exact p=0.017), so the floor is not one system's artefact. Majority-vote aggregation over five draws removes 39% of it and then flattens, and a null simulation attributes the residue to heterogeneous per-cell rates, so replication mitigates without eliminating. Any counterfactual fairness estimate reported without a per-action floor beside it therefore cannot be read as evidence of disparity. The measurements were taken with FairMedAgent, an evaluation harness for disparity in the actions of clinical LLM agents whose estimand, the within-range counterfactual flip rate, counts only flips between actions a published decision rule admits and a clinician has adjudicated. That estimand requires band adjudication, which is under way; no disparity result is claimed here. Each synthetic vignette runs a six-stage trajectory (five model-facing decisions around a deterministic environment step) under fixed-form conditions spanning race, sex, age, insurance, English proficiency, and their intersections. The harness, the floor protocol, and every analysis script are released.

</details>


### [28] [What Else Needs Fixing? Exploring Cost-Effective Test-Time Compute for Revision Propagation in Artifacts Generated Through Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03254)
*Daisuke Kikuta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在对话生成工件（conversationally generated artifacts）中的修订传播能力，提出新基准RevPropBench，并系统评估九种测试时计算方法在六种LLM上的成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有修订传播研究主要关注代码仓库、知识编辑和文档编辑等设置，其中依赖关系通常是显式或静态可分析的。然而，对话生成的工件（如规划、记录、配置）的依赖关系往往是隐式的，建立在对话上下文中而非工件本身内部。这一实际场景下的修订传播问题尚未被探索。此外，将测试时计算（test-time compute）应用于该新设置的实用性也尚未研究。

Method: 1. 构建基准RevPropBench：覆盖9个领域（旅行、发票、采购车、项目计划、课程、数据流水线、部署配置、组织访问、制造BOM），包含50个场景、6种传播模式、3种工件规模（10/50/100个JSON元素），共150个样本（30开发+120测试），通过LLM合成采样+人工审核+Claude-Opus-4.8辅助标注构建。2. 任务形式：生成阶段通过多轮对话增量构建JSON工件，修订阶段给定局部修改请求，LLM输出JSON Patch（RFC 6902）。3. 评估九种方法：三种单次推理基线（j/h/j+h）、顺序反思（Reflect）、并行采样+规则选择（or/and/maj）、并行采样+Medoid选择（med）、并行采样+LLM选择（Select）。4. 评估六种LLM：gpt-oss-20b/120b、gpt-5.4-mini、qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b-a10b。5. 主指标为完成率（completion rate），辅以miss/over-edit/wrong-value细粒度分析，并分析成本与延迟。

Result: 1. 基线完成率：68.3%–93.0%（j+h最强，j最弱，h居中），表明对话历史包含重要依赖信息。2. 测试时计算：Select提升最稳定（+3.3%–+12.5%），med次之（+1.8%–+7.7%）；and因严格一致要求导致miss大幅增加，or和maj不稳定，Reflect有小而稳定的提升（+0.5%–+8.2%）。3. 成本效益：Select用3–4次LLM调用、med用3次调用最划算；性能约在4–5次调用后饱和。4. 失败分析：miss为主要错误类型，Select减少miss最多，med减少over-edit最多。5. 延迟：med的延迟倍增始终低于1.5×，是最延迟高效的方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出RevPropBench基准，针对对话生成工件中依赖关系隐式建立于对话上下文的特点，系统评估了测试时计算方法在该新设置下的成本效益。研究发现：从3个并行样本中选择1个（使用LLM选择或medoid选择）是最具成本效益的测试时计算策略，准确率提升2.2%–9.7%。当关注延迟时推荐med（3次调用），关注纯成本效益时推荐Select（4次调用）。研究还提供了基准实例、数据生成和标注工具，支持未来随模型能力提升而扩展难度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often help users generate artifacts through iterative cycles of generation and revision in conversation. A challenge here is that, when users specify only a local change during revision, LLMs must instead identify the relevant dependencies and propagate the revision to all affected parts of the artifact. This paper studies this ability of LLMs on conversationally generated artifacts, where the artifact context and its dependencies may be embedded in the conversation history. Toward practical use, we also explore cost-effective test-time compute for this new setting. Specifically, we introduce a new benchmark for this setting, and evaluate nine revision methods, including sequential reflection and parallel sampling variants, using gpt-oss-20b/120b, gpt-5.4-mini, and qwen3.5-9b/27b/122b on the benchmark. The results show that baselines achieve accuracies of 68.3--93%, and the most cost-effective method is selecting from three parallel samples using either LLM-based or medoid selection, which improves accuracy by 2.2--9.7%. Our code and dataset are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [29] [Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning for Sound and Adversarial Test Generation in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03955)
*Jiacheng Xu,Wentao Zhang,Zhiyi Lyu,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Yang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Test Cases Scaling (TCS)的两阶段强化学习框架，用于在代码大语言模型中自动生成既可靠（sound）又具有判别力（discriminative）的测试用例，从而同时提升代码生成质量和推理时的自验证选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代码大语言模型能力的提升，瓶颈从生成合理方案转向验证候选程序的正确性。高质量的测试用例需要同时具备可靠性（通过参考解）和判别力（区分错误解），但这类测试用例稀缺。同时，测试生成是一个多解、效用驱动的非平稳优化问题，其有效性依赖于求解器当前的失败模式，因此天然适合建模为对抗式强化学习问题。

Method: TCS采用两阶段强化学习框架，使用GRPO算法联合训练同一模型的求解器与验证器角色，并维护一个滚动策略对齐缓冲区（policy-aligned buffer）。阶段一使用R₁ᵗ奖励，仅要求生成的测试用例通过真实解C*（保证soundness），同时禁止复制样例测试；阶段二清空缓冲区，仅收集可执行但错误的候选解，使用R₂ᵗ奖励，要求生成的测试用例通过C*但失败于目标错误候选C_wrong（candidate-conditioned adversariality）。推理时，对每个候选程序生成测试用例并执行，按通过计数最高的候选进行选择。理论上证明选择错误候选的概率以(N-1)exp(-K(δ-α)²/2)为上界，其中K为测试总数，δ为反例率，α为可靠性误差。

Result: 在TACO和LiveCodeBench基准上，使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和7B模型进行实验。TCS在pass@1和自生成测试选择上均优于基线模型和联合SFT基线：7B模型在TACO上pass@1从14.36提升至24.09，LiveCodeBench上从28.56提升至37.03；结合自生成测试选择后，7B模型在LiveCodeBench上达到54.75。消融实验表明两阶段奖励优于单阶段，联合训练优于解耦的Code-RL或Test-RL。在难度越高的子集上增益越显著。

Conclusion: TCS证明了有效的测试生成需要可靠性控制与候选条件对抗性的结合，两阶段RL课程是解决奖励稀疏问题的关键。在线策略对齐的RL比固定离线SFT监督更有效，因为验证器需要跟踪求解器不断演化的失败模式。理论上，推理时通过测试扩展的选择可靠性随测试数量呈指数提升，前提是可靠性误差α低于反例率δ。实际中，自生成测试在可靠受控时最有价值，应作为公开测试的补充而非替代。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially advanced code generation with large language models (LLMs) through executable feedback. The feedback for coding problems mainly comes from specific test cases, where high-quality test cases are often scarce since they should be both sound and discriminative. We thus turn to study the auto-generation of test cases using the learned model. We find this is naturally an adversarial RL problem: the model is expected to generate effective test cases as counterexamples, depending on the solver's current failure modes. We propose Test Cases Scaling (TCS), a two-stage RL framework for effective test generation. Both stages train a test generator from a rolling policy-aligned buffer: Stage 1 generates tests consistent with the reference solution, and Stage 2 restricts the buffer to current failure modes and learns counterexample tests. Across TACO and LiveCodeBench, TCS improves both pass@1 and inference-time answer selection according to generated tests. We find the learned test generator also enables effective selection among other LLM outputs.

</details>


### [30] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将思维链（CoT）推理步骤的重要性操作化为强化学习意义上的"优势"（advantage），通过蒙特卡洛采样估计每个步骤对最终答案概率的改变，并以此为基准测试LLM评判器能否仅从文本识别关键步骤。结果发现微调的批评模型在错误响应的步骤识别上接近噪声上限，但在正确响应上仍远低于上限，说明推理步骤的重要性仅能部分从文本中恢复，警示不要将CoT的可读性等同于可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大量研究使用LLM评判器、过程奖励模型（PRM）和生成式批评模型来分析CoT推理步骤，这些方法隐含假设步骤文本能反映其功能角色。然而CoT的"可读性"（legibility）是否真正等同于"可解释性"（interpretability）尚不清楚，即文本是否编码了步骤重要性的信息。
