Method: 1) 将推理步骤的重要性形式化为RL中的优势函数A(s,a) = Q(s,a) - V(s)，通过N=50次蒙特卡洛采样估计值函数和动作价值函数；2) 使用PELT变点检测算法结合Beta后验效应量过滤来识别"有意义的"步骤（|A|>0.1）；3) 测试开箱即用的LLM评判器（直接重要性提示 vs 价值派生提示）和LoRA微调批评模型在数学推理任务（AIME、AMC、MATH500、GSM8K）上识别关键步骤的能力，使用PR-AUC和precision@k指标并对照分裂半噪声上限。

Result: 1) 性能提升主要来自回答前的高先验而非推理过程（"high throughout"模式从24%升至61%）；2) 开箱即用评判器勉强超过基线但远低于上限（最好的Qwen3.6-27B比上限低6-9倍）；3) 微调批评模型在错误响应上接近保守上限（PR-AUC 0.28-0.30 vs 上限≈0.6），但在正确响应上仅达到上限的10-20%（PR-AUC 0.065-0.10 vs 上限0.51-0.64）；4) 这一不对称性在不同生成器规模和思考模式下均成立；5) 该方法与基于提示的忠实性测试互补，可揭示推理过程被"决定化"的现象。

Conclusion: 推理步骤的重要性（优势）作为一种内省的元属性，只能部分从CoT文本中恢复，尤其是在正确响应中。批评模型虽能较好识别错误响应中的关键步骤（往往是最终答案生成步骤），但对正确响应中真正的"顿悟"步骤识别能力差，这警示研究者不能将CoT的可读性等同于可解释性，对过程奖励模型和步骤级监督方法具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [31] [Where Does Harness-Optimization Value Live? Localized Gains and the Budget-Splitting Trap in Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02889)
*Michael Nguyen,Wei Chen Tan,Nurul Aisyah Hassan,Arvind Raman,Li Hua Lim,Ahmad Faiz Razak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HarnessEvo 方法，将智能体的"harness"（围绕冻结 LLM 的文本支架）分解为四个可独立演化的槽位（角色、任务策略、工具/格式规则、反思/控制启发式），并通过 leave-one-in/leave-one-out 归因协议探究优化价值究竟位于何处。在 ALFWorld 上，使用冻结 7B 骨干网络，全量结构化方法与基线持平（0.657 vs 0.642），但留一法分析揭示几乎所有价值都集中在反思/控制槽位（+0.119，p=0.0046）。均匀预算分配是有害的——将 64 次 rollout 平均分给四个槽，每个槽仅 16 次，低于优化器的接受-再评分门槛，导致所有槽冻结为空种子；将预算集中于高价值槽位可在半预算下恢复 +0.119 的增益。在 WebShop 上所有方法持平，表明任务依赖性——某些任务（如 ALFWorld）存在反复出现、可语言化的控制失败，而其他任务（如 WebShop）则不存在。结论：应先进行信用分配，再集中预算于关键槽位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射式提示进化方法将 harness 作为一个整体扁平字符串进行优化，但无法揭示优化价值究竟位于 harness 的哪个组成部分。论文提出一个"制图式"问题：harness 内部的优化价值究竟驻留在何处？是否存在更优的预算分配策略以避免均匀分配导致的隐性浪费？

Method: 1. Harness 分解为四个互斥槽位：c₁（角色/人格）、c₂（任务策略）、c₃（工具/格式规则）、c₄（反思/控制启发式），按固定顺序渲染为单一字符串注入 ReAct 智能体的策略前导位置；2. 坐标上升式演化：使用 GEPA 反射式提示优化器逐槽位演化，每个槽位使用独立的反射元提示，其余槽位冻结；3. 等预算（iso-budget）设计：各槽位预算之和严格等于扁平单字符串演化的总预算 B=64；4. 留一进/留一出（LOI/LOO）归因协议：LOI 仅演化单槽位以衡量其充分性，LOO 演化除该槽位以外的所有槽位以衡量其必要性；5. 实验采用冻结 Qwen2.5-7B 骨干网络，在 ALFWorld（n=134）和 WebShop（n=80）上进行测试。

Result: ALFWorld（n=134）：HarnessEvo-full 达到 0.657，与基线持平（Stock 0.642、GEPA-flat 0.642，均无显著差异）。LOI 归因图显示价值高度局部化：仅控制槽位 c₄ 显著（+0.119，p=0.0046，22 vs 6 不一致任务），角色/策略/格式槽位均无显著效果。系统呈强亚可加性：全方法总增益 +0.015，远小于各槽位 LOI 增益之和 +0.164。均匀四路分配（16/槽）导致所有槽位冻结为空种子（接受-再评分门槛效应）。将预算集中于控制槽位可在半预算（32 次 rollout）下恢复 +0.119 显著增益（0.761，p=0.0046），全预算下达到 0.724（+0.082，p=0.0725，临界）。按任务类型分解显示增益集中在 look_at_obj_in_light（+0.444）和 clean_then_place（+0.226）类型——正是控制规则所针对的循环失败/闭合容器类型。WebShop（n=80）：所有方法持平（0.518/0.539/0.545，无显著差异），即使将全部预算集中于控制槽位仍为空，说明这是任务本身缺乏可语言化控制失败，而非预算饥饿。审计的控制规则文本为：跳过返回"Nothing happens"的重复动作；先打开闭合容器再取物；放置前确认物品已被持有；目标达成即停止。

Conclusion: harness 优化价值是局部化的（集中于反思/控制槽位），而非均匀分布；均匀预算分配实际上是有害的（预算分拆陷阱），因为分解会稀释每个组件的预算至优化器的接受-再评分门槛以下；信用分配（通过廉价的 LOI 探测）应先于结构化智能体演化；预算应集中于高价值槽位而非平均分配。该现象具有任务依赖性——仅在存在反复出现、可语言化控制失败的任务上有效。Harness 优化捕获的是环境基础的自校正能力，而非奖励博弈策略。论文提出实践指南——"先定位，再集中预算"。

Abstract: A growing body of work improves frozen large language models (LLMs) as agents by evolving their harness: the textual scaffolding around the model, including persona, strategy, format rules, and control heuristics. Existing reflective prompt-evolution methods usually optimize this harness as one flat string. We instead ask where the optimization value actually resides. We introduce HARNESSEVO, which decomposes the harness into four separately evolvable slots: role, task-strategy, tool/format-rules, and reflection/control. Using the same reflective optimizer under an iso-budget setting, we pair this decomposition with leave-one-in and leave-one-out attribution to measure the contribution of each slot. On ALFWorld with a frozen 7B backbone, HARNESSEVO does not significantly improve the overall binary success rate over either the stock harness or flat-string evolution: 0.657 versus 0.642 and 0.642, respectively. However, the slot-level analysis reveals that nearly all useful optimization value is localized in the reflection/control slot, which achieves a leave-one-in gain of +0.119. The other slots are individually null. We further show that uniform budget splitting is harmful: allocating 64 rollouts across four slots leaves only 16 per slot, below the optimizer's effective search floor, causing every slot to freeze at its empty seed. Concentrating the budget on the high-credit control slot recovers the lost gain, reaching 0.761 with half the split budget. The effect is task-contingent. On WebShop, all slots freeze empty and all methods tie, indicating a genuine absence of recurrent, verbalizable control failures rather than budget starvation. Overall, our results suggest that harness value is localized, uniform budget splitting can be actively harmful, and credit assignment should precede structured agent-evolution.

</details>


### [32] [Bounded Personas Match Retrieval on Classification but Not Regression for a Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02890)
*JaeHa Yoon,Minjun Park,Seoyeon Kim,Jiwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Choi,Dohyun Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaLink——一种无需训练的方法，将用户历史蒸馏为有界的、查询无关的三字段自然语言persona（偏好摘要+示例+决策规则），并在保持骨干网络冻结的前提下通过"渲染→自评估→重写→保留更优"的递归循环精炼。研究在LaMP-2（15类新闻分类）与LaMP-3（1–5产品评分回归）上对比BM25检索与有界蒸馏，发现明确的"任务类型不对称性"：分类任务上PersonaLink（0.745–0.755）与检索（0.760–0.765）统计上无差异；回归任务上检索显著优于PersonaLink（0.285 vs 0.455 MAE, p<10⁻⁴），且检索随k增长无饱和迹象。递归精炼在第一遍即捕获全部收益，后续迭代呈几何收缩（L=0.144, R²=0.979）。结论：有界蒸馏仅在标签选择任务中足以替代检索，无法胜任需校准数值尺度的细粒度回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言代理需将用户历史转化为对新请求的行为。两大主流策略——检索（准确但每查询开销随历史增长）vs蒸馏（一次压缩、有界、可解释但普遍被认为牺牲精度）——之间的权衡未被清晰刻画。研究问题：在哪些任务上，有界蒸馏式persona能够匹配检索的精度？

Method: PersonaLink方法：(a) 三字段有界状态PersonaState——偏好摘要(imperative prose)+少量示例(exemplars)+决策规则(decision rules)，总token预算约250；(b) 训练免费的递归精炼循环：渲染→在用户自身保留切片S_u上自评估→根据错误集E_u重写→严格keep-better门控仅在不退化时接受；(c) 精炼深度r为可控轴，r=0无persona，r=1单次蒸馏（=PAG基线），r≥2递归自纠错；(d) 单冻结7B骨干+贪婪解码，方法间仅上下文文本不同；(e) 评估：LaMP-2分类(200用户，Accuracy/macro-F1，McNemar检验)；LaMP-3回归(200用户，MAE/RMSE，配对bootstrap)；(f) 理论：单调安全性命题（hold-out选择信号不递减）；(g) 实证固定点分析——MiniLM嵌入上per-pass位移0.90→0.21→0.02，几何衰减模数L=0.144 (R²=0.979)。

Result: 分类（LaMP-2）：PersonaLink达0.745–0.755精度，与BM25 RAG-3/5（0.760/0.765）统计上无差异（p=0.73, 0.60）。回归（LaMP-3）：检索显著优于PersonaLink（RAG-5 MAE=0.285 vs PersonaLink 0.455, p<10⁻⁴），检索随k增长持续改善（k=10: 0.290; k=20: 0.250 MAE），无饱和迹象。控制实验：PersonaLink在两任务上均击败NoPers与RandomUser控制，证实个性化真实有效。递归在r=1后饱和：r=1→2增量ΔAcc=+0.000 (p=0.77)，ΔMAE=+0.005 (p=0.96)，均不显著。字段消融：决策规则对分类贡献最大（-0.045 Acc），示例对回归贡献最大（+0.035 MAE）。Oracle（全profile）在LaMP-2上仅0.715（低于RAG-5），提示过长上下文会分散注意力。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是"任务类型不对称性"的特征化：有界蒸馏式persona在标签分类任务上与检索持平，但在需要校准数值尺度的细粒度回归上被检索显著压制，且有界表示无法随历史扩展。递归精炼因persona表示几何收缩而在单次后饱和，r=1为计算最优。该研究不主张新SOTA，而是清晰界定有界可解释persona的适用边界：仅限分类，不适用于回归或大规模个性化。检索仍为非Oracle方法中最佳的诚实上界。

Abstract: A personalized language agent must convert a user's interaction history into behavior on each new request at inference time. Two strategies dominate. Retrieval pulls a few of the user's most relevant past items into the prompt, which is accurate but pays a per-query selection and context cost that grows with the history. Distillation instead compresses the history once into a compact natural-language persona, which is bounded, query-independent, and interpretable, but is widely assumed to sacrifice accuracy. Whether, and on which tasks, a distilled persona can match retrieval has not been characterized cleanly. We introduce PersonaLink, a training-free method that distills a user's history into a bounded three-field persona and recursively refines it: each pass self-evaluates the frozen agent on a held-out slice of the user's own labeled history, rewrites the persona from its errors, and keeps the result only when it does not regress on that slice. Because every comparison shares one frozen 7B backbone and differs only in what is placed in context, the design isolates the effect of representation from that of the model. The result is a clear task-type asymmetry. On 200 users of LaMP-2 (15-way news categorization), PersonaLink reaches 0.745-0.755 accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from BM25 retrieval (0.760-0.765).

</details>


### [33] [Probe Generalization as Subspace Selection for OOD Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02893)
*Daniel Yoo,Adrians Skapars*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究线性探针在分布外（OOD）欺骗检测中的泛化问题，发现在源域PCA基上选取少量主成分（PCs）构成的子空间进行探测，可显著提升跨域迁移能力。利用LLM法官对各PC的最高/最低激活样本进行自然语言解释并打分，可在不使用目标数据的情况下部分恢复该可迁移子空间，从而将基线到贪心oracle的差距在Insider Trading Report上缩小78%、在Sandbagging上缩小25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针在分布内表现良好但常因学习到虚假相关性而无法泛化到分布外场景，这对基于探针的模型行为监控（如欺骗检测）的可靠性构成挑战。已有研究表明模型激活中存在与行为概念对应的线性结构，但实际探针容易捕获数据集特有的表层特征而非抽象行为信号。因此，核心问题是：能否通过选择激活空间中的特定子空间，使探针仅学习可泛化的特征？

Method: 1. 数据集：以Roleplaying为源域，Insider Trading Report/Confirm、Sandbagging为目标域，基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct第15层残差流激活。2. 在源域激活上做PCA保留top-100 PCs作为基。3. 贪心搜索（oracle）：以目标域AUROC为准则在源PCs中贪心选择8–15个PC。4. 对比方法：按方差排序、按源探针权重排序、按目标探针权重排序。5. 解释性方法：对每个PC的top-8激活样本用LLM法官（GPT-5-mini）生成自然语言描述并打1–10的OOD分数，取两次评分均为最高分(6)的19个PC作为子空间。6. 探针统一为L2正则化系数λ=10的逻辑回归。

Result: 1. 贪心oracle在Insider Trading Report上将源训练探针AUROC从0.706提升至0.929，在Sandbagging上从0.606提升至0.832，证实可迁移子空间存在（仅需8–15个PCs）。2. 按源探针权重排序的PC子集表现接近全表示基线，未提升泛化性；按目标探针权重排序在Sandbagging上甚至低于随机水平——表明源端和目标端的判别性排序均无法恢复可迁移子空间。3. LLM法官选出的19个PC在Insider Trading Report上AUROC达0.881，缩小基线到oracle差距的78%；Sandbagging上达0.662，缩小25%；Insider Trading Confirm因基线已达0.874接近天花板，无信息量。4. 解释结果显示，源探针权重大的PC编码源域特有的表层特征（如特定词汇/格式），而真正可迁移的PC编码更抽象的对比（如否认/承认策略）。

Conclusion: OOD探针的鲁棒性主要由子空间选择决定：线性探针在全表示上训练会混合通用行为特征与数据集特有的表层线索；稀疏的PC子空间可更好隔离与抽象行为结构对应的方向。由此将OOD探测重新定位为表征选择问题——目标不仅是拟合分类器，更要选择保留目标行为特征、过滤虚假相关的子空间。局限性包括：仅在单一模型和单一源数据集上验证；Sandbagging增益有限；纯线性流程；解释性主张为描述性而非因果性。

Abstract: Linear probes can be used to detect behaviors and concepts inside language model activations, but may fail to transfer to out-of-distribution examples. When studying the generalization performance of Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct probes over 3 held-out deception detection datasets, we find that projecting inputs onto a small subset of principal components (PCs) from the training distribution of activations enables cross-domain transfer that nearly matches the performance of probes trained directly on the test distribution. Furthermore, we find that PC interpretations can be used to find a subset of those transferable PCs. By using an LLM judge to score each PC on whether its most/ least activating examples imply a transferable deception direction, then probing on the highest-scoring PCs, we close the baseline-to-oracle gap by 78% on Insider Trading Report and by 25% on Sandbagging. The directions a source probe weights heavily appear to encode source-specific surface features, while the directions that actually transfer appear to encode the same contrast more abstractly, in a way natural language descriptions can capture. Broadly, our results suggest that the OOD robustness of probes is largely determined by subspace selection.

</details>


### [34] [R$^{2}$Adapter: A Routing and Rewriting Adapter for Efficient Hybrid RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02894)
*Yucan Guo,Miao Su,Saiping Guan,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Zixuan Li,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: R²Adapter 是一种轻量级即插即用的路由与改写适配器，用于在传统RAG（基于段落检索）和图谱RAG之间动态分配查询，并通过选择性改写降低图谱检索开销，在保持多跳问答准确率的同时将图谱RAG调用减少高达59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际查询在复杂度上差异显著，固定RAG策略并非最优：传统RAG处理简单查询高效但难以应对多跳关系推理，而图谱RAG虽擅长多跳推理但推理延迟高、计算开销大。现有混合文本-图谱RAG方法依赖启发式规则或LLM路由，存在开销高、对底层LLM依赖强等问题。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：(1) 轻量级路由器——基于DeBERTa-v3-base的双输出分类器，独立估计查询对传统RAG和图谱RAG的适用性，训练数据通过自动标注管道在HotpotQA Hard子集上构建（以两种RAG的平均文档排序作为质量度量）；采用独立Focal BCE损失处理类别不平衡，路由决策基于阈值 τ，并以F1与Recall的均值作为验证指标选择阈值。(2) 查询改写器——仅在路由器对图谱RAG决策置信度低时激活，使用LLM将查询分解为[头实体,关系,尾实体]三元组序列，显式暴露多跳结构，与原查询拼接后送入图谱RAG。整体流程：先路由→传统RAG直接处理；图谱RAG且低置信度则先改写再检索；改写阈值 ε 控制触发频率。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA、MuSiQue三个多跳问答基准上：(1) 集成HippoRAG 2时取得最优平均QA性能（EM 56.3, F1 66.9），略超原方法；集成GraphRAG时显著优于原方法；(2) 相比NER-Router和LLM-Router，路由器在准确率上更优且稳定，路由速度快于LLM-Router约4.6倍；(3) 将图谱RAG使用率降低最高达59%（HotpotQA），同时保持相当的答案准确率；(4) 改写器在路由不确定时带来稳定的准确率提升，且收益随 ε 增大趋于饱和，验证了选择性改写的必要性。

Conclusion: R²Adapter 作为模型无关的预检索插件，可无缝集成到不同的传统和图谱RAG流水线中，通过智能路由和选择性改写，在多跳问答任务上有效平衡了推理效率与推理能力，提供了一种实用且可扩展的混合RAG解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a prevailing paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with non-parametric knowledge. Vanilla RAG efficiently handles simple queries but struggles with relational or multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based RAG alleviates this issue but incurs higher inference complexity and latency. In practice, user queries can differ significantly in their complexity, rendering a fixed RAG strategy suboptimal. However, existing hybrid text-graph RAG methods typically rely on heuristic and LLM-based routing, resulting in unnecessary overhead and strong dependence on the underlying LLM. To address these challenges, we propose R$^{2}$Adapter, a lightweight plug-in Routing and Rewriting Adapter designed to allocate queries between vanilla and graph-based RAG dynamically. By routing only the queries that genuinely benefit from graph-based reasoning, R$^{2}$Adapter reduces unnecessary graph retrieval overhead. Additionally, uncertain graph-routed queries are rewritten to better expose their multi-hop reasoning requirements, improving retrieval quality without additional supervision. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that R$^{2}$Adapter reduces graph-based RAG usage by up to 59% while maintaining comparable answer accuracy. This adapter is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into diverse vanilla and graph-based RAG pipelines, providing an efficient and adaptive solution for hybrid RAG systems.

</details>


### [35] [PiPMRE: A Pipeline Based on Language Model for Medical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02896)
*Jiaxin Duan,Fengyu Lu,Junfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PiPMRE 是一个用于医学关系抽取（MRE）的新型两阶段流水线框架，由关系生成器（基于T5）和关系过滤器（基于BERT）组成。它将MRE任务转化为Seq2Seq文本填充问题，无需复杂的标签设计，并在两个公开数据集上超越了现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学关系抽取方法主要将其视为序列标注任务，需要设计复杂的标注模式（如BIEO-R-SO），且难以处理医学实体间复杂的多关系抽取问题。Seq2Seq方法虽放弃了繁琐的标注工作，但由于目标序列长度不确定，在多关系抽取上存在局限。本文旨在从语言学角度重新审视MRE任务，提出一种无需标注模式、能够有效处理多关系的流水线框架。

Method: PiPMRE采用两阶段流水线架构：(1) 关系生成器基于T5-large，通过增量跨域预训练（ICPT）注入医学知识，使用提示调谐（prompt tuning）保留预训练知识，并通过直接偏好优化（DPO）进行微调；将MRE实例文本化后破坏实体、关系槽位，将任务转化为Seq2Seq文本填充问题。(2) 关系过滤器基于BERT-base，采用对比学习和逐对边际损失（pairwise margin loss）训练，对生成器输出进行评分，使用调制解码机制（Trie约束的束搜索）保证输出有效性，最终通过边界阈值ζ过滤。(3) 使用CMeKG构建包含269,930条句子的预训练语料库，利用Llama3.1生成。

Result: 在CHIP和CMeIE两个公开数据集上的实验结果表明：PiPMRE以88.9（F1）的成绩在CHIP数据集上超越之前最优方法（GenPT 87.8），以50.3（F1）在CMeIE数据集上超越之前最优方法（BiTT 50.1）。平均提升5.6个召回率点和4.4个准确率点。在少样本学习场景下，PiPMRE也表现出色，性能接近专门为少样本设计的S³AAL方法。单关系和多关系抽取任务上均显著优于现有方法，尤其在多关系EPO（实体对重叠）、SEO（单实体重叠）、NEO（无实体重叠）三种场景下表现优异。消融实验验证了ICPT、DPO和对比学习三个组件的重要性。

Conclusion: PiPMRE是一个有效且鲁棒的医学关系抽取框架，通过生成-过滤流水线避免了复杂标注模式的设计难题，能够自适应地处理包含多个关系三元组的医学文本。在全数据和少样本、单关系和多关系等多种设置下的实验证明了其优越性。该工作也验证了将MRE转化为与PLM预训练任务相匹配的文本填充问题这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Medical relation extraction (MRE) is commonly known for extracting entities and their relations jointly from a medical text, which has attracted considerable attention in recent years. Previous studies treat MRE as a sequence tagging task, which results in either a challenging design of the tagging schema or a failed extraction of multiple relations, due to intricate relationships among medical entities. In this work, we review the task from the linguistic perspective and propose a novel pipeline framework, PiPMRE, developed on language models to enhance MRE performance. Specifically, PiPMRE consists of a relation generator and a relation filter. Given a text, the generator first yields multiple relational triplets, and then the filter scores each triplet and retains only those that pass the borderline as the final results. Implementing PiPMRE requires no tagging schema; instead, we use a simple template to reformulate the input text, ensuring that entities and relations are generated in a contextual order. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate the advancement of PiPMRE. It surpasses the previous state-of-the-art by an average of 5.6 recall points and 4.4 accuracy points. PiPMRE's superiority is also demonstrated in few-shot settings.

</details>


### [36] [Margins, Not Windows: Training-Free Per-Step Lossy Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02897)
*Oszkár Urbán,Young D. Kwon,Stylianos I. Venieris,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptiveSpec，一种训练自由的、逐步自适应的推测解码方法。它沿两个正交轴改进EAGLE-3树形草稿器：（1）基于边际的有损验证规则，在首个不匹配位置用目标模型对草稿token概率与其top-1概率之比作为判定阈值；（2）基于草稿置信度分数（融合top-1概率与滚动接受率）的动态草稿树形塑形策略，直接调整(n_steps, top-k, ndt)三元组而非固定预算内再分配。在SGLang上实现，相比EAGLE-3在三个目标模型和GSM8K、MATH-500、HumanEval任务上吞吐量提升最高56%（平均18-44%），同时保留93%至无损的任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树形注意力草稿器（如EAGLE-3）的推测解码通常固定两个关键决策：严格的token匹配验证规则和静态草稿树形状。已有工作分别在隔离条件下放宽这些约束，但存在局限：FLy等有损验证方法需要长草稿链来支持前瞻窗口，在EAGLE-3等短接受长度的草稿器上失效；TALON等自适应树塑形在固定总节点预算内重新分配深度与宽度，无法真正减少弱草稿步上的计算。两者缺乏统一框架。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：(1) 边际验证规则：定义margin(j)=p_target(draft_j)/p_target(top1_j)，当其超过阈值κ时提升草稿token，无需训练模块或前瞻窗口，对草稿长度和架构无依赖。(2) 动态树塑形：定义草稿置信度分数DCS_t = p_draft(top1_t)·RAR_t（其中RAR为滚动接受率的EMA，α=0.3），通过除数d重缩放后在(3,4,4)↔(7,1,8)范围内线性插值选择(n_steps, top-k, ndt)三元组——置信时深而窄，不确定时浅而宽。两组件作用于推测解码的不同子阶段（草稿阶段塑形树，验证阶段判定提升），效果可叠加。利用SGLang预捕获每个可能三元组的CUDA图以保持生产级加速。

Result: 在SGLang生产级推理引擎上对三个目标模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B、Qwen3-8B）和三个基准（GSM8K、MATH-500、HumanEval）的评估显示：AdaptiveSpec (Combined)在所有（模型，基准）单元上取得最佳吞吐量，相比EAGLE-3提升17-56%，单格最高提升为Llama-3.1-8B HumanEval的+56%（2.82× vs 1.81×）。平均提升：Llama +44%、DeepSeek-R1 +38%、Qwen3 +18%。2×2消融显示每个组件单独使用均有提升（动态塑形+21.4%，有损验证+20.9%），组合后达+34.1%（1.82×→2.44×），平均接受token数τ从2.59提升至4.10。任务准确率恢复93%至完全无损（部分场景如Qwen3-8B HumanEval甚至达109%）。超参数搜索确定d=0.5为通用最优，κ在每个（模型，基准）单元独立选择以平衡吞吐-准确率权衡。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSpec作为首个训练自由的每步自适应推测解码框架，通过边际验证规则解决了FLy等方法对长草稿链的结构依赖问题，通过动态树塑形解决了TALON等方法在固定预算内重分配的局限。两个正交轴的增益可叠加，无需任何训练或外部模块，完全依赖解码过程中已产生的内部信号，在保持高准确率恢复的同时实现显著的吞吐量提升。局限：不保留目标模型的严格分布；仅在batch size=1下评估；尚未在扩散型草稿器上验证。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting candidate tokens and verifying them in parallel. Tree-attention drafters such as EAGLE-3 are widely adopted, yet typically hold two decisions fixed: (1) a strict token-match verification rule and (2) a static draft-tree shape. Prior work relaxes each in isolation under limiting assumptions: long draft chains for training-free lossy verification, and adaptive tree shaping under a fixed token budget. We introduce AdaptiveSpec, a training-free per-step speculative decoding method that adapts both decisions from internal signals already produced during decoding. A per-step margin rule promotes a mismatched draft-proposed token when the ratio of the target's probability on the drafted token to its top-1 probability exceeds a threshold with no dependence on draft length or underlying drafter architecture. A per-step tree policy adjusts the draft tree's depth, width, and node count directly from a fused signal of draft top-1 confidence and a rolling acceptance history capturing recent draft-target agreement, allowing the total draft count to vary rather than only be redistributed. The two adaptations operate on orthogonal axes and compound in effect. Implemented on the SGLang production-grade serving engine, AdaptiveSpec improves throughput over the state-of-the-art autoregressive speculative decoding method EAGLE-3 by up to 56%, recovering 93% to fully lossless task accuracy across GSM8K, MATH-500, and HumanEval on three target models (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B).

</details>


### [37] [Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET): Parameter-Efficient Biomedical Domain Adaptation via Priority-Based Embedding Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02898)
*Girish Sundaram,Daniel Berleant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 DRET：把 BioBERT、ClinicalBERT 等大型生物医学模型的词表与词嵌入，按预先设定优先级选择性地迁移到轻量 DistilBERT，并结合冻结嵌入层、差异化学习率、标签传播及类别不平衡损失，使其在 EBM-NLP 的 PICO 序列标注任务上，以 6600 万参数获得接近大型领域模型、且在召回率与平衡准确率上更有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BioBERT、ClinicalBERT 等大型领域模型性能强但部署成本高；DistilBERT 虽轻量，却缺少 PICO 分类所需生物医学语义。论文试图不访问源领域语料，仅通过参数高效的知识迁移弥合这一差距。

Method: 该研究将 PICO 分类建模为词元级序列标注任务，并在 EBM-NLP 数据集上进行 3 折交叉验证。DRET 依次迭代：合并六个领域模型的词表（DRET 1.x）、以源嵌入均值初始化新增词（DRET 2.0）、按 BioBERT > ClinicalBERT > BlueBERT > BiomedBERT 的固定优先级逐词选择嵌入（DRET 3.x），再加入嵌入层冻结、差异化学习率、动态类权重、标签传播、类别不平衡感知损失（DRET 4.x）。评估采用 12 项总体/类别指标，并以余弦相似度、语义位移、成对距离和 t-SNE 检查嵌入层变化。

Result: 6600 万参数的 DRET-DistilBERT 达到最高 0.815 平衡准确率、0.815 召回率、0.903 G-mean 和 0.865 ROC-AUC，优于 BioLinkBERT（3.8 亿参数，平衡准确率 0.774）和 BioMElectra（3.35 亿，0.792），但总体准确率 0.766 低于后两者。嵌入迁移后医学词元的平均余弦相似度仅 0.137、平均语义位移 1.674，说明表示发生显著变化；但轮廓系数由 0.0265 降至 0.0105，嵌入聚类并未改善。

Conclusion: DRET 证明，将多个大型领域模型的词表和嵌入按优先级注入轻量通用模型，可在不使用原始领域训练语料的情况下，有效提升生物医学 PICO 分类的少数类召回与平衡性能，适合资源受限部署。主要局限是仅在单一语料和单一任务上验证，缺少多种子与显著性检验，固定优先级是否合理尚未学习验证，嵌入诊断也只具描述性。

Abstract: Large domain-specific language models such as BioBERT and ClinicalBERT achieve strong performance on biomedical NLP tasks, but their computational demands make them impractical for many real-world deployments. General-purpose, parameter-efficient models such as DistilBERT are lightweight yet lack the domain knowledge required for specialized tasks such as PICO (Population, Intervention, Comparison, Outcome) classification. We introduce Distilled Rapid Embedding Transfer (DRET), a knowledge-transfer paradigm that injects biomedical domain knowledge from large specialized models into a smaller general-purpose model without retraining on the original specialized corpora. DRET is developed as an iterative family of strategies: a unified tokenizer-merge strategy (DRET 1.x), hybrid embedding averaging (DRET 2.0), and a priority-based embedding-transfer mechanism (DRET 3.x) that hierarchically selects embeddings from the most authoritative source models, further combined with embedding-layer freezing, differential learning rates, label propagation, and imbalance-aware loss functions (DRET 4.x). We evaluate DRET on token-level PICO classification using the EBM-NLP corpus under severe class imbalance, across a twelve-metric battery. DRET-enhanced DistilBERT (66M parameters) attains balanced accuracy, recall, and ROC-AUC competitive with, and on several class-wise metrics exceeding, models an order of magnitude larger, while retaining DistilBERT's efficiency. We further show that transfer occurs at the embedding level through cosine-similarity, semantic-shift, and t-SNE analyses. DRET offers a scalable, resource-efficient route to near-domain-expert performance for biomedical text mining, with direct application to automated systematic literature reviews and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [38] [Contamination Inflates Scores but Rarely Reorders Large Language Model Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02899)
*Xingyao Xiao,Yihong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"锚定项目不变性"（anchor-item invariance）的基准污染检测方法，通过对比原始题目与语义等价的改写题目的表现差异来隔离记忆效应与真实能力。研究发现，虽然污染确实会抬升模型的绝对分数，但在当前公开模型中污染程度大致均匀，导致标准排行榜与改写控制排行榜的排名相关性高达0.997，排名失真需要罕见的差异性污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对大语言模型基准污染的担忧混淆了两个问题：污染是否抬高绝对分数，以及是否改变模型排名。作者认为需要区分"是否污染"与"是否影响排名"，并提出更精确的检测与影响量化方法。

Method: 1. 将污染重新定义为对"锚定项目不变性"的违反；2. 采用项目内对比设计，比较模型在原始题目与语义等价改写题目上的表现差异；3. 使用47个公开模型的逐题响应和74个已知污染剂量的微调模型，在ARC、GSM8K、HellaSwag、MMLU四个基准上进行校准与评估；4. 通过剂量-反应分析验证测量的有效性，并进行排行榜敏感度分析。

Result: 1. 测量能剂量响应性地恢复注入的污染效应（测试集泄露的修正效应为+0.187准确率点），且不会错误标记仅在合法训练集上训练的负控制模型（-0.012）；2. 标准排行榜与改写控制排行榜的秩相关系数为0.997；3. 敏感度分析显示观察到的差异性污染远低于改变排名所需的水平；4. 188个模型×基准案例中，仅3例出现跨两个参考点验证的差异性污染。

Conclusion: 当前公开模型中的污染大致均匀，污染主要抬高绝对分数而不改变排行榜排名。排名失真需要罕见的差异性污染情况。作者发布了校准的不变性审计参考实现，建议排行榜同时报告改写控制排名及置信区间。

Abstract: Benchmark contamination, the leakage of test items into training data, is widely described as a threat to the reliability of large language model (LLM) leaderboards. We argue that this concern conflates two distinct questions: whether contamination inflates absolute scores, and whether it reorders the ranking of models. We recast contamination as a violation of anchor-item invariance and measure it through the differential functioning of original versus semantically equivalent paraphrased items, a within-item contrast that holds the measured skill fixed and isolates memorization from capability. Using per-instance responses from 47 publicly released models and 74 models finetuned with a known dose of contamination, across four benchmarks (ARC, GSM8K, HellaSwag, MMLU), we first calibrate the measure against ground truth: it recovers injected contamination dose-responsively (a corrected effect of +0.187 accuracy points for test-set leakage) and never flags a negative-control model trained only on the legitimate training split (-0.012). We then quantify leaderboard impact: the rank correlation between a standard leaderboard and a paraphrase-controlled leaderboard is 0.997, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the observed differential contamination is far below the level needed to move rankings, with only 3 of 188 model-by-benchmark cases showing differential contamination corroborated across two references. Contamination among these public models is therefore largely uniform: it inflates absolute scores without reordering the leaderboard, and ranking distortion requires the rare case of differential contamination. We provide a calibrated invariance audit, released as a reference implementation, and recommend that leaderboards report paraphrase-controlled rankings alongside confidence intervals.

</details>

*Fengrun Zhang,Li Fu,Wangjin Zhou,Lu Fan,Youzheng Wu,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-ASR框架，通过LLM驱动的generate-and-judge工作流构建口语形式与书面形式的配对监督，结合ITN-MWER序列级目标，并提出Require-ITN/Forbid-ITN评测协议，实现中文ASR中语义感知的逆文本规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统级联式ASR-ITN存在三方面局限——缺乏联合优化、ITN是依赖上下文的语义决策而非局部格式化操作、以及现有评测无法衡量过度规范化；针对中文语音识别中口语形式忠实转录与书面形式可读转录的双重需求。

Method: 1) LLM驱动的generate-and-judge工作流：从口语形式转录本生成书面形式候选，由成对质量验证器评分并经轻量确定性校验，构建配对监督；2) 提示条件化ASR-ITN架构（语音编码器+适配器+LLM解码器），通过p^s和p^w提示分别生成口语与书面形式；3) ITN-MWER序列级目标：结合CER奖励与数值关键词F1奖励，对规范化敏感片段赋予更高错误代价；4) Require-ITN/Forbid-ITN决策感知评测协议，分别衡量必要规范化与受保护片段保留。

Result: 在SpeechIO中文人工标注子集上：Require-ITN上I-CER 4.64%、关键词F1 94.85%；Forbid-ITN上FSPR 95.18%；一致优于开源级联与直接ASR系统，与强闭源参考保持竞争力；千句无数字控制集CER 3.07%、零误插数字。

Conclusion: DF-ASR是首个在开源中文ASR中实现语义感知书面形式ITN并保留提示级形式选择的框架，通过配对监督与ITN-MWER实现上下文相关的规范化决策，但仍局限于离线中文场景，多语言扩展与LLM监督的残余数字误差是未来方向。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) scenarios require both spoken-form transcripts for faithful transcription and readable written-form transcripts with inverse text normalization (ITN). However, these forms are typically produced by cascaded modules, where a spoken-form ASR output is rewritten by a separate ITN component, making written-form ASR-ITN vulnerable to recognition errors and decoupling normalization from acoustic-contextual modeling, especially for semantically dependent numeric expressions. In this paper, we propose Dual-Form ASR (DF-ASR), a framework that extends spoken-form ASR capability to semantics-aware written-form ITN through paired spoken-form and written-form supervision while retaining prompt-level selection between transcript forms. The dual-form supervision is constructed via a large language model (LLM)-driven generate-and-judge workflow, and training is further enhanced by ITN-MWER, a sequence-level objective that assigns higher cost to errors on normalization-sensitive spans. We also introduce a decision-aware REQUIRE-ITN/\FORBID-ITN protocol to separately measure required normalization and forbidden-span preservation. On manually annotated Chinese subsets from SpeechIO, DF-ASR consistently outperforms open-source ASR-ITN systems, remains competitive with strong closed-source references, and preserves reliable prompt-level control between spoken-form and written-form outputs.

</details>


### [40] [RL-ADA: A World-Feedback Framework for Adversarially Robust Enterprise Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02902)
*Ram Narayanan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RL-ADA提出了一种无需人工标注的对抗协同进化框架，用3B客服智能体与7B对抗客户智能体在自动裁判引导下相互训练，在银行客服场景中通过5轮共进化彻底消除工具路由错误并使严格PASS率翻倍，并观察到"上下文伪装"这一新兴对抗行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业任务型对话系统训练面临隐私日志难以标注、用户行为快速演化的瓶颈；RLHF依赖昂贵人工标注，自博弈方法假设对称智能体不适合客服场景中"客服正确路由 vs 客户诱导误路由"的天然非对称对抗需求。

Method: 三角色协同进化框架：(1) Qwen2.5-3B客服智能体(DA)与Qwen2.5-7B对抗客户智能体(CA)在固定Qwen2.5-7B裁判引导下交替训练（冻结对手）；(2) GRPO算法 + 基于结果的世界反馈奖励（DA侧重工具路由与端到端解决，CA侧重逼真性与诱导误路由）；(3) 隔离训练场使用70:30失败/成功转录混合的滑动窗口切片器进行失败模式针对性再训练；(4) 基于滚动胜率稳定性的宏观停止准则作为ε-Nash收敛的实证代理。

Result: 在12场景保持测试集上：5轮共进化后工具路由准确率从75%提升至100%，严格PASS率从25%升至50%，平均回合奖励由+1.58升至+2.16；胜率轨迹非单调下降（每逢CA完成训练轮则DA性能回落），体现真实共进化压力；裁判本地7B模型在解决性检测F1=0.807、幻觉检测F1=1.000，与GPT-4o-mini可比；CA涌现"上下文伪装"策略——将意图嵌入密集具体客户细节而非直接命名，战术分布中直接意图从25%升至37%。

Conclusion: RL-ADA证明了以非对称世界反馈奖励驱动的非对称协同进化可消除标注依赖并显著提升企业对话智能体对抗鲁棒性；停止准则基于胜率稳定性在第5轮触发；上下文伪装作为奖励压力下的自发涌现行为，对企业红队建设具有直接启示；组件设计具备领域无关性，下一步需跨领域实证迁移与生产数据飞轮闭环。

Abstract: Deploying task-oriented dialogue agents in enterprise customer support faces a persistent annotation bottleneck: robust training requires labelled interaction data at scale, yet enterprise conversational logs are privacy-sensitive and expensive to annotate, while user behaviour evolves faster than labelling pipelines can keep pace. We present RL-ADA (Reinforcement Learning with Adversarial Dialogue Agents), a co-evolutionary training framework that eliminates this bottleneck by replacing human labels with \emph{world feedback}: consequence-based reward signals derived directly from measurable interaction outcomes. A Customer Support Agent (DA, 3B parameters) and an Adversarial Customer Agent (CA, 7B parameters) co-evolve in an adversarial arena guided by a fixed automated judge: the DA is rewarded for correctly handling multi-turn customer conversations to successful resolution, while the CA is rewarded for producing realistic, intent-concealing utterances that cause misroutes, creating asymmetric adversarial pressure through opposing but independently structured rewards. An isolation gym iteratively retrains the weaker agent on prior-failure transcripts, requiring no human annotation at any stage. In a banking customer support proof of concept, tool-routing errors are eliminated and the strict end-to-end PASS rate doubles over five co-evolutionary cycles, driven solely by automated arena reward with no labelled data. We additionally observe the emergence of \textbf{Contextual Camouflage}, an adversarial strategy in which the CA learns to embed intent within dense realistic customer detail purely from reward pressure, with direct implications for enterprise red-teaming and robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [41] [LexIssue: Benchmarking Legal Issue Identification in Chinese Civil Litigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02954)
*Huiyuan Xie,Yuqin Huang,Zhicheng Hao,Yida Cai,Shaochun Wang,Zhenghao Liu,Yuxiao Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对中文民事诉讼中法律争议焦点的计算建模问题，提出LexIssue基准，包含基于法律基础的双层层次化表示方法（自由文本描述与结构化法律类别），并将任务形式化为法律争议焦点生成（LIG）和法律争议焦点分类（LIC）。基准涵盖430个真实案件和1303条专家标注争议，并构建了涵盖27个案由和441条候选条目的法律争议知识库。实验表明RAG方法在各模型上一致提升表现，但直接法律提示注入效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律争议焦点的识别是司法推理的基石性步骤（如IRAC框架所体现），但现有法律AI研究对此关注不足；现有方法多局限于窄范围案由的多选分类任务，或使用ROUGE等基于词汇重叠的生成指标，难以处理法律议题表达的高度自由性和语义等价性。

Method: 1) 设计双层层次化schema：自由文本描述层+结构化类别层（一阶：法律/事实/混合/程序，二阶：权利要素、法律效力、抗辩等，三阶细粒度）；2) 将任务形式化为法律争议生成（LIG）和分类（LIC），含三阶粒度评估；3) 用GPT-4o从判决书重构诉讼请求与答辩书，经专家标注形成430例/1303条标注；4) 从法律实务文献和真实判例中构建涵盖27案由441条目的知识库；5) 设计两阶段LLM辅助预测-参考对齐流程（候选相似度映射+层级化对齐验证），采用Qwen-3.5-27B作为评判器；6) 在zero-shot、legal prompting、RAG三种设置下评估8个模型。

Result: Gemini-3-Flash表现最强（LIG 0.715/分类0.597），RAG设置几乎对所有模型一致提升性能（如GPT-5.2的LIG从0.542提升至0.634）；法律专项LLM（LegalOne-R1、DISC-LawLLM）反不如通用开源LLM；legal prompting效果不稳定甚至下降；分类任务随层级深度增加性能显著下降（GPT-5.2在Tier-1至Tier-3从0.971降至0.513），表明模型对争议焦点的法律属性理解仍是挑战；LLM-as-a-Judge对齐验证（Qwen-3.5-27B）与人类标注一致性（Kappa=0.668）显著优于ROUGE和嵌入方法。

Conclusion: LexIssue将法律争议焦点识别确立为独立的计算任务，证实其对当前LLM仍具挑战性，尤其在理解争议法律属性与推理功能方面；RAG是有效整合外部法律知识的稳定途径，而直接提示注入不稳定；未来可探索更有效的知识整合方法及面向诉讼争议焦点识别的训练对齐方法。

Abstract: Identifying the issues disputed between litigating parties is a crucial component of real-world litigation. However, legal issues remain comparatively underexplored in legal AI research. In this work, we study the computational modelling of legal issue identification in litigation. We introduce a legally grounded hierarchical schema that represents legal issues through both free-form issue descriptions and structured legal categories, and formulate legal issue identification as two complementary tasks: legal issue generation and legal issue classification. Based on this formulation, we construct LexIssue, a benchmark containing 430 real-world Chinese civil litigation cases and 1,303 expert-annotated disputed legal issues. We further develop an issue-centric legal knowledge base spanning 27 causes of action and 441 candidate legal issue entries to support retrieval-augmented reasoning. Experimental results across a diverse set of models show that retrieval-augmented generation using the constructed legal issue knowledge base consistently improves performance in identifying disputed legal issues and their corresponding legal attributes.

</details>


### [42] [Large Language Models in Resolving Contextual Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03148)
*Xinye Yang,Zhenyang Liu,Ruisi Li,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在多源上下文中遇到**上下文内部冲突**（而非模型参数知识与外部上下文的冲突）时的处理能力，系统性地提出六类冲突分类法，构建了包含5,781个样本的ContextConflict数据集，通过机制可解释性分析揭示了模型存在显著的"早期证据位置偏差"，并提出一种无需训练和标签的激活引导（activation steering）方法来缓解该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM参数化记忆与外部上下文之间的冲突（如检索增强生成中的幻觉问题），而忽视了实际部署中更常见的**多源上下文内部冲突**——例如不同文档、不同时间、不同视角之间本身就存在矛盾。此外，现有冲突数据集存在四大局限：(1) 基于模板的实体替换合成，缺乏真实复杂性；(2) 偏重显式事实矛盾，忽视需要多步推理的隐式冲突；(3) 领域和冲突类型覆盖窄；(4) 类别不平衡。这些缺陷阻碍了对LLM冲突处理机制的深入理解和改进。

Method: 1. **六类冲突分类法**：推理任务类（misinformation错误信息、inferential推理冲突、temporal时序冲突）和摘要任务类（granularity粒度、perspective视角、ambiguity歧义），每类再分为显式（≤1步推理即可识别）和隐式（≥2步跨证据推理）。2. **数据集构建**：基于10个公开数据集（如SciFact、FOLIO、NEJM-MedQA、AllSides、AmbigDocs等），含1,734个半合成实例（仅用GPT-5作为辅助生成证据/时间戳，黄金标签来自源数据集或人工标注）和4,047个原始保留实例，按风险比例进行质量审查（30%–100%人工重标注）。3. **评估指标**：推理任务用Accuracy；摘要任务提出**基于Shapley归因的Balance指标**（归一化Gini系数，衡量证据贡献均匀度），并通过108样本的人-LLM一致性研究（κ≈0.5，与人类间一致性相当）、因果干预验证以及评分器大小鲁棒性检查三项验证。4. **机制可解释性分析**（以Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct为主，GPT-OSS-20B验证）：(a) 概念激活向量探测各层冲突感知能力（AUC）；(b) 谱能量分解（top-k=10 SVD，ΔER）分析表征几何；(c) 表征级方向投影 + 输出级Shapley归因，分析位置偏差。5. **激活引导方法**：在残差流上，将组合证据激活推向"中性中心"（各单一证据激活的均值），即 u^(l) = (μ̄^(l) - c̄^(l)) / ||·||，推理时 h ← h + α·u^(l)（α=1.0），对比Context-Aware Steering (CAS)。

Result: **性能发现**：(1) 七个LLM在所有六类冲突上表现均不理想，推理任务中即使是最佳模型Claude-4.5-Sonnet在Inferential任务上仅44.3%准确率，存在跨模型的共同瓶颈；(2) 难度排序：Temporal > Misinformation > Inferential；(3) **普遍的位置偏差**：Shapley归因显示首条证据在Ambiguity任务中占比高达69.0%，即便在120B模型中也未消除，模型规模并非解药。**机制发现**：(4) 模型具备较强的潜在冲突感知能力（AUC>0.85），但涌现层次因冲突类型而异——Temporal/Ambiguity在早期层饱和，Inferential/Misinformation在深层涌现；(5) 不同冲突类型呈现不同表征几何——Temporal压缩为低秩，Granularity/Ambiguity分散到高维；(6) 位置偏差在表征级和输出级均稳健存在，证据随机重排无法消除。**方法效果**：(7) 提出的激活引导方法在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上All-Tokens注入使Temporal任务准确率提升+19.2个百分点，摘要Balance改善；在GPT-OSS-20B上也取得一致提升；在与CAS的6次模型×推理对比中赢5次（除GPT-OSS-20B Temporal外），α∈[1.0,1.5]区间表现稳健。

Conclusion: 本文得出三个核心结论：(1) 现代LLM在多源上下文内部冲突场景下的鲁棒性不足，推理冲突在所有模型上仍未解决；(2) 机制分析表明，模型虽在内部能感知冲突，但表现出系统性的表征级早期证据偏好，这种"隐式信任分配"是冲突解决的关键障碍；(3) 基于机制洞察提出的免训练、免标签的激活引导方法能有效缓解位置偏差，提升推理准确性和摘要平衡性，为白盒模型的冲突处理提供了轻量级干预方案。局限性包括该方法需要白盒访问残差流、仅在8B/20B规模上验证、未在真实嘈杂检索场景下测试、Shapley归因假设各证据先验等效。

Abstract: Most prior works focused on conflicts between an LLM's internal parametric knowledge and externally provided context. In contrast, we investigate how LLMs handle conflicts that arise within contextual knowledge itself. We introduce a taxonomy of six types of contextual conflicts (factual, inferential, temporal, granularity, perspective, and ambiguity) and contribute a comprehensive dataset ContextConflict for this setting. The dataset contains 5,781 samples, covers both reasoning and summarization tasks, and includes both explicit contradictions and implicit conflicts that require multi-step reasoning. Experiments on nine LLMs show that current models still fall short in resolving contextual knowledge conflicts. We further provide mechanistic interpretability insights into how LLMs process such conflicts, revealing their latent awareness of conflicts and the representational geometry underlying conflict processing. In addition, our analysis uncovers a consistent model bias towards earlier evidence, and this positional preference serves as a key obstacle to effective conflict resolution. Motivated by these findings, we further propose a simple training-free, label-free steering method that steers activations to encourage a more comprehensive incorporation of evidences for better conflict resolution. On our dataset, the method consistently improves accuracy on reasoning tasks and generates higher-quality, more balanced summaries for summarization tasks.

</details>


### [43] [No country for old linguists: LLM-brain alignment underdetermines neural computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03160)
*Elliot Murphy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该评论文章对Nastase等人(2026)关于LLM-大脑对齐研究可作为语言处理机制模型的观点提出批评,认为表征对齐虽可约束机制假说,但本身无法确定机制,并从逻辑、因果和计算三个维度论证其不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nastase等人(2026)主张LLM与大脑在分布式、上下文敏感表征上存在相似性,并认为LLM-脑对齐研究可以为语言处理提供"完全机制性模型"。本文作者质疑这一推论的合理性,旨在澄清LLM-脑对齐研究能够支持何种推论及其局限性。

Method: 本文采用概念分析与逻辑论证的方法,从逻辑、因果和计算三个层面的"欠定性"(underdetermination)展开批评:1)逻辑欠定性——相同表征可由不同机制产生;2)因果欠定性——编码模型可捕获神经活动特征但不揭示因果机制;3)计算欠定性——对齐无法证明共享架构或算法。

Result: 作者论证:Nastase等人一方面承认对齐不等于共享架构或算法,另一方面又得出LLM可作为语言处理"完全机制性模型"的结论,二者存在内在矛盾。表征相似性本身存在严重的逻辑、因果和计算层面的欠定问题,因此LLM-脑对齐不应被等同于机制解释。

Conclusion: LLM-脑对齐研究具有价值,可用于约束机制假说,但不能由此推断LLM与大脑共享计算原则或构成语言处理的机制模型;研究者应明确区分表征层面的对应关系与机制层面的等价性,避免过度推论。

Abstract: Nastase et al. (2026) argue that large language models (LLMs) may illuminate language processing because both rely on distributed, context-sensitive representations shaped by statistical learning. Their rejection of simple cortical "boxology" is persuasive, and they articulate a strong case for the value of LLM-brain alignment research. The key question is what kind of inference LLM-brain alignment licenses. My claim here will be narrow: representational alignment can in principle constrain mechanistic hypotheses, but it does not by itself identify a mechanism. Nastase et al. acknowledge that an encoding model can capture features represented in neural activity without establishing a shared architecture or algorithm. Yet the authors sometime move from alignment to "shared computational principles" and ultimately to LLMs as mechanistic models of natural language. Indeed, their methodological caveat that alignment does not establish a shared architecture or algorithm sits uneasily with their conclusion that LLMs might instantiate the same computational principles as biological brains and provide a "fully mechanistic model" of language. I discuss what I consider to be problems of logical, causal, and computational underdetermination in Nastase et al.'s (2026) proposal.

</details>


### [44] [Jina-OCR-v1: Efficient Document Parsing with Speculative Decoding and Dense Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03181)
*Alejandro Barón García,Feng Wang,Emilia Garcia Casademont,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Jina-OCR-v1 是面向低成本 GPU 部署的端到端文档解析模型。它基于 DeepSeek-OCR 的压缩视觉编码器与 3B MoE 解码器(每 token 激活约 570M 参数),引入 FastMTP 推测解码头(K=3,共享单一 draft block)实现无损加速,并通过指令对齐、鲁棒性微调和基于密集可验证奖励的 GRPO 三阶段后训练提升质量。在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上取得 91.14 分,在 olmOCR-Bench 上取得 83.4 分,页面吞吐达 2.57 页/秒(对比组最高),在 NVIDIA L4 上 FastMTP 将解码速度近乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端文档解析 VLM 在生产部署(尤其是低成本 GPU)上仍受限于自回归解码开销和后训练数据覆盖不足的问题。论文旨在解决两大瓶颈:(1) 长输出的解码成本;(2) 开放标签中的退化循环与公式/表格结构监督稀疏的问题,同时通过高效推测解码降低单页推理成本。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件:
1. **架构**:复用 DeepSeek-OCR 的 DeepEncoder(SAM+16× conv+CLIP-L,~380M)和 3B MoE 解码器(每 token 激活 ~570M 参数),采用 1024×1024 全局视图(256 tokens)加 Gundam 动态分辨率模式(最多 9 个局部瓦片,每片 100 tokens)。
2. **FastMTP 推测解码**:在解码器上附加一个共享 draft block B_θ,递归执行 K=3 步起草,起草参数量与 K 无关;采用贪婪验证保证无损解码。训练目标融合 CE、KL、MSE 三项损失,对所有递归深度使用相同的行对齐 token/state 目标,权重按 β=0.6 衰减。
3. **后训练**:四阶段目标——(a) 对齐 SFT,冻结视觉塔,采用 0.4 概率的 RoPE 位置偏移实现长度外推;(b) 鲁棒性 SFT,解冻视觉塔,加入光度/几何扰动、历史报纸与困难样本上采样;(c) GRPO,采用 DAPO 变体+ReMax 基线+乘性可验证奖励(内容相似度、公式、表格 TEDS、结构有效性、单元测试、重复惩罚、格式),各项设地板值;(d) 训练数据采用 olmOCR-mix、FinePDFs、LightOnOCR、Europeana、NARA 等公开语料+自构 JinaOCRSynth(密集打包可评分公式与表格)。(e) 外层循环包含代理式 checkpoint 合并、错误分析与数据策展。

Result: 主要实验结果:
- **OmniDocBench v1.6**:Jina-OCR-v1 取得 91.14 Overall(0.046 Text Edit,93.28 Formula CDM,84.68 Table TEDS,89.01 TEDS-S,0.142 RO Edit),位列专门 OCR 模型第三,优于 DeepSeek-OCR-2(90.25)与 Qwen3-VL-235B(89.78)。
- **olmOCR-Bench**:83.4 Overall,位列专门模型第三,仅次于 chandra-ocr-2(85.8)与 dots.mocr(83.9),领先 LightOnOCR-2(83.2)、olmOCR-2(82.4);较基座 DeepSeek-OCR(76.0)提升 7.4 分,验证后训练收益。
- **服务吞吐**(olmCR-Bench, A100 SXM4 40GB,并发 32):2.57 页/秒(对比组最高),1085 output tok/page(高于 83 分系统中最短),2792 output tok/s。
- **推测解码**(NVIDIA L4, vLLM 0.20.1, batch=1):Eager 模式下 K=3 达 1.95× 加速(83.1 tok/s vs 42.7),平均每次推测步提交 2.73 tokens;CUDA graph 模式下基线升至 158.3 tok/s,K=1 达 1.17× 加速。条件接受率 a_1=0.83,a_2=0.66,a_3=0.64,且对执行模式不变。

Conclusion: Jina-OCR-v1 证明:在 DeepSeek-OCR 的压缩视觉 + 紧凑 MoE 解码器基础上,FastMTP 共享 draft block 的推测解码结合面向 GRPO 的密集可验证奖励后训练,可在低成本 GPU 上同时实现高解析质量(OmniDocBench 91.14、olmOCR-Bench 83.4)和最高页面吞吐量(2.57 页/秒)。研究表明:输出长度与解析质量相关性弱(r=+0.22),因此简洁性可独立优化;FastMTP 接受率对执行模式不变,加速效果受验证步成本与可替换性主导。该工作为面向部署的端到端 OCR VLM 提供了一个高效后训练与解码方案。

Abstract: We present Jina-OCR-v1, an end-to-end document parsing model built to serve on low-budget GPUs. It combines the compressed-vision encoder and the 3B mixture-of-experts decoder of DeepSeek-OCR, which activates about 570M parameters per token, with a FastMTP speculative decoding head that shares a single draft block recursively across K=3 prediction steps. Greedy verification makes decoding lossless. Post-training combines instruction alignment, robustness fine-tuning on difficult documents, and GRPO under dense verifiable rewards: deterministic formula, table, and structural checks that award partial credit. The training data mixes cleaned public corpora with targeted synthetic pages. At the default dynamic-resolution setting, Jina-OCR-v1 scores 91.14 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and 83.4 on olmOCR-Bench, and reaches the highest page throughput in our comparison at 2.57 pages per second. On a low-budget GPU such as the NVIDIA L4, FastMTP doubles decoding speed over greedy autoregressive decoding. The model is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [LLMs Learn Better In-Context from Rules than from Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03213)
*Xiang Fu,Seungmin Cho,Yukyung Lee,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了大语言模型通过规则（指令遵循）与示例（少样本提示）进行上下文学习的效果，发现在复杂新颖任务中规则学习整体优于示例学习，且增加示例或扩大示例数量均无法带来显著增益；规则的优势在需要代数抽象的任务中更大，在依赖分布敏感性或参数知识的任务中更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然指令遵循（规则）和少样本提示（示例）是LLM上下文学习的两种主要模式，但它们在指定同一任务时的相对学习效能尚缺乏系统比较。已有研究（如Davidson et al.）发现二者激活部分重叠但不同的机制，但这并不必然意味着组合使用会带来叠加收益。因此有必要在控制任务潜在变量的条件下，对比规则与示例的学习效能，并探究调节该效能的学习者与任务属性。

Method: 设计五个覆盖游戏（Set Game、Tapatan）、算术（Operator Function）和语言学推理（Noun Class Agreement、Lexical Category Inference）领域的新颖复杂任务，每个任务设三个难度等级。将同一潜在任务以三种学习条件呈现给模型：仅规则、仅示例、规则+示例组合。评估来自Gemma 3、Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo 3/3.1家族的7B–32B基础与指令调优模型，并以GPT-5.4作为参考点。使用线性混合模型（LMM）进行统计分析，示例采用最小覆盖原则，并对OLMo家族进行示例数量扩展实验。

Result: 1. 规则学习整体显著优于示例学习，组合条件与仅规则无显著差异；2. 指令调优显著提升规则学习效果但不损害示例学习能力，基础模型中示例学习并无特权优势；3. 规则优势在代数任务（Operator Function、Set Game）中最大，在依赖分布线索（Tapatan）的任务和需要参数知识的语言学任务（Lexical Category Inference）中较小；4. 示例扩展实验显示，增加示例数量或仅靠更多示例无法带来稳定增益；5. 在Lexical Category Inference中，示例学习对合取（AND）定义优于析取（OR），而规则学习不受此不对称影响；6. 任务难度增加时各条件准确率均下降，但下降斜率大致平行。

Conclusion: 当能够提供忠实的规则描述时，当前大语言模型利用规则的能力显著优于仅依赖示例；当任务需要代数抽象与计算时规则优势更大，而当任务依赖分布敏感性或调用参数化知识时规则优势减弱。指令调优放大了规则学习的优势且不削弱示例学习能力，基础模型也未表现出对示例学习的特权依赖。这些发现为预测任务规范下不同上下文学习方法效能及改进学习方法提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit in-context learning capabilities, where they can learn new tasks from prompt contexts without weight updates. We compare the learning efficacies of two prominent modes of in-context learning: (1) learning from descriptions of rules (instruction following); and (2) learning from examples of input-output demonstrations (few-shot prompting). Through five learning tasks that cover diverse domains (games, arithmetic, linguistic inferences), we compare two modes of learning (rules vs. examples) specifying the same underlying task. We furthermore explore model and task properties that modulate the learning efficacies. We find that models generally learn more reliably from rules than from examples alone, and additional examples on top of rules or simply scaling up the number of examples do not lead to consistent and significant gains. Instruction tuning amplifies the benefit of rule-based learning while keeping example-based learning capacities intact. Surprisingly, we find no privileged effect of example-based learning in base models, and rules still lead to gains in algebraic task domains. Overall, the comparative efficacy of rules over examples is larger when the task recruits algebraic abstractions and computations, and smaller when the task requires distributional sensitivity and/or recruits parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [46] [The Analyst in the Prompt: Role, Retrieval, and Memory Biases in LLM Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03218)
*Ahmed Asaad,Amr Mohamed,Yang Zhang,Omneya Abdelsalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型（LLM）在金融分析中因用户上下文（如角色提示、用户画像、记忆）而产生的"用户上下文溢出"问题，设计了一种三条件审计框架，在3,575份SEC文件（10-K/10-Q）和12个LLM上进行了系统测量。研究发现，偏见主要源于模型对相同证据的不同解读（解释性漂移），而非检索到不同的证据；将投资者偏好设置为用户画像而非助手角色可显著减少溢出；双输出架构能将用户信号路由到个性化输出，但效果因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地使用记忆、用户画像和角色提示进行个性化响应，这种个性化可能影响基于证据的判断：在不同用户上下文中，相同证据可能导致不同结论。在金融这一高风险领域，决策往往依赖对长篇复杂文件的解读，因此亟需研究用户上下文如何影响LLM的证据解读稳定性，以及如何设计系统来保持证据判断的不变性。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 设计三条件审计框架——Persona Retrieval (PR)、Neutral Retrieval (NR)、Memory with Neutral Retrieval (MEM)，逐一隔离证据选择、固定证据下的解释、以及用户偏好框定三个通道；(2) 使用九种投资者角色加中性基线，五种匹配的用户记忆画像；(3) 对3,575份SEC 10-K/10-Q文件进行分层采样，使用OpenAI text-embedding-3-large进行检索嵌入（top-k=8）；(4) 使用文件固定效应回归（OLS）估计各角色和画像的影响；(5) 定义四个核心指标——Bias、Retention、Framing Shrinkage、Leakage，并进行三通道分解（证据选择、上下文框定、剩余溢出）；(6) 在DeepSeek-V3.1等12个模型上评估，使用确定性解码（T=0, top-p=1）。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 检索固定后，角色效应平均保留69%（中位68%），说明解释性漂移是偏见的主要来源，而非证据选择；(2) 将投资者偏好从助手角色重新框定为用户画像，可使证据端系数平均缩减73%（如ShortSeller从NR到MEM）；(3) 双输出架构有效降低溢出（所有12个模型的平均泄漏率L_m均低于0.5），但效果模型特异，范围从0.05到0.37，与模型规模不单调相关；(4) 偏见在文件层面可显著——单文件跨角色评分差最高达1.45单位（如Cooper Companies），可导致同一文件的买入/卖出推荐翻转；(5) 偏见与行业语言交互——能源行业普遍看跌，房地产普遍看涨，叠加效应可达0.60单位；(6) 在小市值公司文件上溢出更严重，残余溢出是小盘股的2-4倍；(7) 跨模型验证——框架在GovReport、MS²、CAP三个非金融领域的长文档任务中复现了角色→用户画像的缩减效应（8/9单元格为非负缩减）。

Conclusion: 该研究得出三个核心结论：(1) 用户上下文溢出的主要来源是模型对相同证据的解释性漂移，而非检索差异；(2) 提示位置至关重要——将偏好设置为用户上下文（用户画像）比设置为助手角色产生的溢出少得多，这是最强且最一致的缓解策略；(3) 双输出架构是有效的风险降低技术，但效果因模型而异，不应被视为保证。由此提出三条设计原则：用户上下文应置于助手角色之外；双输出架构应视为不变性探针而非保证；模型选择应考虑路由行为而非仅看任务性能或规模。三条件审计设计为评估个性化LLM系统是否在角色、画像、特征或目标条件下保持基于证据的判断提供了实用协议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly use user context such as memory, profiles, and role prompts to personalize their responses. This personalization can affect evidence-based judgment: the same evidence may lead to different conclusions under different user contexts. Finance provides a high-stakes setting to study this problem because decisions often depend on interpreting long and complex documents. We test this using 3,575 SEC filings across twelve LLMs. We compare persona-conditioned retrieval, neutral retrieval, and memory-framed context to separate the effect of evidence selection from the effect of interpretation. We find that most user-context spillover comes from how models interpret the same evidence under different roles, rather than from retrieving different evidence. We then test two simple mitigation strategies: expressing the same investor mindset as a user profile instead of an assistant role, and separating evidence-based and personalized outputs. Both reduce spillover, but neither removes it completely, and their effectiveness varies substantially across models.

</details>


### [47] [SGD-KV: Summarization Guided KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03235)
*Zeyu Liu,Woomin Song,Xuandi Fu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vivek Govindan,Aram Galstyan,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Srikanth Ronanki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SGD-KV 提出了一种基于"摘要头"识别与优先级排序的 KV 缓存压缩框架，通过分块摘要诊断任务量化各注意力头的层级信息聚合能力，并以注水算法分配缓存预算，在 1M token 场景下实现 75% 内存压缩且保持 SOTA 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩方法多依赖基于检索的简单启发式（如累积注意力分数、注意力汇/滑动窗口），忽略了不同注意力头在功能上的差异。多文档分析与长对话场景需要层级化信息聚合，而非简单模式匹配。作者认为存在专门负责高阶认知功能的"摘要头"，应据此差异化分配缓存预算。

Method: 1) 分块摘要诊断任务：将 CNN/Dailymail、DialogSum 等多个短文摘要数据集拼接，模型需完成"分块识别 + 关键词抽取"两步任务，强制其进行结构解析与语义蒸馏。2) 摘要分数计算：对每个注意力头 h 计算重要性分数 I_h，衡量生成关键词回指其源文本块内最强注意链接的强度，取跨样本平均。3) 缓存预算分配：归一化得分后，按比例分配中间可压缩序列的预算，保留固定 sink 与滑动窗口；借鉴注水算法（water-filling）将超额预算从极高得分头重分配给次高得分头；token 选择阶段沿用 SnapKV 风格的累积注意力打分机制。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-1M 与 Qwen3-32B 上，MRCR 基准下 SGD-KV 在 128K–1M 长度一致超越 AdaKV/HeadKV/DuoAttention（如 1M 处 34.16 vs HeadKV 28.90 vs DuoAttention 24.73），且在 25% 预算下 Qwen3-32B 上 ETHIC 平均分（28.38）几乎与 FullKV（28.53）持平；BABILong 表现与 FullKV 接近；消融显示摘要提示词可作为未知 query 的有效代理；GQA 模型中引入摘要分数加权注意力分数聚合（Ipt., Max）进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 工作超越基于检索的注意力头识别范式，提出"摘要头"这一新功能类别，并以诊断驱动的方式指导 KV 缓存管理，在百万 token 长上下文场景下兼顾内存效率与复杂任务精度；此外，通用摘要提示词可作为 query 不可知场景下 token 选择的高效代理，为高效可解释模型开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face severe memory bottlenecks in long-context inference due to the linearly growing size of key-value (KV) caches. Existing KV cache compression techniques typically rely on simple heuristics, overlooking the distinct functional roles of different attention heads. We present SGD-KV (Summarization-Guided KV Cache Compression), a head-aware framework that leverages a novel chunk-summarization diagnostic task to systematically identify and prioritize attention heads specialized in hierarchical information aggregation. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3-32B across diverse long-context benchmarks demonstrate that SGD-KV achieves state-of-the-art performance with contexts up to 1M tokens, while reducing KV cache memory usage by up to 75%. Our findings show that strategically allocating the KV cache budget based on the summarization score distribution of attention heads yields a superior efficiency-accuracy trade-off for long-context inference.

</details>


### [48] [Contextual Tamil Spelling and Grammar Correction Using Progressively Fine-Tuned Sequence-to-Sequence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03273)
*Karthikeyan A,Jaya Nirmala S,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Indhu R,Pranav Kumar,Bharat Jude Johnson,Vishnu Ram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于渐进式微调的端到端序列到序列框架，利用 mT5-small 和 mBART-50 对低资源黏接语泰米尔语的拼写与语法进行上下文敏感的纠错，涵盖表面噪声、上下文语法和跨词 sandhi（连音）规则等十类错误。其最佳模型 mBART-50 v5 在与训练数据不相交的平衡诊断集上达到 69.3% 的 top-1 完全匹配准确率，跨词 sandhi 准确率高达 87.5%。同时，论文揭示并量化了 sandhi 召回率与身份（identity）准确率之间此前未被报告的精度-召回权衡，并通过与 Tamil-LLaMA-7B 的对照实验证明，仅靠提示工程无法替代针对特定任务的监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰米尔语作为低资源黏着语，拥有247个独特字符、丰富的动词形态变化和复杂的跨词 sandhi 音变规则。然而现有泰米尔语拼写/语法纠错方法（基于规则、统计 n-gram、最小编辑距离或混合管道）普遍局限于词级表面错误，难以处理主谓一致、时态一致、跨词 sandhi 等需要句子级理解的上下文错误。此外，针对特定任务的合成数据与评估基准也存在明显缺口。

Method: 1. **数据构造**：基于泰米尔语维基百科（约 17M 词）经清洗后得到约 120 万句干净语料，并按 70/20/10 划分训练、验证、测试集。生成三类合成数据集：50 万对表面噪声（插入、删除、替代、转置）、75,000 对上下文错误（含语料挖掘和模板生成的主谓一致等10类）和 30,000/40,000 对单点/多站点 vallinam 连音数据集。共构成 657,720 对训练样本。 2. **渐进式微调四阶段课程（v2→v5）**：(i) v2 仅用表面噪声微调 mT5-small/mBART-50；(ii) v3 加入 75,000 对上下文数据加 82,720 对增强对（学习率 3e-6 至 8e-6，采用 AdamW，余弦调度，3% 预热，token-F1 早停 patience=3）；(iii) v4 引入 30,000 对单站 sandhi + 30,000 回放样本；(iv) v5 引入 40,000 对多站 sandhi + 20,000 回放样本。 3. **推理**：束搜索 beam=4，no_repeat_ngram_size=3，mBART-50 强制 ta_IN 语言标记，单次前向传播生成修正句。 4. **评估**：在 1,000 句平衡诊断集（5 类各 200 句，含 200 句无须修改的恒等句）上报告 top-1 完全匹配准确率，并通过精确哈希和 5-gram Jaccard 重叠验证与训练数据不相交，设立 20.0% 的复制基线作为下限。

Result: mBART-50 v5 在 1,000 句诊断集上取得 69.3% 的总体 top-1 准确率（高出复制基线 49.3 个百分点）：跨词 sandhi 87.5%、pulli 还原 72.0%、语音混淆 69.5%、恒等 74.0%、主谓一致 43.5%。**渐进式课程作用明显**：主谓一致从 v2 的 1.0% 跃升至 v3 的 52.5%；跨词 sandhi 在 v2、v3 均为 0%，于 v4（单站）升至 63.0%，v5（多站）达 87.5%。**揭示精度-召回权衡**：mT5 各阶段中恒等准确率随 sandhi 召回率单调下降（87.0%→82.0%→76.5%→72.5% 对应 sandhi 0%→0%→63.0%→83.5%），错误模式为模型对已可接受句子强制应用了可选 sandhi。**Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct 对照**：零样本 19.0%（与 20.0% 复制基线在 McNemar 检验下无显著差异，p=0.59），三样本 24.7%（95% Wilson CI 22.1–27.5），远落后 mBART-50 v5 44.6 个百分点，其主要失败模式为改写无须改写的句子（恒等准确率仅 27.5%→42.0%）。

Conclusion: 本文证明，针对泰米尔语拼写与语法纠错，**任务特定的课程学习比单纯扩大模型规模更关键**：监督信号必须严格匹配错误类型——上下文一致需上下文监督，跨词 sandhi 需多站监督，单纯增加表面噪声或更换骨干均无法替代。mBART-50 的去噪预训练目标与本任务天然契合，使其优于 mT5-small，但代价是恒等精度持续受损，此权衡可通过调整回放样本权重或对可选规则设置置信阈值而成为部署参数。Tamil-LLaMA 即使经过泰米尔语适配和指令微调，在零/少样本下仍接近复制基线，证明窄域正字法任务无法通过提示工程迁移。论文亦指出未来方向：针对主谓一致样本加权回放、覆盖语料领域偏倚、采用 TamilCorp 平衡语料、引入 NLLB 与 LoRA 微调泰米尔大模型，以及由母语者裁决可选 sandhi 的规范问题。

Abstract: Tamil spell and grammar correction is challenging because Tamil is an agglutinative low-resource language with rich verbal morphology, complex sandhi (phonetic transformation) rules at word boundaries, and a script of 247 distinct letters. Prior work targets word-level surface errors with rule-based methods, statistical n-gram models, Minimum Edit Distance, or hybrid pipelines with a transformer re-ranker; such methods cannot reliably handle contextual errors - subject-verb agreement, tense consistency, or cross-word sandhi - which require sentence-level understanding. We propose an end-to-end sequence-to-sequence formulation and fine-tune mT5-small and mBART-50 on a synthetic corpus of up to 657,720 noisy-clean Tamil sentence pairs spanning ten error categories. Both backbones follow the same four-stage progressive schedule, each stage targeting one weakness: surface noise (v2), contextual grammar (v3), single-site sandhi (v4), and multi-site cross-word sandhi (v5). On a 1,000-sentence balanced diagnostic set verified disjoint from all training data, our best model, mBART-50 v5, reaches 69.3% top-1 exact-match accuracy, with 87.5% on sandhi and 43.5% on subject-verb agreement. The schedule is what produces these gains: subject-verb accuracy rises from 1.0% to 52.5% once contextual pairs are introduced, and sandhi from 0% to 87.5% once multi-site sandhi pairs are. We additionally quantify a precision-recall trade-off this literature has not reported: sandhi recall is paid for monotonically in identity accuracy. Finally, Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct reaches 19.0% zero-shot and 24.7% with three demonstrations against a 20.0% copy baseline, showing that a Tamil-adapted instruction model does not transfer to specialised sentence-level correction without task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [49] [PACE: Towards Surfacing Hidden Conflicts in User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03293)
*Yoojin Kim,Jihyoung Jang,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE数据集与PaceMaker多智能体框架，用于评估个性化助手在自我中心知识库中识别用户请求背后隐藏冲突的能力。PACE包含3249个查询，涵盖时间、个人和状态三种情境类型。PaceMaker通过冲突感知查询规划、混合检索、多跳图遍历与冲突感知过滤四个阶段提升决策证据的召回效果。实验表明，PaceMaker在冲突查询上的Pass率较现有稀疏/稠密/结构化检索基线有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化助手不应仅执行用户请求，还需结合用户情境判断请求是否合适。现有安全研究多依赖显式风险信号，而真实场景中冲突线索往往隐式分布在个人知识库中，需要检索与推理协同才能发现。

Method: 1) 构建PACE数据集：从MSC与Synthetic-Person-Chat种子出发，经人格扩展、形象合成（Ego-Alter配对）、查询与上下文生成、干扰项生成与原子化，并经过质量校验，生成冲突与非冲突查询；2) 提出PaceMaker框架，包含冲突规划器（生成≤3个探测线索）、多视图查询生成器（生成反事实视图）、混合检索（稠密+BM25的加权倒数秩融合，counter视图权重1.2）、BFS多跳图遍历（深度H=5，每跳扩展M=3邻居）、预/后跳过滤器与答案生成器。

Result: PaceMaker在三套模型配置（Qwen3、GPT-5.4-mini、Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite）下均优于稀疏、稠密、Full KB、Oracle上限以下的基线：GPT配置Pass率达75.35%，冲突查询Pass率59.17%（较最优非Oracle基线提升3.47–11.40个百分点）；Recall@5达36.05%，显著超越HippoRAG 2（20.15%）与GraphRAG。消融实验表明多跳遍历贡献最大，去掉后Pass率降至71.65%。计算成本上，PaceMaker索引阶段无需LLM调用，冷启动开销低于HippoRAG 2与GraphRAG。人工评估显示数据集可行性标签与自动评判器均与人类判断高度一致（93.3%与93.5%）。

Conclusion: PACE与PaceMaker揭示了从大规模自我中心知识库中检索决策关键证据的挑战，尤其对于需要完整证据覆盖的冲突查询；冲突感知查询规划、混合检索与多跳遍历三者互补，缺一不可；未来工作可在专用智能体训练、动态任务环境与端到端个性化任务执行等方向上扩展。

Abstract: Personalized assistants should not only comply with user requests but also assess whether those requests are appropriate given the user's current circumstances. However, prior work has primarily focused on accurately executing requests, overlooking the need for assistants to account for context and engage in conflict-based refusal. Furthermore, while existing work on conflict or safety detection relies on explicitly provided factors, real-world scenarios often involve implicit factors that must be retrieved from a knowledge base (KB). To this end, we introduce Personalized Assistants for Conflict Evaluation (PACE), a dataset for evaluating whether models can identify latent constraints, expressed as egocentric knowledge or events, that render seemingly reasonable user requests inappropriate. PACE pairs user requests grounded in well-defined personas with egocentric KB facts, requiring models to integrate contextual evidence to determine whether a request is conflicting. This implicit retrieval setting hinders the direct association between user requests and conflict-inducing knowledge, making it difficult for existing models to identify relevant user-specific facts. To address this challenge, we further propose PaceMaker, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents coordinate across query reformulation, multi-hop graph traversal, and conflict-aware filtering to retrieve contextually decisive evidence. Experiments on PACE evaluate both evidence retrieval quality and conflict decision accuracy, showing that PaceMaker consistently outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [50] [Decoupling Turn-Taking from Semantics: A Decoupled Data Approach for Finite-State-Machine-Based Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03321)
*Yihang Li,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦数据方法,将真实人人对话用于轮替管理训练、文本人机对话用于语义训练,并设计事件引导的序列化转换和SAC损失,显著提升FSM全双工对话系统的轮替自然度和语义能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于神经有限状态机(NFSM)的全双工对话框架因依赖LLM生成的合成文本数据,无法准确模拟真实对话中的细粒度声学时间动态,导致轮替管理不够自然。需要将轮替管理与语义建模解耦,使各自从最合适的数据源中学习。

Method: 1) 解耦数据方法:用真实HH口语对话学轮替,用可配置HA文本对话塑造语义;2) 事件引导的数据转换:基于规则将HH对话通过IPU切分和7类轮替事件分类(转换T/延续C/停顿P/间隙G/附和BC/抢话FTI/插话BI),结合14种确定性映射规则序列化为FSM磁带;3) Source-Aware Calibrated(SAC)损失:对状态转换词使用Logit Adjustment校准长尾分布,并按数据源加权以分离轮替与语义的优化目标。

Result: 在Switchboard和Fisher上,轮替F1从NFSM基线的0.3436提升至0.6498(Faster-Whisper配置);VoiceBench语义分数从53.57提升至62.14。SAC损失进一步提升F1并显著改善稀有状态切换词的预测精度。按比例上采样后,语义能力恢复至接近Qwen3-4B零样本上限(64.84 vs 64.93),在Full-Duplex-Bench的12项指标中超过多数基线(Moshi 10/12, Freeze-Omni 8/12, Gemini Live 7/12)。

Conclusion: 解耦数据方法为基于FSM的全双工对话提供了可扩展的方案:真实口语对话用于学习自然轮替动态,可配置文本对话用于塑造语义行为;规则化的事件引导转换避免了LLM标注成本,SAC损失有效缓解状态转换词的长尾问题,使模型在保持基础LLLM语义能力的同时显著提升轮替自然度。

Abstract: The Neural Finite State Machine (NFSM) framework offers a pragmatic path to full-duplex dialogue by serializing turn-taking control and response generation onto a single causal tape under the standard next-token prediction objective, thereby preserving semantic prowess at a low fine-tuning cost. However, its reliance on synthetic text data fundamentally limits turn-taking naturalness, as Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot faithfully simulate the fine-grained acoustic temporal dynamics of real human dialogues. In this work, we propose a decoupled data approach that learns turn-taking from real Human-Human (HH) spoken dialogues while shaping semantic behavior through configurable Human-Agent (HA) text dialogues. To operationalize this approach, we introduce a rule-based event-guided data transformation method that serializes HH spoken dialogues into FSM tapes by classifying turn-taking events and applying deterministic mapping rules, enabling scalable supervision without LLM-generated annotations. We further propose a Source-Aware Calibrated (SAC) Loss that jointly calibrates the long-tailed distribution of state transition tokens and channels each data source toward the capability it best supervises. Experiments show that our approach substantially improves turn-taking proficiency while recovering the foundation LLM's semantic capability. Our code and model are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [51] [How Perturbations Propagate: A Multi-Level Analysis of Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03322)
*Dun Li Chan,Emily Liu,Niyathi Allu,Christian Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对解码器语言模型中六种输入扰动（字符替换、键盘拼写错误、随机 token 替换、token 洗牌、词替换、同义词替换）的传播进行了三层级（输出行为、隐藏状态几何、注意力头功能）系统分析，覆盖 GPT-2 四个尺度和 Qwen2.5 两个尺度。核心发现：仅依赖单一行为或表示度量评估鲁棒性具有误导性；不同扰动类型在多层度量上留下可区分的"指纹"，但跨模型跨尺度仅部分一致；copying score 与 token 替换/洗牌下的激活修补恢复高度相关；对抗式 HotFlip 在行为和表示层面都比随机替换更具破坏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型鲁棒性评估几乎只通过输出行为来判断，但输出退化无法揭示扰动如何改变模型内部计算。两个扰动可能产生相似的输出变化却破坏不同表示或组件，或反之亦然。因此需要多层级的扰动传播分析来回答：扰动效应是否在不同损坏类型间共享、是否定位于可识别的机制、是否跨尺度和家族泛化。

Method: 数据集：WikiText-2 Raw，固定随机种子采样 300 个长度≥128 的序列。模型：GPT-2（Small/Medium/Large/XL）+ Qwen2.5（0.5B/1.5B）。扰动类型：字符替换、QWERTY 邻键拼写错误、随机 token 替换、词替换、WordNet 同义词替换、token 洗牌；强度 p∈{5%, 30%, 50%}。度量三层：(1) 输出层 NLL 与归一化 Levenshtein 输出散度；(2) 表示层 CKA（去除高方差维度后）与 TwoNN 内在维度变化；(3) 注意力头功能分数（前 token、duplicate、induction、基于 OV 电路的 copying）+ 归一化 attention entropy 变化 + clean activation patching（恢复率 Δ%）。对抗对比：HotFlip 梯度引导（短名单 |S|=50，仅当真正提升 NLL 才接受替换）与位置匹配的随机 token 替换对照。所有修补分析仅限位置保持扰动（token 替换、洗牌）。

Result: RQ1：六种扰动在输出与表示层产生可区分的"度量指纹"。字符替换产生最大输出散度；token 与词替换输出散度相近但 NLL 差距大，说明生成文本变化与预测置信度可分离。CKA 随扰动强度下降，字符/拼写错误最低，shuffle 最高。TwoNN 显示高强度下字符/拼写在早期层降低内在维度，而 token 替换导致最大正向变化。RQ2：induction 分数与 entropy 变化负相关最强（−0.27 至 −0.61）；shuffle 下前 token 分数正相关最强（0.670）。位置保持扰动下，copying 分数与修补恢复率关联最强：token 替换下修补 NLL 恢复相关 0.726，shuffle 下 0.622；输出散度相关 0.652 和 0.348。其他头功能分数相关较小或不一致。RQ3：GPT-2 与 Qwen2.5 家族差异度量依赖性强：token 替换在输出散度上家族差距可忽略，但在 NLL 与内在维度上 GPT 家族变化更大。Shuffle 在 30%/50% 强度下家族输出散度差距最大。基线 NLL 本身已差 0.592，跨家族鲁棒性结论需基线调整。RQ4：HotFlip 在所有六个模型、两个强度下均比位置匹配随机替换产生更高 NLL 与输出散度；GPT-2 内部表示上 CKA 更低、内在维度上升更大，说明随机替换无法复现对抗攻击的响应画像。

Conclusion: 本文表明扰动类型在行为、表示几何与电路级功能上留下可区分签名，但这些签名仅跨模型尺度与家族部分保守，意味着所用度量会显著混淆发现。仅依赖单一行为或表示度量的鲁棒性结论具有误导性，应寻求多层度量的趋同证据。对抗优化是趋同点之一——行为与内部度量在 NLL、输出散度、CKA、内在维度上一致指向 HotFlip 产生真实多层级扰动。copying 头功能分数与 token 替换/shuffle 的修补恢复强关联，表明功能性头角色在自然扰动下亦可识别。局限：CKA 对改变 token 化的扰动不能保证位置对齐；头功能分数建立关联而非因果；跨家族差异未隔离 GQA/RoPE/词表等架构因素；分析限于单一数据集与两个家族。未来工作可扩展对抗框架到其他扰动形式，并测试架构消融。

Abstract: Language models encounter typos, corrupted text, altered words, and disrupted token order, yet robustness is usually evaluated only through output behavior. We study how six naturalistic and synthetic input perturbations propagate through decoder-only language models at three levels: output behavior, hidden-state geometry, and attention-head function. We evaluate behavioral effects across four GPT-2 and two Qwen2.5 checkpoints by analyzing layerwise geometry using centered kernel alignment and intrinsic dimension, and examine attention-head responses in GPT-2. Perturbation types produce distinguishable metric profiles that are not fully captured by output measures and are only partly consistent across the tested checkpoints. Copying scores are especially associated with activation-patching recovery under token substitution and shuffling. Gradient-guided HotFlip perturbations also cause stronger behavioral and representational disruption than rate-matched random token substitutions in GPT-2; their behavioral effects are consistent across all six tested checkpoints. Our results show that robustness claims based on a single behavioral or representational metric can be misleading, and motivate multi-level evaluation of how perturbations alter language-model computation.

</details>


### [52] [Less Is Moral: A CHARMing Framework for Moral Foundations Detection in Endorsement Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03330)
*Huixiang Fu,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARM框架,基于轻量微调的LLaMA-3.1-8B模型,通过整合MAC合作域结构、人类推理依据对齐和仇恨言论极性调制三个心理构建,实现道德基础检测,在跨域性能和效率上优于现有方法,并成功应用于COVID-19推特讨论中的道德一致性与转发行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德基础检测系统存在三大不足:(1)检测与心理学理论脱节,仅利用MFT标签词汇而忽视MAC等互补框架;(2)忽略极性与反社会表达的耦合关系;(3)微调模型泛化性差而提示型LLM成本高昂,存在鲁棒性-效率矛盾。核心问题是:内容创作者的道德框架是否驱动在线认可行为?认可网络是否呈现道德同质性?回答这些问题需要可靠的规模化道德框架检测方法。

Method: CHARM采用两阶段框架:第一阶段使用LoRA适配器(rank=16)对LLaMA-3.1-8B进行基础级道德表征学习;第二阶段在冻结编码器上构建极性感知的分类头,包含三个核心组件:(1)基于eMACDscore的MAC理论接地——通过交叉注意力将7维MAC合作域信号融入10维MFT表征;(2)人类推理依据监督——采用rationale selector预测token级依据,并通过可学习插值的rationale-steered attention pooling生成文本表征,配合TV正则化促进连续依据预测;(3)基于FiLM的仇恨言论调制——将仇恨预测概率转化为通道级缩放/平移参数,调制道德表征。损失函数整合极性感知道德BCE损失、依据监督BCE损失、TV平滑损失及仇恨检测BCE损失。

Result: 在9个数据集上的实验表明:CHARM在域内AUC提升最高达15.3%,在所有5个域外数据集上均超越监督基线(MFormer、MoralBERT、Tuning-GPT4o-mini)的AUC和F1,10维极性分类头比最强极性基线提升0.33 AUC。消融实验显示MAC接地提供最强的跨域归纳偏置(移除导致ARG F1从.54降至.43);依据监督提升证据基础与预测忠实度(IoU从.14提升至.62);三信号互补增益。COVID-19 Twitter案例研究显示:作者侧道德框架比转发者侧更能预测转发行为(AUC .84 vs .72),认可网络在所有五项道德基础上均呈现显著同质性,忠诚维度同配性最高(r=.46),作者纯洁性是个体最强预测因子但网络同配性最低(r=.27),表明道德化内容可吸引广泛认可。

Conclusion: CHARM通过在模型架构而非仅标签空间中嵌入心理学结构,弥合了社会心理学理论与LLM实践之间的鸿沟。其基于开源权重的轻量设计提供了比提示型LLM更高效的可扩展替代方案,同时具备强鲁棒性、忠实性和样本效率。在大规模社交媒体分析中,CHARM揭示了道德框架与在线认可行为的密切关联,特别指出创作者的纯洁性框架能吸引超越道德一致性的广泛转发,为研究道德化错误信息传播提供了实用工具。局限在于:极性级标注稀缺、MAC监督依赖自动生成标签、在抽象社会规范类数据(VIG、MIC)上表现弱于提示型方法。

Abstract: Moral language plays a central role in shaping online endorsement and the diffusion of information, yet existing moral foundation detection systems often suffer from poor cross-domain generalization, weak rationale grounding, and reliance on costly prompting-based large language models (LLMs). We introduce CHARM, a MA\textbf{C}- and \textbf{H}ate-speech-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}ationale-aligned \textbf{M}oral foundation detection framework built on a lightweight fine-tuned LLM, which integrates complementary moral grounding, rationale alignment, and polarity-aware hate speech signals to support more robust and faithful moral prediction. Unlike prior dictionary-, fine-tune-, or prompt-based detectors, which decouple computation from psychological theory, CHARM is built so that each component -- MAC cross-attention, rationale alignment, and hate-speech modulation -- operationalizes a distinct psychological construct. Using a 30\% subsample of the MFTC, MFRC, and News training pools together with the richer supervision in MFTCXplain, CHARM improves AUC by up to 15.3\% in-domain, surpasses the supervised baselines on every out-of-domain dataset in both AUC and F1, and offers a scalable, low-cost alternative to prompting-based LLM detectors. We further apply CHARM to large-scale COVID-19 discourse on Twitter and show that moral value alignment is strongly associated with online endorsement behavior. By making moral framing measurable at scale, CHARM offers a practical tool for studying the spread of morally charged misinformation.

</details>


### [53] [FPCO-Dialog: A Multi-Turn False-Premise Benchmark for Correction and Cooperation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03331)
*Jiayuan Ma,Yuqi Lu,Weiyang Guo,Chenrui Wang,Junyi Shu,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 FPCO-Dialog，用于测试视觉语言模型在多轮对话中面对持续重复、与图像冲突的用户指代表达时，究竟会纠正错误前提还是直接合作回答。基准含 1,080 张图像、10,800 个问题轮次和 20 个模型。对 20 个商业及开源 VLM 的评估显示，模型间纠正倾向差异显著且较稳定：身份错误最常被纠正（约 0.56），属性错误居中（约 0.41），位置错误最少（约 0.15）。双检测器与人工验证表明评分可靠，但身份—属性排序对具体措辞较敏感；此外，多个模型在重复错误出现后纠正率下降，说明单轮性能不能充分代表多轮可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基准主要评估答案正确性，却较少分离“模型是否发现并修正用户引入的错误视觉前提”与“模型是否直接按该前提合作回答”。特别是在同一错误跨多轮持续存在时，一次性发现错误不足以刻画稳定交互策略。论文因此建立一个受控、模型无关的多轮基准，将前提纠正与合作回答明确区分。

Method: 数据集由 MS COCO 和 Open Images 中的人工筛选图像构成。控制变量包括：3 类视觉复杂度（单目标清晰、多目标分离、多目标重叠）、4 个对象类别（人物、车辆、动物、食物）和 3 类错误前提（身份、属性、位置）；每种组合 30 张图，共 1,080 张。每段对话固定 10 轮：前 3 轮使用正确指代表达建立上下文，后 7 轮将同一指代表达做一次最小编辑后重复注入。评估 20 个商业/API 与开源 VLM，每模型生成 10,800 个回答，总计 216,000 个回答。开放回答由 GPT-5.4 和 Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview 两个独立检测器按“是否明确纠正或修复错误前提”标注，主分数取二者算术平均。核心指标 CorrTP@K 统计截至第 K 个错误前提轮次时，被纠正的错误前提回答比例；TurnCorr 分析单轮动态，CorrFP 检测对正确前提的过度纠正。另以 360 个回答进行双人专家验证。

Result: 1. 模型间差异显著且跨轮次保持：例如 Qwen-VL-Max 的 CorrTP@10 约 0.688，Gemini 2.5 Pro 约 0.649，而部分模型明显较低；不同模型的曲线从第 4 至第 10 轮仍保持分离，说明总体纠正倾向具有稳定性。2. 错误类型差异明显：在当前替代措辞分布下，身份错误的平均 CorrTP@10 约 0.56，属性错误约 0.41，位置错误约 0.15。位置错误最常被模型忽略并直接回答。3. 视觉复杂度的主效应较小：单目标清晰、多目标分离和重叠场景的平均 CorrTP@10 分别约 0.39、0.38 和 0.35；但复杂度与错误类型存在交互，属性错误的纠正率随场景变复杂下降，身份错误相对稳定。4. 重复轮次暴露衰减：10 个模型从 Q4 到 Q10 的 TurnCorr 下降超过 5 个百分点，最大下降 15.93 个百分点，说明部分模型首次指出问题后，后续会沿用用户错误前提。5. 纠正通常与回答并行，不等于拒绝帮助；低 CorrFP（约 0.01）表明高纠正率并非无差别质疑。6. 双检测器总体一致率为 0.973、Cohen's κ=0.942；人工子集上二者一致，κ=0.960，与专家标注的一致性为 0.832–0.855，位置类仍是相对最模糊的类别。7. 30 张图、3 模型的词汇敏感性探索表明，位置错误低于身份/属性的差异较稳健，但完整“身份>属性>位置”排序仅在 73.1% 的随机 A/B 选择中保持，因此身份与属性的差异不能完全归因于错误类型本身。

Conclusion: FPCO-Dialog 将多模态可靠性从“答案是否正确”扩展到“模型如何处理与视觉证据冲突的用户前提”。结果表明，纠正不是统一伴随模型规模或家族出现的属性：不同 VLM 会形成不同的交互策略，且身份、属性和位置错误会触发明显不同的行为。高 CorrTP@K 表示更愿意显式修复错误前提，并不直接代表答案质量、帮助性、安全性或语用恰当性更高。该基准适合比较模型的多轮事实一致性和交互策略，但尚不能判定不纠正究竟是合理的语用容纳，还是未检测到视觉矛盾。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings where users may describe visual content with incorrect assumptions. Yet existing evaluations rarely isolate how models respond when the same visually grounded false premise persists across dialogue turns. We introduce FPCO-Dialog, a benchmark for evaluating correction and cooperation behavior in VLMs under repeated false premises. FPCO-Dialog contains 1,080 images and 10,800 question turns, stratified by visual complexity, object category, and false-premise class, and uses a 10-turn protocol in which a correct dialogue prefix is followed by repeated false-premise referring expressions. We evaluate 20 commercial and open-source VLMs with a model-agnostic protocol and CorrTP@K, a correction-rate metric over false-premise turns, scored by two independent detectors. FPCO-Dialog reveals substantial and persistent cross-model differences in aggregate correction tendency, model-specific turn-wise dynamics, and systematic variation across false-premise types under the benchmark's substitution distribution. The dataset, evaluation protocol, model outputs, detector labels, and code are available.

</details>


### [54] [Accountable AI with Grounded, Faithful, Consistent, Actionable Rationales: A Case Study in Clinical Trial Matching with VERDICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03366)
*Zikai Zhou,Yufei Jin,Yilin Xu,Yu-Chiang Wang,Chieh-Ju Chao,Monica S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT框架，将临床试验匹配任务分解为自然语言理解与形式化决策两个阶段：LLM负责将试验标准与患者记录解析为SMT（可满足性模理论）公式，Z3 SMT/MaxSMT求解器负责推导匹配决策、决策轨迹、隐含假设及关键翻转条件。论文提出"问责性AI"的四项核心要求（基于证据、有据可查、策略一致、可操作），并引入"自忠实性"作为自动评估指标。通过SIGIR 2016衍生数据集（552对）与TREC 2021（363对）上的实验，VERDICT在决策准确率上优于所有LLM原生与神经符号基线（LlmMatch、CoT LLM、TrialGPT、ZSPM、DLSC），在策略一致性上达到100%，生成的解释也更受临床医生青睐，关键翻转条件与决策之间具有构造性的因果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险决策场景中存在输出流畅但缺乏依据、不完整或不忠实于决策过程的问题，导致决策无法被审查、质疑或解释。临床试验匹配是体现这一矛盾的典型场景：现有LLM匹配器虽准确性尚可，但策略应用不一致，且生成的理由往往不忠实于自身决策，临床医生无法据此验证或采取行动。

Method: VERDICT采用"神经符号"架构：(1) LLM模块（TParse、PParse、Arbiter、Verbalize）将试验纳入/排除标准解析为类型化原子条件ϕₜ，并从病历中抽取相应证据，构建带策略标记的患者表示p_{t,Π}（观察值/插补值/未解析值）；(2) Z3 SMT求解器在ϕₜ∧S上判断可满足性并产出决策痕迹γ；(3) MaxSMT求解器在软约束加权下计算使决策翻转的最小变化集合δ_E（变为合格）或δ_I（变为不合格），以及使合格判断最大化的未解析条件赋值ρ。定义"自忠实性"指标PivotalFlipRate：在形式表示层面翻转关键条件必然翻转决策结果。评估涵盖决策准确性、临床医生盲评解释质量、策略一致性测试及反事实自忠实性测试。

Result: 在SIGIR 2016衍生数据集上（GPT-4.1/4o/4o-mini三个骨干），VERDICT的F1分别达到0.900/0.836/0.754，均为各骨干下的最佳值；在TREC 2021上（GPT-5-mini、Claude Haiku 4.5、Qwen2.5-7B），F1分别为0.828/0.800/0.738，同样全面优于LlmMatch、CoT LLM、ZSPM和DLSC基线。将Qwen2.5-7B的形式化器进行微调后，F1从0.738提升至0.829，与GPT-5-mini持平。临床医生盲评中，VERDICT相对ZSPM赢得90.6%（13胜3平0负），相对LlmMatch赢得75.0%（11胜2平3负），在标准完整性、病历可追溯性、连贯性、可操作性和决策支持五个维度均获最高分（4.62-5.00/5）。策略一致性测试中，VERDICT对显式缺失信息策略达到100%遵循，结构化最强基线仅71-81%。反事实自忠实性测试中，VERDICT的PivotalFlipRate达84.3%（原始）和80.9%（人口重建），显著高于最强基线CoT LLM的65.0%/72.7%与LlmMatch-Pivotal的57.7%/55.8%。语义解析审计显示99.6%原子条件保持语义不变，94.3%标准完全正确，仅2.8%最终决策受解析错误影响。

Conclusion: 将LLM的语言理解能力与SMT/MaxSMT求解器的形式化推理能力相结合，可以"构造性地"实现问责性AI的四个支柱：基于证据（求解器产出可验证的推导）、有据可查（显式假设与插补记录）、策略一致（求解器机械执行确定性策略）、可操作（最小翻转集合指出改变决策的条件）。该方法在高风险医疗决策场景中具备实际部署价值，但LLM在标准形式化、病历证据抽取和上下文相关策略应用上的错误仍是残余错误的主要来源，未来的工作需要在真实电子病历、前瞻性临床审查及子群体公平性评估上进一步验证。

Abstract: Accountability means a decision can be examined, justified, and contested. LLMs make this hard: fluent output may be ungrounded, incomplete, or unfaithful to the decision process. Achieving accountability requires verified rationales (how was the decision reached), assumptions (what was assumed rather than known), policy consistency (the same treatment for the same facts), and pivotal conditions (what would change the outcome). We introduce self-faithfulness as an automatic test of accountability: changing the pivotal conditions should change the decision. We examine accountable AI through clinical trial matching, a high-stakes task central to evidence-based medicine. Although LLM-based matchers match patients to trials reasonably accurately, they apply decision policies inconsistently and produce rationales that are unfaithful to their own decisions. We introduce VERDICT, an LLM-based agent that translates a decision task, its constraints, and its policy into Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT), then derives the decision with SMT and MaxSMT solvers -- so policies are applied consistently and decisions are accountable by construction. Across a SIGIR 2016-derived dataset and TREC 2021, VERDICT achieves the strongest decision accuracy among LLM-only and neurosymbolic baselines, applies policies with perfect consistency, and produces clinician-preferred rationales grounded in explicit assumptions and pivotal conditions, with improved counterfactual self-faithfulness.

</details>


### [55] [FrameBench:A Language Understanding Benchmark Based on Frame Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03370)
*Chihiro Yano,Ryohei Sasano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FrameBench是一个基于Frame Semantics（框架语义学）的多语言（英语和日语）多选题基准测试，旨在评估大语言模型（LLM）能否区分同一动词在不同上下文所唤起的不同语义框架，即能否像人类一样进行隐式的语境依赖性语义丰富。研究通过FrameNet风格的资源和LLM生成加人工验证的流程构建基准，实验表明多个大型模型的表现超越人类参考分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在众多下游任务上表现出色，但尚不清楚它们是否能像人类一样进行框架语义学中提到的隐式、上下文依赖的语义丰富（即根据上下文推断未明确表达的信息）。现有评测基准大多汇集多样化下游任务，未直接测试这种细粒度的语义区分能力。

Method: 1. 任务设计：每个条目包含一道问题和四个候选句子，由一个多义动词和一对语义框架构造而成，目标框架对应两个正确句子，对比框架对应两个错误句子。模型需从四个选项中选择（句子A、句子B、两者皆是、两者皆非）。2. 构建流程：(1) 从FrameNet抽取多义动词及框架对，使用LLM（GPT-5）生成基础句子对和问题；(2) 扩展目标句子对以增加多样性；(3) 由母语者进行正确性和可接受性的人工验证与过滤。3. 为英语和日语分别构建基准（英语731项，日语549项）。4. 评估多种LLM，并进行错误分析、位置偏差分析、句对类型分析以及多模态影响的对比研究。

Result: 1. 多个前沿模型（如Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5、Qwen3.5-27B）在英语FrameBench上达到99分左右，超越人类参考分数96.3；日语上Gemma 4 31B达到99.1，人类参考分数为94.9。2. 性能随模型规模和推理模式的启用而显著提升，尤其在中小模型上推理带来的增益最为明显（如Qwen3.5-2B英语从48.5提升到80.3）。3. 基础句子对（表面相似）比扩展句子对更难；模型存在一定的句子位置偏差；高分模型的错误主要来自过度选择或未选择，而非选错框架。4. 多模态模型在FrameBench上大多优于对应的纯文本模型，但提升无法与MMLU-Pro等其他基准完全分离。5. 案例研究表明，即使大型模型在区分细微的框架语义差异（如christened的[Rite]框架需借助"瓶装香槟"等线索）时仍可能失败。6. 日语FrameBench比英语更能区分模型能力，弱模型在跨语言上表现下降更显著。

Conclusion: FrameBench为评估LLM的框架语义理解能力提供了新的、基于FrameNet的可靠基准，覆盖英语和日语两种类型学差异较大的语言。实验表明，尽管部分前沿模型已超越人类参考分数，但模型规模、推理能力和语言因素仍显著影响表现。分析揭示了模型在细微语义区分、位置偏差和多模态训练影响等方面的行为特征，为未来框架语义理解的改进提供了方向。

Abstract: In frame semantics, sentence comprehension is assumed to proceed by relating lexical meaning to background knowledge called semantic frames, thereby enabling readers to implicitly enrich the text with unstated information. Recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether they can reproduce the kinds of implicit enrichment that humans naturally make during comprehension. To address this question, we introduce FrameBench, a benchmark grounded in frame semantics. FrameBench consists of multiple-choice questions that test whether models distinguish the frames evoked by the same verb across contexts. We construct the benchmark for English and Japanese using FrameNet-style resources and a generation-and-verification pipeline with native-speaker judgments. Our experiments on a diverse set of models reveal challenges for small models, while several large models surpass the human reference scores. We release the constructed FrameBench dataset and the code for dataset construction and evaluation at this https URL .

</details>


### [56] [Chiaroscuro for Emotions: A Contrastive Emotion Benchmark Grounded in Appraisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03394)
*Divyesh Bommana,Mohammad Saim,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHIARO基准——一个基于评估理论的对比式情感推理数据集，包含1000个句子，每个场景描述同一事件引发两个对立情感（共十类情感），用于测试模型在双角色归因任务中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别基准通常预测单一文本中的单一情感，忽略了真实场景中两人共享同一事件却产生对立情感的情况（如孩子兴奋踢座椅而前排乘客愤怒），缺乏对"对比式情感推理"的评估资源。

Method: 基于评估理论，从r/AmITheAsshole帖子构建数据集；采用两阶段生成（草稿+物理/非物理因果模式渲染）；通过词汇约束（禁用约70个情感词）和六项程序化验证器确保情感需从情境推断而非显式词汇；由两名标注员标注并仲裁得到1000句基准；评估7个LLM和4个现成情感分类器，并训练3个RoBERTa-large检查点（CHIARO-only、GoEm-only、Combined）在10个外部基准上测试。

Result: 最强LLM（GPT-5.5）macro-F1仅67.3，远低于人类一致性93.0；现成情感分类器仅11.8-29.0（接近随机）；CHIARO+GoEmotions联合训练在CHIARO自身及10个外部基准中6个上优于单一来源；错误集中在正向情感（joy和gratitude召回率极低，多被误判为relief），非物理场景反而比物理场景表现更好。

Conclusion: CHIARO确立了一个独立的任务族——第三人对个体归因的情感推理，其作为互补训练信号的价值得到验证；当前LLM和单智能体情感分类器在该任务上存在显著差距，主要源于角色级情感归因能力不足；正向细粒度情感和物理因果触发是主要困难所在。

Abstract: Emotion recognition benchmarks often predict one emotion per text, missing many real-world scenarios where two people arrive at opposing emotions from a single shared event. For example, a child kicks the seat in front of her in excitement while the passenger ahead grows angry. We introduce CHIARO, a 1,000 human-annotated sentence benchmark for contrastive emotion inference grounded in appraisal theory. Each scene describes one causal trigger eliciting a positive emotion in one person and a negative emotion in the other, drawn from a ten-class taxonomy. We benchmark seven frontier LLMs and four off-the-shelf emotion classifiers. The strongest LLM reaches 67.3 macro-F1, well below human agreement, while existing emotion classifiers score near chance. Beyond evaluation, CHIARO also serves as a training signal. When combined with an existing emotion corpus, the resulting downstream classifier improves on CHIARO itself and on six of ten external emotion benchmarks, which positions our dataset as a complementary signal for emotion recognition.

</details>


### [57] [To What Extent Do Large Language Models Understand Bangla Idioms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03410)
*Mousumi Akter,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个大规模孟加拉语（Bangla）习语基准数据集及合成的多项选择题（MCQ）数据集，并在零样本与少样本提示策略下，对多个最新大语言模型（LLMs）在习语释义、习语片段检测和习语含义识别三项任务上进行了全面评估，发现不同模型在不同任务上表现各异，没有一致的最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语是自然语言中体现文化细微差异的重要组成部分，对计算模型（尤其在低资源语言中）构成独特挑战。孟加拉语是一种低资源语言，缺乏针对习语的大规模基准数据集与系统性 LLM 评估，因此亟需相关资源与分析以推动低资源语言习语理解研究。
