Method: 1. 构建首个大规模孟加拉语习语数据集；2. 基于该数据集合成多项选择题（MCQ）数据集用于习语含义识别；3. 选取多个最新 LLMs，在零样本（zero-shot）与少样本（few-shot）提示策略下，评估三项任务：习语释义（paraphrasing）、习语片段检测（idiom span detection）、习语含义识别（meaning identification）；4. 对比分析不同模型在各项任务上的表现差异。

Result: 模型性能差异显著，无单一 LLM 在所有任务上均优于其他模型。具体而言：Phi-4-mini-instruct 在释义任务中表现最佳；Kimi-K2-32b-instruct 在习语片段检测任务中表现最佳；Gemini-2.5-flash 在含义识别任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本文所构建的数据集与系统性分析为未来研究提供了有价值的资源，有助于推动改进对孟加拉语及其他低资源语言习语表达的 LLM 理解能力，同时表明针对不同习语任务需要采用不同模型或定制化策略。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions are an integral part of natural language, reflecting cultural nuances and posing unique challenges for computational models, particularly in low-resource languages. In this paper, we present the first large-scale benchmark dataset of Bangla idioms, complemented by a synthetic multiple-choice question (MCQ) dataset for idiom meaning identification. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of recent large language models (LLMs) across three idiom-related tasks: paraphrasing, idiom span detection, and meaning identification, leveraging zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies. Our results reveal substantial variability in model performance, with no single LLM consistently outperforming others across all tasks. Notably, Phi-4-mini-instruct excels in paraphrasing, Kimi-K2-32b-instruct in span detection, and Gemini-2.5-flash in meaning identification. We believe that our datasets and analyses will provide valuable resources to guide future research in improving LLM comprehension of idiomatic expressions, particularly in Bangla and other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [58] [Lngram v2: Latent N-Gram Memory with Interpretable Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03426)
*Yunao Zheng,Bin Wen,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram v2 是一种插入 Transformer 解码器层的潜在条件记忆模块。它先把每个隐藏状态量化为多个硬离散 ID，再为每个 ID 组合构造精确的 2/3-gram 内存地址，通过上下文感知的分组查询注意力读取检索结果。作者在 Keye2B 和 Keye30B 视觉语言模型上验证：平均性能提升、额外参数和推理开销较低，并发现这些离散 ID 保留了隐藏状态的大量语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 没有原生查找机制，重复的局部多 token 模式需要通过昂贵的全连接计算来近似。Lngram v1 已用潜在 n-gram 内存实现条件检索，但其路由数量与骨干宽度耦合，导致容量、参数量和激活量难以独立扩展。此外，连续隐藏状态被离散化后是否仍保留可解释语义，也缺乏系统验证。论文由此提出两个目标：构建可独立扩展的高效条件记忆；并检验离散地址能否作为内部表示的可查询语义接口。

Method: 方法包括：1）多路由硬离散化：将 RMSNorm 后的隐藏状态投影为 R×M 个 logit，以符号函数生成 M 位代码和 2^M 个 code；不同路线提供并行离散签名。2）精确 n-gram 检索：为每个 n-gram 阶数建立 RK^n×d_m 内存表，将当前窗口中的代码混合编码成路由专属地址，直接索引并屏蔽无效前缀和跨序列边界。3）上下文感知读取：各路线的检索向量作为 memory token，当前隐藏状态作为 query，经 GQA 对路线自适应加权；固定零值 Sink 提供不读取任何内存的选项。大路由数可沿路线维度分块，在线累积 softmax 归一化和加权值。4）反事实代理梯度：前向保持硬路由和精确查表不变，反向通过切换某个符号或位、比较反事实内存内容来估计路由梯度；大模型采用每个位一次翻转的近似，复杂度由 O(K) 降为 O(M)。5）语义分析：仅将 128-route 离散签名作为输入，在 COCO 参考集上确定统计标准和 KNN 读出器，在独立留出集上测试；分析 code 利用率、概念关联以及多路线组合语义。主要性能实验覆盖 2B/30B Keye2-VL，并与基线、PKM、等参数 Sparse FFN、Lngram v1 比较。

Result: 主要结果如下：1）Keye2B 十项基准平均分由 47.25±0.15 提升到 47.93±0.18，+0.68；PKM 为 47.24，参数匹配的 Sparse FFN 为 45.86。2）Keye30B 平均分由 78.23 提升到 79.71，+1.48，95% 配对自助法区间为 [0.86, 2.11]；八项任务优于基线。AI2D 和 MMBench-EN 经 Holm 校正后仍显著，MMBench-CN 经 BH 校正后显著。3）语义分析中，ID-only KNN 保留连续隐藏状态可读语义能力的 65.77%–84.27%；在留出集上，全部六个设置的置换检验 p=1/501。4）每层 128 条路线中，参考集发现 16,304 个可评估候选关联，留出集有 9,220 个通过方向、置信区间和 BH 校正，整体复现率为 65.85%；局部存在任务的两层复现率为 83.01% 和 84.94%。5）多路线组合明显优于最佳单路线，AP 提升 2.02–12.32 倍，说明语义主要分布式编码。6）代码空间未坍缩：每层每条路由平均约 8 个有效 code，2048 个可能 code 均出现。7）效率：r64/m4/KV8 在长度 1024 时，预填充延迟 +6.1%，显存 +0.22 GiB；解码延迟 +8.0%，显存 +0.16 GiB。8）相对 Lngram v1，R=16 的 v2 模块总参数量减少 82.6%，每 token 激活参数量减少 95.2%，验证损失从 2.8609 略降至 2.8584。需要注意：在 2B/30B 主配置中，2B 实际采用 R=121，30B 实际采用 R=256；因此 v1/v2 效率对比使用的是 0.6B 受控模型，不能直接视为两种 VLM 配置下的同路由效率差。

Conclusion: Lngram v2 解耦了路线数、内存维度和骨干宽度，以硬离散精确 n-gram 寻址、GQA 上下文读取、Sink 和反事实代理梯度，实现低参数、低激活、可扩展的潜在条件记忆。它在 2B 与 30B 视觉语言模型上带来稳定提升，推理开销适中，并显著优于等参数的 Sparse FFN。离散 ID 不仅能执行内存索引，还保留隐藏状态的可复现语义结构，因此可作为模型内部表示的语义查询和统计接口。论文还指出一个重要适用条件：骨干表示必须在训练期间相对稳定；否则离散地址频繁漂移，会分散训练信号、削弱记忆和残差注入。主要局限是实验集中于作者体系的 Keye-VL 与单一 0.6B 语言模型对照，跨架构、跨训练阶段和更长上下文上的泛化仍需外部验证。

Abstract: Transformers lack a native lookup mechanism, requiring repeated dense computation to recognize and reuse local static patterns. Lngram v1 introduces tokenizer-independent conditional memory through discrete latent n-gram addressing, but its memory capacity is coupled with the backbone width, limiting scalability due to high parameter and activation costs. We propose Lngram v2, which decouples the number of routes, memory dimension, and backbone width, and introduces a context-aware grouped-query attention readout to scale memory capacity independently. A zero-value Sink and counterfactual surrogate gradients further improve readout selectivity and routing trainability while preserving hard discrete addressing. Experiments across vision--language models (VLMs) of different scales show consistent improvements, including successful scaling to a 30B-parameter model. Compared with Lngram v1, Lngram v2 substantially reduces both total and activated memory parameters while maintaining or improving language modeling performance. Further analysis shows that its discrete IDs preserve substantial semantic structure of continuous hidden states, enabling semantic recovery from IDs alone and stable ID--semantic associations across datasets. These results establish Lngram v2 as an efficient and scalable latent conditional memory mechanism whose discrete addresses also provide a structured interface for analyzing internal model representations.

</details>


### [59] [Random Attention: Rethinking KV Cache Eviction for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03430)
*Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Liangwei Yang,Jiawei Han,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Random Attention——一种无需打分、仅保留 prompt 并在每个注意力头内均匀随机淘汰的 KV Cache 驱逐策略，证明在多个推理任务中其性能可匹配甚至超越现有最强打分型驱逐器，同时在 vLLM 部署中提供 32-43% 的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV Cache 压缩方法均依赖对每个 token 评分后保留 top-k，但本文质疑这一"选择信号决定准确性"的核心假设是否成立。长链思维推理场景中 KV Cache 成为严重内存瓶颈，需要重新审视哪些设计真正有用。

Method: 1. Random Attention 算法：强制保留整个 prompt（位置 1 到 ℓ_p）不淘汰，其余候选位置在每个 KV 头内独立生成 i.i.d. 均匀随机分数，取 top-K 即可；2. 控制实验分离 prompt 保护与选择信号的贡献；3. 植入事实探针实验分析跨头冗余机制；4. 通过 vLLM PagedAttention 集成评估吞吐量。

Result: 在 4 个模型（Qwen3-4B/14B/32B、Phi-4-reasoning）和 6 个推理任务（MATH500、GPQA-D、AIME、HMMT、LiveCodeBench）上，Random Attention 在 60 个基线对比单元中有 31 个显著领先、仅 1 个显著落后（Qwen3-32B 代码任务，差异源于 prompt 长度）；在 vLLM H200 部署中，32k 生成长度下吞吐量较 TriAttention 高 32-43%（Qwen3-32B 上达 2.67× 全注意力吞吐）。Prompt 是缓存脆弱部分，受保护后基线差距大幅消失；推理 trace 通过文本级（模型反复重述所需信息）和跨头级（每头保留独立副本）双重冗余自我保护，均匀随机抽取已足以保留足够副本。

Conclusion: KV Cache 驱逐的准确性由其保护什么决定，而非如何排序其余内容。Random Attention 是无需校准、无需调参、无需打分的实用默认方法，也是任何新选择信号必须超越的基线。未来研究应聚焦长 prompt 预算分配和罕见一次性事实的恢复，而非继续优化评分函数。

Abstract: Large language models achieve superior performance on tasks that require extended reasoning, but long chains of thought make the KV cache a severe memory bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods share one paradigm: score each cached token by some estimate of how much it will matter later, and keep the top-scoring ones. We show that the selection signal contributes almost nothing. Random Attention keeps the prompt and evicts uniformly at random within each attention head, computing no score at all; across four models and six reasoning tasks it matches the strongest prior evictor while serving 32-43% higher throughput than it in vLLM deployment. Controlled experiments explain this by showing that 1) the prompt is the fragile part of the cache, and most of the gap between selectors is just whether their selection signal happened to keep it; 2) the reasoning trace protects itself against eviction with redundancy at two levels, in the text (the model restates what it still needs as it works) and across attention heads (each keeps its own copy of the trace), so once the prompt is safe, a random draw retains enough copies of what the model still needs, and no score is required to pick them. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [60] [Decoupled Analysis-Judging: An Automated Creativity Evaluator Using LLMs in Complex Multi-step Creativity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03432)
*Xiangyu Wang,Jin Wu,Xiaoyu Li,Chanjin Zheng,Yifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CreaEval框架，将LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为记忆增强分析（SoT-LLM提取结构化证据）和证据驱动评分（Judge-LLM基于证据独立评分）两阶段，在CGPST复杂多步创造力评估任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-Judge在评估复杂多步创造力任务（CGPST）时存在显著偏差（如冗长偏差和宽松偏差），且因步骤间依赖、高主观性和宽泛评分范围导致评分不稳定。现有方法（任务特定训练或直接LLM评判）均难以保证可靠评估，亟需更有效的自动化创造力评估框架。

Method: 1. 阶段一（记忆增强分析）：SoT-LLM以Structure-of-Thought（SoT）形式，将每步原始回答按维度逐步提取为结构化评估证据，并引入跨步骤记忆机制（Summary函数）维持上下文依赖。2. 阶段二（证据驱动评分）：Judge-LLM不接触原始回答，仅基于结构化证据和预设评分标准进行评分，约束评分空间以减少偏差。

Result: 在CGPST基准上，CreaEval平均QWK达0.64，较次优基线（SFT）提升0.17；在AUT和TTCW简单创造力任务上同样取得最佳表现。在偏差缓解方面，显著降低宽松偏差和冗长偏差，并提升跨Judge-LLM的评分稳定性。消融实验证实记忆机制、证据提取、解耦设计和逐步处理四个组件均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 解耦的分析-评分设计能有效提升LLM对复杂主观创造力任务的评估可靠性，通过结构化证据约束评分空间来缓解偏差、增强稳定性，为创造力任务自动化评估提供了新思路，且无需任务特定训练即可达到SFT相当甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of creativity tasks remains challenging for LLM-as-a-Judge, as LLM is susceptible to biases such as verbosity bias and leniency bias. Such limitations are particularly evident in Contextually-Grounded and Procedurally-Structured Tasks (CGPST), a complex multi-step creativity task where inter-step dependencies, highly subjectivity, and wide scoring ranges lead to more unstable and biased judgments. Existing approaches either rely on task-specific training or directly apply LLM-as-a-Judge, both of which struggle to ensure reliable evaluation under such complexity. To bridge these gaps, we propose CreaEval, an automated creativity evaluator for CGPST that decouples typical LLM-as-a-Judge into analysis and judging. Correspondingly, CreaEval involves two critical phases: Memory-augmented Analysis, a SoT-LLM converts multi-step responses into structured evaluation evidence, incorporating cross-step memory; and Evidence-based Judging, a Judge-LLM uses the extracted evidence for judging without accessing raw responses. Comprehensive experiments show that CreaEval achieves an average performance improvement of 22.74% over the second-best baselines across CGPST and two classic simple creativity tasks, demonstrating its generalizability. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [61] [When Retrieval Helps: Selective Retrieval for Single-Turn Mental-Health QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03454)
*Hyunseo Oh,Chong-Kwon Kim,Yoonhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究在心理健康问答中检索增强生成（RAG）并非总是有益的，提出了一种轻量级选择性检索策略，通过心理教育需求、应对需求和回答具体性三个效用维度加上基于规则的安全触发器来控制检索激活，从而在保持闭卷生成优势的同时避免无条件检索带来的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问答是一个安全敏感的场景，用户查询往往混合情绪困扰、治疗关切和安全需求。虽然检索增强生成（RAG）可以提高答案的具体性和可靠性，但在该领域中并非统一有益。现有自适应RAG方法主要关注查询复杂度、事实不确定性或模型置信度，未能充分捕捉心理健康支持的具体需求——一个简短查询仍可能需要安全接地、应对指导或心理教育。因此，需要将检索激活视为领域特定的控制问题。

Method: 论文提出一个紧凑的选择性检索框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 使用QLoRA对Gemma-4-E4B-it在MentalChat16K上进行微调；(2) 构建包含应对策略、心理教育和安全资源三个源族的小型指南语料库（40个文档），使用BM25检索（top-k=3）；(3) 推理时采用混合决策门控机制，结合基于规则的硬安全触发器（覆盖自我伤害、自杀、伤害他人、虐待等安全敏感查询）和三个LLM评分的软效用信号（u_info、u_cope、u_spec），使用均值阈值τ=3.25和路由阈值γ=4控制检索激活，并支持源族路由。比较了闭卷、无条件检索和选择性检索三种设置。

Result: 在CounselBench-Eval和CounselBench-Adv上的实验表明：(1) 检索并非统一有益，虽然Always Retrieval改善了具体性（3.92→3.97），但降低了整体质量（4.15→4.12），增加了医疗建议率（0.00→0.01）并引入了额外的安全敏感失败（宏失败率从0.025升至0.0917）；(2) 选择性检索在仅激活9.0%（Eval）和7.5%（Adv）检索的情况下，实现了最佳质量-安全权衡，整体质量（4.17）、共情（4.83）和零安全失败；(3) 专家审计显示选择性检索在安全敏感例子中更受偏好且边界问题更少；(4) 阈值校准表明保守阈值（τ=3.25, γ=4）是必要的，较低阈值会增加检索激活但不改善权衡甚至增加假设失败（Adv上宏失败率从0.025升至0.0417）。

Conclusion: 论文支持将检索激活视为心理健康问答中的安全敏感控制决策。保守的证据使用可以限制无条件检索带来的退化。主要贡献包括：(1) 将检索激活建模为基于信息需求、应对支持、回答具体性和安全风险的领域特定控制问题；(2) 在相同域适配生成器下进行闭卷、无条件检索和选择性检索的受控对比，隔离检索政策效应；(3) 通过标准评估、对抗性压力测试、阈值分析和专家审计表明选择性检索可避免无条件检索的额外失败。未来工作将扩展到多个生成器系列、独立构建的数据集和更大规模的证据集合，以及学习检索门控和密集/混合检索。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve the specificity and grounding of large language model responses, but its effect is not uniformly beneficial in single-turn mental-health question answering, where user queries often combine emotional distress, treatment concerns, and safety-sensitive needs. We study when retrieval helps or hurts mental-health QA, and whether a lightweight selective retrieval policy can better control this trade-off. We operationalize retrieval need using three draft-conditioned utility dimensions: psychoeducational need, coping need, and response specificity, together with a rule-based safety trigger. Following psychotherapy-grounded RAG systems such as coTherapist, we construct a compact and controllable guideline corpus comprising coping-strategy, psychoeducational, and safety resources. We fine-tune an instruction-tuned generator on MentalChat16K using QLoRA and compare Closed-book, Always Retrieval, and Selective Retrieval settings on CounselBench-Eval and CounselBench-Adv. Experiments show that retrieval is not uniformly beneficial in this domain. Always Retrieval improves specificity but lowers overall quality and introduces additional safety-sensitive failures. Selective Retrieval preserves closed-book behavior for low-need cases while avoiding the additional degradation caused by unconditional retrieval, supporting the view that retrieval activation is a safety-sensitive control decision.

</details>


### [62] [Lost in Reordering: Structural Sensitivity of Multilingual LLMs under Semantics-Preserving Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03511)
*Karthika Nhayakkat,Rajat Verma,Maharaj Brahma,Vetcha Gnana Mahesh,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar,Ganesh Ramakrishnan,Rohit Saluja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建 IndicReStruct 基准，评估多语言大模型在印地语和马拉雅拉姆语中对语义保持结构变化的鲁棒性。六个主流 LLM 在成分重排和主动—被动转换后，数学推理能力均显著下降。机制分析表明，错误常与实体—数量对齐被破坏有关，中间 Transformer 层对恢复正确推理尤为关键，说明当前模型尚未充分实现组合不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言 LLM 在标准评测上表现良好，但它们是否真正理解语义，而非依赖特定表层句法结构，仍不明确。既有鲁棒性研究对英语及较固定语序语言关注较多，而对印地语、马拉雅拉姆语等相对自由语序语言中语义等价的结构变化关注不足。论文据此研究结构扰动对数学推理的影响。

Method: 作者从 GSM8K 构建 IndicReStruct 基准，并提供两个变体：GSM8K-Reordered 对受约束的句法成分进行重排，GSM8K-Voice 将主动语态转换为被动语态，同时保持数学语义不变。论文在印地语和马拉雅拉姆语上测试六个先进 LLM，并采用多种提示策略。通过定性错误分析和残差流激活修补，定位与推理恢复相关的内部机制及 Transformer 层。

Result: 所有被测模型在两种结构扰动下都出现一致且显著的数学推理性能下降，说明性能下降并非特定模型或提示方式所致。错误分析显示，失败经常源于实体与数量之间的对应关系被错误对齐。激活修补实验进一步表明，中间层对恢复正确推理的贡献最强，表明表面句法变化会干扰深层组合推理过程。

Conclusion: 当前多语言 LLM 对句法实现方式高度敏感，不能稳定地将语义等价的结构变化映射到一致答案。其推理在实体—数量对齐及跨结构组合泛化方面仍缺乏稳健性。研究结果强调，未来评测不应只覆盖语言或措辞变化，还应系统纳入语义保持的句法结构变化，以衡量真正的组合不变性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong multilingual reasoning performance, yet their robustness to semantics-preserving structural variation remains underexplored, particularly for relatively free word-order languages. We investigate the structural sensitivity of multilingual LLMs using two linguistically grounded perturbation settings in Hindi and Malayalam: constrained constituent reordering and active-passive voice transformation. We introduce a benchmark dataset IndicReStruct, with two variants, GSM8K-Reordered and GSM8K-Voice, constructed from GSM8K while preserving semantic meaning. Across six state-of-the-art LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, we observe consistent and significant degradation in mathematical reasoning performance under structurally perturbed inputs. To further understand these failures, we perform qualitative error analysis and mechanistic interpretability experiments using residual-stream activation patching. Our analyses show that reasoning failures frequently arise from disruptions in entity-quantity alignment and that intermediate transformer layers contribute most strongly toward reasoning restoration. Overall, our findings suggest that current multilingual LLMs remain highly sensitive to surface syntactic realization and lack robust compositional invariance under structurally different but semantically equivalent inputs.

</details>


### [63] [Language, Language Models, and What We're Talking About](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03577)
*Malvina Nissim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，以意大利语大语言模型为案例，反思当前NLP领域中"语言"概念的缺失。作者指出，现有的所谓"意大利语模型"多基于以英文为主的预训练模型，通过机器翻译或合成数据进行指令微调，并在同样非原生的基准上测试；由此追问这些模型是否真正建模了"意大利语"乃至"语言"，并呼吁区分作为技术产品的语言模型与作为语言研究对象的语言模型这两条不同的发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP/LLM研究在训练数据（以英文为主、合成的、翻译的）、指令微调（机器翻译的Alpaca等）以及评估基准（自动翻译的MMLU等）中，逐渐远离了对自然语言本身的关注。"语言模型"中的"语言"究竟指什么？我们究竟希望模型产出什么样的语言？这些问题在领域内被普遍忽视却至关重要。

Method: 这是一篇概念性/反思性的立场论文。作者以意大利语LLM为案例进行论证：先梳理2020—2024年意大利生成式模型谱系（GePpeTto、IT5、Camoscio、Fauno、Anita、Steered-ITA、Minerva），然后逐一拆解其预训练阶段（Stage 1）、指令微调阶段（Stage 2）、对齐阶段（Stage 3）所接触的"语言世界"，并以Camoscio数据集和MMLU-IT基准为例揭示机器翻译和合成数据带来的问题；最后从语言学史（索绪尔—乔姆斯基—usage-based）的角度切入，讨论当前流水线产出"无人之语言"的现象。

Result: 作者揭示了几个关键事实：LLaMA-2预训练中意大利语仅占0.11%；Camoscio等指令数据来自GPT-3.5对Alpaca的自动翻译，存在文化错配、文字游戏失效、语法标注误译等问题；评估基准MMLU-IT等大多为英文基准的自动翻译；模型流水线（合成数据+对齐）倾向于产出同质化、风格扁平、去除个人风格的"nobody's language"。同时，作者也指出了令人欣慰的方向，如MultiNRC、Calamita等原生基准的建设，以及个性化/多元对齐研究的兴起。

Conclusion: 作者认为当前的NLP混淆了两种根本不同的目标——把语言作为研究对象 vs. 把语言技术作为下游应用工具——并用同一套范式同时追求两者，造成矛盾（如既强调语言多样性又将其标准化）。作者建议至少在概念上明确区分两条路径：(1) 应用导向：坦然接受并明确产出人工化、非自然的语言，将其作为辅助工具；(2) 语言研究导向：在生态学保真的数据上训练较小、较少干预的模型，服务于对语言和文化的真正理解。最后，作者以"高级定制与批量生产"为隐喻，预测自然语言的生产与欣赏将逐渐成为稀缺且有意为之的活动。

Abstract: Language models are commonly discussed as technical artefacts, but they are obviously shaped by the linguistic worlds conveyed by data during their training. Using Italian language models as evidence, I want to bring attention to the nature of the systems which result from training and specialising models on translated and synthetic data, and further curating them, and to the meaning of testing them on equally unnatural data. Are these eventually models of Italian? Are they models of language? Does NLP still care about language? These questions yield another, more concrete question: what language do we actually want language models to produce? I argue that this question cannot be answered if we do not first consider a clearer distinction between language models designed as technical products and language models designed as tools for studying language itself. The answers then might be diverse, the languages we are talking about might be diverse, and the picture might not be as pessimistic as we fear.

</details>


### [64] [KhatianDoc: A Human-Verified Benchmark Diagnosing Multimodal LLM Failure on Bengali Legal Land Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03597)
*Tasmiad Hasan,Arafat Zaman Ratul,Sarker Sadman Saalim,S.M. Shah Nawaz Hossain,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KhatianDoc 是首个针对孟加拉国 RS Khatian 土地所有权记录的基准测试，覆盖 Ana-Ganda 十六进制分数系统。评估了六个多模态大模型，发现其在复杂法律推理上完全失败（39.3%的问题全部模型得零分），且在十六进制算术任务上表现劣于常数均值基线。论文同时对评测指标本身进行了自审计，修正了评分缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国土地所有权记录（RS Khatians）使用独特的十六进制 Ana-Ganda 分数系统，无字体支持、无 OCR 覆盖、无分词器词汇，却是数百万地块的法定权属凭证。现有 NLP/OCR 基础设施完全无法处理此类记录，法律 NLP 社区也未曾评估机器能否读取这些文档。本研究旨在量化这一能力鸿沟。

Method: 构建包含 107 份真实 RS Khatian 记录的四任务基准：符号识别（T1）、base-16 转十进制（T2）、结构化字段抽取（T3）、法律文档问答（T4，1634 对）。所有标注由人工完成并经土地法律师二次校验达成完全一致；采用位置令牌匿名化以保留多跳问答所需指代区别。统一通过 OpenRouter 接口在零样本协议下评估六个多模态 LLM（8B 至 72B+），并对评分指标进行自审计。

Result: 1) T4 中五个类别（共 39.3% 问题）所有六个模型全部得零分；2) T2 中所有输出数值的模型 MAE 均高于常数均值基线（0.237），且精确匹配与近似匹配分数重合，表明输出与输入解耦而非近似；3) 各模型呈现家族特异的符号替代模式（如 Gemini 趋向卢比符号、GPT-4o Mini 替换为婆罗米字符或西方阿拉伯数字）；4) 自审计修正了拒绝回答评分错误（使 Gemini EM 从 13.33% 升至 21.67%），并将受常数与空值膨胀的元数据 EM 标记为上界而非可靠指标。

Conclusion: 研究记录的不是性能差距，而是能力的缺失——当前多模态 LLM 在该领域不具备所需能力。贡献包括：首个可机检、经过法律验证的 Ana-Ganda 编码；六模型失败诊断；以及可复用的方法论（指标自审计、位置令牌匿名化、加密图像发布策略）。公开数据集与代码已发布（敏感字段采用遮蔽图像），但对误用部署发出警告。

Abstract: Land ownership in Bangladesh is recorded in Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til, a base-16 positional fraction system with dedicated Unicode glyphs, no mainstream font, and no coverage in any OCR pipeline or tokenizer. The handwritten records that carry these fractions, RS Khatians, are the authoritative title record for millions of parcels and a frequent subject of civil litigation, yet no benchmark has asked whether a machine can read one. We introduce KhatianDoc, a four-task benchmark built from 107 real RS Khatian records from the Vumi (land) Office of Munshiganj, Bangladesh: symbol recognition, base-16-to-decimal conversion, structured field extraction, and legal document question answering over 1,634 QA pairs. Ground truth was transcribed by hand, verified by a land-law practitioner to full agreement, and anonymized through positional tokens that keep the referential distinctions multi-hop questions depend on. We evaluate six multimodal LLMs (8B to 72B+, open and closed) under a fixed zero-shot protocol. Five QA categories, 39.3% of our stratified set, return zero correct answers from every model; on the arithmetic task, every model that emits a number does worse than a constant-mean baseline, with exact- and near-match scores coinciding: decorrelation, not approximation. Auditing our own metrics surfaced two artifacts in opposite directions: we correct a refusal-scoring bug and report the fixed scores beside the originals, and flag an inflated metadata metric as an upper bound. KhatianDoc documents not a performance gap but the absence of a capability, with verified ground truth for future systems. Code and data, with a redacted image release, are publicly available.

</details>


### [65] [The Impact of Synthetic Data Augmentation on Discourse-Pragmatic Function Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03652)
*Sara Sorahi,Kevin Tang,Reza Kazemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据增强在话语语用功能分类任务中,合成样本在嵌入空间中的几何位置(而非数量与多样性)对分类性能的影响。以英语词 look 的四种话语语用功能(注意信号、指令语、话语标记、感叹词)分类为任务,发现靠近真实数据核心的合成样本对 macro-F 提升最大,而距离均衡的混合策略获得最高准确率;所有增强策略均未提升 AUC,说明增强仅平移决策边界而非改善概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据增强研究主要关注生成样本的数量与多样性,但忽略了合成样本在嵌入空间中相对于真实数据的几何位置的影响。在话语语用功能分类这类数据稀疏是结构性特征的任务中,合成样本落点的几何关系可能比数量更关键。

Method: 使用 BNC 语料库中 410 个手动标注的英语 look 实例,采用 Llama 3.1 8B 生成合成训练样本,在冻结的 RoBERTa-base 嵌入空间中按合成样本与同类真实样本的平均余弦距离将其划分为 Near、Middle、Far 三组。比较六种训练条件(Real Only、+Near、+Middle、+Far、+Random、+Balanced),使用多项 Logistic 回归分类器,通过五折分层划分评估 macro-F、准确率和 macro-AUC。

Result: 所有增强条件均提升 macro-F 和准确率。Near 条件 macro-F 提升最大(+0.113,p=0.0016,d=3.43,5/5 胜),Balanced 条件准确率最高(0.748)。AUC 在所有条件下均未显著改善,真实数据基线反而 AUC 最高(0.726)。功能层面 INTJ 受益最大(F1 从 0.14 升至 0.36),DM 稳定提升,DIR 改善较小,AS 因未获增强保持不变。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强的效果取决于样本在嵌入空间中相对于真实数据的位置,而非仅取决于数量。核心近邻样本对 macro-F 提升最显著,距离均衡混合策略准确率最高。增强通过平移决策边界起作用,未改善模型底层概率排序,这对低资源语用分类任务的增强策略设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation has become a common strategy for addressing class imbalance in NLP, but most approaches focus on the quantity and diversity of generated examples rather than their geometric relationship to real training data. We investigate this question in the context of discourse pragmatic function classification, a task where data sparsity is a structural feature rather than a collection artefact. Using 410 manually annotated instances of the English word look drawn from the British National Corpus, spanning four functions: Attention Signal, Directive, Discourse Marker, and Interjection. We generate synthetic training examples with Llama 3.1 and partition them by their cosine distance from real training data in RoBERTa embedding space. We compare six training conditions that differ in the placement of synthetic examples relative to the empirical decision boundary, while holding augmentation quantity constant across conditions. All augmented conditions improve macro F and accuracy over the real only baseline, but core proximal examples (NEAR) yield the largest gains in macro F (0.113), while a distance balanced mix achieves the highest accuracy (0.748). No condition improves AUC, indicating that augmentation shifts the decision boundary rather than improving the model's underlying probability estimates. These findings suggest that where synthetic examples land in representation space matters as much as how many are generated, with implications for low resource pragmatic classification more broadly.

</details>


### [66] [A Circuit for Plural Reference: How LLMs Represent and Retrieve Singular and Plural Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03687)
*Anh Danh,Rick Nouwen,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用机制可解释性方法，研究了大语言模型如何表示和检索单复数实体以进行复数指代消解，识别出一个由三类注意力头组成的"复数指代回路"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数指代消解需要同时追踪多个实体并将它们组合为复数实体，比单数指代更复杂。已有大量心理语言学研究探讨人类如何处理复数指代，但对LLM内部如何表示和检索复数实体的机制尚不清楚，亟需通过可解释性方法揭示其内在工作原理。

Method: 结合机制可解释性技术（激活补丁、路径补丁）与注意力模式分析。构建合成数据集D_pl（含适合复数指代的结构，如"Mary and her sister"）和D_sg（不适合复数指代的结构，如"Mary and her bike"），通过操控第二个实体或连词构造损坏提示。利用线性混合效应回归模型分析影响复数代词偏好的因素（性别、本体相似度、连词类型），然后对Qwen3-0.6B、Qwen3-1.7B和GPT2-medium进行逐层、逐注意力头的因果干预实验。

Result: 发现一个由三类注意力头组成的复数指代回路：(1) 代词选择头（如Qwen3-1.7B的L23H12和L23H13）位于最后一层，直接决定代词预测，注意力集中在候选先行词上；(2) 复数形成头（如L17H9）识别构成复数实体的成分，将整个复数结构纳入注意力范围；(3) 代词解释头（如L13H6）编码指代信息，使代词回溯关注其先行词。统计分析与因果干预一致地表明：本体相似度越高（如人名+人名）、连词为"and"时，模型越倾向于产生复数代词。该回路在Qwen3家族和GPT2-medium中均得到验证。

Conclusion: LLM在处理复数指代时遵循一个紧凑且可解释的回路：复数形成头根据本体相似度和连词类型判断是否构成合格的复数实体，将信息写入残差流后传递给代词选择头，后者据此选择先行词并预测代词。LLM对复数代词的偏好与人类心理语言学实验结果高度一致。研究的局限性在于仅关注最基本的两元素并列名词短语复数结构，未涉及分裂先行词等更复杂情形，且对MLP模块在回路中的作用探讨不足。

Abstract: Coreference resolution is an important task in contextual reasoning. In this paper, we investigate the mechanism for representing and retrieving singular and plural entities for plural reference. We use a combination of mechanistic interpretability and attention pattern analysis to study the process in which LLMs predict a pronoun to refer back to previously mentioned entities. Using a range of causal intervention techniques, we find a set of attention heads that are responsible for (1) representing coreference information in the input, (2) identifying entities that form a plural reference, (3) transferring the information to the component that is responsible for selecting the antecedents and predicting the pronoun. We also find that LLMs align with humans in preference for plural pronoun. Specifically, entities in a plural construction are more likely to be referred to as a plural entity if they are ontologically similar and are linked by the conjunction "and".

</details>


### [67] [Opening mind by opening architecture: analysis strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03719)
*Francesco Vitucci,Giuseppe Silvi,Daniele Giuseppe Annese,Francesco Scagliola,Anthony Di Furia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在电声作曲数字信号处理中，从"黑箱"封闭式架构音频处理器转向"白箱"开放式架构的重要性。以Manfred Schroeder的历史混响器为案例研究，作者展示了如何使用Faust和Csound等文本编程环境构建分析评估工具，并通过脉冲响应（IR）测试与组合结构（par/seq）的实现，建立一种基于科学方法与历史文献的意识研究路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字信号处理工具的民主化与商品化导致"黑箱"封闭式架构音频处理器模型的盛行，用户成为仅"消费"预设服务的"音乐产消者"，丧失了对其内部机理的理解。研究动机在于：面对日益缺失的专业研发环境与批判意识，重建一种科学的研究态度与开放架构的教学范式。

Method: 1. 选择从历史文献出发，以Schroeder 1962年的数字混响算法为案例（由梳状滤波器与全通滤波器构成）。2. 采用Faust（函数式音频流语言）作为初始文本编程环境，因其支持从高级函数到底层原语的逐层透明观察。3. 将项目移植到Csound环境，构建包含两个乐器的脉冲响应分析流程：instr 1生成Dirac脉冲并写入全局表，instr 2通过ftprint/ftsave输出并由Wolfram脚本绘图。4. 在Csound中自定义实现PAR_FDL_SCH（并行结构）与SEQ_APF_SCH（串行结构）两个用户自定义算子（UDO），复现Faust中的par与seq组合原语。

Result: 1. 成功构建了可在Csound中运行的FDL_SCH与APF_SCH基础混响单元UDO。2. 实现了从脉冲生成、信号处理到波形绘制的完整IR分析工具链，获得Schroeder FDL组件的脉冲响应可视化结果。3. 通过自建UDO在Csound中复现了Faust的并行/串行组合结构，使得Schroeder混响器（4个并联梳状滤波器后接2个串联全通滤波器）的整体架构可在Csound环境中重新搭建。4. 验证了该方法在教学与意识性研究路径中的可行性，但也指出Csound在效率上较Faust存在局限，提出下一步将以C语言编写编译型opcode作为发展方向。

Conclusion: 开放架构不仅意味着开放代码，更意味着开放理解与学习的路径。通过历史文献的"镜子"，研究者能够重建科学方法与批判意识；将Faust项目移植到Csound并开发分析工具的过程，证明了跨语言整合的优势。论文强调其强烈的教学属性：开放架构让学生从单纯"消费"环境转变为"生产"环境，从而形成音乐、创意与专业成长的良性循环。未来的研究方向包括开发更通用的Csound组合算子以及编写C语言编译型opcode以突破性能瓶颈。

Abstract: In numerical signal processing for electroacoustic composition, the progressive loss of specific development and research environments caused by the increasing use of digital market tools has favoured the dominance of the closed-architecture audio processor model. This model, while powerful, envisions the possibility of describing output data about its perceived characteristics, but at the cost of ignoring its internal process and interacting systems, which become complex, powerful environments but closed in an inscrutable black box, a loss we must consider. Any digital signal processing technique tells a story. Just as the words of a language incorporate social, historical and technical polysemic layers, a signal processor has its own story of implementation, a gradual technological achievement with its inevitable aesthetic consequences. Through the looking-glass of literature, one can access those environments with renewed awareness by reestablishing a scientific method and an attitude to research. In this specific case, starting from the case study of Manfred Schroeder's historical reverbs, we illustrate the process of building analytical evaluation tools, as well as practical implementation, at the basis of a conscious study path.

</details>


### [68] [Typological Feature Prediction with Large Language Models: An In-Context Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03775)
*Qianwen Wang,York Hay Ng,Aditya Khan,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于上下文学习（ICL）的方法，利用大语言模型（LLMs）结合URIEL+和Glottolog的语言学证据（系统发生邻居、地理邻居和锚定特征）来预测缺失的类型学特征。实验证明该方法显著优于传统基线，且在低资源语言上表现一致，并能提供与证据一致的可解释性推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类型学特征预测方法（如kNN、矩阵补全、随机森林）存在两大不足：(1) 缺乏对预测结果的可解释性；(2) 在不同资源水平语言和不同特征类型上的性能差异未被充分探究。鉴于LLMs具备元语言推理能力并能输出推理依据，作者探索利用LLMs进行类型学特征预测的可行性与优越性。

Method: 将任务形式化为对URIEL+矩阵的二元插值任务，测试集覆盖1200个平衡采样的(语言, 特征)对。提示模板由四个证据模块构成：目标语言元数据、与目标特征最相关的5个锚定特征值及其全局先验、从Glottolog族谱中贪心选择的5个系统发生邻居及其投票统计与对比证据、按Haversine距离选取的5个地理邻居及其投票统计与对比证据。评估了三个LLM（Llama-3.1-70B、Gemma-4-31B、GPT-5.5）在不同输入组合下的Macro F1，并以kNN-cosine、SoftImpute、kNN-phylogenetic、kNN-geographic和Random Forest作为基线。通过5名标注者对180个子样本进行人工标注，评估提示充分性、推理依据一致性及置信度校准。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 仅元数据的零样本提示不足以完成预测（Llama F1=0.465，Gemma=0.521，GPT-5.5=0.661），低于SoftImpute基线（0.821）。(2) 加入邻居证据后，LLMs显著超越所有基线：Llama全输入配置达F1=0.909，Gemma锚定+地理配置达0.893，GPT-5.5锚定+地理配置达0.900。(3) 跨资源水平与特征类型的一致性显著改善：Llama LRL上F1=0.919（vs HRL 0.914），GPT-5.5高低资源差距从0.071缩小至0.012。(4) 在多源证据冲突的困难子集上，LLM仍达F1=0.893，远超简单多数投票（约0.46–0.54）。(5) 人工标注显示提示设计充分（Fleiss κ=0.888，人工F1=0.987），且Llama的多数推理依据与证据一致（仅20.9%被标注者认为存在矛盾）。置信度校准良好：高置信度时Llama准确率达100%。

Conclusion: 在提供充分的语言学证据（系统发生邻居、地理邻居、锚定特征）时，LLMs能够有效整合多源证据进行类型学特征预测，性能显著优于传统矩阵补全和随机森林方法，且不会损害低资源语言的性能。同时，LLM生成的推理依据多数与提供的证据一致，为可解释的类型学特征预测迈出了关键一步。但需注意：(1) 推理依据是合理的解释而非真实内部推理过程的反映；(2) 评估仅覆盖1200个已观察条目，对真正缺失的87%条目的推广性存疑；(3) 方法依赖知识库中的邻居证据可用性，无法替代田野语言学记录。

Abstract: Typological features are widely used in multilingual NLP, and the prediction of such features holds downstream utility. However, existing methods to predict missing values lack interpretable justifications for predictions, while their performance across resource levels and feature types remains underexplored. Given LLMs' abilities in meta-linguistic reasoning and in providing rationales, we investigate LLMs' performance in typological feature prediction via an in-context learning approach with linguistic data from URIEL+ and Glottolog. We find that zero-shot prompting is insufficient, but when given phylogenetic and geographic neighbour evidence, LLMs substantially outperform all baselines without disadvantaging low-resource languages. We further find that most LLM rationales are consistent with the provided evidence, offering a step toward explainable typological feature prediction.

</details>


### [69] [Evaluating Criterion-Conditioned Behaviour of Large Language Models in Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03814)
*Danting Zhang,Bei Peng,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DECO框架与成对评估方法，系统揭示了大型语言模型在内容审核中虽在聚合基准上表现强劲，但无法可靠地将决策条件化于具体的审核标准（尤其是意图vs可操作性），并呼吁建立显式衡量标准条件化行为的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核基准往往将多个审核标准（表面表达、意图、可操作性等）合并为单一聚合标签，掩盖了模型在各个独立标准上的真实表现，无法判断模型是否能可靠地"按标准行事"而非仅依赖记忆式启发或默认安全倾向。

Method: 1）提出DECO——一种与标准无关的内容因子化表征（6个可解释标量特征：敏感词、伤害主题、肯定倾向、可执行步骤、细节生动度、作者距离/中立性），通过LLM标注器对每条内容独立打分；2）用基于预定义启发式函数将这些分数映射为三条独立标准的二值标签：外观标准（AC）、意图标准（IC）、示范标准（DC）；3）提出成对评估指标——同标签对准确率（SPA）和不同标签对准确率（DPA），直接检验模型是否真正按指定标准条件化输出；4）在四个审核数据集（Civil Comments、X-Sensitive、OpenAI Moderation、Toxic-Chat）和四个LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-70B、GPT-5.2、Gemini-2.5-Pro）上评估，并进行阈值敏感性分析与五人盲标人工验证。

Result: 在聚合基准上模型表现强劲（准确率0.64–0.94），但分解到独立标准后表现不均衡：AC上普遍高召回但假阳性多（过度反应于表面敏感词），IC和DC召回显著下降，假阴性增加；最严重失败出现在IC-DC不同标签对上，部分模型的DPA降至0.04–0.14，揭示模型常将"有害意图"与"可操作指导"坍缩为同一判断；阈值敏感性分析与人类标注验证（多数投票human–DECO一致率82%/84%/98% for AC/IC/DC）证实结论稳健；表明应从"标准即提示"转向"标准即操作"，并开发显式衡量标准条件化行为的评估方法。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：聚合基准上的高分无法证明LLM能够可靠地按个体审核标准行事；单标准评估与跨标准成对评估共同揭示了模型在条件化于特定标准（尤其是IC-DC区分）上的根本缺陷；面向未来的内容审核系统应被评估与训练为不仅能复现标准隐含的标签，更能识别给定标准所需的证据并应用对应的决策逻辑。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard content moderation benchmarks. However, these benchmarks often aggregate multiple moderation criteria into a single label, making it unclear whether models can disentangle them and reliably apply each criterion when making decisions. To study whether LLMs exhibit criterion-conditioned behaviour, we introduce Diagnostic Evaluation of COntent (DECO), a criterion-independent factorisation of content that enables controlled, criterion-level evaluation. We also introduce pairwise evaluation to compare model outputs across different criteria for the same input. Across four moderation datasets and four LLMs, we find that strong benchmark performance can hide substantial failures at the criterion level. Models struggle most when correct decisions depend not on overall harmfulness, but on the specific aspect of the content that the criterion requires them to assess. Our results highlight a key limitation of current content moderation benchmarks: strong performance on aggregated labels does not provide sufficient evidence that LLMs can reliably evaluate content with respect to individual moderation criteria. These findings call for the development of evaluation methods that explicitly measure criterion-conditioned behaviour.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Shallow Alignment: How Post-Training Methods Determine Refusal Circuits And Steering Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03887)
*Hoang Cuong Nguyen,Mark Dras,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过机制可解释性方法，系统比较了三种后训练方法（SFT、Ra-SFT、ORPO）在三种不同架构模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B、Qwen3-8B）上如何重塑内部拒绝机制，发现训练目标而非数据决定了拒绝电路结构，并提出"安全对齐三难困境"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流观点将后训练安全对齐视为可靠的二元属性，但实际安全事件（如GTG-1002、墨西哥政府机构攻击）表明行为层面的对齐评估不足以保证安全。以往机制可解释性研究仅在固定模型上刻画拒绝电路，缺乏对训练目标如何系统性地重塑电路结构的跨范式分析。理解训练方法与内部拒绝机制之间的因果关系，对安全关键场景至关重要。

Method: 采用"偏好-推理"二维范式，对SFT、Ra-SFT（带推理链的SFT）、ORPO三种方法在三个架构不同的8B级模型上进行控制变量对比（固定基模型、训练数据、超参数）。方法包括：(1) Difference-in-Means (DIM) 提取并归一化拒绝方向，分析跨层几何特性与余弦相似度；(2) Activation Patching 与 Attribution Patching 进行层/组件（MLP、注意力头）级因果归因；(3) ActAdd 与 ITI 进行推理时激活引导实验；(4) 在 WildJailbreak、StrongREJECT、XSTest、MMLU 上评估行为安全与效用。

Result: 核心发现：(1) 训练目标独立于数据和架构重塑拒绝计算——Ra-SFT在三种架构上均产生MLP主导、分布式的不均匀电路，而SFT在Llama中注意力头集中、在Gemma中均匀冗余、在Qwen中MLP主导；ORPO在Gemma中形成过度约束的均匀电路导致31.6%过度拒答。(2) Ra-SFT的拒绝方向幅度在层间渐进分布而非中段尖锐峰值，是推理监督轴的跨架构签名。(3) 识别层引导比执行层更有效（如Gemma Ra-SFT在α=20时ASR降低18.8pp vs 5.8pp），但Llama的ActAdd迅速崩溃MMLU准确性。(4) 没有任何方法同时满足：分布式拒绝编码、安全/效用可分离性、细粒度可纠正性——构成"对齐三难困境"。(5) 攻击类型脆弱性与电路结构一致：注意力头集中型易受语义攻击，MLP集中型（如Qwen）易受编码攻击。

Conclusion: 后训练方法不应被视为已解决的可靠防御，尤其在安全关键场景。行为层面的对齐评估无法预测由电路结构决定的脆弱性图谱。未来的安全工作需要同时考量分布式编码、可分离性与可纠正性，并研究偏好与推理信号在训练中的交互作用。

Abstract: How do the methods used to train language models to refuse harmful requests shape how that refusal actually works inside the model? We compare three post-training methods - supervised fine-tuning, reasoning-augmented fine-tuning (training on reasoning chains that justify a safety decision), and preference optimization (ORPO) - across three architecturally distinct models (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B, Qwen3-8B). We find that training method, not just data, reshapes how refusal is computed internally: reasoning-augmented training consistently produces a distinct kind of refusal computation, visible across all three models, while architecture independently shapes internal structure and how reliably refusal can be steered. Most importantly, no method we study achieves all three properties we would want from safe alignment at once: refusal that isn't concentrated in a few fragile components, safety gains that don't cost general capability, and safety behavior correctable through small, targeted edits. We caution against treating current post-training methods as a solved, reliable defense, especially for security-critical use. Code and models are available in this https URL .

</details>


### [71] [RuleMem: Active Rule Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03915)
*Xingyuan Zeng,Zuohan Wu,Quanming Yao,Yue Wang,Wei Liu,Libin Zheng,Jiuke Wang,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RuleMem提出了一种基于规则记忆的框架，通过从对话历史中归纳自然语言Horn子句形式的可重用逻辑规则，并利用Rule Perplexity Consistency (RPC)机制进行验证，从而在长对话问答中主动指导证据检索与逻辑推理。在LoCoMo基准上显著优于14个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长对话QA代理的记忆系统存在两个核心挑战：1) 跨语义鸿沟的证据检索困难（即相关证据可能与查询无表面词汇重叠）；2) 基于检索证据的推理不可靠（在长上下文中容易出现逻辑链断裂或幻觉）。现有方法要么仅进行事实记忆（依赖表面相似度），要么进行事实组织（如知识图谱、Zettelkasten网络），但仍局限于实例层面，无法提供抽象的逻辑指导。

Method: RuleMem采用"自底向上归纳→RPC验证→自顶向下演绎"的闭环流程：
1. **自底向上规则构建**：从对话中提取时间四元组构成事实记忆库ℳ_F；通过图上的随机游走采样推理路径；按关系模式分组并由LLM归纳为自然语言Horn子句形式（B₁∧…∧Bₙ⟹H），其中实体被替换为类型占位符（如[Person]、[Event]）。
2. **RPC规则验证**：通过自回归模型的困惑度下降衡量规则质量，包括内部一致性信号Δ_self（规则前提是否降低结论的困惑度）和外部事实一致性信号Δ_fact（加入相关证据后是否进一步降低困惑度），两者加权得RPC分数，过滤低质量规则。
4. **自顶向下QA**：给定问题，先通过余弦相似度激活TopN规则的结论；再以规则体作为检索线索从事实库中召回候选证据；LLM执行语义统一（类型绑定），过滤不匹配的证据；最后将规则（major premises）和证据（minor premises）组织到结构化提示中进行显式推理。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上，RuleMem的平均精度为78.5，BLEU为36.90，比14个基线（涵盖事实记忆、事实组织和RAG三类）表现更佳；在多跳推理（准确率82.43 vs 最佳基线79.79）和开放域任务上提升尤为明显。消融实验显示移除规则抽象与RPC均显著降低性能。引导式回忆将平均召回率从0.56提升至0.79（+41.1%），显式推理将推理失败从120.4降至105.9（-12.0%）。超参数τ=0.5、α=0.4为最佳配置。案例分析展示了规则如何引导跨语义鸿沟的证据召回，但也存在过度泛化的失败案例。

Conclusion: RuleMem验证了将代理记忆从被动存储转变为主动推理工具的可行性，证明了抽象规则层在长对话QA中能有效弥合语义鸿沟并支持可靠的多跳推理。规则抽象与困惑度一致性过滤共同保障了系统性能。但仍存在过度泛化风险，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Question answering agents in long-term conversations must reason over massive, temporally dispersed dialogue histories. However, existing memory mechanisms primarily treat past information as \textit{passively} stored facts, leading to semantic gaps and unreliable reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose RuleMem, a rule-based memory framework that induces reusable logical rules from historical interactions to \textit{actively} guide both evidence retrieval and reasoning. Specifically, RuleMem constructs natural-language Horn clauses from conversations and validates them via a Rule Perplexity Consistency (RPC) mechanism. These induced rules enable the retrieval of semantically distant evidence while providing an explicit logical structure for answer generation. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation of RuleMem on two long-term conversational benchmarks, LoCoMo and LongMemEval_s*. In a rigorous comparison against 14 baselines on LoCoMo, RuleMem achieved the highest accuracy, exceeding the baseline average by 27.47 points (a 54.3% relative improvement).

</details>


### [72] [Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio: Quantifying the Dual-Track Mechanism of Gender Assignment in Latvian Loanwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03930)
*Yelingyun Zhang,Atis Kapenieks,Marina Platonova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出固定后缀依赖率（FSDR）指标，量化拉脱维亚语中英语借词性别分配对固定派生后缀的依赖程度，揭示阳性借词更依赖自由选择、阴性借词更依赖固定后缀的不对称模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究观察到英语借词在多种语言中倾向于聚于阳性，但其成因难以确定——因为形态固定规则与默认分配常被混为一谈。需要一种定量方法来区分形态锚定与自由选择分布。

Method: 提出固定后缀依赖率（FSDR），衡量不同性别对固定派生后缀的依赖程度；对1,832个拉脱维亚语名词词条（lemma types）进行实证检验，分析借词系统中FSDR的不对称性及其历时变化。

Result: 发现借词系统存在显著的FSDR不对称：阴性借词显著更依赖固定派生后缀，阳性借词则更多集中在自由选择区域；该模式具有借词特异性（loanword specificity），且在当代使用中愈发明显。

Conclusion: FSDR为检验默认性别提供了定量框架，揭示了在语言接触条件下阳性默认（masculine default）如何被激活并得到强化。

Abstract: Existing research has repeatedly observed the tendency for English loanwords to cluster in the masculine gender across different recipient languages, yet the origin of this pattern remains difficult to determine, as fixed morphological rules and default assignments are frequently analysed together. This study proposes the Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio (FSDR) to quantify the degree of reliance on fixed derivational suffixes across different genders, and to distinguish between morphological anchoring and free-choice in distribution. By examining 1,832 Latvian noun lemma types, the results reveal a significant FSDR asymmetry within the loanword system: feminine loanwords rely significantly more on fixed derivational suffixes, while masculine loanwords are more concentrated in the free-choice zone. This pattern exhibits loanword specificity and has become more pronounced in contemporary usage. FSDR therefore provides a quantitative framework for testing default gender and shows how masculine default can be activated and reinforced under language contact.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond Majority Vote: Multi-Perspective Adjudication for Medical Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03953)
*Joe Cecil,Marjorie Freedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对医疗领域聊天机器人长文本回答中的事实性错误检测（幻觉），提出了一种多视角标注与裁决框架（Multi-Perspective Adjudication）。研究发现单轮单标注者标注存在严重的低召回问题，而LLM-as-a-Judge虽能补充候选错误但仍不充分，且不同裁决者之间存在分歧，表明即使多轮裁决也无法完全替代专业判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗聊天机器人的事实性错误（幻觉）检测基准普遍采用单轮、单标注者的标注方式，但长文本回答中错误往往细微且嵌入在大量正确信息中，导致标注者容易遗漏。构建可靠的幻觉检测基准对于评估聊天机器人安全性至关重要，但现有方法可能在追求规模的同时牺牲了标注完整性。

Method: 研究构建了MDHJudgments数据集，包含医疗相关聊天机器人回答（囊性纤维化、儿童传染病领域），采用三阶段标注流程：(1) 多标注者首次标注（FP），包括医疗专家、医学生、AI研究者；(2) LLM-as-a-Judge（LaJ）候选错误发现；(3) 双重裁决——医疗专家裁决与基于证据的事实核查裁决。结合统计指标（Krippendorff's α、xRR交叉复现可靠性）和TREC式pooled评估框架，对标注一致性、基准完整性和不同标注群体表现进行系统分析。此外，将该方法应用于MedExpert现有基准作为案例验证。

Result: 关键发现：(1) 首次标注者召回率极低（FP vs FP+ME参考仅约21%，FP vs FP+FC约20%），多数重大和轻微错误被遗漏；(2) LaJ召回率显著高于个体标注者（71-80%），但仍有错误遗漏，且单独使用时精确率仅2%；(3) 裁决者间一致性低（Krippendorff's α：医疗专家0.05、事实核查者0.15），存在显著分歧；(4) 完整反应级判断会掩盖段落级错误（两者相关性仅-0.39）；(5) 将该方法应用于MedExpert，78%被LaJ标记但原数据未标记的错误被裁决确认为真正的has_error；(6) 错误类别超出纯事实准确性范畴，还包括引用错误、缺失信息、清晰度等问题。

Conclusion: 单轮单标注者幻觉基准以规模换取完整性，往往严重低估事实错误率；多轮裁决和LaJ能显著提高候选覆盖率，但无法消除对专业判断的依赖；不同裁决者之间的分歧表明，事实准确性判断本质上需要综合判断、专业知识和证据支撑，不能简化为二元标注任务。该研究为构建和解读事实错误基准提供了重要启示，呼吁采用多视角标注策略。

Abstract: Understanding the frequency of factual errors in chatbot-generated text and evaluating systems that detect these errors is critical for determining chatbot safety. Yet factual-error detection is often treated as a single-pass, single-annotator labeling problem. In long-form chatbot responses, factual errors can be subtle and embedded within mostly correct text. We develop a multi-perspective annotation study of medically relevant chatbot responses, combining first-pass annotation, LLM-as-a-Judge (LaJ) candidate discovery, and two forms of adjudication: medical-expert and evidence-based fact-checking. First-pass annotators frequently miss factual errors later validated by adjudicators. LaJ improves candidate discovery, but is insufficient on its own: It misses factual errors that annotators catch. We also find disagreement among adjudicators, suggesting that adjudication over multiple candidate sources can improve benchmark completeness, but does not eliminate the need to apply judgment and expertise. Applied to an existing benchmark, this technique reveals a similar pattern of missing annotations. Together, these results suggest that in the settings examined here, single-pass hallucination benchmarks may achieve scale at the cost of undercounting factual errors. Multi-pass adjudication can improve coverage, but inferences drawn from the benchmarks are still sensitive to the judgment, expertise, and evidence used to determine error presence.

</details>


### [74] [Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox for Voice Dubbing and Full-Duplex Dialogue Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03992)
*Sanyuan Chen,Min-Jae Hwang,Sho Inoue,Anna Sun,Bokai Yu,David Kant,Dongmin Hyun,Dorian Desblancs,Gregory Antonovsky,Oleg Repin,Peng-Jen Chen,Xutai Ma,Zehai Tu,Juan Pino,Wei-Ning Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox（Text-AB）：一个基于流匹配的 3B 参数潜空间扩散框架，统一支持跨语言配音、立体声全双工对话及情感全双工对话。其核心是以 DAC-VAE 将 48 kHz 音频压缩为 25 Hz 潜变量、用跨注意力让模型直接从原始文本隐式学习对齐，并依靠多阶段重排序与可变长分块的多扩散生成提升最终质量。实验显示，Text-AB 显著优于内部配音与对话基线：短对话的人类相似度 MOS 达 4.53，接近真人 4.62；长对话为 3.86，远高于内部模型的 3.00。配音的可分享性相对基线提升约 0.38–0.40。其主要创新是“免强制对齐的统一语音生成”，主要代价是庞大的数据和算力、32–64 个候选带来的高推理成本，以及依赖内部基准和自动指标进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业配音通常采用 ASR、机器翻译和 TTS 级联系统，而全双工对话常把单轮独白拼接起来，导致跨语言音色迁移、对话轮次协调、插入语与情绪演化不自然。Text-AB 旨在构建一个端到端统一框架，直接以原始文本和音频提示生成高质量语音，并在无须强制对齐和显式时长预测的情况下改善配音、自然度、可控性及长时生成能力。

Method: Text-AB 基于 Diffusion Transformer 和 flow-matching 目标，在 DAC-VAE 潜空间中对音频进行扩散生成。mT5 编码原始文本，DiT 通过跨注意力隐式学习文本—语音对齐；帧级语言 ID 用于多语言控制。模型包含单通道 Text-AB-Mono 和双通道 Text-AB-Stereo，后者联合预测两位说话人的音频。训练采用三阶段方案：480k 小时英西单语语音预训练；约 2.1k 小时配音数据微调；28k 小时双通道全双工对话微调。情感版本进一步加入由自动识别器提取的逐轮 VAD（效价—唤醒度—支配度）嵌入。推理支持约一分钟单次生成，以及采用 30 秒分块、20 秒重叠、加权合并的 multi-diffusion 长时生成。候选结果先按 WavLM 说话人相似度筛选，再按 Whisper-Large-V3 的 WER 排序；情感对话还结合轮次对齐、情绪准确率及自然度。

Result: 配音评测中，Text-AB 相对内部系统的可分享性在 Es→En 与 En→Es 方向分别提高 +0.40 和 +0.38；声音自然度分别提高 +0.39 和 +0.45。模型从 300M 扩至 3B 后，WER 从 44.45% 降至 13.98%，SpkSim 从 0.64 升至 0.76，Aes 从 6.03 升至 6.29。32 个候选重排序将配音 WER 从 4.05% 降至 2.20%，SpkSim 从 0.66 升至 0.74。全双工短对话中，Text-AB 的人类相似度 MOS 为 4.53，真人为 4.62；长对话中为 3.86，对比内部模型为 3.00。情绪对话 SFT 相比无情绪条件模型，愤怒、快乐、悲伤的情绪识别率分别由 0.650/0.396/0.455 提升至 0.814/0.428/0.479，情感交互 MOS 由 3.317 提升至 3.675。

Conclusion: Text-AB 证明，免强制对齐的高压缩 DAC-VAE 潜扩散、跨注意力隐式对齐，以及多阶段训练能够以统一模型同时处理配音和全双工对话，并在短对话上接近真人录音、在长对话和配音上显著超过内部系统。多扩散与多候选重排序有效突破单次生成长度限制并提升质量。不过，其结论主要来自内部数据集和内部基线，尚需公开数据集、独立复现以及安全评估验证。推理还需要大规模算力，并存在文本事实性、跨语言覆盖、长时身份漂移、自动指标与人类偏好不一致等限制。

Abstract: We present Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB), a unified framework for high-quality voice dubbing and full-duplex dialogue synthesis. Building on a Diffusion Transformer trained with a flow-matching objective, Text-AB departs from the Audiobox system along three dimensions. First, it operates in a latent diffusion framework using DAC-VAE features that encode 48 kHz waveforms into a 25 Hz latent sequence, giving over 10x higher compression than previous EnCodec representations while improving resynthesis quality. Second, Text-AB is alignment-free: it consumes raw text via an off-the-shelf text encoder and learns text-speech alignment through cross-attention, removing the need for forced alignment and explicit duration prediction. Third, we scale model and data substantially, pretraining a 3B-parameter model on 480k hours of monolingual speech, followed by supervised fine-tuning on three downstream tasks: cross-lingual voice dubbing, full-duplex dialogue synthesis, and emotional full-duplex dialogue synthesis. At inference, Text-AB supports one-shot generation for up to ~1 min of speech and arbitrarily long-form generation via a multi-diffusion scheme, plus a multi-stage reranking strategy that enhances quality based on automated metrics. On a real-world dubbing benchmark, Text-AB delivers a step-change improvement over the latest internal dubbing system, with large gains in prosody similarity, voice similarity, naturalness, and shareability. For full-duplex dialogue synthesis, it approaches human recordings on short-form conversations and substantially outperforms the latest internal model on long-form human-likeness and expressivity, while natively modeling turn-taking, back-channeling, and emotional dynamics. For emotional dialogue synthesis, emotion conditioning significantly improves emotion alignment and emotional interaction quality over the unconditioned baseline.

</details>

Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J.P.S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan,et al. (144 additional authors not shown)*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"Last Translation Benchmark (LTB)"，这是一个用于机器翻译的持续更新基准测试，包含人工撰写并经同行评审的能导致当前最先进机器翻译模型失败的示例（文本、图像、音频、视频），并附带手工编写的验证规则以实现可靠且可操作的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器翻译基准测试正逐渐趋于饱和，面临性能上限问题。同时，自动翻译指标不可靠、容易被"奖励破解"，且难以提供可操作的反馈；人工评估也缺乏可重复性、客观性和可扩展性。这些问题阻碍了机器翻译领域的客观进展追踪与改进路径识别，亟需更困难、更有诊断性的测试基准与评估方法。

Method: 1. 收集并整理人类撰写、经同行评审的多模态翻译挑战性示例（涵盖文本、图像、音频、视频）。2. 为每个示例设计手工编写的验证规则，明确描述该示例上可能出现的具体失败模式。3. 采用"活数据集"机制，持续接受社区贡献并定期发布新版本。4. 当前发布的版本为 LTBv1，收录截至 2026 年 9 月 1 日前被接受的贡献。

Result: 当前已发布 LTBv1 版本，包含多模态的、具有挑战性的翻译示例及相应的手写验证规则，可用于系统性地测试领先机器翻译模型的失败案例。基准将以持续更新方式运营，未来将随新数据加入发布新版本。

Conclusion: Last Translation Benchmark 提供了一个比传统基准更具挑战性的测试平台，通过结合人类精心设计的失败案例与可验证的评估规则，弥补了自动评估指标和人工评估在可靠性、可重复性和可操作性方面的不足，有助于推动机器翻译领域取得更客观、可衡量的进展。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>

### [76] [Practical Threshold-based Tree Edit Distance Lower-Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03078)
*Lukáš Moravec,Radim Bača*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文针对基于阈值的树编辑距离（TED）相似性搜索问题，提出了一种新的过滤方法SED-struct。该方法通过将字符串编辑距离（SED）下界与轴感知结构约束相结合，显著提高了过滤精度，尤其是在标签多样性较低或树结构异构的数据集上。同时，论文通过引入Ukkonen有界字符串编辑距离算法加速了SED下界的计算，并进行了全面的实验比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阈值约束下的树编辑距离（TED）计算在生物信息学、抄袭检测等领域应用广泛，但精确计算成本高昂。现有的TED下界方法存在权衡：基于统计和结构的方法过滤能力有限，而基于SED的方法精度高但计算昂贵（标准二次动态规划）。以往的研究未能涵盖最新的结构过滤器或阈值感知的SED实现，其实际权衡尚不清楚。

Method: 1. 对现有TED下界（大小、标签交集、二叉分支、结构过滤、SED）进行综合实验比较。
2. 利用Ukkonen有界字符串编辑距离算法加速SED下界计算，将复杂度从二次降至O(τ·min(m, n))。
3. 提出SED-struct阈值过滤器：将SED与轴感知结构约束相结合。定义遍历特定轴集（如前序遍历使用{descendants, followers}），通过结构守卫（structural guard）机制在动态规划中阻断不可行的节点匹配。
4. 形式化证明SED-struct过滤器的正确性（声音性）：若SED-struct得分超过阈值τ，则真实TED必然超过τ。
5. 在合成和真实数据集（生物信息学、书目、 NLP、代码结构等）上进行评估，并提供PostgreSQL扩展实现。

Result: 1. Ukkonen优化使SED下界计算速度提升数个数量级，尤其对于大树。
2. SED-struct在所有真实数据集上实现了最高的过滤精度，特别在Sentiment和PTB数据集上显著降低了整体查询运行时间。
3. 在异构或标签稀缺场景下，SED-struct表现出极强的鲁棒性：当标签池仅含2个标签时，SED精度降至约40%，而SED-struct保持约95%的精度。
4. 遍历组合的选择至关重要：对于SED-struct，rpre/pre（逆前序/前序）组合表现最佳；而对于传统SED，pre/post组合最优。
5. 实验表明，由于Ukkonen优化使SED类过滤器速度接近简单过滤器，多级级联简单过滤器的策略收益甚微。

Conclusion: SED-struct阈值过滤器通过结合结构信息显著提升了TED相似性搜索的过滤精度，同时通过Ukkenen算法保持了实用性。该方法在标签多样性低或结构异构的树集合中尤为有效，是阈值TED搜索的实用且有效的组件。此外，研究表明在使用优化的Ukkonen实现后，简单的多级过滤策略已无必要。代码已公开，并提供了PostgreSQL扩展。

Abstract: Threshold-based similarity search over tree-structured data using tree edit distance (TED) is computationally intensive. Given a query tree and a database of trees, the goal is to retrieve all trees within a predefined TED threshold $\tau$. Because exact TED computation is expensive, practical methods employ lower-bounds to prune dissimilar candidates before verification. Existing lower-bounds exhibit a fundamental trade-off: inexpensive statistical and structural bounds provide limited pruning power, whereas the more precise traversal-based string edit distance (SED) bound is expensive to compute using standard quadratic dynamic programming. Moreover, previous comparative studies do not cover recent structural filters or threshold-aware SED implementations, leaving their practical trade-offs unclear. In this article, we first provide a comprehensive experimental comparison of state-of-the-art TED lower-bounds in terms of pruning precision and computational cost. We then accelerate the SED lower-bound using Ukkonen's bounded string edit distance algorithm, substantially reducing its runtime without affecting its pruning power. Finally, we introduce the SED-struct threshold filter, which strengthens SED with axes-aware constraints capturing structural relationships among tree nodes. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SED-struct consistently achieves the highest filtering precision while retaining practical filtering costs. The results suggest that SED-struct is particularly beneficial for heterogeneous tree collections in which the standard SED lower-bound achieves relatively low precision.

</details>


### [77] [What Happens When the Model Eats the Stack? Rethinking the Research Agenda for Data Agents to Withstand the Bitter Lesson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03141)
*Liana Patel,Siddharth Jha,Negar Arabzadeh,Carlos Guestrin,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对"苦涩教训"对数据系统社区的存亡之问，通过对比通用编码Agent与人工设计的数据Agent在2025-2026年间三代前沿模型（o3、GPT-5、GPT-5.6 Sol）上的表现，发现随着模型能力提升，通用编码Agent在准确性和效率上均显著超越人工设计的数据Agent，验证了苦涩教训的判断。作者进而指出数据Agent跨多查询的持久性语义上下文（persistent semantic context）将成为未来数据库系统亟需支持的一类核心抽象，并围绕语义一致性、上下文构造与物理设计提出了新的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型正快速内化原本需要专门工程化数据Agent的能力（规划、代码生成、调试、验证等），这呼应了Sutton的苦涩教训。在此背景下，数据系统社区面临关键问题：当模型"吃掉整个技术栈"后，哪些系统问题仍会持续存在并变得更加重要？作者质疑了当前以Agent为中心、为低效投机查询优化的数据系统设计方向，认为应转向支持持久化语义上下文的研究路线。

Method: 实证研究方法：在TAG-Bench和DAB两个数据Agent基准上，使用o3（2025初）、GPT-5（2025中）、GPT-5.6 Sol（2026中）三代模型，对比通用编码Agent（Codex harness）与人工设计数据Agent（Agentar-Scale-SQL、DeepEye）的准确性、Token效率、每查询回合数及失败根因分布；分析失败轨迹归纳为5类根因集群；通过离线生成Self-Curated（Accuracy/Latency/Schema）和GEPA四种持久化上下文，在GPT-5.6 Sol编码Agent上评估其对held-out DAB任务的准确率、回合数、延迟、构建成本和内存开销的影响。

Result: 1) 通用编码Agent随模型迭代在TAG-Bench和DAB上均显著超越人工设计数据Agent，GPT-5.6 Sol编码Agent在DAB上较o3提升35+个百分点并实现2× Token效率提升，且回合数减少约4×；2) GPT-5.6 Sol超过60%的剩余错误是环境知识失败（语义误解、错误数据源、实体/连接键不匹配），而非执行或工具错误；3) Self-Curated(Accuracy)上下文带来+19个百分点的准确率增益，Self-Curated(Schema)显著减少在线模式探索回合数，但构建开销显著（Schema上下文构建耗时3,400秒/$9.60/162KB）。这表明持久化语义上下文能有效摊销跨查询的环境理解成本，但也带来显著的构建、存储和维护开销。

Conclusion: 未来数据库系统应将持久化语义上下文作为一等抽象原生支持，以应对苦涩教训下持续存在的"环境知识"难题。研究议程聚焦两大方向：(1) 语义一致性——设计一致性作用域（个人/团队/企业/全局）、一致性模型（强一致/事件/查询触发）以及一致性维护方法（增量维护vs整体重生）；(2) 上下文构造与物理设计——选择合适的数据结构（文本/知识图谱/向量索引等异构组合）、物理设计（查询无关与查询感知上下文的分区与缓存策略）以及针对超大规模上下文的压缩与生命周期管理策略。该工作重新定义了数据库社区在LLM时代的研究焦点。

Abstract: The bitter lesson poses an existential question for the data systems community, whereby large language models (LLMs) trained end-to-end are rapidly internalizing new capabilities that previously required carefully engineered data agents. Guided by empirical insights, we argue that as models continue to improve, many proposed system layers designed to compensate for model limitations on a given task will increasingly be subsumed by the model itself. We instead identify enduring research opportunities, which lie in supporting data agents across many queries with curated contextual information about the data environment, which we call persistent semantic context. We find that these context layers demonstrate strong promise for improving data agent performance, but they also raise significant system challenges. Thus, a key requirement for future data systems will lie in natively serving persistent semantic contexts as a first-class abstraction in order to enable capable data agents working over huge, complex knowledge corpora. Towards this vision, we outline exciting new research opportunities, including designing efficient context data structures, storage methods, compression techniques, and semantic consistency protocols, to ensure integrity and correctness of the stored contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [78] [Does SRL Pave the Road to Explainable Reasoning? Lessons Learned from an Implementer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03441)
*Lander Maes,Bryan-Elliott Tam,Jitse De Smet,Jos De Roo,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文从实现者视角探讨 W3C Shape Rules Language (SRL) 工作草案，通过实现并对比两个 SRL 引擎——一个基于 SPARQL/Comunica 复用，一个独立编写（Eyeleng），评估其在 RDF 推理任务中的正确性、完整性和性能。结论是：基于 SPARQL 引擎可低成本构建合规的 SRL 引擎，但有 2–6 倍的速度损失；独立引擎更快且原生支持可解释性（证明树）。同时指出 SRL 规范中 query 操作缺乏定义、闭包依赖分层、空节点语法在 WHERE 子句中的兼容性等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRL 是 W3C 数据形状工作组提出的第一个 W3C 背书的 RDF 规则推理规范，旨在弥补 RDFS/OWL 固定蕴涵体系僵化、N3/SWRL/RIF 等规则语言碎片化、缺乏统一标准的不足。然而规范尚不成熟，缺乏权威实现，实施者无从参考。本文从实现者角度填补这一空白。

Method: 实现两个 SRL 引擎并对比：(1) 基于 Comunica 的 SPARQL 引擎——通过 Traqula 解析器将 SRL 文本转为 SPARQL 代数，用反排索引 + Kosaraju SCC + Kahn 拓扑排序进行依赖分析与分层，每条规则作为 SPARQL CONSTRUCT 查询执行至不动点；(2) Eyeleng 独立引擎——从头实现解析、依赖分析、分层与规则执行，采用前向/后向混合策略（普通结果前向物化，类似函数计算的谓词按需带表求值）。两者均对照 SRL 一致性测试套件（166 项）进行验证，并在 25 个工作负载上测量性能（中位数 + IQR），改变规则集大小、数据图大小、递归深度、规则体复杂度、触发次数等变量。

Result: 正确性：Eyeleng 通过全部 166 项一致性测试；Comunica 实现通过 154 项（其余 12 项为负向测试中未拒绝的非法输入，差距在于输入校验而非求值）。性能：在全部 25 个工作负载上 Eyeleng 均更快，加速比 2.15×–6.44×；每个规则触发 Comunica 约 193 μs、Eyeleng 约 20 μs（9.6× 差距），每个模式约 263 μs vs 50 μs（5.3×），加速比随规则数增加而增大但趋近斜率比。规范层面发现：(a) query 操作未定义评估流程，是实现可解释证明树的关键缺口；(b) 开/闭依赖边决定了规则分层，Kosaraju+Kahn 可替代规范迭代算法；(c) Turtle 简写空节点 [ … ] / ( … ) 在 WHERE 与 DATA 两侧同时使用时，由于 Traqula 与 Comunica 对空节点标签的处理不一致，导致匹配失败。

Conclusion: 复用 SPARQL 基础设施可以低成本构建合规的 SRL 引擎，代价是中等速度损失（专用实现可恢复）且失去可解释性；独立引擎更快并天然生成证明树。SRL 已具备实用的规则语言骨架（分层、不动点语义、依赖分析），但 provenance 形式与 query 操作语义需由实现者自行填补，规范应明确定义 query 的输出形式（如证明树）以实现解释的互操作性。基于 SPARQL 复用的同时也要继承其边界情况（如空节点标签匹配问题）。

Abstract: The Shape Rules Language (SRL) Working Draft defines how to derive new RDF triples from an RDF graph using inference rules. Each rule matches graph patterns and instantiates triple templates whose output feeds into validation pipelines, SPARQL queries, or further inference. RDF reasoning has traditionally relied on fixed entailment regimes (RDFS, OWL), rule-based ad-hoc languages such as N3, or other implementation-specific solutions without a shared standard. SRL introduces user-defined production rules with a defined grammar, dependency analysis, execution ordering, and termination guarantees. However, no authoritative implementation exists, leaving practitioners with little guidance on how to build a conformant engine or on what problems the language can solve. We implemented two SRL engines and evaluated both on classical RDF reasoning tasks for soundness, completeness, and speed. The first reuses an existing SPARQL query engine and its query parser; the second is a dedicated engine. The SPARQL-based engine reused an existing modular parser for query construction and SPARQL CONSTRUCT for triple production, reducing engine-specific work. The dedicated engine was two to six times faster, the gap widening as rule sets grow. Both engines were validated against the SRL conformance test suite, supplemented by additional use-case-driven tests. A usable SRL engine can be built inexpensively on top of a SPARQL engine, with a moderate speed trade-off that a dedicated implementation recovers. Despite the specification's immaturity, the language already supports practically useful reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [79] [KnowFeat: Knowledge-Guided Feature Engineering via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03529)
*Chengsong You,Wangyue Li,Weiqiao Que,Qizhou Chen,Kunyan Wu,Wei Deng,Feng Zhu,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: KnowFeat是一个知识引导的特征工程框架，通过LLM代理注入五类领域知识（模式元数据、风险指标、检测规则、专家意见、法庭证据），结合L1代码执行、L2统计质量、L3模型效果三阶段验证流水线，在12个公共基准上取得最佳平均排名（2.3），并通过provenance card提供完整的特征可追溯性，特别适用于反洗钱等受监管领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的特征工程方法存在三大局限：缺乏结构化领域知识注入、特征验证不充分、可解释性有限，无法满足金融、医疗等受监管行业对特征语义可追溯性的硬性要求。

Method: 方法包含五大核心组件：(1) 五类领域知识体系，按特异性递增分为模式元数据、风险指标、检测规则、专家意见、法庭证据；(2) 基于ReAct范式的LLM代理，配备数据探索、特征注册、验证触发三类工具；(3) L1→L2→L3三阶段渐进式验证流水线（代码沙箱→噪声校准的IV/KS统计过滤→XGBoost五种子模型效果评估）；(4) 基于语义维度的智能搜索策略（SHAP反向加权预算重分配+失败记忆+前向选择+跨维度传播）；(5) 为每个被采纳特征生成结构化provenance card，记录知识资产、推理链、计算步骤与验证结果。

Result: 在12个公共基准的严格无泄漏留出协议下，KnowFeat平均排名2.3居首（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.017），跨XGBoost/LightGBM/CatBoost三种模型家族增益均显著；在电信流失数据集峰值AUC提升+11.6pp；在SimECNY合成AML数据集（20万笔交易）上4个被验证特征维持基线F1，而FeatLLM的1124个未验证特征使F1下降；在Elliptic比特币AML数据集（46K笔）AUC达0.9982；来自中国邮政储蓄银行的从业者反馈将合规审查时间从2–3小时缩短至15–30分钟。

Conclusion: KnowFeat证明了结构化领域知识注入与渐进式验证的协同价值：在受监管场景下，质量胜过数量，provenance使特征审计成为可能；其核心机制是验证流水线而非LLM的token级确定性（三次独立运行特征名Jaccard≤0.04但测试AUC标准差≤0.016）；局限在于领域知识的人工策划成本、L3每候选重新训练的算力开销，以及语义维度命名对探索方向的影响。

Abstract: Automated feature engineering with large language models (LLMs) can produce semantically meaningful features for tabular data, yet existing methods lack structured domain knowledge, rigorous verification, and explainable provenance. We propose KnowFeat, a knowledge-guided feature engineering framework that organizes domain knowledge into five types -- schema metadata, regulatory indicators, detection rules, expert opinions, and court document evidence -- and injects them as structured context into an LLM agent. A three-stage verification pipeline filters candidates through code execution, statistical quality checks, and model effectiveness evaluation. Every accepted feature carries a provenance record tracing its design to specific knowledge assets. Under a strict held-out protocol that eliminates feature-selection leakage, KnowFeat ranks first (avg. rank 2.3) across twelve public benchmarks among seven methods (one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.017), with a peak gain of +11.6 pp AUC on a telecom churn dataset. On a real-world Bitcoin anti-money laundering (AML) dataset (Elliptic) and a synthetic digital currency AML benchmark (SimECNY), KnowFeat maintains competitive detection performance with full provenance traceability.

</details>


### [80] [Tree Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03538)
*Nicolas Spyratos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以带标签有向树为基本结构的树数据库模型。树数据库由若干棵树组成，节点表示数据集，边表示全函数。通过函数代数（限制、复合、配对、笛卡尔积）定义树操作（限制、复合、投影、笛卡尔积）和查询语言（遍历查询与分析查询），在统一形式框架下支持数据访问与分析。展示了继承支持、在树数据库上定义关系数据库、作为关系数据用户友好接口三个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系模型中，遍历类查询（关系代数）和分析类查询（SQL Group-by）处于不同形式框架中，前者作为基础，后者定义于其外。本文旨在提出一种以树为基本结构的数据库模型，将两类查询统一在同一函数代数框架内，从而实现数据访问与数据分析的形式化统一，同时支持继承、关系数据库语义层构建及友好接口等应用。

Method: 模型定义：树数据库为带节点标识的有限有向标记树集合，根视为实体，非根节点为属性。引入函数代数（四种操作：笛卡尔积、限制、配对、复合）作为基础。树操作包括树限制、树复合、树投影、树的笛卡尔积，构成树代数。查询语言定义两类查询：遍历查询（路径表达式及其配对）和平凡元查询（分组函数、度量函数、聚合操作的三元组），二者在同一函数代数框架下定义。引入终端节点与终端边扩展表达能力。分析表达式通过复合规则与配对规则支持重写与递归求值。完整性约束包括树间包含约束与树内细化约束（与函数依赖对应）。

Result: 1) 形式化定义了树数据库、树实例、函数代数、树代数、完整性约束、视图等核心概念；2) 证明了函数代数操作可模拟 Armstrong 公理（自反性、传递性、增广性）的可靠性；3) 定义了树复合、树限制、树投影、树的笛卡尔积及其语义计算方法；4) 给出分析表达式的复合规则与配对规则两种重写规则，支持递归求值与计算共享；5) 展示了三个应用：(a) 通过 Isa 边与树复合实现单继承与多继承（含菱形问题解决方案）；(b) 在树数据库上通过混合查询定义一致性关系数据库，为每个关系属性赋予语义；(c) 利用函数依赖将关系数据库模式转换为等价树数据库作为访问接口。

Conclusion: 树数据库模型以树为基本结构，以函数为一等公民，关系为派生概念。其核心贡献在于将遍历查询与分析查询统一于同一函数代数形式框架，区别于关系模型中分析查询独立于关系代数的设计。模型能无缝支持继承，可作为关系数据库的语义层与用户友好接口。未来研究方向包括查询重写、增量更新算法、树数据库设计准则、关系模型理论再审视及数据可视化，并最终构建完整的树数据库管理系统。

Abstract: We propose a novel database model whose basic structure is a labeled, directed tree with node identities. Intuitively, the root of the tree is seen as an object (or entity), the non-root nodes as attributes of the object and the semantics of each attribute is represented by the unique path leading from the root to the attribute. We define a tree database to be a set of such trees. The trees of the database can be combined to produce new trees using a set of operations on trees that we define in the paper. The query language of our model offers two types of queries, traversal queries and analytic queries. A query (whether traversal or analytic) is always defined over a tree, which is either a tree in the database or a tree derived from other trees using tree operations. The operations on trees and the query language are both defined using a simple functional algebra whose operations are: restriction of a function, composition of functions, pairing of functions and Cartesian product of sets. A distinctive feature of our model is that traversal queries and analytic queries are both defined within the same formal framework; and in fact, traversal queries serve as the building blocks for analytic queries. This is in sharp contrast to the relational model, where analytic queries are defined outside the relational algebra, in the form of SQL Group-by queries. Therefore our model supports data access and data analysis within the same formal framework. We demonstrate the expressive power of our model by showing: (a) how our model can support inheritance in a seamless manner, (b) how one can define consistent relational databases on top of a tree database - with the tree database playing the role of an underlying semantic layer and (c) how a tree database can be used as a user-friendly interface for accessing and analyzing relational data.

</details>


### [81] [Convolution Sum-Product Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03672)
*Kyle Deeds,Timo Camillo Merkl,Dan Suciu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积和积查询（CSPQ），将SPQ扩展为允许原子中包含变量的线性组合（如 A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)），覆盖图像卷积、k-SUM、min,+卷积等场景，并基于线性代数技术（秩分析、商空间、变量替换）设计了稠密/稀疏评估算法及树分解因子化方法，定义了三种宽度度量以刻画复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SPQ不支持变量线性组合，在矩阵卷积、图像处理、k-SUM猜想等场景中受限；现有含线性约束的查询评估技术次优，需将线性表达式作为一等公民重新设计评估算法与分解框架。

Method: 1) 用商空间定义CSPQ语义保证有限性；2) 适配Worst-Case Optimal Join达到AGM上界；3) 定义Convolution Tree Decomposition (CTD)及三种宽度ctw/atw/fcw，分别基于域值大小、活跃域大小和数据库大小给出因子化执行；5) 推广至任意域。

Result: 稠密评估 O(|adom|^k)；稀疏评估 O(|D|^{ρ*})；基于CTD的三种因子化算法分别达到 O(n(D)^{ctw+1})、O(|adom|^{atw+1})、O(|D|^{fcw})；在传统CQ上与经典树宽/fhtw一致。

Conclusion: CSPQ在保留SPQ表达力的同时原生支持线性表达式，所提算法在包含k-SUM、矩阵卷积等应用中匹配已知最优复杂度，为该类查询提供了系统的评估框架和宽度分析工具。

Abstract: We study query evaluation for an extension of sum-product queries (SPQ) that allows atoms with linear combinations of variables (e.g. $A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)$), which we call convolution sum-product queries (CSPQs). These queries arise in both practical settings (e.g. image processing workloads) and theoretical ones (e.g. the $(\min,+)$-convolution and $k$-SUM conjectures), and capture SPQs with added linear equality constraints. While prior work has considered the impact of linear in- and dis-equality constraints on query evaluation, the techniques developed in that setting are asymptotically sub-optimal for CSPQs. To address this, we describe several evaluation algorithms for CSPQs that leverage linear algebra techniques like rank analysis, quotient spaces, and variable substitution. First, we adapt Worst-Case Optimal Joins to CSPQs, achieving a runtime similar in spirit to that for conjunctive queries. Then, we extend the definition of tree decompositions (TDs) to CSPQs, and describe a factorized execution. In this extension, linear combinations are first-class citizens and play the same role as variables in traditional TDs. We define three width measures that bound the complexity of this execution with respect to the size of the domain values, of the active domain, and of the relation's support. Lastly, we show how these methods can be applied to arbitrary fields beyond the rationals.

</details>


### [82] [Aker: Density-Aware Approximate Caching for Vector Search (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03712)
*Sukjoon Oh,Minki Kang,Dohyun Kim,Baotong Lu,Jing Liu,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Youjip Won*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Aker提出了一种面向向量数据库的密度感知近似缓存方案，通过每查询相似度阈值和del-consistency一致性模型，解决了现有近似缓存在高维空间中难以兼顾吞吐量与准确率的问题，并支持轻量级缓存刷新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁盘上的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）因索引遍历过程中的读放大问题（可达10²-10³倍）而面临严重的I/O瓶颈。现有result-centric近似缓存方案存在两大缺陷：1）使用单一全局相似度阈值无法适应高维空间中变化的局部邻域密度，难以同时保证高吞吐和高准确率；2）缺乏有效的缓存刷新机制来应对向量更新导致的结果陈旧问题。

Method: Aker的核心设计包含两个关键机制：(1) 密度感知的每查询相似度阈值τ_q——为每个缓存查询维护独立的阈值，并基于命中模式（等值命中时乘以α_L=1.1增大，近似命中时乘以α_T=0.25缩小）进行动态调整，以适应不同区域的密度差异；(2) del-consistency一致性模型——删除立即生效（通过引用计数和neighbor对象池保证有效性），插入则延迟处理，通过维护(k+Δ)大小的候选池和基于风险因子R=r̄·Ū/W的慢路径批量刷新机制来限制陈旧度。系统还采用representative/alias条目结构共享top-k结果，HNSW-based query filter实现高效相似查询检索，以及active/standby双索引设计避免细粒度删除开销。

Result: 将Aker集成到pgvector后，在SPACEV和TriviaQA数据集上的评估显示：相比Potluck和Proximity等现有方案，Aker在召回率上最高提升64个百分点；在QPS上相比pgvector默认shared buffer最高提升3.2倍，同时内存占用仅为其0.6倍。读放大降低至原来的0.4-0.7倍。Aker的缓存查找延迟低于0.1ms，缓存刷新开销仅0.22ms（约为搜索延迟的4.4%）。即使在25%删除率和5%插入的压力测试下，召回率下降不超过2个百分点。

Conclusion: Aker通过密度感知的每查询阈值控制和del-consistency一致性模型，成功地使基于相似度的近似缓存在磁盘ANNS系统中变得实用。该系统在显著提升吞吐量和召回率的同时，大幅降低了内存开销和读放大，为大规模向量搜索提供了一种高效且可扩展的缓存解决方案。

Abstract: Disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) incurs high I/O overhead due to frequent disk accesses during index traversal. Approximate caching, which reuses the results of past queries to serve future similar queries, offers a promising approach to bypass expensive disk searches. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations. First, their approximate hit predicates fail to simultaneously achieve high throughput and high accuracy, as they do not adapt to the varying local neighbor density in high-dimensional spaces. Second, they lack an effective refresh mechanism to maintain cache correctness under vector updates. We present Aker, an approximate cache for disk-based ANNS. Aker addresses these limitations through two core design choices. First, we introduce a per-query similarity threshold, where each cache entry maintains its own threshold that is dynamically adjusted based on observed cache hit patterns. This design enables Aker to adapt to neighborhood densities to preserve both efficiency and accuracy. Second, we propose del-consistency, a consistency model for ANNS caches that applies deletions eagerly and insertions lazily. Under this model, Aker implements a low-overhead refresh mechanism that bounds cache staleness and preserves high search accuracy. We integrate Aker into pgvector and evaluate it on representative workloads. Aker improves recall by up to 64 percentage points over prior solutions and increases QPS by up to 3.2x, while using 0.6x the memory of pgvector's shared buffers.

</details>


### [83] [From Data Querying to Data Investigations: Rethinking Natural Language Interfaces for Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03898)
*Fabian Wenz,Zixuan Chen,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文批评传统Text-to-SQL范式过于局限,认为用户实际需要的是解决数据问题(数据调查),而非回答单一查询。论文提出"数据调查系统"新范式,设计原型D²,采用多智能体架构(数据侦探)与外部化共享记忆(数据调查板),并基于Murder Mystery构建新基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Text-to-SQL范式假设用户问题可由一条SQL查询回答,但实际用户(医生、分析师等)提出的数据问题通常需要多步探索、多假设评估和证据收集的迭代过程。现有LLM agent方法存在过早收敛于单一假设、推理过程难以审计两大不足,因此需要一种新的NL接口范式。

Method: 提出两大系统抽象:(1) Data Detective——多智能体团队,包含Hypothesizer(生成候选假设)、Orchestrator(全局调度)、Evidence Collectors(并行收集证据)、Judge(评估证据并可精炼假设)、Reporter(生成可审计报告);(2) Data Investigation Board——基于SQLite的外部化共享状态,含Hypotheses、Evidence、Links三张表,记录假设、证据及其支持/反驳关系,实现状态与执行分离。基于Murder Mystery构建50个虚构案件的基准,使用正确性、完整性、可验证性三个维度对比D²与Claude Code、OpenClaw,并进行消融实验。

Result: 在50个案件基准上:D²正确性95.26%,与基线(OpenClaw 94.64%, Claude Code 93.37%)相当;但完整性达76.02%(基线仅约32%),可验证性达68.88%(基线仅约2-3%)。在所有难度等级(简单、适中、困难、极难)上D²均显著优于基线。消融实验表明Judge对完整性和正确性贡献最大,Reporter对可验证性贡献最大,合并Hypothesizer与Orchestrator或退化为单智能体均显著降低完整性。

Conclusion: 数据调查代表一种新的数据管理工作负载,需要新的范式(超越Text-to-SQL)与系统层;D²作为首个原型证明了多智能体+外部化调查板的可行性。未来研究方向包括:多目标调查优化(准确性、完整性、成本)、调查溯源与审计、扩展基准至真实大规模工作负载、集成异构数据与专用算子(图、文、约束求解器等)。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) interfaces to databases have been optimized for the wrong problem. The dominant Text-to-SQL paradigm assumes that users ask questions that can be answered by single SQL queries. In practice, however, users seek assistance with solving data problems. This requires searching a database by sequences of SQL queries while reasoning over intermediate results instead of just running one SQL query. This paper therefore introduces a new paradigm for NL interfaces to data, which we call data investigations. We present D^2, a first prototype of a data investigation system that embodies this vision by autonomously searching, reasoning over, and collecting data to solve data problems. Using a newly constructed benchmark based on the Murder Mystery dataset, we demonstrate the potential of D^2 for tasks that require data investigations with evidence-backed decisions, extending beyond the capabilities of traditional single-query question answering.

</details>


### [84] [Property Testing for Recursive Query Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03908)
*Isolde Adler,Carsten Lutz,Quentin Manière,Marcin Przybyłko,Lukas Schulze*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文首次将Chen-Yoshida（2019）的数据库属性测试框架从非递归的布尔合取查询（UCQ）扩展到递归查询语言，主要研究双向正则路径查询（2RPQ）和一元Datalog（MDLog）。核心结果包括：任意2RPQ的非回答可在常数次查询内单边错误测试；强线性MDLog程序和简单分支程序（SBP）也可常数查询测试；但若自连接无关的MDLog程序不等价于α-acyclic程序，则其假性不可常数查询测试。证明中关键技术包括：归结到多图不可达性、Menger定理的应用，以及"关键模式"（弦圈或四面体）的精细结构分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据集规模巨大，传统的精确查询计算变得不切实际。属性测试提供了一种通过亚线性探测以高置信度判断查询答案的范式。Chen和Yoshida（2019）已建立非递归UCQ的属性测试二分法，但递归查询语言（2RPQ、MDLog）的属性测试尚未被研究，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Chen-Yoshida的数据库属性测试模型（含completion/size查询）。技术上：
- 2RPQ情况：归结到多图不可达性问题（利用Yoshida-Kobayashi 2012结果），通过构造数据库与查询的乘积多重图。
- 强线性MDLog：先消除自环和重边，归结到2RPQ情况。
- 简单分支程序（SBP）：利用Menger定理保证大量边不相交路径，进一步通过UCQ方法实现常数查询测试。
- 下界证明：引入"α-acyclic近似"Π*，对自连接无关程序证明存在数据库D和关键弦圈/四面体X在每个推导中必须被使用，从而归约到Chen-Yoshida对弦圈和四面体CQ的硬度结果。

Result: 主要结果：
1. 任意2RPQ的非回答是常数查询单边错误可测试。
2. 强线性MDLog程序（每条规则体含恰好两个变量，且无IDB原子包含头变量）的假性可常数查询单边错误测试。
3. 简单分支程序（SBP——线性递归沿单一关系，两端为星查询）的假性可常数查询单边错误测试。
4. 若自连接无关的布尔MDLog程序不等价于α-acyclic程序，则其假性不可常数查询单边错误测试。
证明的副产品还包括对简单查询q_2的有向Steiner树打包相关开放问题（Problem 4.13）。

Conclusion: 该论文建立了递归查询语言属性测试的基础二分法框架，证明了2RPQ的正向结果和递归MDLog的硬度下界（自连接无关情况下）。研究的核心技术工具包括数据库-查询乘积图、Menger定理（作为"删除引理"）、以及"关键模式"的概念。论文识别出有向弧不相交Steiner树打包（ADSTP）中的长期开放问题与递归α-acyclic MDLog程序常数查询可测试性之间的深刻联系，为未来研究指明了方向：填补线性α-acyclic MDLog程序的空白，理解包语义与集合语义的差异。

Abstract: In the context of database querying, property testing provides a framework for testing query answers with high confidence while inspecting only a sublinear part of the database, through completion queries and size queries. A fundamental result of Chen and Yoshida (2019) states that non-satisfaction of a Boolean conjunctive query $q$ is testable with a constant number of such queries and one-sided error if and only if $q$ is equivalent to an $\alpha$-acyclic query. In this article, we initiate the study of property testing for recursive query languages, focusing on two-way regular path queries (2RPQs) and monadic Datalog. One of our main results is positive: non-answers to any 2RPQ are constant query testable with one-sided error. We extend this slightly to a certain class of monadic Datalog programs in which recursion is restricted to be linear and rule bodies must be $\alpha$-acyclic. Turning towards unrestricted monadic Datalog, we next show that if a monadic Datalog program $\Pi$ is not equivalent to an $\alpha$-acyclic program, then falsity of $\Pi$ is not constant query testable with one-sided error. This is under the assumption that all rule-bodies are self-join free. We leave open the case of monadic Datalog programs with $\alpha$-acyclic rule bodies that are not restricted to linear recursion, but observe as a first step that there exist $\alpha$-acyclic programs that are mildly non-linear and constant query testable with one-sided error.

</details>
