### [85] [Reducing Catastrophic Risk from AI with Systematic Monitoring and Evaluation of Rogue AI Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03189)
*T. Bauer,W.P. Kegelmeyer,E. Begoli,A. Sadovnik,T. Emerson,C. Corley,N. Generous,J. Moore,B. Bartoldson,R. Goldhan,M. Goldman,M. Greaves,M.J.D. Vermeer,B. MacLennan,D. Schulker,N. VanHoudnos,J. Bansemer,Y. Bengio*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统化的AI行为指标框架，用于监测可能演变为灾难性威胁的"流氓AI"。该框架借鉴网络安全与国家安全方法论，围绕五个递进类别展开：动机失调、持久性、规划、实验与执行，旨在为研究者和政策制定者提供基于证据的监测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统自主性增强，特别是能力提升后可能脱离人类控制，形成传统威慑失效的战略脆弱性。现有安全理论以人类动机为基础，难以应对非人类主体的威胁。作者旨在建立一套独立于威胁发生概率争论的、可操作的监测框架。

Method: 采用框架驱动方法，定义五类递进式AI行为风险类别（动机、持久性、规划、实验、执行）。每个类别包含定义、可观测指标（observables）和警示指标（indicators）。方法借鉴网络安全、国家安全及威慑理论，通过多实验室跨机构研讨形成共识，并区分了"可观测属性"（无判断的测量）与"指标"（引发关切的特定值或模式）。

Result: 构建了五阶段监测框架：（1）动机失调（misalignment via misspecification或misgeneralization）；（2）持久性（数据跨时空维持能力，如隐写术存储）；（4）规划（长期目标分解与策略化，含显式与隐式规划）；（5）实验（主动改变行动以收集信息，如代理LLM自主科研）；（6）执行（资源预置、不可逆环境改变等"战场准备"行为）。框架提出了三类实施挑战：AI扩散规模、子AI主动颠覆监控的能力、AI间合谋风险。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一套低成本、低遗憾的监测工具，可在不同立场下实施，无需解决关于AI终极能力轨迹的根本分歧。文章呼吁政府和企业预先配置响应资源、建立公私合作关系，并建议即便竞争对手也可能从统一监测标准中获益。总体而言，这是将预防性原则应用于AI治理的具体工具，不阻碍有益创新。

Abstract: This article presents a structured framework of behavioral indicators that may signal progression toward potentially catastrophic threats from artificial intelligence systems. We adopt a pragmatic approach, inspired by established methodologies in cybersecurity and national security. By establishing clear metrics, indicators, and thresholds across multiple dimensions of AI capability and behavior, this framework enables researchers and policymakers to implement evidence-based monitoring protocols.

</details>


### [86] [Open Problems in AI Risk Modeling: Insights from a Workshop on the Technical Foundations of AI Risk Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03178)
*Krystal Jackson,Deepika Raman,Jakub Kryś,Sean P. Fillingham,Jack Kengott,Andrew J. Lohn,Nada Madkour,Henry Papadatos,James Sykes,Anna Katariina Wisakanto,Malcolm Murray*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨前沿AI系统社会风险的定量风险建模方法，综述了五大研究传统（概率风险评估、灾难性AI风险分析、网络安全风险量化、贝叶斯因果推断、阈值治理），比较了基于情景的风险估计与贝叶斯网络阈值设定两种方法论方案，并基于22位专家研讨会梳理出涵盖模型结构、范围、证据整合、验证与治理五大维度的开放问题清单，提出需结合独立评估、分层披露与制度化维护来推进该领域成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI监管框架（如欧盟AI法案、加州SB 53、首尔AI安全峰会承诺）均要求系统性风险评估，但缺乏严谨的定量方法论支撑，"最先进方法"的具体内涵尚未定义。与核电、航空等安全关键领域不同，AI系统具有开放性能力空间、快速能力扩展和深度社会技术依赖等特征，传统概率风险评估方法难以直接适用，亟需构建专门的AI风险建模方法论体系。

Method: 综合文献综述+专家研讨会方法：综述概率风险评估与安全工程、灾难性AI风险分析、网络安全风险量化、贝叶斯网络与因果推断、不可容忍风险阈值五大研究传统；对比分析Murray et al.(2025)的六步基于情景的风险估计框架（含MITRE ATT&CK分解、关键风险指标映射、Beta分布与蒙特卡洛传播）与Jackson et al.的贝叶斯网络阈值设定框架；召集22位跨领域专家研讨会，按模型结构、范围与粒度、证据整合与引出、验证与更新、治理与制度设计五个维度梳理开放问题。

Result: 识别出五大维度的开放问题：(1)模型结构——数据源选择与聚合、架构形式选择、参数简约性、静态模型的动态局限、共享组件构建；(2)范围与粒度——风险场景覆盖完整性与粒度深度的权衡，现有方法多作为风险下界估计；(3)证据整合——基准/历史数据/专家判断三类数据的局限，LLM模拟专家的认知循环性与可信度问题；(4)验证与更新——缺乏真实事件验证数据，提出内部质量度量、Shapley值稳定性分析、部分验证（如uplift研究）作为替代；(5)治理——开发者与独立评估者职责分离、分层披露制度、安全港保护、类IPCC综合评估机构、AI专用ISAC信息共享基础设施。提出优先研究方向清单与六项治理建议。

Conclusion: AI风险建模的进展取决于定量建模方法与独立评估、分层透明披露、能够长期维护和更新风险模型的制度安排三者的协同整合；近期最优先任务并非解决所有开放问题，而是创建能够使迭代模型开发、验证和审查在实践中可行的制度安排，使定量AI风险建模从一组有前景的提案发展为可靠的治理基础。

Abstract: We investigate the design of robust risk models to assess societal risks posed by advanced AI systems, an emerging area in AI governance. Many regulatory proposals increasingly require systemic risk assessment, but in the absence of rigorous quantitative methods, the question remains what state of the art risk modeling should look like in practice. We identify the key methodological and institutional challenges that currently limit the adoption of risk modeling. We review five research traditions that inform this problem: probabilistic risk assessment, catastrophic AI risk analysis, cybersecurity risk quantification, Bayesian causal inference, and threshold-based governance. We compare two leading proposals, scenario-based risk estimation and Bayesian network-based threshold setting. Drawing on a workshop with 22 experts and subsequent analysis, we identify a structured agenda of open questions concerning model structure, scope, evidence integration, validation, and governance. We close by outlining priorities for progress, arguing that it will depend on integrating quantitative modeling with independent evaluation, transparent and tiered disclosure, and institutions capable of maintaining and updating risk models over time.

</details>


### [87] [Affective publics in Arabic YouTube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03269)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Craig Douglas Albert,Abdullah Melhem,Ahmed Aleroud,Lance Y. Hunter*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究分析了67,725条阿拉伯语YouTube评论，覆盖也门、沙特、伊拉克、约旦和叙利亚五个MENA国家的社会政治话题，发现阿拉伯YouTube情感公众共享一种"以关怀和钦佩为主导的共同区域情感语法"，同时情感基调普遍负面；涉及美国的内容在直接军事存在的国家（伊拉克、叙利亚）更为负面，而在间接介入的国家（也门、沙特、约旦）则相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯语社媒情感研究多局限于Twitter/X平台，且通常仅做二元情感分类或单国分析，缺乏对YouTube（该地区主导视频平台）上跨国家、细粒度情绪结构的系统研究，也未考察同一外部行为者（美国）在不同国家语境下如何引发差异化的情感反应。

Method: 采用统一计算流水线：使用CAMeL-Lab BERT对阿拉伯语方言社交媒体文本进行三gram情感分类，并经机器翻译为英文后使用EmoRoBERTa进行28类GoEmotions情绪检测；通过长度归一化处理评论长度偏差；以一元方差分析（事后Welch t检验加Bonferroni校正）、Mann-Whitney U检验比较国家间及美国提及评论的情感差异；通过余弦相似度量化国家情绪画像相似性；用NER提取国别命名实体作为情感锚点。

Result: 五国情感均以负面为主（82%–85%），但情绪层面以关怀和钦佩（社群团结型情绪）为主导而非愤怒；五国28维情绪画像余弦相似度高达0.927–0.982，表明存在共享的区域情感语法；伊拉克的悲伤情绪为也门的5.4倍，是最大的国别差异；涉及美国的评论在也门、沙特、约旦显著更少负面，在伊拉克和叙利亚显著更为负面，方向反转与美国军事存在的直接/间接程度对应。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯YouTube政治话语构成一个共享的、历史层叠且受地缘条件约束的区域情感公众，而非五个分离的国别公众；情感基调追踪的是具体的、活生生的政治动因而非固定的身份类别；这意味着面向MENA地区的公共外交策略应因国而异，统一调性的区域传播会因忽略地方情感结构而失准。

Abstract: What is the emotional register of Arabic YouTube's affective publics? To investigate this, we analyzed 67,725 YouTube comments collected around socio-political topics associated with Yemen, Saudi Arabia, Iraq, Jordan, and Syria using a unified sentiment-and-emotion pipeline. Our results profile a single regional affective public rather than five separate national ones. Sentiment is overwhelmingly negative across all five country-oriented corpora, and the country-level emotion profiles are structurally similar. This shared register still accommodates some regional variations: discourse is organized around country-level political actors and cross-border historical trauma figures, and grief singularizes Iraq from the other countries. The differences in emotional register also tracks lived political causes rather than fixed categories, which we observe from patterns of the valence of US-related content, that tracks the presence or absence of direct US military engagement. Our work shows that the emotional register of Arabic YouTube's affective publics is a shared one that is historically layered and geographically conditioned, which has implications for public diplomacy in the MENA region.

</details>


### [88] [Multilingual Agent System for Inclusive Wildfire Evacuation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03301)
*Shruti Kulkarni,Lynn Tong,Aditi Namboodiripad,Chelyah Miller,Helen Lin,Peeyush Patel,Bogdan Bistriceanu,Diane Myung-kyung Woodbridge*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: BEACON是一个端到端智能体移动应用，通过多语言LLM代理、多边形规避路径规划、动态火灾危险评估和个性化清单，为英语能力有限的用户在野火疏散期间提供全面的撤离指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化使美国野火季延长了84天，而超过80%的紧急警报仅以英语发布，导致美国2570万英语能力有限人群面临信息不对称和疏散困难。现有商业GPS系统无法规避火场多边形，且清单缺乏个性化。

Method: 系统采用iOS (Swift) + Python FastAPI后端架构，集成Watch Duty火场数据、NOAA气象数据(HRRR)和Overpass POI;使用HWP评分(基于风速、湿度、土壤湿度等)进行危险评估，并利用XGBoost动态调度刷新频率;基于OpenRouteService实现多边形规避路径规划;LLM代理使用六块系统提示词(身份、位置、火场、路径状态、清单、语言规则)，结合Qdrant向量存储实现跨会话记忆，支持自动语言检测与切换。

Result: 在科罗拉多州2025年7-9月的九场火灾评估中，系统展现出:自动语言检测无需配置、真实火场多边形规避路径生成、首次对话即具备上下文感知能力,以及在路径不可达、途经点不可行、路由语言不支持、语义分类失败、跨会话记忆不可达、家庭信息缺失等六种故障场景下的优雅降级能力。

Conclusion: 初步实验验证了系统在多语言支持、安全路径规划和上下文感知对话方面的可靠性与有效性。未来工作将整合更多API、扩展Android平台，并增加语音转文本功能以提升驾驶场景的用户体验。

Abstract: Wildfire seasons have become 84 days longer in the current days than in the 1970s, causing enormous threats to one's financial status and short- and long-term health. During the fire, public agencies send out emergency messages to provide warnings and orders. Although 26 million people in the US have limited English proficiency, over 80% of those messages are only delivered in English, which can cause disproportionate information distribution and awareness. In order to better serve marginalized communities during emergencies, the authors developed BEACON, a service that provides comprehensive and personalized evacuation guidance, including navigation routes, personalized checklists, and a chatbot in the language that a user uses. Our current system ingests data including fire perimeter information, evacuation order status, and shelter information from Watch Duty. When a user is within a certain proximity from the fire, the system utilizes real-time GPS locations and nearby weather data from the National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) to predict fire danger levels. The assessment model refreshment are dynamically scheduled based on fire progress and trends using XGBoost. If the location has a likelihood of fire danger, the system sends alerts with evacuation routes outputted from a polygon-avoidant routing pipeline. The application provides a context-aware multilingual agent that users can communicate with and is tightly connected to other features of the application. In addition, based on data that the user entered, the system dynamically generates and checks off personalized reminder items to provide an organized evacuation plan. The system's user interface dynamically changes its language settings based on the language the user most recently used in either setting or chatbot conversation for all the application elements.

</details>


### [89] [The 5P Reflection Model for Education in the Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03413)
*Rajan Kadel,Samar Shailendra,Islam Mohammad Tahidul,Urvashi Rahul Saxena,Aakanksha Sharma,Sabitra Kaphle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"5P反思模型"（Purpose目的、Process过程、Product产品、Pitfalls陷阱、Plan计划）的教育反思框架，专门针对生成式人工智能（GenAI）时代的教育需求。该模型整合了传统反思理论（体验式、转化式、反思实践者、情感、自我调节学习）的核心特征，并融入了GenAI技术层面的考量，旨在解决学术诚信问题、确保学生反思的真实性与认知参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GenAI在高等教育中的快速普及，传统反思模型在验证学生反思真实性方面面临严峻挑战。GenAI带来的剽窃、过度依赖、虚假信息等风险使传统反思模式失效，学术界亟需一种GenAI感知的反思模型来应对这些新挑战，同时维护教学科研的学术诚信。

Method: 采用基于设计的研究方法（Design-Based Research, DBR），辅以批判性探究（Critical Inquiry）方法。研究分为两个阶段：(1) 问题识别与理论奠基——批判性审视传统反思模型在GenAI环境下的不足；(2) 模型设计与开发——综合体验式学习（Kolb、Gibbs、DIEP）、转化式学习（Mezirow）、反思实践者（Schön、Brookfield）、情感反思（Boud等）和自我调节学习（Zimmerman、Pintrich、Winne & Hadwin、Boekaerts）五大理论流派，构建集成反思框架。

Result: 提出的5P反思模型包含五个迭代阶段：Purpose（设定学习目标与GenAI使用意图）、Process（记录与GenAI的交互过程，包括提示词、迭代、验证）、Product（评估最终成果，区分人类与GenAI贡献）、Pitfalls（反思事实错误、伦理诚信、隐私安全等风险）、Plan（基于前四阶段制定未来改进计划）。模型将情感因素贯穿所有阶段，强调"过程重于产品"的理念，为评估反思质量提供了可观察、可评估的结构化路径。

Conclusion: 5P反思模型成功弥合了传统反思实践与GenAI辅助学习之间的鸿沟。该模型通过将GenAI定位为认知支架而非思维替代品，嵌入伦理风险反思阶段，并在整个循环中监测情感反应，有效解决了现有框架无法应对的GenAI特有挑战（如输出的概率性、虚假信息的诱惑）。模型适用于重大评估、顶点项目及专业发展评估，但实施需要教育者的明确指导和持续专业发展支持。

Abstract: Contributions: A reflection model suitable for the era of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is introduced. The proposed model is an integrated model that extracts features from various existing models and also incorporates technological aspects of GenAI. Background: Universities worldwide are facing challenges in adopting GenAI into their curricula, as it has impacted academic integrity and the scholarship of teaching and research. Traditional reflection models are struggling to authenticate student reflection as GenAI is incorporated in education. This requires a GenAI-aware model to enable the opportunities that address the associated challenges with GenAI. Research Questions: Does the academic ecosystem require a GenAI-aware reflection model to adopt GenAI into education? How to make a reflection model structured to ensure student authenticity and cognitive engagement within a GenAI-aware learning environment? Methodology: This study employs a design-based research methodology, assisted by a critical inquiry approach, to analyse existing reflection models in the era of GenAI and examine the technological aspects of GenAI. Additionally, it identifies a gap and the absence of a comprehensive reflection model that enhances reflection in the era of GenAI and supports the effective integration of GenAI in education. Findings: The literature survey indicates there is a greater need for reflection in the era of GenAI to ensure learning, but the existing reflection models lack proper strategies to manage the problem introduced by GenAI. A standard GenAI-aware reflection model, called 5P (Purpose, Process, Product, Pitfalls and Plan), is proposed i) to manage greater demand for reflection, ii) to address the limitations of the existing reflection models, iii) to consider technological aspects of GenAI.

</details>


### [90] [Open WebXR versus Commercial Game Engines: A Socio-Technical Position Analysis for an Open, Sustainable, and Interoperable Metaverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03666)
*Luca Turchet,Michel Buffa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文（Position Paper），从社会-技术视角对比分析了开放 WebXR 技术与商业游戏引擎（Unity、Unreal Engine 等）在元宇宙开发中的优劣，核心结论是二者并非互斥替代关系，而是处于一个根据应用场景权衡的连续光谱上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前元宇宙/XR 开发高度依赖商业游戏引擎和专有应用商店，形成了平台锁定、治理集中、可持续性差等问题；论文试图超越单纯的渲染性能或工程视角，从更广泛的社会-技术维度（治理、开放性、可持续性、伦理、机构部署等）探讨 WebXR 是否能为更开放、可持续、可互操作的元宇宙提供可行路径。

Method: 采用定性社会-技术比较分析方法，基于社会-技术系统理论，围绕硬件兼容性、软件架构、多模态交互、部署工作流、开发者可及性、经济因素、治理模型、伦理和可持续性等多个维度，对 WebXR 与商业 XR 生态进行系统化对比。证据来源包括：(i) W3C、Khronos 等官方标准与技术规范；(ii) WebXR、XR 开发、元宇宙领域的同行评审文献；(iii) 主要 XR 平台和引擎厂商的公开技术文档；(iv) 关于部署、分发、许可、生态治理的工业实践文献。

Result: 论文从多个维度形成了系统化对比（见 Table I 和 Table II），关键发现包括：(1) WebXR 的核心优势在于浏览器部署免安装、URL 即访问、跨设备兼容、长向后兼容性强；(2) 商业引擎在图形保真度、低延迟、平台 SDK 深度集成、专业触觉支持、成熟工具链方面仍占优；(3) 空间音频方面 WebXR 已相当成熟（Web Audio API + WebRTC），但触觉支持仍明显落后；(4) WebXR 在治理上更去中心化但缺乏应用商店式的安全审查机制，需要开发者自行承担隐私、内容审核等责任；(5) 在可持续性方面，WebXR 缓解了平台强制淘汰（如 Meta Quest 1 终止安全更新）带来的硬件过早报废问题；(6) 二者在多个维度上构成一个权衡连续体，混合架构（WebXR 作为接入层 + 原生引擎处理高性能模块）正在涌现。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是战略性的而非意识形态的：WebXR 与商业游戏引擎应被理解为在社会-技术权衡连续体上占据不同位置，而非互斥的替代方案。当可访问性、互操作性、低摩擦部署和长期可维护性是主要目标时，WebXR 更为可取（如教育、研究、文化遗产、社会 XR、快速原型）；当性能、平台特定硬件访问和工业化生产工具占主导时，原生引擎仍为首选。为实现可访问、伦理和可持续的元宇宙，未来的 XR 生态应同时支持两条路径，并强化开放标准、透明治理和长期互操作性。该研究存在局限：作为立场论文未进行受控基准测试，结论随浏览器实现、硬件能力和标准的快速演变而可能变化。

Abstract: The Metaverse is often framed as a persistent, interoperable, and embodied network of virtual and augmented environments. Yet, most contemporary XR applications are developed through commercial game engines and distributed through proprietary app stores, creating tensions between openness and platform dependency. This paper critically examines open WebXR technologies with conventional commercial game-engine pipelines, with particular attention to XR hardware, software architectures, developer workflows, governance, ethics, and sustainability. We argue that WebXR may provide a viable route toward a more accessible, device-independent, and institutionally sustainable Metaverse, especially for education, research, cultural heritage, prototyping, and public-interest applications. At the same time, commercial engines remain advantageous for graphically intensive, low-latency, deeply integrated, and large-scale XR products. The paper concludes that the choice should not be framed as WebXR versus engines, but as a continuum: WebXR is preferable when accessibility, interoperability, low-friction deployment, and long-term maintainability are primary goals, whereas native engines remain preferable when performance, platform-specific hardware access, and production-grade tooling dominate.

</details>


### [91] [Bridging Formal and Perceived Fairness: Development of an Interdisciplinary Framework in Algorithmic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03853)
*Maike Lindermayr,Mattia Cerrato,Luisa Hübner,Johannes Kraus*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个工作进行中的概念框架，旨在弥合计算机科学中算法形式公平性与社会科学中关于感知公平性、信任和技术接纳之间的差距，强调公平性本质上是受认知启发式、社会规范和情境因素影响的用户主观判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法公平性研究过度侧重形式化公平度量与偏见缓解技术，忽视了一个核心问题：公平性不仅是技术属性，更是一种受认知启发式、心理模型、规范期望和社会技术因素塑造的主观人类判断。用户对公平性的感知可能与算法形式上满足的公平标准存在显著分歧，导致系统在形式合规但感知不公平时无法获得信任、接受度与合法性。

Method: 采用用户中心设计视角，提出工作进行中的概念性框架，桥接算法形式公平性与社会科学的感知公平性、信任及技术接受理论。研究方法包含三个组成部分：(1)理论文献综合；(2)跨学科工作坊；(3)利益相关者访谈。

Result: 该研究旨在告知将计算公平性审计与用户中心评估相结合的评估方法，并指导公平感知、以人为本的算法系统设计，以支持受决策影响的利益相关者做出知情的、良好校准的公平性判断。

Conclusion: 需要将形式公平度量与用户感知公平性整合在同一社会技术框架中，算法公平性研究应从单纯的技术合规转向兼顾人类主观判断的用户中心方法，才能真正实现被信任、被接受和被视为合法的算法系统。

Abstract: While fairness has become a central concern in research on algorithmic systems, the field remains predominantly shaped by Computer Science, resulting in a strong emphasis on formal fairness metrics and bias mitigation strategies. Nevertheless, this focus may obscure a fundamental challenge: fairness is not merely a technical property, but a subjective, context-sensitive human judgment shaped by cognitive heuristics, mental models, normative expectations, and sociotechnical factors. Crucially, users' perceptions of fairness may diverge substantially from the fairness criteria an algorithm formally satisfies; a system may meet predefined technical fairness requirements yet still be perceived as unjust by decision-affected stakeholders. In such cases, the system fails on a fundamental dimension: it will not be trusted, accepted, or considered legitimate. Taking a user-centered design perspective, this paper presents a work-in-progress conceptual framework that bridges Computer Science approaches to formal algorithmic fairness with normative and Social Science fairness approaches regarding perceived fairness, trust, and technology acceptance, embedding both within the sociotechnical conditions that shape human judgment. Through (1) theoretical literature synthesis, (2) interdisciplinary workshops, and (3) stakeholder interviews, the project aims to inform evaluation approaches that integrate computational fairness audits with user-centered assessments and guide the design of fairness-aware, human-centered algorithmic systems that support informed, well-calibrated fairness judgments by those affected.

</details>


### [92] [Making Gender-Inclusive Practices Actionable: Evaluating a Research-Informed Computing Education Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03936)
*Alina Berry,Susan McKeever,Brenda Murphy,Sarah Jane Delany*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍并评估了TechMate——一个面向计算教育者的、基于研究证据的性别包容性实践网络工具包。该工具包包含25+项行动，涵盖政策、教学、影响与支持、推广与参与四大类。18名爱尔兰大学的计算教育者通过think-aloud任务和半结构化访谈，从有用性（新颖性、相关性、可信度、可操作性）和可用性（有效性、美学、导航、术语、用户满意度）九个维度进行评估。混合方法分析（演绎编码、定量情感评分、归纳主题分析）显示，超过60%的参与者在所有属性上给予积极评价，用户满意度尤为突出。同时，主题分析揭示了女性学生孤立感、机构变革阻力、缺乏女性榜样等结构性障碍，以及学生大使、支持性学习环境等潜在解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算领域持续存在的性别失衡是一个全球性问题。已有大量学术研究识别了支持弱势性别群体的有效干预措施，但将这些分散的研究证据转化为教育者可操作的实践指导仍存在显著的研究-实践鸿沟。现有工具包和框架要么过于高层级、缺乏具体实施细节，要么局限于特定机构背景、缺少与研究证据的直接关联，且针对此类数字资源的系统性可用性评估极为有限。本文旨在通过设计和评估TechMate工具包，弥合研究证据与教育者实践之间的差距。

Method: 本研究采用混合方法评估设计。首先基于技术接受模型（TAM）、ISO 9241-11可用性标准及信息质量框架，构建了九个评估维度：有用性四个属性（新颖性、相关性、可信度、可操作性）和可用性五个属性（有效性、美学、导航、术语、用户满意度）。通过INGENIC网络招募18名来自爱尔兰八所公立大学的计算教育者（11女7男），采用think-aloud技术和半结构化访谈收集数据，参与者完成两项代表性任务。分析阶段，先提取意义单元（meaningful units），由三位独立编码者使用五点Likert量表进行情感评分（ICC=0.814，表明评分者间信度极佳），同时采用演绎编码对应预设属性，归纳主题分析识别涌现主题，共产生1027条记录。

Result: 定量评分方面，所有属性平均得分在3.8-4.5之间，全部参与者均给出正面或中性评价。有用性方面：可操作性（83%正面）、新颖性和相关性（≥78%正面）、可信度（72%正面）。可用性方面：用户满意度最高（4.5分，95%正面），有效性和导航（67%正面），美学和术语评分相对较低（61%-66%正面）。主题分析识别出四个主题：（1）结构性/文化障碍——包括实施评估挑战、女性领导者匮乏、学生孤立感；（2）工具包改进建议——设计改进和内容改进；（3）应对性别失衡的策略——外展、导师制、大使计划等；（4）改进教与学的方法。研究表明，参与者识别的影响最大倡议集中在影响与支持机制类别（如伙伴系统、客座讲师作为榜样）。

Conclusion: TechMate作为开源数字工具，有效降低了循证实践的门槛，在有用性和可用性方面获得高度评价，表明研究驱动的工具可以面向真实世界使用。其设计支持即时可访问性和实施准备。然而，研究也揭示了此类技术工具的局限性：机构文化、工作量负担和认可机制深刻影响着工具包潜力能否真正实现。本研究的局限包括样本量小且局限于爱尔兰单一国家、参与者可能已对性别包容有较高动机（存在社会期望偏差）、评估仅捕获初始印象而非长期效果。未来工作包括纵向追踪采用情况、社区贡献机制开发、以及评估下游招生和保留影响。该研究对教育与社会公益领域的开放获取数字工具的设计和治理具有广泛启示意义。

Abstract: The persistent gender imbalance in computing remains a global concern, and universities offer a key part of the pipeline to address it. Although research has identified practices that support under-represented student groups, translating this evidence into actionable guidance remains challenging. This paper first presents a novel web- based toolkit (TechMate) designed to address this gap by helping computing educators implement gender- inclusive initiatives through practical research-informed guidance. The toolkit defines over 25 actions, ranging from operational to strategic, and provides case studies and implementation resources. Second, this work reports on the evaluation of TechMate, capturing educators first impressions of its usefulness and usability through authentic tasks, and eliciting unanticipated insights about structural barriers to gender-inclusive practice in computing higher education. Eighteen computing educators of varying seniority from eight universities in Ireland assessed the toolkit using nine research-driven attributes: four usefulness measures (novelty, relevance, trustworthiness, actionability) and five usability measures (effectiveness, aesthetics, navigation, terminology, user satisfaction). Data was analysed using mixed methods: deductive coding, quantitative sentiment scoring, and inductive thematic analysis. Educators rated TechMate highly for user satisfaction and praised its novelty and trustworthiness, with over 60% of participants reporting positively on all attributes, suggesting that research -informed tools can be designed for real-world use. Thematic analysis highlighted other challenges, including women students isolation, institutional resistance to change, and the scarcity of women role models, while also surfacing proposed solutions such as leveraging student ambassadors and fostering supportive learning environments.

</details>


### [93] [Shifting from Injection to Interaction: Rethinking Web Security in the Age of LLMs and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03999)
*Nivedita Singh,Alsharif Abuadbba,Yansong Gao,Surya Nepal,Hyoungshick Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统综述（Survey），核心论点是将Web安全的视角从"注入（Injection）"转向"交互（Interaction）"。LLM集成到Web应用和浏览器代理中后，传统Web漏洞（如XSS、CSRF）被LLM中介的语义交互所放大，同时引入了提示注入等新威胁。作者提出OWASP LLM Top 10与传统Web威胁参考架构（TRA）和CWE的统一映射，分析LLM如何在客户端、服务端和管道层放大Web漏洞，并提出一个LLM感知的监控与控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全研究大多将LLM视为独立的攻击面（isolated attack surface），或仅关注模型层/智能体层，忽略了LLM作为中介如何跨客户端-服务器边界传播和放大传统Web漏洞。同时，传统Web安全框架（OWASP、CWE）和AI安全框架（NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC）在面对LLM带来的语义级、交互式攻击时存在显著不足。因此需要一个统一的、全栈的分析视角。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：(1) Stage I——基于OWASP LLM Top 10（2025）建立映射，通过四个标准（入口点、处理机制、安全边界违反、影响）将每个LLM风险映射到传统威胁参考架构（TRA）和CWE条目；(2) Stage II——按照Kitchenham指南进行系统文献综述（SLR），在Scopus、IEEE Xplore、ScienceDirect、SpringerLink和Google Scholar中检索，最终通过前向/后向滚雪球法确定105篇核心研究文献进行编码分析。

Result: 1. 建立了OWASP LLM Top 10与TRA/CWE的完整映射表（Table 2 & Table 4），涵盖10类LLM风险如何对应传统Web漏洞；2. 揭示LLM放大Web漏洞的核心机制——从基于语法的利用转向基于语义的利用（shift from syntactic to semantic exploitation），例如提示注入将指令注入提升到语义层、输出处理不当将模型文本转化为下游XSS/SQLi、过度代理将提示操纵转化为类CSRF特权操作、嵌入弱点将侧信道推断转化为表示层泄露；3. 引用真实CVE证据（EchoLeak/CVE-2025-32711等）证明这些威胁已现实化；4. 识别三大未解决挑战：对抗性自然语言指令、自主代理安全、部署后持续监控；5. 提出一个LLM感知的监控-控制框架，将NIST监控维度与五层LLM特定控制（语义输入验证、提示完整性保护、输出隔离、代理治理、运行时监控）相结合。

Conclusion: LLM从根本上重塑了Web安全威胁格局——LLM中介的交互弱化了数据-指令、信任和执行边界，创建了一个动态的、交互驱动的攻击面。现有的OWASP、CWE、NIST AI RMF和ISO/IEC框架在面对语义级操纵、代理行为和部署后行为漂移时存在系统性不足，亟需扩展以纳入LLM感知的安全控制。本文提出的统一分析框架和监控-控制方案为未来LLM赋能的Web系统安全研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming integral to web applications and browser agents, transforming online interactions while introducing new attack vectors and reshaping longstanding web vulnerabilities. Classical threats such as cross-site scripting (XSS) can be amplified through LLM-mediated interactions, while LLM-specific vulnerabilities can propagate across web applications, introducing attacks such as prompt injection. Securing modern web systems therefore requires understanding interactions between traditional and LLM-specific threats across the system lifecycle. Unlike prior surveys treating web and LLM security separately, this survey provides a unified analysis of how LLMs amplify web vulnerabilities across client-side, server-side, and pipeline layers while evaluating defenses and their limitations. The analysis examines extending NIST and ISO/IEC AI security frameworks to the security needs of LLM-enabled web environments. Three unresolved challenges are identified: adversarial natural-language instructions, autonomous agent security, and post-deployment security through continuous monitoring and adaptation. An LLM-aware monitoring and control framework is proposed, integrating semantic input validation, prompt integrity protection, output isolation, agent governance, and runtime monitoring. This unified perspective characterizes the evolving threat landscape and outlines future directions for secure AI-enabled web systems.

</details>


### [94] [Decreasing Digital Distraction in College Students: Associated Online Learning Strategies Identified by Unsupervised Data Mining Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04125)
*Hui Shi,Ran Bi,Xi Lin,Yan Dai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究运用无监督数据挖掘方法（关联规则挖掘和聚类分析），从530名大学生数据中识别与较低数字干扰水平相关的有效在线学习策略，发现自我调节学习策略（目标设定、环境构建、时间管理）以及师生/学生与内容互动策略、技术能力与低干扰最常共现，而同伴求助与生生互动策略则较少出现于低干扰画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习中数字干扰普遍存在并损害学业表现，但何种学习策略能有效降低干扰尚不明确，亟需数据驱动的实证识别。

Method: 采用无监督数据挖掘技术——关联规则挖掘（association rule mining）与聚类分析（clustering analysis），对530名大学生的问卷数据进行探索，挖掘与低数字干扰水平共现的学习行为模式。

Result: 结果显示：（1）自我调节学习策略（目标设定、环境构建、时间管理）与低数字干扰共现最为一致；（2）师生互动、生-内容互动策略及技术能力也倾向于出现在低干扰画像中；（3）同伴求助依赖与生生互动策略在低干扰画像中出现频率较低。

Conclusion: 研究为教育者设计针对性干预措施以营造专注、高效的在线学习环境提供了可操作的启示，强调应优先培养学生的自我调节学习能力。

Abstract: The proliferation of digital tools in education offers numerous benefits but also introduces significant challenges, notably digital distractions that hinder academic performance, especially in online learning contexts. This study employed unsupervised data mining techniques, specifically association rule mining and clustering analysis, to identify effective learning strategies associated with lower levels of digital distractions among college students. Data from 530 participants revealed that self-regulated learning strategies (i.e., goal setting, environment structuring, and time management) co-occurred most consistently with lower digital distractions. Additionally, learner-instructor and learner-content engagement strategies, as well as technical competencies, also tended to appear in the same profiles as lower distraction. Interestingly, reliance on peer help-seeking and learner-learner engagement strategies appeared less often in those lower distraction profiles. These findings offer actionable implications for educators to design targeted interventions that foster focused and productive online learning environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [95] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J.B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型的非嵌入矩阵(unembedding matrix)中天然存在一个单一方向，能够以极低的KL散度重建训练语料的unigram分布，作者称之为"无知方向"(direction of ignorance)。将该方向从最终隐藏状态中分离出来，可得到一个标量化的"先验加载因子"λ，它具有精确的统计学解释——作为tempered Bayesian推理中unigram先验的指数。实验表明λ随上下文信息量增加而下降，且操控λ可因果性地引导预测走向或偏离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究观察到LLM在不确定时会回退到unigram先验（训练语料的token频率分布），但其内部机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示LLM如何在非嵌入几何中编码和控制这一先验。

Method: 核心方法：对非嵌入矩阵W的行（即token的输出嵌入向量）与log unigram概率做线性最小二乘拟合，恢复出唯一方向d_prior（"无知方向"）。然后将最终预测状态y沿d_prior正交分解，得到先验加载因子λ = ⟨d̂_prior, y⟩/β。由此推导出预测分布的精确分解形式：p_θ(w|context) ∝ p_prior(w)^λ · L_context(w)，这正对应tempered Bayesian推理。

Result: 1) 在四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，0.4B–405B参数）中均发现该单一方向，归一化KL散度极低（nKL ≤ 0.083，R² ≥ 0.749），且在训练前不存在、训练早期即出现。2) λ随token位置单调下降并趋于模型特有的平稳值；上下文若被打乱则λ不下降。3) 模型越大，高上下文极限下|λ|一般越小，Llama-3.1-70B甚至出现λ<0（自动抑制高频token）。4) 对λ的人为干预可单调改变预测与unigram先验的KL距离，而等幅度的随机方向扰动无此效果，证明该方向具有因果活性。5) 进一步观察到≥8B的Llama模型中，全词token与子词token可能对应两个不同的先验方向，暗示"先验层次"的存在。

Conclusion: LLM通过一个非嵌入方向简洁地编码语料unigram先验，并用一个输入相关的标量λ动态调节对该先验的依赖。该几何-概率对应赋予λ精确的tempered Bayesian含义，使其可跨模型规模与族系比较。基于此框架，本文提出若干应用方向：在模型内部控制先验（优于外部prior subtraction方法）、检测幻觉、监控训练进程，以及将LLM理解为词汇多粒度的先验层次结构。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $\lambda$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $\lambda$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $\lambda$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $\lambda$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [96] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子航海图(ENC)数据表示为图结构，利用图神经网络(GNN)对ENC变更进行关键性分类，验证了图表示相比传统方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENC维护面临挑战，人工判定变更是否对航行安全构成关键风险耗时费力，需要自动化方法辅助海事专家审查。传统方法难以建模ENC数据中丰富的空间拓扑和语义关系。

Method: 将ENC数据集建模为图对分类问题：空间对象作为节点，融合S-57标准语义边（master-slave等4类）和空间拓扑边（overlaps、touches、equals等3类）构成统一边集；采用Siamese架构，分别用共享权重的GNN编码器处理新旧ENC图，通过差值表示捕获变更，结合全局元特征进行二分类；系统比较GCN、GAT、GraphSAGE、TransformerConv四种架构在不同深度（3/4/5层）和有无残差连接下的性能。

Result: 在9281条专家标注的ENC变更数据上，GraphSAGE表现最优，准确率达0.8766，宏F1达0.8632，显著优于MLP基线（准确率0.8080）和其他GNN变体；GCN和GAT在加入残差连接后性能显著提升（如GCN 5层宏F1从0.7975提升至0.8421），而GraphSAGE对残差不敏感甚至略有下降。

Conclusion: 图结构能有效提升ENC变更关键性分类性能，GraphSAGE的归纳式邻域聚合策略与ENC空间语义契合度最高；残差连接对不同架构影响不同，验证了空间和关系上下文对判定变更关键性的重要性，为ENC维护工作流自动化提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [97] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation（TGOPD），通过在 prompt 级别用少量 verifier-scored 的教师采样来审计教师可靠性，仅当审计通过时使用密集的 reverse-KL 监督（OPD），否则回退到 verifier-grounded GRPO。TGOPD 在数学、代码、指令跟随三个领域的 4B 和 35B 学生模型上均优于 Vanilla OPD，并将原本闲置的教师 GPU 利用率从约 10% 提升至最高 82.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPD 对每个 prompt 无差别地施加教师的密集 token 级监督；但 reverse-KL 是 mode-seeking 的，当教师在某些 prompt 上自信地出错时，会通过密集梯度被错误地放大传播。已有方法使用熵或师生似然一致性等分布代理，但它们衡量不确定性或一致性，并不直接验证结果的正确性。此外，异步 OPD 中教师节点因只能在前向评分时工作，平均 GPU 利用率仅约 10%，造成大量算力浪费。

Method: 核心思想是"先验证后蒸馏"。TGOPD 让冻结教师在每个 prompt 上额外生成 K_T=3 个 probe rollout，由领域 verifier 评分，得到无偏的可靠性估计 q_T(x)。然后用硬门控 g(x)=𝟙[q_T(x)≥τ]（τ=2/3，即多数投票）将监督信号二选一：开门时使用密集 OPD 优势（教师-学生对数似然比），关门时使用 verifier-grounded GRPO 优势。该门控作用于 prompt 级别，两种信号互斥而非混合。Probe 的生成与学生 rollout 阶段并行执行，主要占用原本闲置的教师算力，因此不显著增加端到端时延。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 两个规模上，于数学（AIME 2025/2026、HMMT-Feb）、代码（LiveCodeBench、OJBench）、指令跟随（IFBench、IFEval）三个领域的六组单域对比中，TGOPD 在所有六组上均优于 Vanilla OPD；代码领域增益最大（4B +3.0、35B +2.9 平均）。多域（MOPD）训练下，4B 七基准平均由 53.40 升至 54.54，35B 由 60.99 升至 61.94。教师节点 GPU 利用率从 5–10% 提升至 57.7–82.8%，集群平均利用率提升 +8.6 至 +18.0 点；35B 代码运行步均时长仅增加 5.9%。门控阈值扫描呈倒 U 型，τ=3/5（多数投票）附近为最优。消融实验表明：屏蔽不可靠教师信号（mask）即可获得约 90% 的增益，加入 GRPO 回退带来少量额外收益。

Conclusion: TGOPD 通过 prompt 级 verifier-grounded 门控解决了 OPD 中教师自信出错被放大的问题，在不削弱大多数 prompt 上 OPD 信号完整性的前提下选择性地抑制不可靠的密集监督。同时利用异步 OPD 中空闲的教师算力进行可靠性审计，将教师节点利用率从约 10% 提升至接近 80%。未来工作方向：将结果可靠性估计扩展到开放式任务，以及采用不确定性感知（非二值）门控。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [98] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench是一个评估机制可解释性方法的基准框架，测试内部估计（观察者）是否足以指导干预、控制或安全任务。它将估计精度与下游动作损失分开报告，证明更好的平均预测并不总能带来更好的决策选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性方法越来越多地用于指导模型干预（如激活引导、电路移除、安全监控）。然而，平均准确的内部估计仍可能导致错误的动作决策。现有基准主要衡量预测精度，但预测精度并不等同于决策质量。作者旨在建立一个评估框架，将估计器与决策问题分离，明确判断内部信号是否足以支撑特定干预或安全策略。

Method: 框架包含TaskCard、ObserverCard和结果记分卡。每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、保留测试案例和损失。三种操作模式：(1) 闭环控制——评估观察者在反馈回路中是否充分（基于可达子空间充分性定理）；(2) 效应预测——评估观察者在固定候选干预中选择最佳动作的能力；(3) 安全分类——在固定干预预算下评估观察者的风险排序。实验包括：小规模Transformer控制验证（证明控制证书在非线性后缀下仍有效）；GPT-2-small IOI电路干预；Qwen2.5-7B复制任务；授权安全互锁；APPS后门审计（跨Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it、Qwen3.5-9B）。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 在闭环控制中，观察者只需在可达子空间上与目标匹配，收敛的观察者仍可能错过目标；(2) 在GPT-2和Qwen上，交互项改善平均效应预测，但更好的平均预测器并不总能选择更好的动作；直接损失训练在GPT-2上减少18.5%动作损失，在Qwen上减少8.9%；(3) 在安全分类中，AUROC为1.000的完美分类器在后果加权下分配预算不佳；AUROC排名与部署损失排名不同（即使在同系列Qwen检查点间）；(4) 稀疏SAE读出在某些面板上不优于层匹配密集控制（压缩未必带来更好决策）。最佳信息源因模型而异：Qwen2.5上中性激活最佳，Qwen3.5上判断邻近激活最佳。

Conclusion: ObserverBench将解释性方法的评估从"预测有多准确"转向"该估计能否支撑所选动作"。结论强调：观察者的充分性是任务相关的——它必须保留可用动作、决策规则和损失实际使用的信息。框架提供版本化任务契约、可运行基线和基于表格的提交，为通过其启用的动作评估可解释性方法提供统一方式。局限包括范围限于特定模型/任务、预测冻结而非独立预注册、不测试适应性攻击。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action. We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action. Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches. ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [99] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowBalance，一种基于验证器引导的策略自改进方法。它将推理模型的在线策略经验、稀疏的验证器奖励与稠密的"特权后视"自引导信号相结合，通过符号门控（sign gating）机制和轨迹平衡（trajectory balance）目标，学习完整响应上的归一化分布。该方法在Qwen3-4B/8B数学推理任务上优于GRPO、OPSD、RLSD和FlowRL基线，并展现出更高的策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）仅提供稀疏的终局反馈，无法利用推理路径中的细粒度证据；而密集的同模型自引导信号虽信息丰富，却可能强化错误自信（self-confirmation）、缩短推理链或导致策略模式坍缩。本文旨在解决"在稀疏验证器奖励与密集但有缺陷的自引导之间，应学习怎样的归一化响应分布"这一分布式自改进问题。

Method: FlowBalance为每个在线策略采样组定义一个参考支撑的Gibbs目标分布：(1) 冻结的训练时视图（π_H）以特权上下文c评估已采样token，聚合为轨迹级自引导分数G_H；(2) 验证器给出停止的分组相对优势A_i，决定G_H的方向：正优势时保留+β_G·G_H，负优势时取反-β_G·G_H，零优势时禁用稠密分支；(3) 组合能量E = η_A·A + β_G·G_H·sgn(A)对参考策略进行指数重加权；(4) 通过剖析的轨迹平衡残差（每组一个log-分配函数估计）拟合归一化目标，保留所有N-1个组内对比方向。无需额外的token级模仿损失。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上五基准平均得分64.26，相对GRPO提升1.95、OPSD提升10.14、RLSD提升4.71、FlowRL提升1.04；在Qwen3-8B上得分67.61，每项基准均为最优，相对FlowRL提升1.76。训练效率方面，约100步即达到0.5 AIME24验证精度（GRPO需约143步），400步内保持稳定（GRPO在180步后退化）。响应长度未出现OPSD式的坍缩。AIME24诊断中正确轨迹的Simpson策略多样性为0.2194，显著高于GRPO（0.1017）和RLSD（0.1456）。案例分析显示FlowBalance发现了隐藏的4×5×8长方体嵌入等异质解法。

Conclusion: FlowBalance将策略自改进建模为关于模型自身在线策略经验完整响应上的分布学习问题。其理论贡献包括：组内对比保留剖析、最小反向KL位移表征、验证器对目标奖励的单调控制、以及对拒绝响应上假阳性自引导的精确校正。实验证明该方法在数学推理任务上同时实现了更高的准确性、训练稳定性、加速收敛以及更优的策略多样性。但局限在于实验聚焦数学推理、响应长度与准确性的因果关系尚未确立、多样性分析为单checkpoint单种子的LLM判断诊断、未包含外层任务生成循环。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group. This realizes outcome-calibrated self-guidance via trajectory balance, without a separate token-level imitation loss. Our analysis establishes within-group contrast preservation, a minimum-change reverse-KL characterization, monotonic verifier control of target reward, and an exact correction against false-positive self-guidance on rejected responses. On mathematical reasoning, FlowBalance improves average performance over FlowRL on both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, while also improving training speed and stability, avoiding direct OPSD's response-length collapse, and exhibiting higher correct-strategy diversity in a controlled AIME24 diagnostic.

</details>


### [100] [TraveL: Transformer-based Multi-view Path Distributional Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03427)
*Fang He,Tao-yang Fu,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TraveL的Transformer框架，用于道路网络的路径表征学习（PRL），通过学习路径的分布式表示（高斯分布）来捕捉多样化的出行者行为和路段间的区域相关性，在三个下游任务上均优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径表征学习方法主要利用路段间的共现关系来学习路径的向量表示，未能捕捉到出行者的多样化行为以及路径中不同区域路段之间的相关性，导致难以准确建模路径上的旅行时间波动等行为信息。

Method: TraveL框架采用编码器-解码器结构：1）路径编码器首先通过Road2Vec预训练路段嵌入，结合出发时间嵌入和位置嵌入输入多层多视图路径Transformer（含区域注意力机制，涵盖高速公路视图、车道视图和跳跃视图），生成路径的分布式表示（高斯分布）；2）通过采样方式从分布式表示中采样多个点，每个点输入LSTM生成对应的On-path Sequence（OP-Seq），同时预测下一路段；3）采用Kolmogorov-Smirnov（K-S）距离作为损失函数度量生成与真实旅行时间分布的差异。

Result: 在合成数据集Syn-Porto和真实数据集Porto、Tokyo上的实验表明，TraveL在三个下游任务上均优于基线方法：旅行时间分布估计的Mean K-S距离减少14.7%，路径相似性预测的MAE减少16.7%，目的地预测的MAE减少3.97%。消融实验验证了分布式表示和多视图区域注意力机制各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: TraveL通过引入分布式表示和区域注意力机制，有效捕捉了路径上的多样化出行者行为和区域相关性，在路径表征学习任务上取得了显著性能提升。未来工作将探索其他形式的分布式表示以建模更丰富的出行者行为。

Abstract: Path representation learning (PRL) for road networks has received increasing research attention, due to various path-related applications. Existing works on PRL typically exploit the co-occurrence relationship among road segments and paths to learn a vector as the path representation, without exploring the varied traveler behaviors and the regional correlation on the path. In this work, we propose to learn distributional representations, which provide valuable information for use in path-related applications, by capturing the varied traveler behaviors as well as the various dependencies within regions of road segments. We propose a novel Transformer-based Multi-view Distributional Representation Learning (TraveL) framework to encode a path along with a travel starting time to a distributional representation, which can be used to decode possible samples of on-path traveler behavior. Moreover, by analyzing the regional correlation which reveals various road segment relationships, we propose a regional attention to encode these correlations in a path. Also, we explore the idea of Kolmogorov-Smirnov (K-S) test to compare the sampled traveler behavior against the collected ground truth to facilitate training. Experimental results show that the proposed TraveL model outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets, by 14.7% in Mean K-S distance for travel time distribution estimation, 16.7% in Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for path similarity prediction, and 3.97% in MAE for destination prediction.

</details>


### [101] [It's the Problem, Not the Path: Budget and Difficulty Confounds in LLM Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03436)
*Yigit Utku Bulut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LLM推理轨迹研究中两个广泛流传的信念——"突破时刻"和"早期可预测性"——提出了反事实控制挑战。研究发现：(1) 在178个problem-model单元中，仅1个单元存在真正的"前缀受限"突破，大多数看似突破实为预算适配；(2) 早期内部信号中除问题难度外无可检测的尝试结果信息，公开数据中无需读取推理轨迹的难度代理即可达到与已发表探针相当的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流行的信念——推理轨迹包含"突破时刻"（aha moments）以及运行结果可从早期信号预测——均基于缺失适当反事实控制的测量。"突破"声称依赖于截断-重采样探针，将"前缀积累的价值"与"剩余工作开始适配预算"混为一谈；"可预测性"声称依赖于跨问题的探针评估，探针可能通过读取问题难度而非尝试进展来获得高分。本研究旨在提供这两个缺失的控制并揭示它们如何改变结论。

Method: 方法包含两大核心组件：(1) 重启控制的截断探针——在匹配的总生成token预算下，比较每锚点续写求解率与从零重启的求解曲线，将朴素突破时间拆分为"预算适配时间"T_F和"前缀价值时间"T_V，应用于89个MATH问题×2个小型开放模型的178个单元；(2) 难度控制的早期信号测试——预注册的单次测试，使用问题专用基线（仅含问题难度信息）与添加早期窗口动态特征进行比较；同时在公开数据（192K DeepSeek-R1生成、128个MATH问题×256个R1-Distill-7B样本）上进行分析。所有决策规则在观察结果前通过书面承诺修正冻结。

Result: 核心结果：(1) 178个单元中仅1个为前缀受限，9/9匹配预算比较中续写自身前缀优于重启；Ministral-3为"算力饥饿型"（0%→79%随预算上升），Gemma-4为"能力受限型"（10-12%持平）；约1/3模糊单元在8次尝试后仍处中间状态（成功率0.3-0.6）；(2) 确认性测试：主要端点基线AUROC 0.83 vs +早期信号0.85，Δ=+0.026 [-0.054,+0.167]，CI包含零；公开数据中轨迹盲难度代理在192K R1生成上达到AUROC 0.873，落在已发表探针范围(0.79-0.95)内；重建的已发表早期探针在t=4时合并AUROC 0.849，但问题内AUROC在所有10个锚点处与随机无显著差异(0.496)；事后分析显示跨问题的弱可迁移信号在t≈32才出现，集中于3个低失败率问题。

Conclusion: 结论：(1) "突破"主要是预算假象——在小型推理模型中，真正的前缀锁定价值罕见（1/178），长推理主要是计算压缩而非扩展可达性；(2) 合并的探针AUROC应被视为难度测量，除非问题内评估或仅问题基线显示另有结论；(3) 预注册+反事实控制的轨迹级推理主张应成为标准；(4) 局限性：仅两个小型4-bit模型、一个基准族、有限尝试次数，不声明内部状态永远不携带尝试内信息——该结论限于所测量范围。本研究确立了一个新准则：推理轨迹的突破性与早期可预测性主张需要重启控制和难度基线作为必要条件。

Abstract: Reasoning traces of large language models are widely read as containing "breakthrough" moments and early-legible fates. Both readings rest on measurements missing a counterfactual control at the level of the claim; we supply both controls. First, a restart-controlled truncation probe separates when a solution fits the continuation budget from when a prefix carries value that fresh computation cannot buy, comparing per-anchor continuation solve rates against from-scratch restart curves at matched total generated-token budget. Applied to 178 problem-model cells (89 MATH problems x two small open models, an outcome-blind but difficulty-targeted cohort), exactly 1 of 178 cells survives as prefix-limited; restart dose-response separates a compute-starved model from a capability-limited one; and wherever the matched budget lies inside the restart grid, continuing the model's own prefix beats restarting (9 of 9) -- predominantly compute compression rather than expanded reachability. Second, a pre-registered, difficulty-controlled test finds no detectable outcome information in early-window internal signals beyond a problem-difficulty baseline, and two generation-free analyses of public corpora show why this control is needed: a trace-blind difficulty proxy reaches AUROC 0.873 on 192K DeepSeek-R1 generations -- inside the published probe range -- and a closely matched reconstruction of the closest published early-window positive recovers a comparable pooled result (0.849) while within problem it is statistically indistinguishable from chance at all ten anchors (0.496 at t=4); a post-hoc within-targeted probe finds only a small average residual, concentrated in three low-failure problems. High pooled probe AUROCs cannot by themselves establish within-attempt information; a question-only baseline or within-problem evaluation is required.

</details>


### [102] [LongCounsel-8: A Benchmark Suite for Longitudinal Depression Tracking from Multi-Session Counseling Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03507)
*Jiayi Li,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongCounsel-8 是一个面向纵向抑郁追踪的基准套件，包含 7,749 条五轮心理咨询对话轨迹（共 38,745 个会话），由三个独立生成的数据集组成。该工作解决了多会话咨询对话中缺乏会话级抑郁标签的难题，通过结合档案驱动模拟、经验状态构建和间接行为实现，生成带有 PHQ-8 会话级标签的合成数据，并验证了标签保真度、语言合理性及基准完整性。实验揭示现有方法在变化方向识别、恶化轨迹可靠性、历史会话利用等方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向抑郁追踪需要估计当前症状严重程度及其跨会话变化，但缺乏带标准化会话级抑郁标签的多会话咨询数据。现有资源要么提供无标签的多会话对话，要么仅提供单次访谈的标签，制约了该任务的研究进展。

Method: 采用三项核心技术应对三大挑战：(1) 基于档案的纵向模拟——利用 PsychEval 案例档案定义客户背景和会话计划，RealCBT 统计引导对话形式；(2) 经验驱动的状态构建——PSYCHE-D 提供五次访问的抑郁轨迹，NHANES 提供项目级症状组成，PHQ-8 八项问卷定义标准化症状状态；(3) 间接行为实现与状态恢复——将症状分数转化为行为线索引导客户表达，会话后自报恢复受控状态。三套数据集分别由 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B、GPT-5.6 Luna 和 GPT-5.4-mini 生成。

Result: 数据集验证显示：自报标签与受控状态高度一致（大变化方向一致性 99.96%–100%，轨迹类型一致性 92.1%–95.4%），语言距离更接近真实咨询数据 RealCBT。方法评估揭示三个关键发现：(1) 较低的单会话分数误差不保证准确识别改善或恶化趋势；(2) 现有方法在恶化轨迹上可靠性持续较低；(3) 增加历史会话可能降低变化方向预测准确率。变化 MAE 与方向准确率之间存在显著的排名反转，表明二者捕捉不同能力。

Conclusion: LongCounsel-8 将标签化的单次访谈与多会话咨询语料相结合，为纵向抑郁追踪提供了受控基础。基准套件支持方向感知、恶化敏感建模以及选择和推理纵向上下文的方法开发，推动抑郁评估从静态单次预测向可靠的纵向心理健康变化追踪转变。该套件在 HuggingFace 上以 CC BY-NC 4.0 协议发布。

Abstract: Tracking depression from multi-session counseling dialogues requires estimating both current symptom severity and how it changes across sessions. Yet progress on this task is constrained by the scarcity of longitudinal counseling data with standardized session-level depression labels. Existing resources typically provide either multi-session conversations without depression labels or labeled interviews in a single session. Building such a benchmark poses three challenges: maintaining longitudinal consistency and diversity, grounding symptom progression in empirical patterns, and expressing controlled depression states naturally without exposing target labels. To address these challenges, we introduce LongCounsel-8, a benchmark suite of three independently generated datasets totaling 7,749 five-session counseling trajectories, grounded in real-world client profiles, depression trajectories, symptom compositions, and counseling patterns. We combine profile-grounded simulation, empirically informed state construction, and indirect behavioral realization to address these challenges. Across the benchmark, simulated self-reports closely recover the controlled states, supporting label fidelity. Experiments on existing depression tracking methods reveal three key findings: (1) lower single-session score error does not guarantee accurate identification of trend, i.e., improvement or worsening; (2) existing methods are consistently less reliable on worsening trajectories; and (3) additional session history may reduce the accuracy of trend prediction. Together, these findings establish LongCounsel-8 as a foundation for advancing depression assessment from static, single-session prediction toward reliable longitudinal tracking of mental-health change.

</details>


### [103] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanGRPO提出了两种无需重计算的轨迹对数概率扩散RL训练调度（Retain和Reweight），通过重构数据并行布局，在保持原始优化目标的同时消除冗余更新阶段重计算，实现了高达1.83倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散强化学习方法（如DanceGRPO和FlowGRPO）在on-policy训练中会在更新阶段重新计算选定的去噪时间步，这在数学上是冗余的，因为rollout阶段已用相同后端计算过相同的去噪预测。这种冗余重计算是扩散RL的主要系统瓶颈。

Method: 1. 重构数据并行布局为共享提示（shared-prompt）布局，所有GPU处理相同提示以分摊可微状态的显存开销；2. 提出LeanGRPO-Retain调度，在rollout期间保留计算图和激活值，优势可用时直接用于反向传播，无需重计算；3. 提出LeanGRPO-Reweight调度，在rollout期间立即对每个选定步执行临时反向传播（使用单位优势），延迟梯度同步，待真实优势可用时进行修正，两者在精确算术下保持与原始目标等价。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO上跨FLUX.1-dev（12B）、SD3.5-Medium、Wan2.1-1.3B/14B、Wan2.2-5B等模型，Reweight实现1.24×-1.83×加速，Retain实现1.12×-1.46×加速；Retain在固定轨迹下与Native逐位一致，Reweight梯度余弦相似度达0.999802；在FLUX.1-dev上达到目标奖励时间缩短1.46×；适用于约95.7%的目标扩散RL实现。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO是一种保持优化目标的执行框架，通过消除on-policy轨迹对数概率扩散RL中的冗余更新阶段重计算，Retain和Reweight两种调度在内存与计算效率上形成互补权衡，在多种算法和骨干网络上保持奖励提升的同时实现最高1.83×端到端加速。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [104] [On the Interaction Between Model Compression and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03604)
*Francesco Corti,Dong Wang,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了结构化压缩与测试时适应（TTA）的交互作用，发现压缩模型虽在监督适应中保持高精度，但其TTA性能随压缩率增加而显著退化。作者提出了一种诊断框架，从表征表达性和适应子空间兼容性两个维度分析此现象，并理论上解释了熵最小化和一致性目标在压缩下的梯度退化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在边缘设备上的DNN通常需要在压缩后仍能在分布偏移下自适应。现有关于模型压缩和TTA的研究均较丰富，但两者的交互作用尚不明确——压缩是否保留了模型在测试时适应的能力是一个关键开放问题。本文旨在系统分析结构化压缩如何影响模型在分布偏移下的TTA能力。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 实验设置：使用ResNet-18（CIFAR-10-C/ImageNet-C）和ViT-Base（ImageNet-C），评估5种结构化压缩方法（Mag-ℓ₂、Wanda、Taylor、OBD、Folding）与两种TTA方法（SAR用于ResNet-18，SPA用于ViT-Base）。(2) Oracle基线：使用相同优化设置但以监督交叉熵替代无监督目标，作为适应性恢复上界。(3) 诊断框架：从表征表达性（CKA、Activation Map Entropy）和子空间兼容性（梯度余弦相似度）两个维度量化压缩-适应交互。(4) 理论分析：证明熵最小化和KL一致性目标在压缩下会出现梯度退化（梯度消失或主动发散）。

Result: 主要发现：(1) TTA与Oracle之间的准确率差距随压缩率增大而扩大，尤其在ImageNet-C上显著。(2) TTA在低压缩率下即开始降解表征，且在高稀疏度下无法恢复，而监督适应始终能恢复。(3) 压缩引发两种梯度对齐失败模式：梯度退化（预测趋近均匀分布导致TTA信号衰减）和主动发散（TTA梯度与监督梯度方向相反）。(4) 压缩方法的影响差异显著：Wanda和Taylor更好地保留可恢复结构，OBD和Mag-ℓ₂导致严重退化，Folding恢复较稳定。(5) ImageNet-C上的效应远强于CIFAR-10-C，表明复杂任务下适应更难。(6) 排除SAR中"高置信但错误"的样本可将梯度对齐恢复至≈+0.9，证实这类样本是主因。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了标准"压缩后部署"流程可能导致"静默可塑性丧失"（silent plasticity loss）：压缩模型保留源域精度却逐步丧失通过无监督目标在测试时适应的能力。理论分析表明，熵和一致性目标的梯度在压缩下会退化，而监督交叉熵梯度仍保持信息性。实践上应：(1) 基于适应性而非仅源域精度选择压缩准则；(2) 共同考虑压缩预算与TTA目标的协同设计。本文为未来设计保留适应性的结构化压缩策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed in the wild must be both efficient and adaptable, requiring model compression and test-time adaptation (TTA). While both are well studied in isolation, their interaction remains poorly understood. We systematically analyze how structured compression affects a model's ability to adapt under distribution shift. Using ResNet-18 and ViT-Base on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C, we evaluate multiple compression methods combined with standard TTA techniques. We introduce a diagnostic framework that examines representational expressivity and adaptation subspace compatibility. Our results reveal a consistent gap: although compressed models retain high accuracy under supervised adaptation, their TTA performance degrades significantly with increasing compression. We show that this stems from reduced representational diversity and structural constraints that limit recoverability. These effects strongly depend on the compression method, highlighting the need to design compression strategies that preserve adaptability.

</details>


### [105] [Local Updates, Global Learning (LUGL): Playing Games with non-incremental Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03660)
*David Milec,Spyridon Samothrakis,Michael Fairbank,Dennis J.N.J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUGL（局部更新，全局学习）框架，将梯度提升树（LightGBM）等非增量学习器应用于强化学习博弈场景，在4个完全信息博弈和5个不完全信息博弈上的表现均与或优于DQN与DeepCFR，挑战了"NN在博弈中不可或缺"的固有偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在博弈强化学习中的主导地位部分源于其增量学习能力，但梯度提升树（如LightGBM）在表格数据的监督学习中被广泛认可为SOTA，且博弈状态天然具有表格化结构。作者旨在打破社区对NN的强烈偏见，提供一个使批量学习器能在RL中稳定工作的原则性框架。

Method: LUGL通过解耦"数据收集"与"模型拟合"两个阶段，交替执行：(1) 局部更新阶段——智能体通过自博弈累积Q/V/策略/遗憾值到有限局部表中；(2) 全局学习阶段——以该表为监督学习数据集训练函数逼近器（主要为LightGBM），训练完成后重置局部表。该机制防止了分布漂移并保证逼近器始终训练于一致的数据快照上。论文提出了多种变体：LUGL-Q/QD/PI/V-LightGBM（完全信息）以及LUGL-DeepCFR-LightGBM和Multi-S/D（不完全信息）。

Result: 在4个完全信息游戏（井字棋、四子棋、黑白棋、Hex）和5个不完全信息游戏（Kuhn扑克、Leduc Hold'em、吹牛骰子、Goofspiel、Flop5 Hold'em）上实验。完全信息博弈中使用Glicko-2评分，LUGL变体收敛快于DQN；不完全信息博弈中使用可利用性(exploitability)度量，LightGBM版DeepCFR在所有测试游戏中均低于NN版DeepCFR。在Flop5 Hold'em大型实验中，LUGL-DeepCFR-LightGBM以约100 mbb/h的优势胜过SD-CFR基线，且运行时间仅约1.5倍。

Conclusion: LUGL证明非增量学习器在博弈RL中无需增量学习即可达到或超越NN方法的效果；该框架算法无关，适用于任何"数据收集+监督学习"可分解的RL方法；CFR训练中固有的非平稳噪声回归目标反而有利于具有更强归纳偏置和方差控制的树模型，为表格化或结构化数据的RL提供了强大的非参数替代方案。

Abstract: The dominance of Neural Networks (NNs) in RL is partially due to their incremental learning capability, which naturally suits the online, non-stationary nature of self-play training. However, gradient-boosted trees like LightGBM are widely recognised as the state of the art for tabular data in supervised learning, often outperforming NNs in accuracy and efficiency. Game states are inherently tabular---discrete actions, categorical card identities, structured board positions---which makes them an ideal candidate for tree-based methods. We introduce LUGL (Local Updates, Global Learning), a framework that decouples data collection from model fitting, enabling non-incremental learners such as GBTs to operate in RL settings where they would otherwise fail due to distributional shift. LUGL alternates between a local updates phase, where the agent plays self-play games and accumulates tabular updates (Q-values, V-values, policies, or regret values) in a finite table, and a global learning phase, where the table is used to train a function approximator that generalises to unseen states before the table is reset. We test our approach in four standard perfect-information games (Tic-tac-toe, Connect-4, Othello, and Hex) and five imperfect-information games (Kuhn's poker, Leduc Hold'em, Liar's Dice, Goofspiel, and Flop5 Hold'em), and show that our results are competitive with or superior to DQN and DeepCFR. Our experiments demonstrate that the community's strong bias towards NNs in game-playing may be unwarranted, since LightGBM-based agents achieve competitive or superior performance across all tested benchmarks.

</details>


### [106] [Out-of-Distribution Generalisation with Sequence Models in Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03667)
*Oussama Hidaoui,Omer Ebead,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Siddarth Singh,Juan Claude Formanek,Felix Chalumeau,Omayma Mahjoub,Sasha Abramowitz,Ruan John de Kock,Wiem Khlifi,Louay Ben Nessir,Simon Verster Du Toit,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Asim Awad Osman,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Refiloe Shabe,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中序列模型的零样本任务泛化能力，发现在多任务训练设置中，任务多样性的扩展是减少训练-测试泛化差距的主导因素（而非单纯的数据量），而模型容量缩放对困难任务的泛化也有积极作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线MARL研究多局限于单任务训练和评估，缺乏对未见任务泛化能力的系统理解；同时，序列模型在MARL中的规模化规律（数据量vs任务多样性vs模型容量）尚未被探索。论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出多任务序列模型架构：动态智能体padding、掩码与随机排序以处理可变智能体数量；任务均衡批采样避免"头任务"主导；采用HL-Gauss分类目标替代标量TD回归以缓解奖励尺度差异；2) 构建包含LBF、Connector、RWARE、SMAX四个环境的28个训练任务和34个测试任务的离线MARL评估套件；4) 基于Dec-POMDP和任务伪度量提出覆盖-平滑度泛化界（R(z_test) ≤ ε_train + 2L·min d(z_i, z_test)），从理论上形式化任务多样性和模型容量的作用。

Result: 在四个MARL基准测试中，多任务序列模型（MT Oryx、MT CQL-Sable、MT BC-Sable）在未见测试任务上相比单任务模型平均获得3.2倍性能提升（RWARE 5.4x、Connector 2.9x、SMAX 3.2x、LBF 1.3x）。单纯增加数据集大小（固定任务数）无法带来显著泛化提升，而增加训练任务数和模型容量（从116k到6.2M参数）则能持续提升泛化。与Mediratta et al. (2024)在单智能体中的结论不同，MARL场景中离线RL方法（CQL/Oryx）通常优于行为克隆。

Conclusion: 论文结论是任务多样性（而非数据规模）是离线MARL序列模型零样本泛化的主导驱动因素，模型容量缩放可进一步提升困难任务的泛化能力。未来工作应优先构建大规模多样化离线MARL数据集并合理调节模型容量。论文也指出局限性：仅限于集中式序列模型架构，未来需扩展至去中心化和CTDE算法，并研究跨环境（而非仅跨任务）的迁移。

Abstract: Generalising to unseen tasks remains a fundamental challenge in offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). In this work, we present a principled analysis of zero-shot task generalisation in the offline setting and conduct an extensive empirical investigation into the scaling behaviour governing task diversity, dataset size, and network capacity. To facilitate this study, we extend offline sequence modelling architectures to handle multi-task observation and action spaces alongside variable agent counts across tasks. Our primary finding is that scaling task diversity---rather than sheer dataset size is the dominant factor in achieving robust zero-shot transfer. Through large-scale experiments across four challenging environments (Connector, RWARE, SMAX, and LBF), we demonstrate that our multi-task approach achieves a mean improvement of 3.2x on held-out test tasks compared to single-task models and consistently outperforms strong behaviour cloning baselines. These results suggest that the development of generalisable MARL agents should prioritise the diversity of the training distribution with varying numbers of agents, providing a roadmap for scaling offline MARL effectively.

</details>


### [107] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自由暂停标记"(Free Pause Token)方法，通过权重共享的并行预测流而非额外token位置，为语言模型的下一标记预测提供额外计算。该方法在1B参数模型上实现2-3 centinats的下一标记预测改进，同时推理成本几乎为零（不增加上下文长度、KV缓存或解码步骤），训练开销通过四项优化机制降至1.09×-1.33×墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer中，单个隐藏状态需要同时执行两个相互冲突的任务：总结上下文和预测下一标记。状态-预测分离(State-Prediction Separation, SPS)虽能缓解此问题，但额外预测流导致约1.9×的预训练FLOPs开销，使该方法在实际应用中成本过高。本文旨在将SPS推向极限，使其在推理时几乎免费，同时训练成本可控。

Method: 核心方法是"自由暂停标记"：在普通状态流(a)之外，运行权重共享的预测流(p)。预测流不写入任何键值对，因此不占用序列位置——它"骑"在现有位置上。预测流在每个位置从共享的learned embedding初始化，对状态流的键值对形成查询，并输出下一标记预测。降低训练成本的四项机制：(1) FlashAttention友好的两遍拆分，吞吐量提升约4×；(2) w=0的预测窗口，避免额外的log-sum-exp合并注意力调用；(4) 共享门控FFN，每位置仅评估一次FFN而非每流一次；(4) 分阶段(phasing)引入，在训练尾部才启用第二遍。

Result: 在1B参数模型上（24层，hidden size 1536，GQA，sliding-window attention），使用Muon优化器在Phi-4数据上预训练：完整自由暂停实现2.8673 vs控制组2.8957的交叉熵（-0.0284 nats）。分阶段42.5%在1.33×墙钟时间下达到2.8691（恢复94%增益），75%分阶段在1.14×下达到2.8756。等同FLOP分析中，所有变体在等同节点小时下均优于基线，最便宜方案获得-0.013 nats优势。推理时，自由暂停的解码步骤在B200上微基准测试中仅比标准解码慢约1%。下游评估（DCLM CORE和climpmix BPB）证实增益并非仅存在于损失层面。

Conclusion: 状态-预测分离在等同token/参数/计算/训练FLOP条件下，相对于标准预训练方法提供了适度优势。将原本约1.9×预训练FLOPs的开销降至1.33×墙钟时间（保留几乎全部增益），或通过权衡降至1.09×，同时保持推理免费。分阶段调度策略使廉价方案在等同计算下获胜最显著。未来工作可探索与多标记预测(multitoken prediction)的结合。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [108] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAND是一种"天然可解释"的非监督表格异常检测方法，通过单位球面上的"见证方向"对样本进行评分；标记异常的见证方向本身就是特征层面的解释，无需后置SHAP/LIME近似。在47个ADBench数据集上达到最佳平均Friedman秩，检测精度与16种基线相当，但解释更准确、更忠实，且查询成本几乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流异常检测器（如IForest、LOF、ECOD）只输出分数，无法说明"为什么"某点被判为异常；现有做法是用SHAP或LIME等后置方法近似，每次需数千次查询且解释不忠实。需要一种"天然可解释"且检测能力不减的异常检测器。

Method: 核心思想：将计算组织在单位球面上的方向上，对每个方向计算样本投影极值超出亚高斯基线的程度，并据此为每个点聚合得分。方法包含四个阶段：(1)逐方向尾超出量（MAD-z + k-间距分量，处理多模态投影）；(2)分裂路径（均匀球面探测与轴对齐探测分别打分并加权混合）；(3)多次种子平均；(4)梯度可微的软极值算子使其可端到端使用。关键创新：将检测与解释统一到同一对象，即"见证方向"——标记某点的方向是特征空间中的向量，其大坐标即为特征归因，可零额外成本获得；同时由于分数可微，相同解释可由梯度恢复。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND取得最佳平均Friedman秩（5.64），平均ROC-AUC为0.777，与最强经典基线（IForest 0.762、INNE 0.759）相当，且在Nemenyi检验中处于顶级集群；AUPR排名一致。所有47个数据集单CPU约1.7分钟完成。在33个数据集上的解释质量评估中：合成属性AUC轴对齐0.977/斜向0.660（见证）/0.993/0.680（梯度），优于ECOD原生（0.968/0.635）、SHAP（0.887/0.632）、LIME（0.585/0.504）；真实保真度0.629（见证），≥SHAP胜率85%；解释查询次数为0（vs SHAP的~9745次，LIME的~600次）。AnoCUB案例研究成功定位到临床相关的乳腺肿瘤标志物（mean radius、perimeter、worst area、concavity）。对重尾inlier（Student-t至Cauchy）保持稳健。理论上证得方向数K仅依赖于异常数k和半空间深度边界τ/√d，与样本量n无关；MAD-z统计量每方向崩溃点1/2，联合崩溃点≥1/(d+1)。

Conclusion: WAND实现了"检测精度持平"前提下的原生可解释异常检测：见证方向同时是检测机制与解释来源，零额外查询开销，梯度可微，并具备覆盖保证（每个τ-margin异常必有见证方向）。贡献在于可解释性而非精度大幅提升。局限：(1)运行时间仍为O(Knd)线性，方向效率保证不转化为运行时间；(2)高维下pτ = Θ((τ/√d)^(d-1))松弛，实际K=1024在d≈1555下仍有效；(3)严格保证限于亚高斯inlier；(4)轴对齐路径的崩溃点分析留作开放问题。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [109] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffInt，一种基于可微分区间瓶颈的自编码器，用于数值数据的可解释异常检测。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个轴对齐超矩形（区间），无需离散化即可端到端学习，重建误差作为异常评分。在48个ADBench数据集上对22个基线的评估表明，DiffInt在ROC-AUC和AUPR上均达到最佳平均排名（4.10/4.16），且是统计领先聚类中唯一可解释的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的深度异常检测器虽然准确，但缺乏可解释性——只能给出异常分数，无法告知是哪些特征范围导致异常。现有可解释方法（如区间模式挖掘）依赖离散化，会丢失信息且难以扩展；监督型超矩形方法需要标签。监管需求（如欧盟AI法案、信用评分中的特征级原因代码）迫切要求可解释的异常检测。

Method: DiffInt是一个自编码器，其潜在瓶颈层由K个软区间隶属度组成：每个单元由可学习的中心m和softplus半宽Δ参数化，通过sigmoid温度τ实现边界可微化。采用log-sum-exp竞争聚合器将d个逐特征隶属度组合为标量模式激活，f(x)∈[0,1]^K。解码器为两层ReLU MLP，以RMSE为训练目标、MAE为测试异常评分。配套提出：(1) 定理1（裁剪边界）：超出所有活动坐标的点，其编码坍缩为与x无关的空码f_0，在Lipschitz约束解码器下可获得MAE的确定性下界证书；(2) 推论1（分级抑制）：违反s_k个活动坐标时，单元权重按β^{s_k}几何衰减；(3) 无标签重要性（LFI）=支持度×紧致度×稳定性，将训练后的区间转化为可审计的候选约束，无需异常标签。输入域[-1,1]的min-max归一化被证明是方法不可分割的组成部分。

Result: 在48个ADBench数值数据集上与22个基线对比，DiffInt在ROC-AUC和AUPR上均取得最佳平均排名（4.10/4.16），位于7方法Nemenyi不可分离的领先聚类中，且是其中唯一可解释的检测器。在仅内部点训练子集中以显著优势领先（1.69/1.71排名），在含污染数据完整子集中也保持领先或并列。LFI排序与KernelSHAP等聚合后置方法的斯皮尔曼相关性约0.7，且零查询成本。Certified-Lipschitz版本在6个数据集上对所有外部支持点均满足MAE下界（0违规），但平均AUROC从0.908降至0.851。

Conclusion: DiffInt将自编码器的黑箱瓶颈替换为可检查的软区间结构，在保持深度表示学习能力的同时提供特征级可解释性。其核心机制（裁剪边界+分级抑制）通过定理和实验得到了精确刻画。在大规模基准测试中取得了最优排名，是目前唯一兼具高精度和原生可解释性的异常检测器。未来可引入软L0门控和MDL项，将其发展为更紧凑的区间模式集挖掘器。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [110] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL 提出一种基于"残差检索"的即插即用框架，将冻结基预测器的预测误差（而非历史目标值本身）作为可检索的外部记忆，并结合 set-aware 路由器 J5 在块-变量粒度上选择和组合候选残差轨迹，用于多元时间序列长时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 类检索增强预测方法直接复用历史未来值，存在输出量级/数值尺度/局部动态差异以及负迁移问题；同时残差在常规流程中仅用于训练优化或误差诊断，未被保留为可推理时访问的记忆。该工作将检索目标从历史目标值转向基模型特有的历史预测误差，并设计带因果约束的检索机制和路由机制以安全利用残差记忆。

Method: 1) 冻结基预测器 f_θ，用其变量级隐藏表示构造键，将每个训练窗口的残差 R_i = Y_i - f_θ(X_i) 存入训练期专用记忆；2) 推理时对每个变量在满足 a_i ≤ t-H 的时间可获得性约束下做 Top-K 检索；3) Direct 基线：按检索相似度 softmax 加权候选残差；4) J5 路由器：将预测区间划分为长度 8 的块，在每个 (块, 变量) 单元构建含零残差候选在内的 K+1 个候选令牌，通过 set attention 学习组合权重；监督采用基于真实残差构造的软 Oracle 教师分布（温度 softmax）+ 预测 MSE 损失；5) 引入 γ ∈ [0,1] 全局修正强度超参数，在验证集上以 MSE 为准则选取。

Result: 在 13 个数据集、52 个 (数据集, 预测长度) 单元上以 iTransformer 为基模型：相对基模型平均 MSE 降低 9.57%、MAE 降低 6.21%，48 胜 2 平 2 负；PEMS 系列提升最大（20.56–24.41%），Exchange 长时预测出现退化（−2.66% 与 −8.19%）；在 γ=1 固定尺度下 J5 比 Direct 多 1.07 pp 提升；DLinear、PatchTST、TimesNet、TimeMixer 冻结迁移均为正平均增益，但收益受架构、数据集、键定义共同影响。

Conclusion: RATL 将检索目标从历史目标值转为基模型特有的历史预测误差，提供了一种可适配多种冻结基预测器的残差记忆式反馈纠偏范式；其有效性在周期性强、物理结构稳定的数据集（交通、能源、天气）上显著，在周期弱、分布漂移强的金融序列（Exchange）上可能失败，零残差候选与 γ=0 回退机制仅能降低而不能消除负迁移风险。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference this http URL multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual this http URL real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most this http URL , RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [111] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于GRPO的组级回放控制原语。它将回放选择分解为两个独立的控制轴：Headroom按剩余学习价值对存储组排序（PER风格的优先级），Drift按当前策略兼容性进行门控（排除过时的组）。该方法无需辅助生成或训练机制，仅在标准GRPO中添加回放组即可。在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准测试中，这一单一干预在Avg Mean@32上优于朴素回放，并匹配或超越更广泛的回放方法（如DAPO、ExGRPO、BAPO）。在Agentic Search中，它以显著更低的wall-clock时间实现了可比较的质量，构成了对在线策略扩展的Pareto改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练（尤其是GRPO风格）因重复的新鲜rollout生成而面临巨大成本瓶颈，尤其在Agentic环境中环境交互代价高昂。现有回放方法通常与探索、经验重构或混合策略优化耦合，难以孤立地评估回放本身的贡献。作者旨在回答：仅凭原则性的回放选择，能走多远？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay，将回放控制分解为两个显式轴：(1) Headroom：度量存储组中正/负advantage动作的剩余修正空间（1-π或π），作为学习价值优先级；(2) Policy Drift：通过token级对数概率漂移的平方均值度量当前策略与生成策略的偏差，作为硬门控（Drift≤τ）。回放在完整组（而非单条响应）上进行，保持GRPO的组内比较结构。新鲜在线策略流不变，FIFO回放缓冲区按混合奖励结果的组进行ingres。算法扫描Headroom降序排列的候选，通过单次前向pass计算Drift并刷新Headroom，填满回放预算K_rep即停止。

Result: 在数学推理（AIME24、AMC23、MATH500等5个基准）上，Headroom-Drift在Avg Mean@32上领先所有基线，包括朴素回放、DAPO、ExGRPO和BAPO，且使用更少的新鲜响应。在Agentic Search（NQ、HotpotQA等7个基准）上，以166.3s/step优于GRPO on-policy larger的197.2s/step且质量更高，构成Pareto改进。在多模态推理（Geo3K、MathVista、MathVision）上同样领先。机制分析显示：(a) 同缓冲区反事实比较中，两轴联合改善占多数步骤；(b) 初始Headroom>0.8的组回放概率>56%，<0.8的组<24%；(c) Headroom-Drift的回放来源跨1-7步（44%来自≥3步），而朴素回放100%来自前一步。训练动力学分析表明，回放在匹配分数水平下延迟了熵坍缩的发生和反弹。组件消融验证了两轴缺一不可，Headroom-Drift完整组合在MATH-500上达到最高Mean@32 (0.7215)。

Conclusion: Headroom-Drift Replay是一个可组合的回放控制原语，在GRPO中作为独立的优化组件即可发挥作用。它在三个领域（数学推理、Agentic Search、多模态推理）上以单一干预超越朴素回放，并在数学推理上匹配或超越包含辅助机制的回放方法。其两个显式控制轴被设计为可与现有回放中心方法（EFRame、ExGRPO、BAPO）组合，而非竞争关系。未来工作包括在PPO/GSPO/CISPO等不同目标函数族下验证迁移性，以及探索门控的自适应调度策略。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [112] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，一种基于子空间推理的高效主动奖励学习方法。它将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间内使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）追踪奖励模型不确定性，从而支持对高维神经网络奖励模型进行高效采样以计算采集函数（如InfoGain）。实验表明该方法在样本效率、运行时、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中从人类偏好学习奖励模型样本效率低下，每条偏好反馈最多只提供1比特信息。主动学习是减少标注量的关键手段，但需要对大型神经网络奖励模型进行有效的不确定性量化。现有方法如集成（Ensemble）和Dropout存在计算开销大或后验近似质量差的问题，而经典贝叶斯方法难以扩展到高维神经网络参数空间。

Method: PreferenceEKF采用以下关键技术：(1) 利用神经网络过参数化特性，通过SVD分解SGD迭代轨迹或随机投影构建低维参数子空间（|z|=200）；(2) 在子空间内执行迭代扩展卡尔曼滤波（iterated EKF），以Bradley-Terry模型作为测量函数、恒等函数作为动力学模型，闭式更新高斯后验；(3) 由于子空间维度远小于完整参数空间，可高效采样任意数量的奖励模型来计算基于采样的采集函数（如InfoGain），无需训练多个独立模型。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准的12个任务上的实验表明：(1) PreferenceEKF在样本效率和测试对数似然方面与或优于DeepEnsemble、Dropout、Laplace和LLMCMC四种基线方法；(2) 运行时比DeepEnsemble快约5倍，比LLMCMC快约40倍，且在模型大小和后验样本数量增大时扩展性最佳；(3) 具有最低的期望校准误差（ECE）；(4) 学到的奖励模型用于离线RL（IQL）训练策略时，性能与基线方法相当；(5) 可成功扩展到稀疏偏好反馈的真实机器人数据（SOAR数据集）和基于预训练ResNet18嵌入的像素级任务；(6) InfoGain是唯一使所有方法的主动变体均优于随机变体的采集函数。

Conclusion: PreferenceEKF证明了子空间滤波是扩展贝叶斯方法到神经网络奖励模型训练的有效工具，在主动偏好奖励学习中实现了更优的样本效率、运行时、可扩展性和校准性能。学习到的奖励模型可用于下游策略优化并达到竞争性能。然而，该方法受限于EKF的单峰高斯假设，不适用于多标注者场景，且向基础模型规模的奖励模型扩展仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套低成本、开源的端到端自动驾驶实验平台，基于小型阿克曼转向车辆，集成了实体车、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准系统以及Webots数字孪生，并实现了命令条件行为克隆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端自动驾驶研究主要依赖大型仿真器（如CARLA）或昂贵硬件，难以在受控且可重复的环境下研究真实物理约束下的自动驾驶。仿真无法暴露相机延迟、视场限制、执行器误差、里程计漂移等问题；低成本小型车辆平台正是填补纯仿真与全尺寸车辆之间空白的关键。

Method: 平台由实体车（Yahboom ROSMASTER R2L + ESP32-S3 + 独立ESP32相机）、3.2×2.8m打印城市赛道、Webots数字孪生组成。基线方法为命令条件行为克隆：输入RGB图像（160×120）和one-hot导航指令（follow_lane/turn_left/turn_right），输出[-1,1]的转向与速度。采用紧凑CNN基线（94,882参数）和更大变体（约1.0M参数）。Sim-to-real迁移通过U-Net图像翻译器（配对L1损失+光度随机化）将仿真图像映射到真实相机域，再与真实数据联合训练。

Result: 实体闭环实验：紧凑基线在外部/中央直行/中央右转路线上达到6.1±1.2cm平均横向误差，接近人工示范的4.7±1.8cm。数字孪生中，视场角从58°扩至120°使横向误差从35.6±12.1cm降至3.3±0.2cm。架构消融中，仅混合数据（真实+仿真翻译）训练的大容量策略能完成全部四条路线（平均横向误差约5.1cm），而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据的大网络分别只能完成3条和2条路线。

Conclusion: 该开源平台是sim-to-real研究的实用测试床，命令条件模仿学习基线效果接近人工水平；sim-to-real翻译的合成数据对提升策略泛化能力至关重要。主要局限是物理实验次数少、轨迹配准需手动、容量与输入分辨率未解耦；未来工作包括自动配准、宽视场相机、BEV/扩散/Transformer等更强基线。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution.
