As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [114] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重写"（Equation Recast）框架，将参数化算子学习重构为单一规范算子的学习。参数引起的算子变化从控制方程中解析导出并吸收到有效源中，从而实现跨新参数域的零样本预测。该方法支持外推、整合稀疏异构数据，并通过迭代收敛性提供失效诊断。最终在高保真托卡马克核聚变模拟中统一了四种装置几何的电子温度数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化偏微分方程（PDE）的代理模型学习面临三大挑战：（1）参数变化下需要广泛的输入函数与物理参数覆盖；（2）训练分布外的预测可能静默失败；（3）现有方法（参数化学习、迁移学习、经典扰动方法）均未能同时解决参数变化表示、数据稀疏性与可靠性诊断三个问题。核聚变等高价值应用中，数据稀疏、参数覆盖不均且不同装置几何差异显著，传统方法难以构建可复用的代理模型。

Method: 核心思想：固定参考参数 𝐩* 定义规范算子 𝒪* = 𝒪(𝐩*)，目标参数 𝐩 下的算子差 𝒪δ(δ𝐩)[u] = 𝒪(𝐩)[u] - 𝒪*[u] 由控制方程解析导出，将PDE重写为 𝒪*[u] = S - 𝒪δ(δ𝐩)[u] = S_eff。神经网络仅学习规范逆算子 𝒢*N ≈ (𝒪*)^(-1)，推理通过定点迭代 u^(k+1) = 𝒢*N[S_eff^k] 完成。对于几何变化，通过调和映射将不规则物理域映射到共享规范域，几何依赖通过 Jacobian 矩阵 K 依赖的度量项解析引入有效源。实现采用 Fourier Neural Operator（FNO）和 LocalNO 作为骨干网络，使用 Aitken Δ² 松弛加速定点迭代。

Result: 1) 一维对流-扩散-反应方程：在 (Pe*, Da*) = (4,2) 单一规范点训练，在 (Pe, Da) ∈ [1,20]² 参数域上实现零样本外推，1% 误差等高线覆盖大部分区域。2) 一维反应-扩散方程：在 k_new = 0.05 处的异构数据通过有效源重写整合，使低 k 区域的预测误差降低近一个数量级。3) 一维 Helmholtz 方程：迭代次数随共振点附近急剧增加，超过 k_3 后迭代不收敛，提供失效诊断信号。4) 二维非线性 Navier-Stokes：规范 FNO 在 Re* = 250 训练，在 Re ∈ [50, 400] 范围内（特别是低 Re 方向）显著优于参数化 FNO 和 PINO；数据预算 N=1500 时，重新分配的重写模型达到 4.0% L² 误差。5) 多装置托卡马克：Alcator C-Mod、SPARC、ARC_V2A 等四种几何通过调和映射统一，LocalNO-L 在 ΔT_e 上达到 2.2%–3.4% L² 误差。

Conclusion: 方程重写将参数化算子学习从"学习整个参数索引的逆算子族"转变为"学习单一规范逆算子+解析处理参数变化"，实现了方程引导的迁移、数据效率与可监控推断的统一。该框架为可复用的神经 PDE 求解器提供了新路径，在核聚变等高保真模拟中展现了实际可行性。未来工作包括：发展有效源覆盖度的系统化度量与对应采样策略，以及将框架推广到更一般的几何映射方法。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [115] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于协作学习（Collaborative Learning）的综述论文，系统地梳理了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习方法，特别关注联邦学习和去中心化学习。研究通过"有效性、效率、隐私"这三个核心维度建立了分析框架，并提出了针对图结构数据的数据划分分类法、统计异质性表征以及标准化的问题表述和算法框架，最后识别了该新兴领域中的开放挑战和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的集中式机器学习方法面临可扩展性和隐私方面的根本性限制。现有协作学习研究主要集中于欧几里得数据（如图形、文本等具有规则网格结构的数据），但这种方法无法捕捉现实应用中普遍存在的关系模式（如图数据）。虽然图上的消息传递机制天然适合协作环境，但图结构数据在协作学习中的机会和挑战尚未得到充分探索。该综述旨在填补这一空白，将协作学习从欧几里得数据扩展到图结构数据领域。

Method: 该综述采用结构化的分析方法，分两大部分：1）首先回顾欧几里得数据协作学习的基础原理，围绕有效性、效率和隐私三个核心维度进行组织；2）将讨论扩展到图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类法、表征相关统计异质性、开发标准化问题表述和算法框架。具体涵盖：联邦学习（FedAvg、FedProx等）、去中心化学习、垂直联邦学习、消息传递神经网络（MPNN）、图神经网络（GNN）协作训练、盲计算与差分隐私等技术，以及集中式、分布式、分层式聚合模式，异步聚合，通信效率优化等设计选择。

Result: 综述系统性地编制了该新兴领域的图谱，通过三个方面的新颖贡献对协作学习进行了全面整理：1）提出了图结构数据分布的统一分类法（拓扑划分、特征划分、模式划分），区分了子图划分和自我图划分的水平与垂直情况；2）特征化了图特定的统计异质性，包括特征偏移、结构偏移、概念偏移和同质冲突等新维度；3）开发了标准化的算法框架（Algorithm 3用于子图GNN的联邦训练）。该综述还识别了若干开放挑战：协作图重布线、演变图上的协作学习、拓扑深度学习的协作适配、图基础模型的协作部署、多智能体隐私放大以及隐私机制与防御机制的整合等。

Conclusion: 协作学习已成功解锁此前不可用的分布式数据源，从欧几里得数据的联邦学习和去中心化学习，延伸到了图结构数据的涌现解决方案。将协作学习扩展到图结构数据，开启了超越集中式图机器学习的机遇——其能够跨传感器网络到分子生物学等数据领域进行挖掘。综述指出了图结构数据协作学习的两大范式：隔离推理和协作推理。由于GNN的消息传递机制，协作推理在图结构数据中成为更核心的范式。结合分布式关系数据与协作训练和/或推理提出了独特的约束条件，需要新的解决方案，涉及拓扑、通信、隐私和异质性的联合考量。该综述为下一代协作图学习方法的发展奠定了统一基础，有望在药物发现、医疗保健、可持续产业和智慧城市等领域产生重要影响。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [116] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，基于 RoPE 的现代 Transformer 内部已形成以注意力头为单位的隐式功能混合：低频、弱位置依赖的头主要负责近似位置无关的全局语义检索；一个与训练长度相关的显著中低频带及其以上则主要承担位置建模。作者将该边界命名为“全局位置带”（GPBand），并据此提出：位置建模应局部化，全局访问应由位置无关机制完成；两种功能应按头划分，并随层深变化分配。基于此构建的 Head-wise Hybrid Architecture（HwH）使用少量 NoPE Full Attention 提供全局检索、较多 Gated DeltaNet Linear Attention 承担局部位置建模。在从头预训练实验中，HwH 以低于 1:3 的 FA:LA 总维度比，保持语言建模和常识推理能力，同时获得最佳综合检索性能，并将 NIAH-Single-2 的有效零样本长度外推提升到训练上下文 2K 的约两倍。核心价值在于把混合架构设计从经验配比转化为由 RoPE 频率机制导出的结构原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Full Attention 与 Linear Attention 混合架构通常采用固定或经验性的层间比例、逐层交错或统一头比例，缺少“为什么这样分配、分配给谁”的机制依据。论文从现代 RoPE Transformer 学到的注意力头功能分化出发，寻求可解释、可干预验证且能指导从头设计混合架构的原则。

Method: 首先用 QRscore 衡量真实上下文中的检索倾向，并用 LDscore 衡量局部流式注意倾向；由于二者不能形成完整分类，作者进一步提出两个基于干预的指标：RFIS 通过移除某一 RoPE 频率分量并测量完整注意力分布的 Jensen–Shannon 变化，衡量该频率的因果重要性；RPD 将同一频率的相对 RoPE 旋转替换为单位变换，以隔离真正的位置调制依赖。RFIS 发现低频与中低频带及高频存在不同模式，RPD 验证中低频带以上主要具有位置依赖性。匹配 380M 模型的受控实验改变 RoPE base 和训练长度，显示边界频率索引虽移动，但对应周期始终处于训练长度尺度，由此命名为 GPBand。基于检索/位置两类头在深度上分布不均，作者提出局部位置建模、位置无关全局检索，以及头粒度、层特定分配两条原则，并实现 HwH：NoPE FA 负责全局检索，GDN 负责局部位置建模。标准 HwH 首位层仅用 GDN，中间半层 FA:LA 为 1:3，其余外层为 1:7。实验从头预训练 380M/1.4B 模型，与 RoPE Transformer、纯 GDN 和逐层 1:3 的 Inter 比较，并通过替换 RoPE/SWA/FA 及改变逐层比例完成消融。

Result: RFIS 与 RPD 在 Qwen3 系列、Llama3.1 和 Gemma-7B 上均支持检索头与位置头的二类机制划分；Qwen3 的多种训练阶段使生产模型呈多个 GPBand-like 结构，而受控模型呈单一清晰边界。受控实验中，训练长度 2048 时，RoPE base 从 10^4 改为 10^6，显著带由频率组 21–22 移至 14–15，但对应周期仍约为训练长度尺度；训练长度增至 4096 时，边界进一步移至约组 24。HwH 在 380M 和 1.4B 上保持有竞争力的语言建模与常识推理。1.4B 模型真实检索平均分：Transformer 40.17、GDN 29.33、Inter 43.08、HwH 44.64，HwH 最高。1.4B NIAH-Single-2 在 1K/2K/4K 上，Transformer 为 100/99.8/0，HwH 为 99.8/100/99.6。380M 模型在 NIAH-Single-2 上，HwH 接近完美外推至 4K，并在超过 4K 后优于 GDN 和 Inter。组件消融显示：RoPE FA 替换 NoPE FA 后真实检索平均分由 28.98 降至 23.67，突显位置无关全局检索的重要性；GDN 相比滑窗注意力具有更好的常识能力，而 SWA 的检索和长上下文外推略强。逐层消融显示，从统一 1:3 到移除首尾 FA，再到外层降至 1:7，逐步改善外推和检索，支持层特定分配。

Conclusion: 论文将现代 RoPE Transformer 解释为“隐式混合架构”：其注意力头在机制上可分化为全局检索头和位置建模头，二者由训练长度相关的 GPBand 隔开。该发现意味着混合架构不应只依赖经验性的层间配比，而应按头、按层深度显式分工。HwH 的实验支持“局部 LA 建模位置、全局 NoPE FA 完成检索”的设计原则：少量 FA 即可获得强检索和显著更好的零样本长度外推，同时维持语言建模与常识推理能力。该结论为下一代基础模型的组件选择和资源分配提供了机制依据。局限包括实验规模主要为 380M 与 1.4B、仅在从头预训练及文本任务上验证；GPBand 在多阶段生产模型中可能分裂为多个深度相关边界；GQA 共享 K/V 会使检索和位置特征相互干扰；RfIS/RPD 计算需逐层、逐头、逐频率干预，成本较高；Gemma-7B 的位置带偏移仍未完全解释。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [117] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tail-Likelihood强化学习(TailRL)，一种针对连续奖励的强化学习方法。TailRL通过最大化超过随机奖励阈值的对数概率，直接优化奖励分布的上尾概率，而非仅优化平均奖励。该方法可分解为Best-of-k梯度的调和级数，与推理时的Best-of-k性能直接对齐。实验表明，TailRL在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI定位和代码优化任务上均优于GRPO和RLOO等基线，尤其在高质量rollout罕见时或存在安全但次优的捷径时表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习优化平均奖励，但平均奖励无法区分两个策略在产生罕见高奖励rollout概率上的差异。随着训练和推理中采样增多，保留高奖励结果的概率质量至关重要。已有研究表明标准RL训练会逐渐丧失对罕见高奖励rollout的覆盖能力，表现为Best-of-k性能的下降以及推理时扩展的局限性。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 定义尾概率pθ(x,τ)=Pr[r(x,z)>τ]，将连续奖励视为一系列二元成功事件的家族；(2) 提出TailRL目标：对均匀采样的奖励阈值τ，最大化log pθ(x,τ)的期望，即J_TailRL=∫₀¹ log pθ(x,τ)dτ；(3) 证明其梯度可分解为Best-of-k梯度的调和级数：∇J_TailRL=∑(1/k)∇Best-of-k；(4) 给出截断的阶T目标族，从N=1（标准期望奖励）平滑过渡到N→∞（总体TailRL）；(5) 推导无偏的有限rollout估计器，仅需修改优势函数；(6) 证明对二元奖励，TailRL退化为MaxRL。

Result: 在四个任务上的实验结果：(1) ImageNet目标定位：总体TailRL匹配或超过使用真实框监督的L1+GIoU损失；(2) 文本迷宫导航：从0.01%成功率的初始策略学习，而GRPO/RLOO失败；(3) GUI定位：在ScreenSpot-Pro上以128×（3B）和256×（7B）更少的推理rollouts匹配RLOO的Pass@1024；(4) 代码运行时优化：在PIIE数据集上达到7.7× Best-of-1024加速，而GRPO/RLOO收敛于复制输入的捷径（速度仅0.98×/0.96×）。

Conclusion: TailRL提供了一种实用方式优化连续奖励分布的尾似然，恢复MaxRL（二元奖励特例），并与推理时Best-of-k扩展直接对齐。它在高质量rollout罕见或训练受次优稳定解吸引时优势最大，能更可靠地从罕见结果中学习，获得更多训练和推理样本的收益，并避免次优坍缩。一个连续奖励不仅是平均值，它定义了成功事件家族——每个奖励水平对应一个。TailRL提供了一种实用的方式来优化这些事件的似然。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [118] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在四面体TCAD网格上运行的物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，用于替代昂贵的Sentaurus漂移-扩散求解器。代理预测每个网格节点的静电势和电子/空穴准费米能级，并通过有限体积电流连续性残差嵌入物理约束。核心优势是尺寸可迁移性（小网格训练直接用于大阵列）和深度集成不确定性驱动的主动学习循环，使多目标设计空间探索速度比直接TCAD快约四个数量级，在大尺寸多鳍FinFET上的优势更加显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCAD漂移-扩散仿真对3D FinFET器件设计至关重要，但计算成本极高（单设计点耗分钟到小时），严重阻碍了多目标设计空间探索。现有ML代理要么仅映射少量标量品质因数（丢弃物理信息且无法跨几何迁移），要么缺乏在主动学习循环中作为全场重建代理的能力。需要一种网格原生、物理信息、可跨器件尺寸泛化的高保真代理，以实现TCAD-in-the-Loop设计空间探索。

Method: 方法包含四个核心组成部分：(1) 网格图表示：将四面体TCAD网格转为图结构，节点携带17维特征（几何、材料、掺杂、Al组分、接触标志、偏压、相对介电常数等），边携带5维特征（距离、位移、跨材料指示符）；(2) 物理信息GAT代理：8层GATv2残差网络（含LayerNorm），约110万参数，通过注意力机制加权邻居以处理异质结各向异性；(3) 训练目标：组合z分数归一化的数据MSE损失与有限体积电流连续性残差损失（log(1+x²)鲁棒形式），使用预测场重构载流子密度后计算散度，物理权重随训练周期斜坡上升；(4) TCAD-in-the-Loop主动学习：5个独立种子GAT成员构成深度集成，仅执行SDE网格生成（不求解），通过集合分歧度得到每节点不确定性，使用UCB式采集分数选择设计，迭代终止基于不确定性中位数改善率。

Result: 代理预测三个漂移-扩散场的逐场RMSE为0.16–1.3V（训练范围内Nfins∈{1,…,5}），外推到Nfins=38时退化2–12倍；数据消融实验无物理损失时外推误差增长18–22倍且无法产出有效Vth。在Nfins=1–40范围内，单次推理时间从65ms增长到1.7s，对比Sentaurus求解器的19分钟到7.5小时，实现约1.6×10⁴–4.1×10⁴倍加速（约四个数量级）。代理在8个鳍片以上仍保持<0.29V的Vth误差，而数据消融在Nfins=8处Id,max误差达约300%。训练集本身由主动学习循环组装：147个引导设计+53个采集选中设计（共200个Sentaurus运行），其中64%的采集选中设计集中在Nfins≥3的不确定区域。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了网格原生物理信息GNN可以替代3D TCAD漂移-扩散求解器用于全场重建，并在主动学习框架下实现实际的多目标设计空间探索。四个组合性归纳偏置（网格图承载几何、有限体积残差强制守恒、深度集成提供认知不确定性、主动学习驱动不确定性下降）共同提供了超过黑盒优化的优势。代理对器件族是"天然不可知的"——仅材料身份编码是GaN特有的，迁移到其他材料体系只需替换为学习的材料嵌入向量。未来方向包括：耦合参数空间梯度优化以闭合反设计循环、引入通用材料嵌入、扩大训练范围覆盖更高鳍片数和更丰富偏压扫描。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [119] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是首个将时序记忆直接存储在接触边上的图网络模拟器，用于颗粒动力学模拟。在2D和3D Sand基准测试中，相比GNS和NMGNS，长程位置误差降低31-62%，最终堆积误差降低58-89%，同时参数更少、几乎无粒子穿透，并相对MPM实现12.2×（2D）和8.9×（3D）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料的运动强烈依赖于颗粒间接触历史（如切向位移累积、摩擦锥约束），而现有学习图模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级循环记忆中，无法有效保留接触形成、断裂、重组过程中的历史信息，因此难以准确建模历史依赖的接触力学行为。

Method: TRACE采用编码器-处理器-解码器框架，关键创新包括：(1) 在Encoder与Processor之间引入时空接触边记忆模块，每条边维护持久记忆向量，通过注意力池化聚合邻居边信息形成空间上下文，再用GRU融合当前边潜在特征、空间上下文与历史记忆进行更新；(2) 设计边身份字典（基于eid(i,j)=i·N+j的唯一标识符与行索引映射）解决动态图重建中边顺序变化导致的记忆检索难题，新接触由InitNet生成起始状态；(3) 物理结构化解码器从节点表示预测外力、从边记忆预测法向力、切向力和摩擦系数，通过softplus保证法向力非负、缩放sigmoid约束摩擦系数，并将切向力投影到Coulomb摩擦锥内，对两端粒子施加等大反向力保证动量守恒；(4) 训练采用单步预训练（教师强制+Huber损失+窗口截断BPTT）后接自回归展开微调（pushforward策略：先无梯度漂移段生成偏离状态，再对位置进行有监督校正）；(5) 推理时采用半隐式Euler积分，后处理加入非穿透投影和边界投影。

Result: 在2D-Sand（30测试场景）和3D-Sand（100测试场景，粒子数4975-19762）长程自回归展开（300步，无真值校正）上：2D平均位置RMSE=0.093（峰值约0.110），3D=0.099（峰值约0.104）；2D沉积误差均值0.078，3D为0.154；2D能量释放峰值比中位数0.992，3D一致；接触数预测比中位数1.025。相对于GNS和NMGNS：2D RMSE降低30.8%和42.4%，3D降低36.0%和61.5%；2D沉积误差降低71.8%和81.1%，3D降低58.0%和88.7%；长程位置误差整体降低31-62%，沉积误差降低58-89%。粒子重叠TRACE近零，而基线平均重叠达27-39%粒子直径。计算效率：2D 12.01 ms/帧（12.2×加速），3D 107.63 ms/帧（8.9×加速），时间随粒子数线性增长（17.7 ms/千粒子）。最大场景同时维护超1.4×10⁵条接触边记忆。主要失效模式：当大量材料同时被抛出时模型倾向于低估喷射强度（splash under-estimation），但所有展开均保持数值稳定且收敛至物理合理的沉积形态。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将交互历史直接锚定在接触边上，提供了与颗粒接触力学物理结构相匹配的学习归纳偏置，有效抑制了动态图重建中的信息丢失和自回归误差累积，在精度、物理一致性和计算效率上均显著优于节点记忆基线和传统MPM求解器，为颗粒流模拟及更广泛的路径依赖粒子系统提供了通用框架，未来可拓展至流-粒耦合、碎屑流、滑坡、断裂演化及多相物质输运等场景。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLIWBO方法，通过有限库选择输入扭曲核来扩展GP-UCB，在数据自适应非线性几何下保持高概率次线性累积遗憾保证，并以多种基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO中固定核的UCB收敛保证要求核匹配输入几何，但原始坐标常与目标几何不匹配（如对数缩放超参数、局部峰值），而输入扭曲虽能提高样本效率却缺乏理论保证。

Method: 提出FLIWBO：维护有限光滑输入扭曲库，每个扭曲诱导一个GP核分支，共用观测历史，每轮通过任意历史相关规则选择活跃分支并用UCB采集函数选择下一查询点。理论分析证明在RKHS见证有界性、Sobolev-RKHS等价性和统一扭曲正则性假设下，同时建立全库GP-UCB置信界并推导次线性累积遗憾界。

Result: 在四个基准（扭曲合成目标、置信栅栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST HPO）和20维MAS设计中，FLIWBO-UCB优于原始坐标GP-UCB，在几何误设下修复失配，逃离使oracle-warp EI受困的陷阱，并恢复人工对数缩放的大部分收益。在具有匹配遗憾保证的可部署方法中取得最佳最终均值。

Conclusion: FLIWBO成功将经典GP-UCB扩展到在线有限扭曲核选择，在统一平滑性和RKHS-Sobolev兼容性下为任意历史相关选择器提供高概率次线性累积遗憾，实验证明其在几何误设下的实用价值，同时保持理论保证。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于因果基础模型（Causal Foundation Models, CFMs）的实践性介绍论文，介绍了如何将基础模型的范式应用于因果推断任务。CFMs是预训练的神经网络，能够通过上下文学习在全新的数据集上估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果推断需要为每个新问题设计定制化流程：提出因果机制、选择兼容的估计器、训练模型。同时，机器学习已转向基础模型范式——一次大规模预训练，无需微调即可应用于新任务。作者旨在将这一范式引入因果推断，解决传统流程效率低下的问题。

Method: 1. 基于先验数据拟合网络（PFN）框架，使用Transformer架构实现上下文学习；2. 设计因果先验数据损失（causal prior-data loss）训练模型直接从观测数据映射到因果量后验预测分布（PPD）；3. 针对不同因果估计目标（CEPO-PPD、CID-PPD、CDTE-PPD）设计对应的损失函数；4. 使用基于结构因果模型（SCM）的合成先验，通过观察数据和模拟干预数据生成训练样本；5. 比较三种代表性CFM：Do-PFN、CausalPFN、CausalFM，它们在预测目标、先验设计、识别策略和架构上各有不同。

Result: 在RealCause-Lalonde基准测试上，CausalPFN取得了CFM中最低的平均排名，接近经过充分调参的T-Learner基线，并在Lalonde-CPS上优于后者。在ATE相对误差方面，CausalPFN表现最佳（0.17），而Do-PFN和CausalFM接近1，呈现系统性收缩。运行速度方面，CFMs比传统估计器快1-2个数量级。CausalPFN在CPU上运行最快，仅需18.4秒，而传统方法需要901-2707秒。

Conclusion: CFMs作为因果推断的新范式展现了显著效果，大幅减少部署时间的同时保持了竞争力。本文通过Jupyter笔记本和代码示例为读者提供实践指引。未来方向包括：支持多治疗场景的统一模型、整合更多数据源和领域知识、改进先验覆盖度的定量度量、扩展到因果发现和领域特定应用。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [122] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出了一种神经算子框架,将预训练局部算子与可学习的组合模块分离设计。局部算子在小子域上预训练后冻结,面对新场景(如更大计算域或新的运行工况)时,仅训练轻量级的组合模块来协调局部预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在面对训练分布外的几何、尺寸或运行条件时精度下降,而传统的监督适应需要昂贵的高保真模拟。本文旨在通过协同设计预训练与迁移机制,降低部署到新场景的成本。

Method: 核心方法是"局部预训练 + 可学习组合"的两阶段流程:(1)在固定大小的子域上预训练局部神经算子,使其掌握基本的局部场响应;(2)冻结该算子,训练一个轻量级Transformer组合模块,该模块接收各子域的初次预测并输出对各子域输入条件的修正,然后再次调用同一冻结算子得到最终场。理论上将该误差分解为"局部覆盖误差"和"组合选择误差"两项之和,并与全域Transformer(Point/Slice/Patch)和迭代式神经DDM(如Schwarz Neural Inference, SNI)对比。

Result: 在两项任务上验证:(1) Darcy流动:在16个目标仿真适应后,大尺寸域(4×4至16×16子域)的相对L2误差比容量匹配的全域方法低36–56%,比迭代式SNI快2.34×–4.91×;(2) 俯仰翼型非定常流动:在快俯仰频率(超出训练范围)上,H20(20步)场预测误差零样本下比最佳全域方法低28%,适应后比SNI低19–25%、比最佳适应全域方法低31–33%。

Conclusion: 共同设计的局部预训练与组合层迁移可作为物理基础模型的设计原则——广泛预训练的局部算子提供可复用的场响应,任务相关的轻量组合模块以有限数据完成适应。未来工作将研究组合模块设计、跨物理系统扩展局部预训练,以及将LatentDDM用作控制任务的可微分求解器。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [123] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream提出了一种用于设备端LLM推理的流式"推测-精炼"框架，通过细粒度的GPU-I/O协同设计，解决激活稀疏推理中预测精度与计算-存储重叠效率之间的核心矛盾，显著降低内存占用并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理受限于DRAM容量，需将权重卸载到SSD/闪存并利用激活稀疏性动态加载子模型。但存在核心系统权衡：精确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文（导致串行化），而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测（导致冗余权重加载和缓存开销）。

Method: 1) 流式推测-精炼框架：利用GPU部分中间结果持续精炼计算、加载和缓存保留优先级；2) 线程块级非阻塞非对称协调原语，基于SoC统一内存架构实现GPU-CPU细粒度通信；3) 基于随机模型预测控制的动态流控策略（离线求解+在线查表）；4) 基于神经元共激活分布嵌入的I/O放置优化和置换不变执行；5) 堆叠可学习哈希作为轻量级预测器（基于位运算和表查找），替代传统MLP控制器。

Result: 在Mistral-7B、Llama2-7B、Qwen2.5-7B三个模型，Jetson AGX Orin/Xavier和OnePlus 13三个平台上，相比DejaVu和PowerInfer-2等基线：在先验最优吞吐量下内存占用降低4.8×-7.5×，token生成吞吐提升1.6×-2.1×；缓存缺失率在25%内存预算下仅0.11（vs LRU 0.81）；预测器仅需23-24MB和<100μs延迟。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过统一"等待最新上下文"与"长程预测"两种策略，以渐进式精炼方式实现细粒度计算-I/O协调，在资源受限的移动和嵌入式平台上实现了显著的设备端LLM推理效率提升，且不损失模型精度。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [124] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在矩阵式连续思维链模型中，对潜在矩阵 Z 进行秩-k 投影消融后，准确率几乎不变（波动 <0.6 个百分点），表明模型对该操作不敏感；同时实验证实这种"秩不敏感性"可能源于位置无关性而非真正的秩结构编码缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 若矩阵型潜变量以叠加方式编码多条并行推理路径，则其矩阵秩应反映并行分量数；据此假设，对 Z 做低秩截断应会损害依赖多分量协同的任务精度。本文旨在检验该假设。

Method: 在矩阵-CODI 模型（ProsQA 三种训练机制 + GSM8K-Aug 未达学习阈值的一种）上实施秩-k 投影消融；用三粒种复现；以双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU、SVD 增强、Z Z^T 二次型四种读出器排除读出端平坦性；以原始隐藏态线性探针对比 AUC；在 GPT-2 SFT 上做相同干预作为位置无关性负对照。

Result: 秩-k 曲线在所有四种训练机制与四种读出器下均平坦（Spearman p ∈ {0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46}）；三粒种精度 81.0±2.0 pp 时有效秩跨越 {4, 12, 13}；线性探针 AUC 0.673 低于原始隐藏态 0.846；GPT-2 SFT 负对照在相同干预下亦呈平坦曲线（池化均值极差 0.20 pp），与随机敏感度下限同精度。

Conclusion: 在所用范式下，秩-k 消融本身无法区分"秩盲"与"位置无关"，因此该负结果不能证伪潜在 Z 以秩编码并行推理路径的假设；解读矩阵型 CoT 潜变量是否承载叠加结构需要规避位置无关性混淆的更严格探针设计。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [125] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用深度光流（RAFT/WindFlow）替代GEO-GEO立体风场反演中的窗口互相关（NCC）特征跟踪，并通过可微求解器端到端微调、进而蒸馏为单星学生模型，实现全圆盘三维风场（u, v, h）反演，消除对NWP背景的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV使用窗口互相关和NWP红外亮温高度赋值，存在循环依赖、高计算成本和稀疏反演问题；立体风虽几何独立于NWP但仍计算昂贵且覆盖有限（仅重叠区约33%）。本文旨在用深度光流提高立体风密度与效率，并通过知识蒸馏将覆盖扩展到全球静止气象卫星全圆盘。

Method: 1) 将WindFlow（RAFT骨干）光流替代NCC计算4对视差场（A-/A+, B-, B+），输入可微5状态加权最小二乘（WLS）求解器恢复高度h、位置修正p和速度V。2) 联合自监督几何残差χ²、无线电探空仪风监督（Huber损失）和高度锚定（KL正则）端到端微调。3) 用微调后的立体模型作为教师，在重叠区生成密集伪标签，通过异方差高斯KL蒸馏训练单星学生模型（53通道输入），并学习复制教师的σ和χ²。

Result: 1) 预训练WindFlow与Carr NCC立体风高度一致（偏差-0.12 km，RMS 1.20 km，r=0.93），密度提升约208倍。2) 探空仪微调使五个红外波段聚合RMSVD从17.25降至5.88 m/s（提升18.9%），消除约2 m/s慢速偏差；水汽波段（6.2/6.9/7.3 μm）提升最大（35-46%）。3) 三重交叉验证显示立体风本征误差在水汽波段全面低于NOAA业务AMV（C08: 3.31 vs 3.76；C09: 4.82 vs 5.17；C10: 4.77 vs 5.28 m/s），但长波窗C14（11.2 μm）相反（4.17 vs 3.04）。4) 蒸馏学生模型将覆盖从33%扩展至全圆盘，代价为1.34 m/s RMSVD退化；EarthCARE CPR/ATLID过境证实教师正确恢复双层相反风向，立体高度与雷达回波结构吻合。

Conclusion: 深度光流立体风与业务AMV互补：水汽通道优势显著，窗区通道被NCC超越。单星蒸馏模型以可接受精度换取全球覆盖。未来工作包括：学习观测权重替代W=I、引入辐射纹理掩膜筛除虚假平滑外推、利用多对GEO（如GOES-East与MTG）扩展教师训练地理，以及从高度时间序列反演垂直速度w并期待下一代Aeolus Doppler LiDAR提供晴空参考。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 {\mu}m), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 {\mu}m) band.

</details>


### [126] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次在精算定价（actuarial ratemaking）的表格数据场景下系统性地研究了神经网络的缩放规律（scaling laws）。基于一个约450万条记录的机动车保险数据集，作者训练了GLM、FFN、多类Transformer变体（含Multi-CLS、TokenMoE、自监督学习等改进）以及TabM/TabM-mini等模型族，以Poisson偏差作为评估指标，按数据量、参数和计算量三个维度拟合幂律关系。研究发现TabM/TabM-mini表现出最强的数据缩放能力，而纯监督的Transformer在参数缩放上表现较弱，必须借助额外的归纳偏置（如TabM适配）或自监督信号才能有效扩展。论文还推导了Chinchilla风格的最优资源分配规则，为精算模型选择提供了基于数据规模的定量指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中的缩放规律主要在LLM场景下被发现和验证，但精算定价涉及低信噪比、异构且嘈杂的表格数据，且GLM仍是强有力的基线。该论文旨在回答一个关键问题：类似的缩放规律是否也存在于精算表格任务中？不同模型族在数据量增加时的表现如何？模型选择应如何随数据规模变化？这些定量关系还能为精算实践中的模型治理和数据投资决策提供原则性依据。论文还将传统可信度理论（credibility theory）与现代缩放规律联系起来。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 数据：约450万条保单期记录，多家保险公司匿名化机动车数据，90/10 IID划分，嵌套训练子集{f ∈ 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00}；(2) 特征工程：所有变量通过分位数分箱转为类别索引，学习实体嵌入；(3) 模型族：GLM、FFN（5个尺寸）、vanilla Transformer、MultiCLS Transformer、Enhanced/head-fix Transformer、TokenMoE、TokenMoE+SSL（交换式自监督）、TabM、TabM-mini及Transformer+TabM混合体；(4) 训练：AdamW，Poisson偏差损失，5个随机种子做种子平均集成，早停机制；(5) 评估：保留下来的测试集上的Poisson偏差；(7) 缩放规律拟合：形式为L(X) ≈ L∞ + A·X^(-k)的数据/参数/计算三轴幂律拟合，以及Chinchilla风格的联合拟合L(P,N) ≈ L∞ + A_N·N^(-α) + A_P·P^(-β)。

Result: 核心发现是精算表格数据中存在平滑的幂律缩放行为。数据缩放指数：TabM/TabM-mini表现出最强(α ≈ 0.31-0.41，包含全数据扩展)，显著高于纯监督Transformer(α ≈ 0.18-0.20)。参数缩放指数：TabM最强(β ≈ 0.148)，TokenMoE+SSL次之(β ≈ 0.055)，纯监督Transformer最弱(β ≈ 0.025-0.037)。Transformer需要TabM风格的适配或交换式自监督才能有效缩放。模型选择存在数据规模依赖：小数据时GLM仍有竞争力，Transformer+TabM在5%-25%数据时最优，TabM在75%-100%数据时领先。最佳模型为TabM-mini(gigantic)，在全部数据上Poisson偏差为0.28885。Chinchilla风格分析显示TabM的参数与数据协同增长分配比例β/α ≈ 0.36，参数增长快于数据增长。种子平均集成对纯监督Transformer收益最大(平均0.001003)，对TabM较小(0.000360)。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个主要结论：(1) 精算频率模型可处于真正的缩放区间，所有模型族的测试Poisson偏差均随数据增加而平滑下降，遵循幂律；(2) 模型族与缩放资源同等重要，GLM在数据有限时仍难超越，TabM/TabM-mini在百万级数据下能更有效利用额外数据和参数；(3) Transformer在表格场景下的有效缩放需要额外的归纳偏置或训练信号，纯增加模型大小收效甚微。对精算实践的意义在于：从~10^5到~10^6行的扩展阶段，算计最优选择发生转移，高容量模型值得投资；自监督预训练可缓解有标签数据的稀缺。论文提出若干新型Transformer改进（Multi-CLS池化、层间v嵌入、TokenMoE路由、交换式自监督），虽然增强了Transformer的缩放能力，但仍无法匹敌最强的TabM-mini，提示归纳偏置设计仍是该领域的主导因素。论文存在若干局限：仅基于单一数据集和单一险种，未包含梯度提升树基线，未进行时间序列验证，外推至更大数据集需额外实验。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [127] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经场(NF)中经典神经正切核(NTK)回归的两个核心局限——函数空间的线性本质与缺乏可复用任务先验——提出了三种算法:NTK-KIP(通过蒸馏诱导点集注入非线性)、MetaQuill(元学习共享初始化实现特征学习与快速适应)以及融合两者的MetaQuill-KIP。实验表明,该混合方法在极稀疏观测下能实现高PSNR重建与语义合理的修复,且无需依赖大型预训练扩散先验,仅需秒级单图适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场在从稀疏观测进行快速高质量重建时面临挑战。经典NTK回归虽提供闭式解,但本质上是线性的,无法积累可复用任务先验;同时缺乏真正的特征学习能力与跨任务迁移能力,使其在稀疏观测修复等需要语义理解的任务中表现受限。扩散模型虽强大但依赖大型预训练先验且单图调优成本高。因此,需要一种既能注入非线性表达力,又支持元学习快速适应,且自包含的NTK驱动神经场方法。

Method: 三种互补算法:(1) NTK-KIP——通过优化少量诱导坐标及其标签(可微分通过NTK回归),将NTK拟合从"求解所有像素"转为"学习小支持集再求解",从而注入非线性并降低核成本;(2) MetaQuill——基于有限宽度NTK线性化,同时学习共享初始化θ_S与每任务小偏移Δθ,模拟MAML但无需昂贵内循环,实现特征学习与跨任务迁移;(3) MetaQuill-KIP——先用KIP生成非线性热启动残差Δθ_KIP,再围绕冻结的θ_S对Δθ进行少量非线性梯度精炼,统一了非线性表达力、元学习可复用性与秒级快速适应。架构为5层MLP(宽度256),输入使用原始或傅里叶位置编码。

Result: 在MNIST上,MetaQuill-KIP经50步精炼即达32.49 dB PSNR(约0.46秒),显著优于纯核回归(10.77 dB)、纯tangent MetaQuill(18.32 dB)及DDPM(16.09 dB)/GSDM(6.70 dB)。在Flowers 180×180 RGB上,全监督MetaQuill-KIP达26.29 dB(约3.12秒)。随机掩码修复在中等至高观测率(φ∈{0.5,0.8})时PSNR达18.89/21.71 dB,优于SD-IA在中等覆盖率下的表现(17.56 dB),并展现出比Single-INR更语义一致的孔洞填充。NTK-KIP在FPE下以20%观测达54.67 dB、95%观测达20.43 dB。整体在极稀疏观测(1%像素)下仍能合成语义合理的花瓣与叶片结构。

Conclusion: 工作证明NTK驱动的神经场可同时具备非线性和可元学习性,弥合了解析核方法与实际少样本重建之间的差距。NTK-KIP解决了NTK的非线性缺失但仅限单实例;MetaQuill解决了快速适应与可重用性但仍局部线性;MetaQuill-KIP融合二者,产出快速、自包含、真正非线性的元学习神经场重建流程,适用于边缘设备或单场景个性化等无法依赖大型扩散先验的场景。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [128] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了MoE+LoRA多领域微调中的一种隐蔽故障模式——尽管专家路由近乎完全分离（领域间专家使用重叠度仅≈0.056），近乎正交的领域梯度仍会在共享的低秩适配器子空间中产生干扰，导致显著的负迁移。作者提出Jaccard路由重叠和适配器梯度余弦相似度两种诊断工具，并据此提出SpawnLoRA方法：当检测到适配器级冲突时，在专家内部动态添加带ReLU门控的子适配器（保持路由器固定），以提供结构化分离。实验表明，在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B两个模型上，SpawnLoRA在生物医学和数学推理两个冲突领域设置下均优于固定秩LoRA和秩自适应DR-LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE架构上为多个领域进行参数高效微调时，常见做法是将LoRA适配器附加到MoE专家上，并假设token级路由足以分离不同领域的更新。但这一假设从未被严格验证过——即使路由成功分离了专家选择，领域特定的梯度更新仍可能在专家内部的共享低秩适配器中相互干扰，造成负迁移。本文的动机即在于诊断并解决这种"路由足够吗？"的根本问题。

Method: 1) **诊断工具**：Jaccard路由重叠（衡量专家共享程度）和适配器梯度余弦相似度（衡量更新兼容性）。2) **SpawnLoRA方法**：每个专家内保留冻结权重W_k和始终活跃的基础适配器L_{k,0}，当检测到冲突时（适配器重要性分数停滞 + 领域间EMA损失差距超过阈值δ，且两条件在滚动窗口W内持续满足），通过SVD从残差梯度方向初始化并新增带ReLU门控的子适配器L_{k,j}（B_{k,j}=0初始化保证不立即改变输出）。3) **触发条件**：双变量触发——基础适配器参数范数重要性分数的斜率小于τ（表示停滞），且两个领域的EMA损失差距超过δ（表示冲突持续）。4) **训练设置**：将Python代码（HumanEval）作为主领域，分别与生物医学文本（PubMedQA，严重冲突）和数学推理（GSM8K，中等冲突）配对，设置单一域（Run A，0%）、80/20混合（Run B）、50/50混合（Run C）三种数据配比，2000样本、3000步训练。

Result: 1) **诊断发现**：在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct上，路由Jaccard重叠仅≈0.056（路由几乎完全分离），但梯度余弦相似度为−0.002±0.003（近正交），证实干扰源于共享适配器子空间内的梯度竞争而非路由冲突。2) **主结果**：在生物医学冲突下，Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct的Run C（50%）上，SpawnLoRA将负迁移从LoRA的+683.16和DR-LoRA的+1128.09降至+613.94；OLMoE-1B-7B的Run B（20%）上，SpawnLoRA完全消除负迁移（−4.85），Run C降低26.9%代码困惑度。3) **MBPP和语法通过率**：OLMoE严重冲突下，SpawnLoRA的MBPP token准确率为33.18%（LoRA为19.71%），语法通过率为30.0%（LoRA为22.0%，DR-LoRA仅2.7%）。4) **多层扩展**：在Phi上额外启用L1或L3层的spawn可将代码困惑度从684.52降至361.24和352.45（甚至转为正收益）。5) **关键反直觉发现**：DR-LoRA在严重冲突下的表现劣于固定秩LoRA，表明在近正交梯度条件下扩展共享秩反而会加剧冲突。6) **参数效率**：SpawnLoRA的峰值容量仅相当于固定秩LoRA r≈17（OLMoE）和r≈21（Phi），直接固定秩r=17对比LoRA仅减少6%和2%负迁移，而SpawnLoRA减少83%和44%，证实增益源于结构分离而非参数增加。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个核心结论：(1) MoE中成功的token级路由与适配器级干扰可以共存，路由分离不足以防止多领域负迁移；(2) 干扰主要源于近乎正交的领域梯度在共享低秩适配器子空间内的竞争（作者称之为"intra-adapter subspace contention"）；(3) 在专家内部添加门控子适配器以提供结构化分离比扩展共享秩或单纯依赖路由更能有效减少负迁移。论文结论是：**结构化分离优于共享容量扩展**。主要局限性包括：领域配对覆盖有限、模型规模在1–8B参数范围、缺乏功能性下游任务评估（如HumanEval pass@1在训练规模下不具信息量）、架构覆盖仅限两个MoE家族，DeepSeek-V2等具有共享专家设计的架构可能呈现不同的冲突模式。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [129] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了Anthropic前沿推理大模型（Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.8、Opus 5）在连续与离散黑盒优化（BBO）任务中作为批优化器的性能。研究发现：连续数值任务上LLM与GP-EI贝叶斯优化竞争力相当但表现脆弱（受batch size、维度、函数变换影响且存在记忆泄漏）；而在离散分子优化（PMO基准）任务中表现优异，仅用210次oracle调用预算即在多个任务上超越多数专用方法，凸显其作为离散空间优化先验的强大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿推理LLM在编程、数学、科学发现等领域展现强大能力，但其在黑盒优化（BBO）场景——尤其是现代推理模型的批优化能力——尚未被充分评估。科学场景（如小分子药物发现）中目标函数评估昂贵且搜索空间复杂，因此亟需量化LLM作为优化策略的有效性、失败模式及改进方向。

Method: 采用Anthropic三款推理LLM（Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.8、Opus 5）作为自回归策略π(y|x)，对比单轮与多轮协议、batch size q∈{1,2,4}。(1) 连续任务：5个经典测试函数（Branin、Hartmann-3/6、Ackley、Rastrigin）的原始版与伪装版（域双射+仿射输出变换以减少训练集记忆利用），预算N=120，与GP-EI及随机基线对比简单遗憾。(2) 离散任务：PMO基准全部23个oracle，仅用210次oracle调用预算（10种子+20批×10），以Top-10 AUC和Top-10均值与9种专用方法对比，并测量样本效率倍数。

Result: 连续任务中：Opus 5多轮在多数任务上优于GP-EI，但batch size增大、维度升高（Hartmann-6/Ackley/Rastrigin）及伪装变换会显著降低性能，模型存在函数名记忆泄漏现象（从推理trace中可检测）；离散PMO任务中：Opus 5多轮以210次调用在17/23 oracle上Top-10 AUC最佳、20/23 Top-10均值最佳，平均样本效率远超REINVENT、SynNet等专用方法，与GenMol相当（平均0.596 vs 0.590）但低于ExLLM（0.738）。

Conclusion: 前沿推理LLM作为批优化先验在离散、结构类似预训练数据的设置中表现卓越且样本高效，无需额外微调或脚手架；连续数值优化场景中虽与GP-EI相当但表现脆弱。模型代际（Sonnet→Opus 5）与多轮对话状态均显著提升性能。未来方向包括扩展至更多BBO问题设定及设计专门增强开源推理模型BBO能力的中后训练协议。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [130] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将因果公平性干预（FTU、CF、DemPar）从DECAF框架移植到九种合成数据生成器，跨越边际合成、GAN和扩散三大类，并在差分隐私保证下验证。核心发现是因果分解而非特定架构承载了公平性机制；新提出的因果扩散骨干在公平性和保真度上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计机构发布合成数据替代敏感记录已成趋势，但DECAF的公平性干预仅限于单一非私有GAN，未验证机制是否属于因果分解本身。当公平性机制局限于不可用的生成器时，机构实际上无法获得该干预手段。

Method: 将三种公平性定义实现为单一接口（FTU切断直接边S→Y；DemPar切断所有S↝Y路径；CF保留经由可接受属性A的路径）。该接口应用于三类共九种生成器：（1）边际合成器MST、PrivBayes、PrivSyn（对无向图增量检查路径约束）；（2）因果GAN（DECAF、CTGAN变体及DP-SGD变体，保留DAG上的代理替换）；（3）因果扩散（用监督噪声预测的小去噪器替换子网络）。在Adult和COMPAS数据集上，每个数据集三个角色分割，五个种子，共2,520次匹配对运行。六种生成器携带差分隐私保证，ε∈{1,10,1000}，δ=10⁻⁹。

Result: 机制可移植性得到验证：所有三个定义在九种生成器、两个数据集、每个隐私预算下均能减少下游人口统计奇偶差距，DemPar>CF>FTU的预测顺序在六个家族×数据集单元格中成立五次。因果扩散骨干产生最公平的发布——CF后平均奇偶差距0.099，DemPar后0.081，优于因果GAN（0.128/0.089）和边际合成器（0.152/0.136）。公平性干预对保真度影响极小（2-way TVD偏移仅0.001-0.004），下游分类器AUC成本CF约0.07、DemPar约0.15。差分隐私预算收紧不影响公平性效果。DP-GAN在47%运行中失败（结果崩溃为单一类别），原因是DP-SGD应用于权重裁剪D判别器。每条边的奇偶差距削减率跨图类型基本一致（≈0.03），但DAG边切割的AUC成本约为边际图边切割的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 边手术属于因果分解而非DECAF特定架构。机构可选择合成器时兼顾隐私与数据质量，同时保留可移植的因果公平性干预。边际合成器在保真度和隐私上仍为首选；因果扩散在公平性干预必须有力时为最优选择；因果GAN在保真度和效用上落后。生成器内的干预是唯一随表本身交付的干预方式，下游缓解措施可被使用者丢弃或替换。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [131] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioGlyph 将网络拓扑编译为可解释的结构角色语言描述，让冻结的小型开源LLM能够更好地进行网络结构推理，在20个网络、5个领域上显著优于边列表、文本描述、原始数值表和学习型图表示等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然在自然语言推理上表现出色，但在处理以边列表、句子或数值表形式给出的网络时，难以推断其结构含义，导致在判断节点中心性、跨社区连接、移除节点后果等结构推理任务上频繁出错。现有方法要么依赖LLM从连接中自行推断结构，要么使用难以解释的学习型数值表示，缺少一种既能被LLM理解又能被研究者理解的显式结构表示方式。

Method: BioGlyph结合图划分（Leiden算法）和结构度量（度、介数、PageRank、参与系数、k-core、边连通性等），通过固定规则识别11种结构角色（CUT_NODE、BRIDGE_EDGE、HUB、CROSS_COMMUNITY_CONNECTOR、COMMUNITY_CORE、BOUNDARY_NODE、BOTTLENECK_LINK、ISOLATE、PERIPHERAL、FRAGILE_REGION、AUTHORITY），将每个网络元素的结构角色、支撑证据和语义后果转化为自然语言描述。对比基线包括无边信息（R0）、边列表（R1）、邻接句子（R2）、原始度量表（R3）、学习型角色（R4，含GCN/GraphSAGE/GAT/GIN）。使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B两个冻结8B模型在20个网络、1239个基准问题上评估，答案由精确图算法判定。

Result: 在8个主网络1239个问题上，BioGlyph系统准确率达70.6%（95% CI: 69.4-71.9%），优于无边信息基线（50.7%）、边列表（48.7%）、邻接文本（49.4%）、原始度量表（39.5%）及所有四种学习型编码器（49.1-50.0%）。消融实验表明性能提升主要来自显式陈述角色的结构后果（consequence step贡献最大），而非仅仅列出角色名称。在密集、社区结构明显的网络中增益最大（最高26个百分点），在稀疏网络中优势缩小。BioGlyph的提示长度中位数仅为6,229个token（而原始度量表为19,723个），几乎所有提示都能装入24,576-token上下文窗口。在酵母蛋白互作网络中，跨社区连接器中必需基因占57.6%（网络平均值30.9%），社区核心46.9%，中心节点42.1%，而外围蛋白仅12.9%，表明结构角色具有独立的生物学意义。Reactome网络中cut节点对癌症选择性依赖的富集比为13.3% vs 8.8%（Fisher p=2.4×10⁻³），EP300移除后分离76个蛋白。BioGlyph还支持多轮对话推理（八轮准确率75.2%）并在被挑战时更好地保持正确答案。

Conclusion: BioGlyph通过将网络拓扑编译为简洁、可解释的结构角色描述，使冻结的小型语言模型无需修改即可利用网络信息进行结构推理。该方法在密集和社区结构网络中效果尤为突出，其优势源于选择性压缩和显式传达结构后果。在生物网络中，BioGlyph识别的结构角色与基因必需性和癌症依赖性等独立生物学指标相关，为定量网络分析与自然语言推理之间提供了可审计的接口。BioGlyph是对精确图算法的补充而非替代，适用于可在本地运行的开源模型，而前沿大模型在充足上下文窗口下差距缩小。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [132] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2026 PNPL竞赛发布LibriBrain100数据集(含33名被试的MEG语音数据)，设置Deep(单被试深度)和Broad(跨被试泛化)两条赛道，任务为从MEG信号中分类50个目标词，推动非侵入式语音脑机接口发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式脑机接口(BCI)需从分钟级数据泛化到新用户，而现有研究多依赖单一被试大量数据或侵入式方案。竞赛旨在通过标准化基准和大规模多被试MEG数据集，推动数据高效的跨被试语音解码研究，向临床可行的非侵入式BCI迈进。

Method: 基于LibriBrain100数据集(MEG录音，306通道)构建两条并行赛道：(1)Deep赛道使用单一被试(Subject 0)约80小时数据进行词分类；(2)Broad赛道使用32名被试的层级化数据(~40/~20/~10分钟)及8名零样本被试进行跨被试泛化评估。任务为在时间窗口(0.2-0.6秒)内预测50词词汇表中的目标词，主评估指标为Top-10平衡准确率(BAcc@10)，辅以开放词汇互信息(OVMI)用于跨词汇表比较。提供参考模型：Deep赛道使用d'Ascoli等(2025)监督模型，Broad赛道使用自监督基础模型MEG-XL。

Result: Deep赛道参考模型在竞赛词汇表上达到BAcc@1=25.60%、BAcc@10=73.23%；Broad赛道MEG-XL参考模型达到BAcc@1=6.20%、BAcc@10=42.96%。两者均显著超越随机基线(BAcc@10=20%)。竞赛已于2026年7月15日开放，10月15日截止，共设5000美元奖金，覆盖两赛道前三名。

Conclusion: 2026 PNPL竞赛通过大规模多被试MEG数据集LibriBrain100和双赛道设计(深度+广度)，首次将跨被试泛化作为非侵入式语音解码竞赛的核心目标，建立了从词分类到最终脑转文本(B2T)研究路径上的关键中间基准，为开发临床可行的非侵入式语音BCI提供了标准化社区基础设施。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours. The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [133] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文面向一次性销售、购买周期漫长的B2B营销场景，构建了一套从数据清洗、联系人聚合、企业级特征生成到销售漏斗转化预测与营销行动建议的完整框架。其核心流程包括：利用搜索与网页抓取、翻译、大小写及停用词处理、字符二元组编码、PCA和k-means进行公司名词聚类；再以词簇ID构建TF-IDF文档表示，通过阈值相似度和最大团对公司名分组；结合公司名、国家、城市和Account ID，利用连通分量将联系人聚合为93,568个客户账户；提取156项企业属性与营销活动特征，经筛选形成40个特征；最终以CatBoost进行转化预测。40特征模型在平衡后的2,194个正样本与2,194个负样本上取得91.0%的测试准确率，优于随机森林82.5%、逻辑回归74.7%和RNN 77.4%。论文还使用SHAP识别关键影响因素，并据此讨论缩短营销活动间隔、增加多人参与等行动建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B2B业务通常购买周期长、购买频率低，传统面向快消或电商的LRFM模型难以适用；同时，联系人级数据存在Account ID缺失、公司名拼写错误、缩写不一致、语言差异以及多地同名主体等问题。论文希望解决三个实际问题：将分散的联系人记录可靠聚合到客户账户、构造能够反映企业与多联系人营销互动的预测特征，并识别高潜客户以支持个性化营销活动。

Method: 数据预处理：对国家和城市做小写化、特殊字符清理、翻译及人工校正；对公司名删除无辨识度词，并通过公开企业资料搜索、抓取和模糊匹配进行纠错。公司名清洗方面，将词汇转为字符二元组one-hot表示，求和形成词向量，PCA保留95%方差后执行k-means，以词簇编号作为标准词表。字符串则表示为词簇ID的TF-IDF向量；余弦等价点积超过0.7的字符串连边，并依次提取各连通分量中的最大团，抑制传递相似导致的过度聚合，文中k取30,000。联系人聚合以公司名、国家和城市构成基础组，再以共享Account ID和国家的组建立边，通过连通分量确定账户。最终93,568条记录形成93,568个账户。每个账户提取156项特征，包括地区、区域、行业、收入、员工数、产品、活动次数、触达人数、触达次数、时间间隔、最近活动、类别子序列等；去除常量、高相关和零重要性特征，再以LightGBM重要性与SFFS筛至40项。预测采用CatBoost有序目标统计和梯度提升树处理数值与类别特征，并与随机森林、逻辑回归和RNN比较。模型在至少两次营销活动的账户中，以2,194名转化客户和随机抽取的2,194名未转化客户组成平衡数据，按75/25划分训练和测试。CatBoost以AUC选模，学习率0.15、最大10棵树、L2系数9；另以TreeSHAP解释特征及具体客户。

Result: 数据聚合阶段生成93,568个客户账户。特征阶段从156项中保留85个数值特征，再经SFFS选出33个，与7个类别特征共同构成40维输入。模型比较中，随机森林准确率由81.5%升至82.5%，逻辑回归由75.2%降至74.7%，CatBoost由90.2%升至91.0%，RNN为77.4%。因此，特征筛选结合CatBoost取得最高准确率。SHAP显示，平均相邻活动间隔不超过3个季度通常正向影响转化，平均每次活动参与人数超过1也具有正向作用。论文据此提出对高潜或低潜账户缩短触达间隔、鼓励多人参与同一活动等候选建议；但这些关系是相关性的预测解释，不构成因果证据。

Conclusion: 论文证明，先处理非标准公司名并以多键、图连通分量完成客户级聚合，再使用企业属性、营销统计和时间序列特征及CatBoost，可以在一次性销售型B2B场景中较准确地预测客户进入销售漏斗。40个筛选特征的CatBoost模型达到91.0%准确率，且优于所比较模型；SHAP进一步提供个体级特征解释和行动线索。不过，结论受不平衡采样、小测试集、单次随机划分、准确率而非业务增益指标、未报告置信区间及时间外验证等因素限制。SHAP建议不能直接解释为营销动作的因果效果，实际部署前应使用时间切分、留出验证、校准指标、统计不确定性分析及因果或准实验设计进行验证。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [134] [Selective Hypergraph Refinement for Frozen Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03265)
*Zimo Si*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究图聚类模型在训练完成并冻结后的无标签后处理问题。提出选择性超图精化方法(SHR)，利用属性超图生成候选残差方向，并结合图结构、节点属性和匹配零证据进行无标签可靠性评估，仅对支持充分的节点更新聚类分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类方法主要通过优化模型参数和节点表示来提升性能，但对于已训练冻结模型的聚类结果进行进一步改进的有效手段有限。全局超图精化可能带来性能提升也可能产生错误更新，核心问题在于哪些候选精化应当被接受以及干预应扩展到何种程度。

Method: 1) 利用属性超图构建多尺度属性上下文，通过K-means和匈牙利对齐生成候选残差Δ；2) 定义临界精化强度η_crit分析节点变化的隐式排序；3) 构建效应校准的候选路径（7个目标变化率）；4) 基于拓扑/属性证据、匹配零控制和覆盖复杂度进行无标签状态选择；5) 节点级LCB筛选和运行级属性溯源否决确定最终更新。

Result: 在受控公共套件评估中，15个骨干-数据集单元中13个获得正平均宏增益，单元均等宏增益为0.066pp（95% CI [0.030, 0.107]pp），平均仅改变0.209%的硬分配。更广泛的15组合原生接口评估获得0.137pp宏增益，平均变化率0.375%。SHR相比CS-BAYES灾难性运行更少（3 vs 12），但两者统计差异不显著。

Conclusion: 冻结的图聚类输出在训练后保留有限但可度量的精化空间。SHR作为冻结聚类输出的局部后处理而非新聚类结果的重建，其效果在骨干-数据集对之间存在显著异质性。源消融显示真实节点-超边对应相对随机化超图有增量贡献，但多尺度超图并未一致优于k-NN图或原始图传播。当前无标签证据在限制精化范围方面更可靠，但在跨数据集一致识别有益精化方面仍有限。

Abstract: Existing graph-clustering methods typically improve clustering performance by optimizing model parameters and node representations. Effective means of further improving the clustering results of an already trained and frozen model, however, remain limited. We study post-processing for frozen graph clustering. After checkpoint fixation, the procedure uses no labels and updates neither model parameters, node representations, nor the original graph structure. Instead, it exploits an attribute hypergraph to supplement higher-order relations that ordinary graphs cannot readily express, thereby refining existing cluster assignments. Because global hypergraph refinement can yield both performance gains and erroneous updates, we propose Selective Hypergraph Refinement (SHR). The method generates candidate residual directions from the hypergraph and evaluates their reliability using graph structure, node attributes, and matched-null evidence. It updates only nodes with sufficient support and otherwise retains their original assignments. Further analysis shows that whether a node changes cluster is jointly governed by its native assignment gap and the directional strength of the refinement. In a controlled common-suite evaluation, 13 of 15 backbone-dataset cells had a positive mean macro gain, one produced exact no-action, and one was negative. The cell-equal macro gain was 0.066 pp (95% bootstrap CI, [0.030, 0.107] pp), while only 0.209% of hard assignments changed on average. A broader 15-combination native-interface evaluation yielded a macro gain of 0.137 pp at a mean change ratio of 0.375%. These results indicate that frozen clustering outputs retain a limited but measurable refinement space after training. The effect is heterogeneous across backbone-dataset pairs, and broader coverage also increases exposure to negative transfer.

</details>


### [135] [Latent Energy Action Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03294)
*Phu Pham,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAP是一种基于冻结LeWorldModel的可微分动作规划器，通过结合潜在目标匹配与解码器预测的终端状态匹配能量项，使用L-BFGS优化器在四个控制任务上将平均成功率从77.5%提升至94.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的潜在世界模型进行规划时，仅优化单一潜在目标会导致优化循环漏洞：规划器可能选择使解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符不一致的动作序列，即使潜在目标得分降低，物理动作也会偏离演示数据。

Method: LEAP将完整动作视界视为可微分变量，通过冻结的LeWM自回归展开进行反向传播。能量由两部分组成：LeWM原生潜在目标成本（终端潜在匹配）和终端窗口状态能量（解码器预测的终端描述符与目标描述符匹配）。使用冻结的目标条件动作提议提供初始化，L-BFGS拟牛顿求解器优化动作，优化后将动作投影到合法范围。

Result: 在Push-T、OGBench-Cube、Reacher、TwoRooms四个任务上，使用官方LeWM检查点，LEAP将平均成功率从LeWM+CEM的77.5%提升至94.8%，提升17.3个百分点，每 trial额外增加0.08秒规划时间。能量消融实验表明潜在目标与终端状态匹配互补，λ_s=10为最优权重。

Conclusion: LEAP通过可微分动作规划与跨表征终端能量一致性，将冻结的世界模型从基于采样的评分器转变为可微分的动作规划模型，在不重训练表征或动力学的前提下显著提升任务成功率，未来可扩展到几何感知的视觉目标表示。

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model predictive control from high-dimensional observations, yet optimizing a single learned latent objective can favor action sequences whose decoder-predicted terminal descriptor does not match the goal descriptor. We introduce Latent Energy Action Planning (LEAP), which treats the complete action horizon as a differentiable variable and optimizes it through a frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM). LEAP couples terminal latent goal matching with a terminal-window state energy. Low energy requires the predicted terminal latent to agree with the goal latent and the decoder-predicted terminal descriptor to agree with the goal descriptor. A frozen goal-conditioned proposal initializes the search, a quasi-Newton solver refines actions through the autoregressive rollout, and post-optimization projection enforces the admissible action range. Across four control domains using the officially released LeWM checkpoints, the complete LEAP planning system raises mean success from 77.5% for LeWM planned with the cross-entropy method (LeWM+CEM) to 94.8% under a matched protocol, a 17.3-percentage-point improvement, while retaining the frozen LeWM representation and predictor.

</details>


### [136] [Geometry-Aware Graph Construction via Adaptive Spectral Bandwidth Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03306)
*Ecem Bozkurt,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高斯核图构造的逐节点自适应带宽选择准则，通过对齐核的有效秩与局部内蕴维度来选择合适的带宽尺度，从而在谱聚类、扩散映射、NNK图等核化图方法中改善下游推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核图方法（如谱聚类、扩散映射、NNK图）的性能高度依赖带宽σ的选择：σ过小导致核趋近单位矩阵、图碎片化；σ过大导致核坍缩为秩1矩阵、条件数发散，几何判别性丧失。现有带宽选择方法（基于邻域距离、密度归一化、对数-对数尺度等）均缺乏统一原则，未将"核算子的谱复杂度"与"局部流形的几何复杂度"进行匹配。

Method: 1. 提出**谱复杂度原则**：当核算子谱所揭示的复杂度（用有效秩 $r_{\mathrm{eff}}$ 度量）与局部数据流形的内蕴复杂度（用基于最小生成树的 dimMST 估计量 $\hat{d}$ 度量）一致时，核处于正确尺度。2. 提出**逐节点带宽优化准则** $J_i(\sigma)$，由两项组成：（a）谱-流形复杂度匹配项 $|r_{\mathrm{eff}}-\hat{d}_i|/\hat{d}_i$；（b）流形尺度一致性项（对数-对数斜率惩罚）。通过对数间隔网格搜索最小化 $J_i(\sigma)$ 得到 $\sigma_i^\star$。3. 引入**权重锐化**作为后处理，依据 $\sigma_i^\star$ 与全局中位数 $\sigma_g$ 的比值调整幂指数 $p_i$，重新分配边权重。复杂度为 $O(n \cdot k_{\mathrm{cand}}^2)$，每节点为常数时间操作。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上使用六种 SSL 编码器（SimCLR、MoCo v2、BYOL、Barlow Twins、VICReg、DINO）评估：1. **局部评估**：dimMST 与 $r_{\mathrm{eff}}$ 的 Spearman 相关性达 0.88–0.91，远高于 Levina–Bickel 的 0.43–0.63，证明 dimMST 在 $\ell_2$ 归一化嵌入的距离集中下更鲁棒。2. **LOO 分类**：顺序 kNN < kNN-σ-α < NNK-fixed < NNK-σ 在所有六种编码器上成立，NNK-σ 全面领先。3. **标签传播（LP）**：NNK-σ 在 CIFAR-100 所有编码器与标签比例下均最优，在 ≥10 标签/类时增益最显著。4. **消融验证**：带宽适应在密集图（kNN→kNN-σ-α）和稀疏图（NNK-fixed→NNK-σ）上均有提升，二者互补。5. **尺度敏感性**：当有效秩目标 $\gamma \hat{d}$ 中 $\gamma \geq 1.0$ 时准则鲁棒，$\gamma < 0.75$ 时带宽过小导致精度下降。

Conclusion: 所提出的逐节点带宽准则——将核的有效秩与基于 MST 的局部内蕴维度对齐——能够一致地产生更良态的图算子，并提升下游性能。该准则独立于具体图构造方法（NNK、kNN 等），可作为任何高斯核图构造的通用前置步骤。核有效秩应与局部内蕴维度保持一致这一原则，将谱图信号处理与底层数据流形的几何联系起来。

Abstract: Kernelized graph methods - spectral clustering, diffusion maps, and sparse kernel -regression graphs - that use Gaussian kernels depend on the choice of Gaussian bandwidth sigma, which governs the spectral character of the local kernel operator. When sigma is too small, the kernel overestimates local complexity and treats each sample as an independent direction; when sigma is too large, the kernel collapses multiple directions together, the condition number diverges, and all geometric discrimination is lost. We propose a choice of scale to make the spectral complexity of the kernel consistent with the intrinsic complexity of the underlying manifold. We propose a per-node bandwidth criterion that operationalizes this principle by jointly matching the kernel's effective rank to the local intrinsic dimension estimated via minimum spanning tree, anchoring the search in the manifold-consistent log-log scaling regime. We evaluate SSL embeddings from six encoders on CIFAR-100, showing that adaptive bandwidth consistently improves leave-one-out (LOO) classification and label propagation (LP) accuracy over fixed-bandwidth methods and competing adaptive methods.

</details>


### [137] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对配电网优化运行中风险与异常识别的联合问题，提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）与不确定性量化（UQ）相结合的二阶不确定性感知框架。该框架将分布强化学习与贝叶斯深度强化学习相融合，把总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（AU，表征内在运行风险）和认知不确定性（EU，用于分布外异常检测）。认知不确定性在训练阶段驱动探索，在部署阶段作为可靠性指标触发回退控制（切换至基于MISOCP的保守OPF控制器）。通过MV Oberrhein和IEEE欧洲低压网络两个测试案例的仿真表明，该方法在AU跟踪、EU异常检测（AUROC达0.9856）、探索效率以及EU触发的安全保障干预方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网因可再生能源、储能和分布式柴油发电机的引入而具有随机性、高维性和时序耦合性。传统DRL方法缺乏不确定性量化能力，在分布外场景（如异常负荷模式、极端天气、网络物理攻击）下可能产生自信但次优或不安全的动作。同时，现有的不确定性感知方法要么只关注单一类型的不确定性，要么将总不确定性作为聚合量处理，无法区分可约的认知不确定性和不可约的偶然不确定性，限制了其在安全关键配电网运行中的部署。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于CMDP的配电网运行建模，采用拉格朗日松弛处理约束，并通过Lagrangian奖励将约束优化转化为鞍点问题；(2) 基于IQN的分布式actor-Critic架构，结合MC Dropout和深度集成构建贝叶斯分布式critic，产生B=MK个随机回报分布样本；(4) 基于距离的二阶UQ方法，通过三个参考族（完全确定性S_tot、零偶然族S_al、零认知族S_ep）和Wasserstein-2距离将总不确定性分解为AU和EU。在回报空间中，利用分位数表示和Wasserstein重心实现可计算化，并证明其与原始定义的等价性及MC估计器的一致性。训练中EU作为内在好奇心奖励驱动探索，部署中EU作为阈值化回退信号，在EU超过阈值时切换至MISOCP基线OPF控制器。

Result: 在MV Oberrhein（20kV，153台发电机）和IEEE欧洲低压（907节点，286维动作空间）两个测试案例上：(1) AU估计值随环境噪声增大单调上升，且估计方差低于基线；(2) EU估计在分布外场景（观测噪声、FDIA攻击、负荷/PV偏移、频率域扰动、VAE近OOD、外部数据集）下显著高于分布内基线；(3) OOD检测AUROC达0.9856，优于KDE/KNN/GMM/Mahalanobis等特征空间基线；(4) EU与运行退化（奖励差距ΔR和约束成本差距ΔC）正相关，Spearman秩相关系数分别为0.61和0.46，显著优于基线；(5) 在ε_miss=0.10的闭环回退中，回退触发率75.8%，移除94.0%的约束成本差距和90.7%的奖励差距；(6) 探索效率方面，UQ探索获得最大的有用状态空间覆盖率和最高的可行区域比例。

Conclusion: 本文提出的二阶UQ分解方法成功地将DRL中的总不确定性分解为AU（表征不可约环境随机性导致的内在运行风险）和EU（反映因数据不足或分布偏移导致的模型不确定性）。EU在训练中作为内在好奇心信号驱动探索，在部署中作为认知可靠性门控触发MISOCP回退控制。该框架将现有分布DRL和贝叶斯DRL方法纳入其子模块，统一了风险敏感和分布外异常检测两个问题。仿真结果在配电网优化运行中验证了框架的有效性，表明不确定性感知是安全关键型DRL部署的必要前提。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [138] [DE-Venus: A Data-Efficient RLVR Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03324)
*Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Xing Zheng,Haobo Wang,Yingfan Ma,Bowen Song,Bo Han,Bo An,Lei Feng,Weiqiang Wang,Junbo Zhao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DE-Venus：面向LLM的RLVR数据高效统一框架，基于verl实现，支持7种代表性方法，通过主动数据选择、弱监督构建、训练时监督精炼三大模块，仅用10%标签或13%相关数据即可保持/超越全监督性能，并在业务场景中减少63%–75%收敛步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在扩展时面临两大瓶颈：on-policy rollout计算昂贵、大规模获取可靠标注成本高。现有方法（数据选择、弱监督、噪声标签处理）各自独立实现，将监督逻辑与分布式训练纠缠在一起，导致难以进行受控对比和复用，亟需一个统一的数据高效RLVR框架。

Method: 将监督视为跨数据准备与策略优化的演化状态，分为三个互补干预模块：(1) 主动数据选择——离线generate-score-route管道，按验证难度、不确定性或探针校准分配标注/训练预算；(2) 弱监督构建——方法级本地在线训练器，输出伪目标（TTRL、Co-Rewarding）或无目标奖励（Intuitor、EMRL）；(3) 训练时监督精炼——基于轨迹（TraPO）、表征几何（GeoMin）或在线标签修正（OLR）过滤/重加权/修正监督。基于verl，通过四类原生契约接口（target/reward/batch/advantage）实现方法特定决策的"最小入侵"集成，保留分布式执行后端。

Result: 在公开数学/通用推理基准上，GeoMin仅用10%标签即达47.9% ID和69.5% OOD，超全监督基线（46.7%/69.0%）；PivotTrace用57.9%训练数据+29.3%标注超全监督。噪声标签场景下OLR在ρ∈{0.1–0.9}全范围一致提升ID/OOD。三个业务场景中：信贷场景GeoMin带来14点归一化增益；医疗共情训练去除28%数据仍保持质量；内生安全训练仅用13%–30.67%数据，收敛步数减少63%–75%。

Conclusion: DE-Venus将RLVR数据效率问题建模为端到端监督生命周期问题而非孤立的训练集大小或奖励函数选择问题，通过在verl之上的轻量监督控制平面，实现了异构数据高效RLVR方法的统一实现、评估与扩展，为开发可复现的数据高效推理算法提供实践基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves large language model reasoning, but its practical scaling is constrained by expensive on-policy rollouts and the cost of obtaining reliable targets at scale. Existing methods address sample selection, incomplete supervision, or noisy labels separately, often entangling supervision logic with distributed training and hindering controlled comparison and reuse. We present DE-Venus, a unified framework for data-efficient RLVR that treats supervision as evolving state across data preparation and policy optimization. It organizes this lifecycle into three modules: Active Data Selection allocates training and annotation budgets; Weak Supervision Construction derives learning signals from unlabeled examples; and Training-Time Supervision Refinement filters or corrects unreliable supervision. DE-Venus supports seven representative methods and a data-selection pipeline by expressing method-specific decisions as offline dataset transitions or online transformations of targets, rewards, batches, and advantages while preserving verl's distributed execution contracts. Across public benchmarks and three business scenarios, separate configurations preserve or improve model quality with only 10% of labels or as little as 13% of relevant data; selected business configurations also reduce observed convergence steps by 63%--75%. DE-Venus thus reduces annotation and training costs without sacrificing scalable RL execution.

</details>


### [139] [A Large Open Multi-Energy Corpus of Soil Compaction Tests, with Machine-Learning Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03337)
*Sompote Youwai,Chana Phutthananon,Warat Kongkitkul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个大规模、多击实能量的土壤压实实验开放语料库（2,854条记录），并提出了基于机器学习的最大干密度（MDD）和最优含水率（OMC）预测模型及封闭公式表达式。研究通过零空气孔隙线筛选发现约11.8%的已发表压实数据在物理上不可能存在，并量化了Tatsuoka提出的最优饱和度为0.815（变异系数11%）。模型评估采用三种交叉验证方案（随机、分组按来源、整源留出），重点揭示了随机划分因来源泄漏而高估模型迁移能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有击实参数（MDD和OMC）估计的相关性研究受限于数据集规模小（100-400个试样）、通常来自单一实验室和单一击实能量、且数据很少公开。工程师面对新借土材料时，无法判断已发表相关性是否适用于类似土体，也无法在新实验室数据上验证模型。这促使作者建立一个大规模、多来源、公开可用的语料库，以解决数据稀缺和迁移能力不可知的问题。

Method: 1. 数据收集：遵循PRISMA 2020协议，通过DataCite API和OpenAlex搜索，从618个候选数据集中筛选出6个满足条件的数据源，共2,854条记录，涵盖162个来源组和4个Proctor能量等级。2. 数据筛选与协调：对粒径、击实能量和比重进行来源间协调审计；按零空气孔隙（ZAV）条件进行物理可行性筛选，剔除11.8%的记录。3. 建模方法：使用6个输入特征（塑限、塑性指数、细粒含量、砂含量、击实能量对数、比重），采用三类学习器（TabPFN、XGBoost、感知机）进行MDD和OMC预测；采用三种交叉验证方案（随机5折、按162个来源组分折、6个整源留出）评估。4. 符号回归：使用PySR搜索MDD和OMC的封闭公式表达式；通过相关系数关系（恒定最优饱和度）耦合密度和含水率预测，确保物理可行性。5. 消融研究：系统评估各输入特征组、一致性界限选择、击实能量对不同能量层级的影响。

Result: 1. 语料库：2,854条记录，涵盖四种Proctor能量等级，69%来自LTPP；公开数据集比现有相关性文献数据集大一个数量级。2. 物理可行性筛选：11.8%的协调记录超过零空气孔隙线；当比重为测量值时仅5.7%被剔除，表明文献中相当比例的压实数据在物理上不可能。3. 最优饱和度：整个语料库均值为0.815，变异系数11%，按土组分层从0.706（粗粒非塑性）到0.853（细粒高塑性）变化，说明该参数是基线而非恒定常数。4. 模型精度：TabPFN在随机划分下MDD R²=0.824，OMC R²=0.784；分组划分降至0.727和0.696；整源留出仅0.520和0.614，说明数据来源泄漏显著影响随机划分结果。5. 击实能量的条件性影响：在修改Proctor的66条记录上，有击实能量输入时R²=0.740，去除后降至-0.651，表明该变量在边际上不重要但在条件上决定性。6. 封闭公式：MDD和OMC的封闭公式在随机划分下达到R²=0.710和0.688；通过相关系数关系耦合后，预测对在所有设计下均不超过零空气孔隙线。7. 输入特征重要性：一致性界限（塑限和塑性指数）和级配（细粒含量）是主要载荷因素，各贡献R² 0.096-0.213；砂含量、击实能量和比重的单独贡献不超过0.030。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是构建了一个大型、多能量、公开的土壤压实实验语料库，附带每条记录的来源标识和完整的筛选协议。三个关键结论：(1) 通过零空气孔隙线筛选证明文献中约11.8%的压实数据物理上不可能，建议作为标准筛选步骤；(2) Tatsuoka框架的最优饱和度在整个语料库上均值为0.815（CV=11%），应作为基线而非材料无关常数；(3) 模型评估中随机交叉验证因数据来源泄漏严重高估了模型的迁移能力——将划分边界改为按来源分组在相同训练集规模下损失约0.10的R²，这要求所有关于新实验室迁移性的声明必须使用迁移导向的划分设计。封闭公式虽精度低于集成模型（约损失0.11 R²），但通过相关系数关系耦合后能保证物理可行性，适合写入规范供人工核查。该研究建议未来数据集应记录击实标准、报告测量比重、携带实验室标识符，以实现新实验室的迁移评估。

Abstract: Every engineered fill is specified by a maximum dry density and an optimum moisture content. Each determination needs a full Proctor test. Published correlations rest on one to four hundred specimens, usually from one laboratory at one compactive energy, and are seldom released. This paper releases a corpus without those limits. It holds 2,854 laboratory compaction tests from six public sources, across 162 provenance groups and four Proctor energy levels, with fines from 1.5 to 100%. Every record is audited to the Proctor method its source names, and no energy is inferred. Screening on the zero-air-voids condition removed 11.8% of harmonised records, and 5.7% of those with a measured specific gravity. A material share of published compaction data is physically impossible. The optimum degree of saturation over the corpus is 0.815 at a coefficient of variation of 11%. That is a baseline, not a constant. Both parameters are then estimated from one classification suite and the compaction standard. A tabular foundation model reaches R2 0.824 for density and 0.784 for water content under random folds. It reaches 0.727 and 0.696 with folds drawn around provenance, and 0.520 and 0.614 with a whole source held out. Compactive energy is negligible marginally yet decisive conditionally. Density on the 66 modified-Proctor records is predicted at R2 0.740 with it and -0.651 without. Symbolic regression yields closed forms coupled through a phase relation. No predicted pair can then exceed the zero-air-voids line. The predictions are for screening, not acceptance.

</details>


### [140] [Gradients Know What Outcomes Don't: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning with Gradient-Aligned Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03342)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Fang Niu,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Jie Wu,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度对齐的密集过程奖励机制GAR，用于解决RLVR中二元结果奖励无法区分正确推理轨迹的问题。GAR通过截断反向传播提取策略的梯度-激活向量，利用专家CoT作为锚点，通过余弦相似度计算密集奖励，仅增加不到9%的计算开销。实验证明GAR在数学竞赛基准上持续优于GRPO等基线，并能跨领域迁移到GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中二元结果奖励在多个正确轨迹间分配恒定奖励，无法区分推理质量，导致信用分配问题。现有密集奖励方法存在局限：忽略语料中已有的专家CoT；仅依赖表面启发式；或需要昂贵的离线过程标注。GAR旨在利用训练语料中免费提供的专家CoT，在梯度空间中构建推理感知的密集奖励信号。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1)截断反向传播仅通过输出投影层提取d维梯度-激活向量；(2)利用梯度与隐状态的逐元素乘积构造信号S_t = G_t ⊙ h_t；(3)以NuminaMath-CoT中专家CoT为锚点，计算余弦相似度得分作为密集奖励；(4)采用验证器门控确保仅对正确响应计算梯度；(5)在GRPO框架内进行组内居中和非负裁剪以保证策略安全的奖励塑形。理论上证明了余弦对齐与NTK对齐的双Lipschitz等价性，以及梯度对齐的乘法分解为预测误差因子和激活模式因子。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B模型上，GAR在IMO-AnswerBench、HMMT '25/'26、AIME '26四个数学竞赛基准上持续优于GRPO、REINFORCE++、Grad2Reward、G2RL等基线，pass@1最大相对增益达52.4%（HMMT 2025, 4B）。在GPQA Diamond和MMLU-Pro上零样本迁移效果优于所有基线。计算开销仅增加8.3%（低于9%），远低于Grad2Reward的+43.7%和G2RL的+37.1%。训练中余弦对齐均值从0.10增至0.42，方差缩小，表明奖励信号具有实际指导意义。

Conclusion: GAR成功将训练语料中已有的专家CoT转化为梯度空间参考向量，无需额外标注即可提供密集过程监督。在数学推理和通用推理任务上均验证了其有效性，同时具备轻量级在线计算、理论可解释性和策略安全性保证，为RLVR中解决正确轨迹间的信用分配问题提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models, yet its binary outcome reward cannot distinguish among correct trajectories. Existing dense reward alternatives, from surface heuristics to process reward models, either ignore the expert solutions already present in training corpora or require expensive offline annotation. We propose Gradient-Aligned Reward (GAR), which operates in the policy's own gradient space: truncated backpropagation through the output projection layer extracts a compact gradient vector for each rollout, and cosine similarity with an expert-anchor gradient yields a dense, reasoning-aware reward with less than 9% wall-clock overhead.
