We prove that this cosine admits a multiplicative decomposition into prediction-error and activation-pattern factors, providing a concrete characterization of what the alignment signal measures. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, GAR consistently improves over GRPO and other baselines on competition-level math benchmarks and transfers to GPQA Diamond and MMLU-Pro without domain-specific data. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [From Zero to Hero: An Open LLM Ecosystem for Armenian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03350)
*Erik Arakelyan,Khatun Avetisyan,Meri Davtyan,Heghine Grigoryan,Nane Khachatryan,Hayk Shahsuvaryan,Henrik Sergoyan,Vahan Martirosyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对亚美尼亚语这一形态丰富、低资源语言，本文发布了一个端到端的开源大模型生态系统，包括两个高质量数据集 ArmWeb（437万篇亚美尼亚新闻文档）和 ArmSTEM（37.3万条翻译并验证的英亚双语STEM问题），并通过对 Gemma-4-E4B 进行持续预训练得到 arm-gemma-e4b，成为首个附带完整训练数据和配方公开发布的开源亚美尼亚语大模型，在多项评测中超越所有现有开源亚美尼亚语模型及其基座。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚美尼亚语虽然有多语种网页抓取切片，但缺乏针对性的语料筛选与开源模型生态；现有开源模型只发布权重，不公开训练数据和训练流程，无法复现或审计；评估基准还存在与训练语料严重污染的问题，导致评测结果虚高。论文旨在填补亚美尼亚语 LLM 生态在开源训练数据、可复现配方和评测卫生方面的空白。

Method: 1）构建 ArmWeb：基于15年单运营者爬取的亚美尼亚新闻语料，采用轻量清洗、GlotLID 语言识别、MinHash+LSH 感知新闻转载的全局去重、多阶段泄漏门控和针对10个亚美尼亚评测集的13-gram 去污染；3）通过410M/1.3B 规模的对照消融比较 ArmWeb 与 CulturaX、HPLT-v2、FineWeb-2 等爬取切片，验证策展语料与爬取语料互补；4）构建 ArmSTEM：使用占位符掩码翻译 LaTeX 数学文本，通过"盲重做+答案一致"三阶段门控（G0/G1/G2）验证翻译质量，并进行双语双侧去污染；5）以 69% ArmWeb + 6% ArmSTEM + 20% 英语回放 + 5% 代码的五流混合，对 Gemma-4-E4B 进行10B token 的持续预训练，3e-5 学习率余弦调度，并通过缩放阶梯验证小型消融结论在大模型上的可迁移性。

Result: arm-gemma-e4b 在6任务似然套件上取得0.50的平均准确率，超过未适配基座 Gemma-4-E4B（0.48）以及 HyGPT-10b（0.44）、tweety-7b（0.35）、ArmenianGPT-1.0-3B（0.47）等所有开源亚美尼亚语模型；消融实验表明纯新闻持续预训练会出现典型的灾难性遗忘（在 Belebele 上损失21.2个百分点），降低学习率可恢复约2/3的损失，而仅用6%比例的 ArmSTEM 替换新闻份额即可使平均分反超基座2.2个百分点；缩放阶梯的幂律拟合可对独立的13亿参数模型以0.47%的外推误差预测损失；评测卫生扫描发现 CulturaX/HPLT-v2/FineWeb-2 三大公公开亚美尼亚语料与评测集有 7.9%-17.4% 的文档级重叠，包括 FineWeb-2 自带训练/测试集泄漏。

Conclusion: 通过严谨策展的语料和经过答案一致性验证的翻译STEM数据，可在低资源语言持续预训练中把"流利度换知识"的权衡转为净增益；该流程不依赖亚美尼亚语特有机制，可推广到其他低资源语言；开源生态的下一步包括 ArmSTEM 的竞赛数学子集和指令微调版 arm-gemma-e4b。

Abstract: Pretraining data for Armenian, a morphologically rich and low-resource language, is scarce, and no open Armenian LLM has been released with the data and recipe needed to reproduce it. To address this gap, we curate and release two datasets. ArmWeb is an extensively validated corpus of 4.37M Armenian news documents. ArmSTEM is a parallel English-Armenian collection of 373K math and science problems with step-by-step solutions, translated into Armenian and verified through both answer-preserving LLM judgment and human evaluation. Continued pretraining of Gemma-4-E4B on these datasets yields arm-gemma-e4b, which outperforms every existing open Armenian model as well as its unadapted base, and is the first open Armenian LLM with complete training data and recipe. Our ablations show that news-only continued pretraining improves fluency while eroding knowledge, a pattern we also observe in existing Armenian models, and that a small share of verified translated STEM data reverses the loss. We further find that the largest public Armenian corpora overlap web-derived evaluation panels heavily, including a train/test self-overlap inside FineWeb-2. We openly release all data, models, and code.

</details>


### [142] [Time Without Timesteps: Simulating Coupled Dynamical Systems via Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03358)
*Liyu Zerihun,Mark Shinyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 "Time Without Timesteps" (TWT) 框架,将耦合动力学子系统的仿真重新表述为完整轨迹上的自洽性不动点问题,而非逐步时间推进。该方法用学习到的轨迹算子替代传统波形松弛中的数值积分,通过 Newton-Krylov 方法求解耦合轨迹,并利用 GMRES 进行隐式微分。在 van der Pol 振荡器和 Hodgkin-Huxley 神经元网络上的实验表明,该方法可在 4-10 次 Newton 迭代(参考积分器需 1500 步)内完成耦合轨迹求解,且梯度计算内存与求解深度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力系统仿真以时间步进方式组织,状态依次计算。对于耦合系统,这种顺序结构导致:(1)前向求解深度与时间步数线性相关;(2)梯度计算要么需要展开积分器(内存线性),要么需要连续伴随方法(仍需反向扫描整个时域)。作者希望通过将耦合仿真重新表述为轨迹级不动点问题,从根本上改变计算结构,使求解深度独立于轨迹长度,并实现深度无关的内存梯度计算。

Method: 1. **模块级轨迹代理**:对每类子系统训练神经网络代理 S_φ,将完整驱动输入轨迹和初始条件映射到完整输出轨迹(残差 MLP,768 隐藏维度,6 个残差块,~7.5-8.6M 参数)。2. **自洽性求解**:耦合解定义为 X = F(X) 的不动点,其中 F(X)_i = S_φ(θ_i, g_i({X_j}), x_{i,0})。3. **Jacobian-free Newton-Krylov 求解**:用 Newton 法求解 G(X) = X - F(X) = 0,线性子系统用 GMRES 求解,无需形成 Jacobian。4. **隐式微分**:梯度通过 (I - J_F^T)v = ∇J 的线性系统求解,由隐函数定理导出,使用 GMRES 而非 Neumann 级数展开。5. **轨迹表示**:振荡器系统使用 Chebyshev 系数,神经元系统使用原始采样。6. **训练数据生成**:采用滚动缓冲区策略,组合先前生成的轨迹作为驱动输入。

Result: **振荡器系统**(N=20, 全连接扩散耦合):- k_c ≤ 1.5 时全部 5 个种子收敛,误差 1.0%-18.2%(前馈基线 >100%)- Newton 迭代 4-10 次,处理 1500 时间步的轨迹- 谱半径预测收敛边界 k_c* = 1/ρ,跨越耦合强度和刚度均成立- 隐式梯度在 ρ > 1 区域(耦合 0.5)保持正确(相对误差 0.04%),而展开反传发散(83 倍偏差),Neumann 伴随返回非有限值- 六个振荡器环的耦合权重恢复:参数误差从 83.2% 降至 1.2%(中位数)**神经元系统**(Hodgkin-Huxley):- 自洽性将前馈误差减半,但 1.9% 的精确算子底限表明大部分残余误差来自代理而非公式- w_rec=5 时求解不收敛,反映固定点条件恶化**关键发现**:代理的孤立精度与可组合性是不同的性质,不能同步提升;Jacobian 正则化的朴素方法无效(随机探测估计的是 Frobenius 范数而非谱半径)。

Conclusion: TWT 将耦合动力系统仿真重新框架为轨迹级自洽性问题,改变了前向求解的顺序结构(以迭代次数而非时间步数为深度)和梯度的计算结构(以无时间递归的线性系统替代反向扫描)。该方法在耦合 van der Pol 振荡器和 Hodgkin-Huxley 神经元网络上得到验证,证明学习算子的谱半径可预测耦合求解的收敛边界,且此性质与孤立精度无关。该范式并非经典数值积分的通用替代品,但为可微分、模块化的仿真提供了一条新路径,其失效模式可通过系统测量表征。主要瓶颈包括代理精度、固定点条件,以及训练分布覆盖——尤其是耦合诱导的输入分布在事前未知这一核心未解问题。

Abstract: Numerical simulation of dynamical systems is usually organized as a causal march through time: each state is computed from the previous one. We explore a different formulation for coupled systems. For each subsystem type we train a neural surrogate mapping a full driving trajectory and initial condition directly to a full output trajectory; following classical waveform relaxation, coupled systems are assembled by enforcing self-consistency among these trajectories: simulation becomes a fixed-point problem over complete trajectories rather than a stepwise rollout. On coupled van der Pol oscillators and Hodgkin-Huxley neuron networks, sequential depth becomes the number of solver iterations: 4-10 Newton iterations where the reference integrator takes 1500 steps. The gradient likewise loses its time recursion: it becomes a linear system solved by GMRES at memory independent of solver depth. A single scalar measured from the learned operator, the spectral radius of its Jacobian, predicts in advance where the coupled solve will converge; past that boundary, unrolled backpropagation diverges and a Neumann adjoint fails, while the implicit gradient remains correct to 0.04%. We report where the approach succeeds and where surrogate error degrades it.

</details>


### [143] [SimpleDesign: A Joint Model for Protein Sequence and Structure Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03377)
*Jiarui Lu,Yuyang Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu,Navdeep Jaitly,Joshua M. Susskind,Miguel Ángel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimpleDesign提出了一种用于蛋白质序列与结构协同设计的单阶段、多模态生成模型，直接在数据空间进行训练（无需结构tokenizer），结合离散交叉熵（序列）和连续回归（结构）目标，在2M+序列-结构对上训练，在共设计基准上取得了与token化多模态PLM相当或更优的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质共设计模型依赖多阶段训练（先训练自编码器对结构和序列进行token化，再在潜空间训练生成模型），该流程复杂且存在开销。作者假设这种多阶段训练并非必要，试图验证直接在数据空间进行单阶段端到端训练即可获得具有竞争力的共设计模型。

Method: 1) 联合目标函数：序列采用时间依赖的离散掩码过程（交叉熵损失），结构采用线性插值的流匹配/MSE速度场回归，两者通过独立时间步t和t'实现联合建模；2) 架构：采用Transformer-based多模态骨干（默认Mixture-of-Transformer，模态特定的QKV投影与FFN但保留全局自注意力），序列与Cα坐标的潜在表示沿残基维度拼接，通过共享残基索引的位置编码实现对齐；3) 从ESM2-650M初始化，AFESM+SwissProt数据集训练。

Result: 在无条件共生成基准（长度100-500，N=100）上，SimpleDesign在多模态PLM类别中表现竞争力强（γ=0.3时scTM≥0.9的共设计性达0.74，优于DPLM2的0.46），序列层面pLDDT达81.19、ProGen2 PPL为5.18，与DPLM2等基于token的多模态PLM相当或更优；在结构设计上亦优于ESM3和DPLM2；消融表明vanilla Transformer骨干与MoT性能相当，SwissProt微调可显著提升共设计性。

Conclusion: SimpleDesign证明结构token化并非实现有竞争力序列-结构共设计的必要前提，其单阶段、数据空间的简单配方在多模态PLM范式中具有竞争力，并展现了在序列合理性（pLDDT、PPL）方面相对于几何设计模型的显著优势，为可扩展、易适配的多模态蛋白质生成提供了更简洁的路径。

Abstract: Proteins are fundamental to biological processes, with their function determined by the complex interplay between the amino acid sequence and the three-dimensional structure. Developing generative models capable of understanding this intrinsically multi-modal relationship is crucial for fields like drug discovery and protein engineering. Existing models often rely on a multi-stage training process where autoencoders that tokenize data into latent representations are trained in a first stage. Secondly, a generative model is trained on the latent representation of the autoencoder(s), i.e., generative modeling in a latent space. We hypothesize that this multi-stage training is not necessary to obtain performant co-design models and thus present SimpleDesign, an effective multi-modal protein design model trained directly in the data space. SimpleDesign leverages a single-stage end-to-end objective that combines discrete cross-entropy for sequences and a regression objective for structures. In order to effectively model the difference in sequence and structure modalities, we develop a Mixture-of-Transformer architecture that allows modality-specific processing while keeping global self-attention over both modalities. We train SimpleDesign on over 2M sequence-structure pairs achieving strong performance across co-design and unconditional sequence/structure generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [144] [RecurTrace: Adaptive Latent Reasoning with Loop-Time Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03379)
*Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Yingda Shen,Haoning Xu,Junyu Wang,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecurTrace通过引入循环时间轴上的自注意力(LMA)和由oracle监督的停顿头，解决潜空间循环推理中"遗忘"和"固定深度预算"两大问题，在多个规模模型上提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间循环推理(looping)能增加有效推理深度但有两个局限：每次迭代只能看到上一轮输出，无法访问更早计算；固定的循环次数对简单输入浪费、对困难输入不足。

Method: 1. Loop Memory Attention (LMA)：在循环时间轴上做同位置的自注意力，让每个循环层读取自己之前若干轮的输出状态；2. 输入重注入(ReZero风格)；3. 变量深度训练(对数正态采样循环次数)；4. Oracle监督的停顿头：以"继续循环是否会降低损失"为监督信号训练二分类器决定何时停止。

Result: 在MathQA(1.7B)上以平均2.0次循环达到56.9%准确率，超过最佳固定深度(T=2) 2.2个点，超过ACT/PonderNet/CALM/LoopUS-Conf/TaH-Mismatch等基线；在0.6B/1.7B/4B/8B四个规模上均超越同预算微调基线，收益随规模增大(0.6→3.4点)；停顿头将计算分配给困难样本(困难项平均3.1轮，简单项2.5轮)。

Conclusion: 循环自身的轨迹足以同时解决记忆和预算分配两个问题，无需外部存储或置信度规则；RecurTrace在效率、规模和任务广度上均优于现有自适应计算基线。

Abstract: Repeating a small block of middle layers increases a language model's effective inference depth without adding parameters or generating extra tokens, and recent work shows that this latent recurrence improves reasoning. However, two design choices limit these gains. Each iteration sees only the previous output and cannot directly access earlier computations. Moreover, a fixed loop count wastes depth on easy inputs while leaving hard ones with too little computation. We introduce RecurTrace, which addresses both limitations using the loop's own trajectory. Specifically, Loop Memory Attention lets each looped layer attend to its own states from previous iterations along the loop-time axis, so the model can revisit earlier computations instead of relying on the latest state alone. A halting head then reads the loop state and predicts whether to continue, with supervision from an oracle that identifies when additional depth still reduces loss. In a controlled MathQA comparison on the same looped backbone, RecurTrace achieves 56.9% accuracy with an average of 2.0 loops, exceeding the best fixed loop depth by 2.2 points at matched compute. By comparison, ACT and PonderNet collapse to one loop, and CALM reaches only 54.1% with 5.6 loops, while the stronger LoopUS-Conf and TaH-Mismatch baselines reach 55.3% at 3.2 loops and 55.7% at 2.1 loops. Finally, RecurTrace improves generation accuracy over same-budget fine-tuned baselines at 0.6B, 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with the gain growing with model size from 0.6 to 3.4 points.

</details>


### [145] [TIGPO: Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03383)
*Jinwei Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 TIGPO，通过跨策略更新保存任务级状态转移图、主动重访历史任务，并使用跨时间结果比较来稳定小批量条件下的优势估计。策略损失仅包含当前策略轨迹，历史上只作为图结构和脱离梯度的统计参考。在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上，TIGPO 分别达到 91.28% 总体成功率和 88.65 平均分/77.54% 成功率，整体优于所比较方法；消融实验表明持久图、任务重访和跨时间比较具有互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体面临稀疏延迟奖励与细粒度信用分配问题。现有 GraphGPO 等方法仅在单个策略更新内构建转移图，会丢弃早期策略发现的有效连接；当每任务 rollout 数较小时，组内归一化优势又容易因个别成功或失败样本而不稳定。论文旨在复用跨更新经验，同时避免直接重放旧动作和 log-probability 所造成的离策略分布错配。

Method: 为每个稳定任务身份维护跨更新的持久状态转移图。固定任务组预算被分为 Exploration 与 Revisit：前者探索新任务，后者按延迟 Δ 重访已探索任务，并在相同任务上用当前策略产生新 rollout。将历史图与当前图取并集，在合并图上重新计算当前转移到成功终点的折扣最短路距离并形成时间图优势。对 Revisit，将当前组得分与其对应历史 Exploration 组得分拼接后做标准化，得到跨时间 episode 优势；再用二者与原策略优化目标结合。历史 token、动作及 log-probability 不进入损失。实验采用 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上与提示方法、PPO/RLOO、GRPO、GiGPO、GraphGPO 对比，并进行组件消融、记忆命中分析和训练开销比较。

Result: TIGPO 在 ALFWorld 获得 91.28% 总体成功率，较 GiGPO 高 0.26 个百分点、较 GraphGPO 高 1.96 个百分点；在 WebShop 获得 88.65 平均分和 77.54% 成功率，分别较 GraphGPO 高 1.31 分和 1.17 个百分点。消融中，当前更新图、持久图、持久图加重访、完整 TIGPO 的总体成功率依次为 89.32%、90.69%、89.58%、91.28%，说明持久图和跨时间比较互补，而单纯重访会因组规模减小和局部归一化不稳定而未必增益。持久图提高 Look 与 Pick2；完整方法达到最佳综合表现。TIGPO 相对 GraphGPO 的单更新时间下降 4.35%，核心 LLM 计算下降 1.43%，峰值 GPU 内存基本相同，但论文认为差异主要反映相当的开销，而非实际加速。

Conclusion: 将结构化任务经验跨策略更新保留，并以受控重访和跨时间、同任务比较加以激活，可为长时程 LLM 智能体提供更密集、更稳定的信用信号，同时保持当前策略上的在策略优化。实验支持该机制在具身与网页交互环境中提升综合性能，并显示相对 GraphGPO 没有可观测的端到端时间或显存负担。未来应验证更大模型和更多环境，并研究图压缩与自适应检索。

Abstract: Graph-based policy optimization improves credit assignment for long-horizon LLM agents by organizing rollout trajectories into state-transition graphs. However, existing methods construct graphs independently within each policy update, discarding transitions discovered by earlier policies and limiting advantage estimation to small, batch-local rollout groups. We propose \emph{Temporal Instance-Graph Policy Optimization} (TIGPO), which extends graph-based credit assignment across policy updates. TIGPO maintains a persistent transition graph for each task, allowing valid transitions discovered by different policy versions to jointly determine credit for current rollouts. To actively reconnect current exploration with historical experience, TIGPO allocates a fixed rollout budget between Exploration slots for ordinary task sampling and Revisit slots for delayed reattempts of previously explored tasks. For each revisit, TIGPO pairs the current rollout group with its corresponding earlier Exploration group to construct a cross-temporal reference. The enlarged reference is designed to stabilize relative advantage estimation under small rollout groups, while comparison on the same task directly captures policy improvement across training stages. Historical transitions and scores serve only as structural and detached statistical references and are never replayed in the policy loss. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that TIGPO consistently outperforms prior group-based and graph-based policy optimization methods.

</details>


### [146] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成过程多样性"(generative-process diversity)概念，通过对原始模型输出应用归一化压缩距离(NCD)并进行置换控制残差化处理，来度量语言模型对之间的过程层面差异。在38个语言模型和10个不相交的基准测试上，该度量能预测模型对的纠错失败相关性，且效果优于语义相似度和能力控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型系统常通过冗余来提升可靠性，但语义相似度仅捕获输出含义的差异，不能反映模型在失败时的独立性。生态学中的响应多样性理论表明，相关性失败(common-mode failure)的缓冲取决于组件对扰动的不同响应方式，而非仅仅是表面输出的差异。论文旨在区分功能多样性与响应多样性，提出针对失效模式的过程级多样性度量。

Method: 核心方法：(1) 在Infinity-Chat分类法的100个开放式提示上，为38个模型各采样50个响应；(2) 使用PPMd压缩器对原始UTF-8字节串计算NCD；(3) 对每个响应生成20次确定性随机字节置换，构建保留字节频率但破坏顺序的置换控制；(4) 在每个提示内对模型对的原始NCD与置换控制距离进行OLS回归，取残差并跨提示平均，得到生成过程多样性度量 $d^{\mathrm{IGP}}$；(5) 失效指标采用"历元原生共同错误答案一致率"(epoch-native CWA)及其机会校正形式 $\kappa_{ij}$，通过留出对(out leave-pair-out)问题特定干扰项吸引力作为基线；(6) 使用模型节点自助法(2,000次重复)处理双偶依赖性，估计跨10个基准的偏Spearman秩相关，控制语义距离、模型能力水平和能力差距。

Result: 在10个基准测试家族上，$d^{\mathrm{IGP}}$ 与纠错失败相关的偏Spearman秩关联系数为 $-0.216$（95%置信区间 $[-0.309, -0.122]$），且在所有10个基准上均为负值。仅控制能力时为 $-0.148$。语义距离在仅控制能力时未分辨（$-0.019$），但在交叉控制下变为 $+0.160$。原始NCD与失败呈正相关 ($+0.101$)，仅频率保留成分呈更强正相关 ($+0.150$)，仅顺序特异性残差与失败呈负相关——表明置换控制反转了相关性符号。最低到最高距离带之间，$\kappa$ 下降48%。结果在压缩度量家族 $V_1, V_2, V_\infty$ 间稳健。

Conclusion: 生成过程多样性是一种可操作的黑盒审计方法，无需访问模型权重、架构或训练数据，即可识别具有独立失效模式的模型对。置换控制残差化是关键：仅顺序特异性成分（而非边际字节组成）与减少的相关性失败相关。语义相似度不能替代过程多样性度量，因为其失效相关信息正是与压缩多样性共享的成分。该方法为多模型系统在安全关键场景中的有效冗余评估提供了实用工具。未来工作需扩展到智能体层面的动作-观察序列，并测试选择远距离模型是否实际改善集成性能。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [147] [Guide, Not Bind: Why Defeasible Priors Fail in Augmented Lagrangian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03442)
*Sairam Sundararaman,Sara Girdhar,Manit Narasimha Murthy,Samrudh N,Bhaskarjyoti Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了可微分因果发现中"引导而非绑定"（guide, not bind）设计范式失败的两个独立机制根源：一是ALM惩罚的指数级ramping会在数据自适应松弛机制生效前过早压制真实边（早期抑制陷阱）；二是基于相关性的拟合目标（correlation matching）因丢弃方差信息而在闭合形式上将真实边与其反方向严格绑定（tie），而协方差匹配则可证明地分离两者。作者构建了同时满足三个必要条件的AdaptiveRelax松弛算子并结合协方差匹配进行组合修复，在768对配对试验中显著优于DADU（p<10⁻⁵），但正确边恢复率仍为少数派，反向边吸收成为主导失败模式。关键发现是：最小二乘目标（NOTEARS/DAGMA风格）在保持可识别性收益的同时具有最佳全图SHD；而文献视为"安全选择"的似然目标（GOLEM风格）在抑制率上反而最差（d=32时达100%）。两个障碍彼此独立，目标选择可能改善一个却恶化另一个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分因果发现方法日益采用ALM惩罚将专家先验编码为禁止边约束，假设数据自适应松弛机制会逐步弱化并最终覆盖与数据持续矛盾的规则（"引导而非绑定"）。本文旨在揭示该设计在两个独立维度上失败，并探索是否存在可行的修复方案。

Method: 理论分析方面：(1) 推导ALM数据自适应松弛算子的三个必要条件（C1抑制前评估、C2速率条件、C3算子条件，命题1）并证明DADU违反全部三条（推论1）；(2) 通过两变量隔离设置在闭合形式上证明相关性匹配使两个方向代价均为2r²（引理1），而协方差匹配以至少w₀⁴的间隔分离两者（引理2），并将此推广至一般图覆盖边反转（命题2）。实验方面：构建满足所有三个条件的AdaptiveRelax松弛算子（定义5/命题3），在d∈{4,8,16,32}、密度∈{0.15,0.30}、噪声σ∈{0.5,1.0,2.0}、等方差与异方差噪声、16图/格共6144次训练的合成高斯SEM上进行评估，并替换拟合目标为NOTEARS/DAGMA式最小二乘和GOLEM-EV式似然目标进行目标无关性测试。

Result: (1) 在3072次训练中，DADU下单一错误先验在87-97%的试验中压制真实边（早期抑制陷阱确认）；(2) 相关性匹配在d=4至32下将两方向损失差绑定到浮点精度（≤10⁻¹²），协方差匹配在等方差噪声下间隔从2.64增长至11.71；(3) AdaptiveRelax+协方差匹配在配对试验中27-52倍更频繁恢复错误禁止边（p<10⁻⁵），但反向边吸引仍占41.1%对比正确恢复的24.7%；(4) 最小二乘目标获得最佳全图SHD（31.1）且保持可识别性收益，似然目标最差（SHD=82.1）且d=32时100%抑制，证伪了"似然=安全选择"的假设；(5) 异方差噪声下最小二乘和似然目标的方向间隔反转为负值，系统性偏向错误方向。

Conclusion: "引导而非绑定"因两个独立且精确刻画的机制失败：ALM惩罚ramping压制真实边于自适应检查之前（早期抑制陷阱，命题1/推论1）；相关性匹配目标严格绑定两个方向的代价，并非因模型不可识别，而是因归一化丢弃了方差信号（引理1/备注1），协方差匹配可恢复可证明的间隔（引理2）。组合修复（AdaptiveRelax+协方差匹配）显著但仅部分有效：ALM约束的结构性不对称（仅约束一个方向）使反向边始终为权重吸收路径，无修复触及。关键纠正是：修复一个障碍可能恶化另一个——似然目标在抑制率上比相关性匹配更差。实践中推荐NOTEARS/DAGMA式最小二乘目标而非作者最初提出的协方差修复，因为前者兼具可识别性收益与最佳全图恢复精度。

Abstract: Differentiable causal discovery methods increasingly encode expert priors as forbidden-edge constraints enforced by an Augmented Lagrangian (ALM) penalty, on the assumption that a data-adaptive relaxation mechanism will discount and eventually override a rule the data consistently contradicts. We show this design, which we call \emph{guide, not bind}, fails for two independent, precisely characterized reasons, and that directly repairing both restores it only partially. First, sequential penalty-ramping ALM suppresses a wrongly-forbidden true edge before any counterfactual check can detect it: we give three necessary conditions any adaptive relaxation must satisfy to avoid this (Proposition~\ref{prop:conditions}), prove that DADU---the natural relaxation rule this paper introduces as the object of study---violates all three (Corollary~\ref{cor:dadu_failure}), and confirm the failure across 3{,}072 training runs spanning graphs from 4 to 32 nodes, where a single wrong prior suppresses a true edge in 87--97\% of trials under DADU. Second, and independent of any fix to the mechanism, we prove in closed form that the standard correlation-matching objective ties a true edge and its reverse to an identical cost of exactly $2r^2$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:tie}), not because the underlying equal-variance model is unidentifiable, but because normalizing to correlation discards exactly the variance information that would make it identifiable; covariance matching instead separates the two directions by a provable margin of at least $w_0^4$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:separation}).

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Straightness: Non-Crossing Flow Matching via Quantile AlignTree Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03443)
*Junyi Lin,Mengyu Li,Jingxuan Hu,Kejun He,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QAT-FM，一种基于分位数对齐二叉树的Flow Matching源-目标耦合策略，实现非交叉路径与改进的路径分离，显著降低耦合构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Flow Matching中，独立耦合导致路径交叉和局部速度模糊，而OT耦合虽能缓解但构建成本高昂，难以扩展到大规模任务。

Method: 通过在数据上构建二叉划分树（最大方差坐标+均值阈值分裂），将树拓扑按分位数对齐映射到高斯源空间，每个叶节点对应一个轴对齐超矩形区域，源采样归结为各坐标截断正态的独立采样；条件生成时联合树仅在数据坐标上分裂源侧。

Result: 理论上证明了非交叉性和中间时刻路径分离严格优于独立耦合；实验上在CIFAR-10、ImageNet-32类条件生成、CelebA-64文本条件生成、ImageNet-256潜在空间生成中均取得竞争性FID，构建复杂度O(Nd log N)、采样复杂度O(d)，远低于OT方法。

Conclusion: QAT-FM提供了一种高效、结构化的耦合机制，在保持高斯先验对齐的同时实现非交叉路径和路径分离改善，可自然扩展至条件生成，并兼容现代潜在空间流模型骨干。

Abstract: The performance of Flow Matching largely depends on the quality of the coupling between the source and target distributions. However, independent coupling often leads to path crossings and local velocity ambiguity, while OT-based couplings typically incur high construction costs. To address this challenge, we propose Quantile AlignTree Flow Matching (QAT-FM), an efficient structured coupling strategy that constructs a hierarchical coupling between a Gaussian prior and the target data distribution via a quantile-aligned tree structure. QAT-FM constructs the coupling in $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log N)$ time and supports per-pair source sampling with $\mathcal{O}(d)$ complexity, enabling scalable training for large-scale high-dimensional generative tasks. Theoretically, we prove that the QAT coupling satisfies marginal consistency, induces non-crossing linear interpolation paths, and consistently improves path separation at intermediate times compared with independent coupling, thereby alleviating local velocity ambiguity. QAT-FM further extends naturally to conditional generation, enabling structured conditional coupling while preserving global Gaussian alignment. Experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that QAT-FM achieves competitive generative performance while substantially reducing coupling construction cost.

</details>


### [149] [A Two-Stage Forecasting System for CPU Workload Prediction in Private Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03457)
*Ashir Javeed,Anton Borg,Håkan Grahn,Lars Lundberg,Dhyey Patel,Sogand Shirinbab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段集成预测系统，用于私有云环境中的CPU工作负载预测。该系统首先使用XGBoost预测客户服务请求（TPS），然后基于TPS预测结果估计未来CPU利用率，结合扩展窗口策略的自适应在线重训练以应对概念漂移。在10个真实云应用上的实验表明，多数应用SMAPE低于7%，最佳应用MAE为0.7372，RMSE为1.1866，SMAPE为3.57%，R²为0.9185，且平均推理时间低于264ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统云资源预测方法直接从历史CPU资源轨迹预测未来CPU负载，忽略了客户服务需求与资源消耗之间的因果关系。这种因果关系的缺失限制了预测在高动态云工作负载下的鲁棒性。同时，现有方法在预测准确性、计算效率和实时适应性之间难以取得平衡，且深度学习方法计算开销大，不适合轻量级流式部署。

Method: 提出两阶段级联学习架构：(1) 第一阶段：多输出XGBoost回归模型基于滑动窗口（hw=512）递归预测多变量TPS特征的未来h步值；(2) 第二阶段：独立的XGBoost CPU估计模型以TPS特征向量为输入，预测未来β步内的滚动最大CPU值。采用扩展窗口策略（每r=1000个观测重训练）应对概念漂移。使用walk-forward验证，在10个私有云应用上评估MAE、RMSE、SMAPE、rMUE、rMOE、R²和平均推理时间等指标，并进行60步预测范围内的漂移分析。

Result: 两阶段集成模型在多数应用中SMAPE低于7%；最佳应用（应用A）MAE=0.7372，RMSE=1.1866，SMAPE=3.57%，R²=0.9185；平均推理时间约201ms（178.50–263.70ms）。CPU独立模型R²达0.9145–0.9903，推理时间仅0.77–7.67ms。漂移分析显示60步预测范围内误差累积受控，3/5的TPS特征保持稳定递归预测。相比直接CPU预测方法，两阶段模型在鲁棒性、计算效率和可解释性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 两阶段TPS驱动的CPU预测系统通过显式建模客户需求与资源消耗的因果关系，实现了高精度和低延迟的CPU预测，R²为0.9185（中位SMAPE 5.9%），集成管道延迟低于264ms，CPU估计延迟低于1ms，适合实时云自动伸缩部署。局限在于仅关注CPU资源、未涉及内存/存储/网络带宽，扩展窗口重训练随数据增长计算开销增大。未来工作将扩展至多资源预测，并结合Transformer序列模型、持续学习和强化学习自动伸缩机制。

Abstract: Accurate cloud resource forecasting is essential for proactive resource provisioning, maintaining Quality of Service (QoS), and reducing operational costs in dynamic cloud environments. The existing forecasting approaches predominantly estimate future CPU workload directly from historical resource traces, which often overlook the relationship between customer service demand and subsequent resource consumption. This study proposes a two-stage integrated forecasting model that explicitly models this dependency by first forecasting customer service requests, expressed as Transactions Per Second (TPS), and subsequently estimating future CPU workload from the TPS forecast. Both the forecasting component and resource prediction component employed the XGBoost model within a cascaded learning architecture, complemented by adaptive online retraining using an expanding-window strategy to address concept drift in continuously evolving cloud workloads. The proposed work was evaluated using real-world traces collected from a private cloud environment comprising ten applications. Experimental results demonstrate robust forecasting performance by achieving Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error (SMAPE) below $7\%$ for most applications, with the best-performing application achieving an MAE of $0.7372$, RMSE of $1.1866$, SMAPE of $3.57\%$, and an R2 of $0.9185$. Horizon-wise drift analysis confirmed stable recursive forecasting behavior with controlled error accumulation across a 60-step prediction horizon. Compared with the conventional direct CPU forecasting method, the proposed two-stage integrated model gives improved forecasting robustness, computational efficiency, and interpretability, making it well-suited for proactive resource management and intelligent auto-scaling in cloud computing environments.

</details>


### [150] [Mind the Gap: Robustness Risks in PII Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03464)
*Adeel Zafar,Slawomir Nowaczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对PII（个人可识别信息）检测系统在真实部署场景下的鲁棒性问题进行研究。作者构建了一个涵盖七类自然分布偏移的压力测试基准，对三种主流架构——编码器式NER（SpaCy）、基于规则的混合检测（Presidio）和生成式LLM抽取（Qwen2.5-3B）——进行OOD评估，发现所有架构在非分布内输入下都出现显著性能下降，但各自具有不同且互补的失败模式，并据此提出混合检测流水线与QA驱动的迭代改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PII检测是数据保护的基础设施，漏检将直接造成隐私和安全风险。然而现有系统在标准基准上报告超过90%的F1分数，这些基准由编辑良好、结构化的文本构成，而真实部署中的输入是混乱、非结构化且高度可变的。这种基准与部署现实之间的差距可能掩盖关键的系统性鲁棒性失败，使组织错误估计其数据保护管线的合规性和安全性。论文的核心动机不是寻找最优PII检测器，而是系统性地揭示不同检测范式在分布偏移下的互补失败模式，为构建生产级可靠PII系统提供经验依据。

Method: 论文构建了一个合成压力测试基准，包含两个评估集：Set A（100个干净模板，350个标注）和Set B（560个OOD示例，2330个标注，覆盖七类分布偏移：连写文本、口语化、重叠实体、缩写、混合上下文、拼写错误、代码切换）。评估采用松弛跨度匹配（仅要求类型匹配且跨度重叠）。选取三个代表性系统：SpaCy en_core_web_lg（编码器式NER）、Microsoft Presidio（基于规则+编码器的混合框架）、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct（零样本提示的生成式LLM）。使用鲁棒性差距Δ = F1_SetA - F1_SetB衡量对分布偏移的敏感性，并在实体类型和偏移类别两个维度上进行细分分析。论文还提出了一个三模型并行的混合检测流水线架构，包含实体合并层和基于分类化生产失败案例的QA驱动反馈循环。

Result: 所有三个架构在OOD数据上均出现显著性能下降。LLM（Qwen）在两个集合上整体F1最高（0.921/0.848），但鲁棒性差距最大（Δ=0.073），Presidio差距最小（Δ=0.057），SpaCy居中（Δ=0.064）。各类架构表现出互补的失败模式：编码器模型（SpaCy）在未见过的表面形式和边界检测上失败（Location召回率从0.725降至0.457，吸收职业头衔等后续token）；基于规则的Presidio在非标准格式上失败（拼写式邮箱、带分机号的电话号码），且日期识别器产生大量假阳性（Date of Birth精确率仅0.492）；LLM表现出实体类型混淆（45.7%的信用卡号被误标为SSN）和生成不稳定性（2.5%的输入产生无法解析的输出，14个JSON解析错误）。刚性格式实体（SSN、IP地址）在Presidio下保持完美召回，而灵活格式实体（Location）跨所有架构召回率均崩溃至约0.46。OOD基准上即使是最佳模型（Qwen F1=0.848）也漏检21.3%的PII实体，Location有54%的漏检率。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数掩盖了部署关键性弱点，没有任何单一架构在所有PII类别上具有统一可靠性。鉴于失败模式具有互补性，论文提出混合检测流水线（并行运行正则层、编码器模型和LLM回退，经实体合并层整合）使整体漏检率受限于失败集的交集而非并集。此外，通过结构化的QA驱动反馈循环持续收集生产失败案例、针对性微调和回归测试，可迭代降低风险，同时警惕灾难性遗忘。当前基准为合成且限于英语通用领域，所观察的鲁棒性差距可能是真实部署中所遇到问题的下限；未来工作应纳入多领域自然文本、多语言场景，并在更强模型和微调变体上验证OOD退化是否持续存在。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection is a foundational component of data protection infrastructure where missed entities constitute direct privacy and security risks. Although modern PII systems report strong performance on standard benchmarks, we show that these evaluations mask substantial robustness failures under realistic distribution shifts encountered in deployment. Rather than comparing state-of-the-art accuracy, we study how different PII detection paradigms fail under noisy, unstructured, and informal inputs. We construct a stress test benchmark spanning seven categories of natural distribution shift and evaluate representative systems from three widely deployed architectural families: encoder-based NER (SpaCy), rule-based hybrid detection (Presidio), and generative LLM extraction (Qwen2.5-3B). All three exhibit significant degradation on out-of-distribution inputs, but with distinct and complementary failure modes. Encoder models primarily fail on unseen surface forms and boundary detection, rule-based systems fail on non-standard formats, and LLMs exhibit entity-type confusion and generation instability. These results show that aggregate benchmark scores obscure deployment-critical weaknesses and that no single architecture is uniformly reliable across PII categories. Motivated by these findings, we propose a hybrid detection pipeline with a QA-driven feedback loop for iterative risk mitigation, and release our benchmark to support OOD-aware evaluation of PII systems.

</details>


### [151] [Spectral characteristics of autoencoder parameters as a vector representation of data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03495)
*Maria Nikitina,Anton Bishuk,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自编码器参数的谱特性（奇异值向量）能否作为训练数据集的密集向量表示。理论上证明了模型参数矩阵的奇异值与训练数据协方差矩阵的特征值之间存在联系，实验上利用CIFAR-10和FashionMNIST数据集验证了该向量表示能以高准确率区分训练在不同数据子集上的模型，且对数据混合和噪声添加呈现可预测的敏感响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型嵌入方法多依赖外部特征、辅助网络或原始数据访问，缺乏直接从模型参数中提取数据集统计信息的通用方法。本文旨在探究训练好的神经网络参数本身是否可作为数据集的紧凑嵌入表示，从而实现无需访问原始数据的数据集分析与比较。

Method: 提出基于模型参数矩阵奇异值的向量化方法（Algorithm 1）：对每层权重矩阵进行SVD分解，保留每层前r大奇异值（r由保留80%能量比确定），按层顺序拼接并归一化形成嵌入向量。理论上证明了线性AE（无激活函数）的参数奇异值与数据协方差矩阵特征值的关系（Theorem 1），以及更一般的非高斯情况下模型嵌入差异与数据嵌入差异的下界关系（Theorem 2）。实验采用浅层AE/VAE（1-2个全连接层，含/不含非线性激活，隐空间维度256，AdamW优化器训练20轮），通过逻辑回归在嵌入向量上进行分类。

Result: 基线二分类实验中，AE（含非线性）准确率达0.98±0.02，VAE达0.97±0.03，无非线性AE达0.99±0.01。第三类混合实验中，分类性能随混合比例α（0.6→0.99）增大而可预测地下降，验证了数据集收敛时模型嵌入也收敛的结论。噪声添加实验中，差异性越大的噪声导致分离性能下降越快。FashionMNIST上的数据奇异值距离与模型嵌入距离呈显著线性相关（Pearson检验）。一对一两两比较实验中，部分类别对（如coat vs dress）可近乎完美区分，而视觉相似类别区分难度较大。

Conclusion: 训练好的自编码器参数可视为数据集的自然嵌入表示。仅通过参数矩阵的奇异值（无需复杂架构、预训练模型或辅助网络）即可高准确地区分训练在不同数据集上的模型。该嵌入空间正确反映了数据集间的统计差异及其扰动，在数据集检索、分布偏移分析、自动化分类等任务中具有应用前景，无需存储或直接访问原始数据。未来研究方向包括将该方法推广至更深层、更复杂的网络架构。

Abstract: This paper examines the relationship between the parameters of autoencoder models and the statistical properties of the data on which they are trained. Autoencoders are defined as models with an encoder-decoder architecture, trained to reconstruct input data through a compressed latent representation. It is proposed that the model parameters can be viewed as a dense vector representation of the corresponding sample. To test this hypothesis, a theoretical and experimental study is conducted in which a vector representation is formed based on the spectral characteristics of the autoencoder parameter matrices. Theoretical analysis shows that the singular values of the model parameter matrices are related to the eigenvalues of the covariance matrix of the training data, ensuring the transfer of information between the data space and the parameter space. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and FashionMNIST datasets confirm that the resulting vector representations allow for a high degree of accuracy in distinguishing between models trained on different data subsets, without resorting to complex vector generation algorithms or using the original samples. These results suggest that the parameters of trained autoencoders can be viewed as sample representations.

</details>


### [152] [Restricted Eigenvalues Beyond Gaussian Width: Threshold Occupancy under Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03504)
*Shi Fu,Huibo Xu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对COLT 2015提出的开放问题——均匀小球条件下,各向同性重尾设计是否仍服从高斯宽度样本复杂度法则——给出了系统性的否定回答。核心发现是:同时阈值占据(simultaneous threshold occupancy)是真正的障碍,而非高斯过程几何。作者构造了常宽度路径上的反例,证明了每个有限范围空间可被精确编码进任意窄的球冠并提升为完整下降锥截面,并给出了固定阈值VC维数下的尖锐最坏样本复杂度Θ(β⁻¹[dlog(1/β)+log(1/δ)])。该分离在各向同性、所有有限阶矩以及光滑密度条件下均成立;各向同性下以仿射维数乘以包围半径平方作为分布无关的fallback界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子高斯设计下,限制特征值(RE)由高斯宽度w(A)²控制,样本量约为1+w(A)²。COLT 2015的Banerjee等人开放问题问:仅凭均匀小球条件,各向同性重尾设计是否仍服从同一法则?若肯定,则高斯宽度相图将在无上尾集中或高阶矩假设下普遍成立,影响高维稀疏恢复、稳健统计等领域的理论基础。

Method: 采用四步结构:(1)在常宽度下降锥上构造路径上确定性失效的反例(固定小球常数下,样本量n≤m/2时Λ_n=0);(2)将任意有限范围空间的关联事件精确编码进任意窄球冠,并通过ℓ∞范数的Tε传输映射提升为完整多面体下降锥截面,保持均匀小球质量;(3)利用Komlós-Pach-Tardos型ε-网下界和VC维相对逼近,确定固定VC维数d下低质量区的尖锐最坏样本复杂度Θ(β⁻¹[dlog(1/β)+log(1/δ)]);(4)构建各向同性锚定紧框架(锚定Rademacher向量白化),用投影残差u_D=(I-P_D)u_★作为自适应核方向,使重尾设计在n≤√(p/log p)时路径失效,而高斯设计用O(1+log(1/δ))样本即可恢复;高斯平滑产生正C∞密度但限制能量仍任意小;各向同性下通过投影Rademacher过程的期望上界W_n≤r(A)√q(A)得到维数-半径下界。

Result: (1)定理1:对每个偶数m,存在m维空间V_m、多面体范数R_m、方向重尾律P_m,使w(A_m)≤2√(2/π)、Q_{1/(2√2)}≥1/3、VC≥m/2,但对每个n≤m/2和每个样本实现,Λ_n(X,A_m)=0;推论2给出高斯宽度法则的一致失效。(2)定理3-4:每个有限概率范围空间可精确表示为阈值事件,w(A)≤η任意小;上升锥版本保持Q₁≥β/(2-β)。(3)定理5:固定VC维数d下,N_RE(d,β,δ)的尖锐界为Θ(β⁻¹[dlog(1/β)+log(1/δ)]),下界由d子集/单例范围/单范围三种机制组合证明。(4)定理7-8:在同一锥A_{p,k}上,各向同性重尾律对每个n≤k≤cp/log p均有Λ_n=0(路径上),而高斯律仅需O(1+log(1/δ))样本即获Λ_n≥c_G。(5)推论9:高斯平滑后Z_{H,τ}有正C∞密度、各阶矩有限、无指数矩,但P{Λ_n≤2τ²}≥1-exp(-c_s n)。(6)定理10-11:各向同性下inf‖Xu‖₂≥αβ√n/2−2r(A)√q(A)−αt/2,样本量n≥C(q(A)r(A)²/(α²β²)+log(1/δ)/β²)时Λ_n≥α²β²/16,在锚定框架族上匹配Θ(k)的路径尺度。

Conclusion: COLT 2015提出的高斯宽度法则不能仅从均匀小球条件推出:即使设计精确各向同性、具有所有有限阶矩、甚至有正光滑密度,RE仍可在常宽度下降锥上路径失效。真正的障碍是同时阈值占据——单个样本必须共同覆盖所有方向,而非逐点可见。高斯和子高斯增量控制耦合附近方向的可见事件,均匀小球假设则不。各向同性不能恢复高斯宽度控制,但能给出由仿射维数和包围半径刻画的分布无关下界,在锚定框架族上尖锐。自然的后续问题是:在边际可见性和完全增量控制之间,何种最小结构能恢复高斯宽度法则?

Abstract: Restricted eigenvalue (RE) bounds govern stable recovery by norm-regularized estimators. For isotropic sub-Gaussian measurements, the benchmark sample size is $1+w(A)^2$, where $w(A)$ is the Gaussian width of the normalized descent cone. The COLT 2015 open-problem note (Banerjee et al., 2015) asked whether the same law follows for heavy-tailed designs from a uniform small-ball condition alone. We give an explicit and systematic negative answer to the general question as formulated there: the proposed law fails in its full dimension-free, arbitrary-set form, and the missing obstruction is simultaneous threshold occupancy. A constant-width polyhedral descent cone with fixed small-ball constants has zero empirical RE on every sample path up to half the ambient dimension. More generally, every finite range space admits exact threshold encoding in an arbitrarily narrow spherical cap and a lift to a full polyhedral descent-cone section. For every fixed threshold VC dimension $d$, as $\beta\downarrow0$, the sharp worst-case sample complexity is $\Theta(\beta^{-1}[d\log(1/\beta)+\log(1/\delta)])$. The separation persists under exact isotropy and all finite moments: on the same constant-width cone, Gaussian measurements succeed with $O(1+\log(1/\delta))$ samples, whereas an isotropic heavy-tailed design fails pathwise for $n\lesssim\sqrt{p/\log p}$. Gaussian smoothing yields an everywhere-positive $C^\infty$ density while retaining arbitrarily poor RE. Under isotropy, a distribution-free fallback governed by affine dimension times squared enclosing radius is sharp on this family.

</details>


### [153] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多元IoT流量异常检测的零样本学习框架，结合对抗域适配与对比学习到序列式LSTM-VAE架构中。在六个IoT/网络安全数据集上构建44种跨域迁移场景进行评估，在多个目标域上表现出较强的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络异常检测面临设备异构性、标注稀缺以及跨域分布偏移的固难。现有方法要么依赖标注、要么缺乏严格零样本泛化机制。本文拟在不解锁目标域数据/标签的前提下实现跨域鲁棒的异常检测。

Method: 提出Contrastive Adversarially-Adaptive LSTM-VAE框架，采用两阶段训练：阶段一在主源域上预训练LSTM-VAE；阶段二通过梯度反转层（GRL）进行对抗域适配，并以余弦对比损失在 latent space 中进行语义结构化。引入轻量级编/解码适配器以对齐跨域特征分布，支持变长序列。最终阶段为严格零样本推理：不使用目标域标签、损失或微调，通过重构误差与隐空间偏差综合判定异常。

Result: 在CICIDS2017/2018、UNSW-NB15、TON-IoT、ACI-IoT-2023、WUSTL-IIoT-2021六个数据集的44种源-辅助-目标组合上评估。当目标域为CICIDS2017/2018时多场景达到近完美准确率（如CICIDS2018→CICIDS2017为1.000）；但TON-IoT与ACI-IoT目标场景表现明显退化。与DACAD基准相比表现呈互补：本文方法在结构相似域占优，DACAD在严重分布偏移场景（NB15/TON/ACI为目标）下多数场景更优。

Conclusion: 所提框架在多个跨域场景下能实现零样本异常检测的强泛化能力，验证了对抗对齐与对比语义结构化在变长IoT流检测中的有效性。但严格零样本设置在高度偏移的智能家居与军事IoT场景下仍存局限，提示未来可结合不确定性拒绝或轻量适配器校准以提升对源域未覆盖的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [154] [Coupled Scaling: A Representational Accessibility Framework for Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03533)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"耦合缩放"框架,将神经缩放视为任务结构与架构-优化系统可达几何之间的关系,通过模式截断模型推导出严格下界与有限预算残差率的乘积律,并给出可检验的静态水平预测与跨任务指数反转预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放理论主要从数据几何或预设数据-模型谱推导,但相同数据上训练的架构-优化系统会因表征可达性差异而产生不同缩放,缺乏统一的对比框架来解释模型端依赖。

Method: 构建最小可解的模式截断见证模型:架构提供支持集,架构-优化耦合提供优先级顺序;推导支撑外的严格渐近下界与有限预算残差的累积尾部-已完成前缀覆盖区间;在有界前缀外增益条件下获得乘积律 α=ργ;通过固定核特化从任务加权谱测度的零附近尾部推导训练时间指数;设计2×2因式实验将静态几何水平与匹配预算损失配对,将多尺度几何与拟合指数配对,并预注册跨任务反转预测。

Result: 在可解见证中,残差指数位于[ργ, γ]区间,有界前缀外增益下下端点精确取到 α=ργ,幂律情形 α=ρ(b-1);固定核特化下训练时间指数等于谱尾指数 β;对已发布的涌现轨迹进行识别审计,确认需要耦合、任务、几何测量、随机重复四要素联合控制方可实施直接检验。

Conclusion: 耦合缩放将缩放定律重新定位为任务与学习系统之间的耦合关系,严格区分了严格下界(由架构支持决定)与有限预算率(由累积尾部率与已完成前缀覆盖率的乘积决定),为跨架构-优化系统比较提供了结构支持与有限预算获取相分离的统一框架,并给出明确的实证检验路径。

Abstract: Existing theories derive neural scaling from data geometry or a specified data-model spectrum, but systems trained on the same data can scale differently when architecture or optimization changes the representations they can efficiently reach. We introduce Coupled Scaling, a task-conditioned framework in which finite-budget scaling depends on the relation between task structure and the geometry accessible to an architecture-optimization system. In a solvable mode-truncation model, loss separates into target energy outside architectural support and an unresolved supported tail. For an arbitrary priority order, the residual lies between the best-N supported tail and the tail beyond the largest completed high-value prefix. If the cumulative-tail and coverage log-rates are $\gamma_{A,T}$ and $\rho_{A,O,T}$, the residual exponent lies in $[\rho_{A,O,T}\gamma_{A,T},\gamma_{A,T}]$. Under bounded off-prefix gain, the completed prefix is rate-determining and $\alpha_{A,O,T}=\rho_{A,O,T}\gamma_{A,T}$; for $a_{A,T,j}\asymp j^{-b_{A,T}}$, this gives $\alpha_{A,O,T}=\rho_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)$. A fixed-kernel specialization derives the training-time exponent from the near-zero tail of a task-weighted spectral measure defined independently of the loss fit. The framework separates architectural support from finite-budget acquisition and motivates two tests: static task-relevant geometry should track loss at a common budget, while multiscale geometry should track coupling-specific exponent ordering, including reversal across contrasting tasks. An audit of released emergence trajectories identifies the controls needed for a direct factorial test that measures geometry separately from the scaling fit.

</details>


### [155] [WeatherNext 3: Increasing resolution and performance of global weather models with raw observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03582)
*Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Dominic Masters,Andrew El-Kadi,Samier Merchant,Guy Shalev,Ilan Price,Fred Zyda,Remi Lam,Sasha Shysheya,Matthew Willson,Stratis Markou,Shreya Agrawal,Suhani Vora,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Sunny Mak,Tom R. Andersson,Megan Bela,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Ferran Alet,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherNext 3 (WN3) 是 Google DeepMind/Google Research 发布的第三代全球 AI 天气模型，相比前代 WN2 在分辨率、初始化方式和观测数据利用上实现重大突破。它将时间分辨率提升至 1 小时（替代传统 6 小时），空间分辨率达 0.1°（单层变量），可直接摄入低延迟地球静止卫星数据，并新增直接预测卫星降水估计、热带气旋以及地面站点观测（如 2m 温度、露点）的能力，将数据同化、预报和后处理融为一体，在分析与观测基准上均达到当前最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 天气模型的空间/时间分辨率低于最优物理模型，且仅基于分析数据训练，无法直接利用观测，受分析偏差影响。WN3 旨在通过更高分辨率、每小时初始化和直接利用原始观测数据来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 基于 Functional Generative Networks (FGN) 的编码-处理-解码架构：1) 引入多模态、多分辨率处理（0.25° 和 0.1° 双网格），通过独立的编码器/解码器处理地球静止卫星、分析、降水估计等不同输入；2) 直接以稀疏站点元数据（高程、海陆掩码）为条件，进行连续时空查询的站点输出头；3) 使用"认知 Dropout"和功能扰动集合来捕捉认知不确定性；4) 多阶段训练课程（1° → 0.25° → 0.1°，并进行自回归微调和站点冻结微调）；5) 损失函数采用 marginal CRPS 并为稀疏模态加权和全局池化项。

Result: 1) 分析评估：相比 WN2，对流层中层位势高度 CRPS 改善约 5%，相当于新增 6 小时有效预报时效；多数单层变量（如 2m 温度、地表太阳辐射、云量）在 0.1° 上优于 WN2 和 ECMWF ENS；2) 站点评估：相对 WN2/ENS，2m 温度 CRPS 短期改善 30-40%，2m 相对湿度大幅领先（且对未见过的站点泛化良好）；3) 降水评估：新型 PARDIG 头在 IMERG/MRMS/雨量计上 CRPS 比 WN2 降低 10%-60%，概率可靠性显著优于 WN2 和 ENS；4) 热带气旋：全球均匀评估中，路径、强度和范围误差均优于 WN2；5) 实时 6 周评估：相比 ECMWF AIFS ENS v2，对流层高层的 CRPS 整体改善约 10%，且具有显著的 2-3 小时提前量。

Conclusion: WN3 通过原生支持多模态、每小时初始化和连续时空站点查询，证明 AI 模型可以有效融合数据同化、预报和后处理阶段，在保持高效率的同时超越了基于分析的纯物理模型和现有 AI 模型。其在观测驱动预测、空间泛化方面表现优异，但仍存在六小时边界处时间不连续和网格状伪影等局限。模型已投入业务运行。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI weather models have shown impressive medium-range forecast skill and computational efficiency, but suffer two key shortcomings: their forecasts have lower spatial and temporal resolution than the best physics-based models and they are exclusively initialized with and trained on analysis data. As a result, they cannot directly make use of observations, and any biases in the analysis are inherited by the forecast. WeatherNext 3 addresses these shortcomings and establishes a new state-of-the-art for probabilistic medium-range forecasting skill. First, WeatherNext 3 generates new forecasts every hour (rather than every 6 hours like traditional global models) by ingesting low-latency geostationary satellite data. Second, WeatherNext 3's temporal and spatial resolution are on par with physics-based global models, with hourly time steps and 0.1 degree resolution for single-level variables, including solar radiation and cloud cover. Third, WeatherNext 3 moves beyond traditional analysis variables by learning to predict satellite-derived precipitation estimates, as well as tropical cyclone and station observations. Modelling sparse station data allows WeatherNext 3 to make 2m temperature and dewpoint predictions at any location and time, conditioned on local geographical features, with substantially lower error than competing global models, even when evaluated against unseen stations. Together, WeatherNext 3's capabilities move operational AI-based weather forecasting beyond emulating the traditionally distinct stages of data assimilation, forecasting and post-processing, which helps to further push the frontier of performance and granularity for global weather prediction.

</details>


### [156] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN-Maxent，将Maxent的固定手工特征字典替换为门控循环单元（GRU），以端到端反向传播训练，保留Maxent的仅存在数据统计基础和概率校准，仅将非线性由"先验固定"改为"数据驱动学习"。在沙漠蝗虫生境建模任务上，使用ERA5-Land、MODIS、Sentinel-3的50天环境时间序列，RNN-Maxent在ROC-AUC（0.862 vs 0.792）和F1（0.671 vs 0.590）上均优于标准Maxent，且能以更小的告警面积捕获同等数量的真实蝗虫发现。文章还提出基于RNN-Maxent输出的二维格点上偏置随机游走迁移模型，融合Destination Earth数据，模拟成虫扩散与蝗群形成，用于早期预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Maxent依赖线性组合加手工特征字典（平方、阶跃等），需将时间序列协变量展平为独立特征，丢弃时序结构，难以刻画生态监测中常见的非线性时序关系（如沙漠蝗虫繁殖与降水、土壤湿度、植被指数的动态耦合）。在仅存在数据框架中，需要一种既保留Maxent统计基础，又能从数据中学习非线性时间动态的扩展方法。

Method: 将Maxent的固定特征字典η(z)=b+wz中的线性部分替换为神经网络特征映射η(z)=b+wφ(z,θ)。具体实现采用GRU处理50天×多变量环境时间序列，PyTorch+PyTorch-Lightning端到端反向传播联合训练。训练后沿用Maxent的闭式归一化常数α和熵H，将原始输出校准为存在概率。数据为FAO Locust Watch的Hoppers/Bands记录，协变量含ERA5-Land土壤水、降水、温度；MODIS MXD13C1和Sentinel-3 SY_2_V10的NDVI。训练用2000–2018年、验证2019、测试2020–2021（Sentinel-3 NDVI），坐标吸附到0.05°网格去重，环境序列与存在记录之间保留7天间隔以实现预测行为。RNN-Maxent因非凸优化需以10个不同种子训练，给出均值±标准差与极差。在迁移层面，提出二维格点偏置随机游走（Moore邻域），以RNN-Maxent概率图经随机采样初始化成虫分布，按LAI>0.065、风速>10 m/s、850 hPa温度283.15–313.15 K及最大行程100 km/天的停止/移动规则迭代，并由风向产生有偏方向。模型以35天为新生成虫摄入间隔，融合Destination Earth Digital Twin（CCADT温度、U/V风、EO.CLMS LAI）数据，使用FAO报告以200 km容差命中率校准（2025年10–12月西非校准命中率达91.8%，2025年1–3月东非/阿拉伯半岛独立验证78.1%），最终汇总为像素级"至少一次场景出现概率"与群规模统计。

Result: 测试集n=75,775（22,233真实存在+53,542背景，2020–2021，Sentinel-3 NDVI）。在0.5阈值下，RNN-Maxent相对Vanilla Maxent：ROC-AUC 0.862±0.036（范围0.815–0.904）vs 0.792（+0.070，10/10种子超基线）；F1 0.671±0.056 vs 0.590（+0.081，10/10）；精确率0.612±0.126 vs 0.464（+0.148，9/10）；召回率0.778±0.080 vs 0.808（−0.030）。0.5阈值下RNN-Maxent平均仅标记39.6%±12.5%样本为存在（最保守一轮25.3%），保留17,302/22,233真实存在，仅产生12,716次虚警，相对基线以约96%灵敏度换取少39%虚警。迁移模型在西非2025年10–12月校准期200 km命中率91.8%（中位距离32.8 km），东非/阿拉伯半岛2025年1–3月独立验证200 km命中率78.1%（中位距离65.4 km）。

Conclusion: RNN-Maxent是一种保持Maxent仅存在统计基础与概率校准、用神经网络替代固定特征字典的通用扩展，能够从数据中学习非线性时序关系。在沙漠蝗虫生境制图上，RNN-Maxent在阈值无关与阈值依赖指标上一致优于Vanilla Maxent，主要由精确率驱动，代价是召回率小幅下降和非凸优化带来的种子间方差。叠加偏置随机游走迁移模型与Destination Earth数据，可在West Africa至Northwest Asia大尺度、远程难达区域为早期预警系统提供蝗群形成与扩散预测，模型已在DestinE平台的Desert Locust Monitoring Service上线。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [157] [Extracting Forgotten Prompts from Targeted Unlearned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03662)
*Au Ashley Hoi-Ting,Meghdad Kurmanji,William F. Shen,Nicholas D. Lane,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"目标主动搜索"（TAS）的黑盒攻击方法，揭示了拒绝式机器遗忘（refusal-based unlearning）中的新漏洞：攻击者无需预先知道遗忘提示，即可仅凭保留数据和文本级模型访问推断出被遗忘的实体并重构被遗忘的提示本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对遗忘模型（unlearned models）的攻击均预设遗忘提示已知，专注于恢复其对应答案。然而，遗忘提示本身可能编码敏感信息（如临床隐私、商业机密）。本文指出被遗忘的提示词本身也可被恢复，构成新的隐私与安全威胁。需要评估遗忘干预是否会使遗忘目标变得可推断，以及传统遗忘评估指标（仅衡量答案抑制）是否会掩盖此类目标泄露（target leakage）。

Method: 方法名为"目标主动搜索"（Targeted Active Search, TAS）。核心步骤：1）从保留提示构建候选实体池和可复用模板集（canonical templates），分离"内容"与"结构"；2）使用正则+语义嵌入的组合评分器评估拒绝强度；3）维护槽位感知的Beta后验分布（实体后验和模板后验），采用三阶段查询预算分配——覆盖性预热（≤20%）、Thompson采样自适应搜索（≥70%）、确认阶段（≤10%）；4）对多槽位设置（如PISTOL的双实体关系）进行方向性测试，并采用"软责任"信用分配（soft responsibility credit assignment）；5）通过最大间隔（largest-gap）估计遗忘实体数量，并基于拒绝强度对重构提示进行排序。

Result: 在3个遗忘方法（DPO、NPO、LUNAR）×3个模型家族（Llama2-7B、Llama3-8B、Gemma-7B）×3个基准（DUSK、TOFU、PISTOL）上：TAS在实体识别上达到100%准确率（零方差），提示重构召回率最高达95%，相比暴力查询减少99.7%的查询开销。在多实体场景下排序仍保持完美（MAP=1.0），但基数估计存在一定误差。关键发现：传统遗忘评估指标（Forget ROUGE-1、Forget probability）与提示可重构性不相关——NPO在PISTOL上达成完美答案抑制（ROUGE-1=0.000）的同时，仍有96.1%的遗忘提示可被TAS重构。不同遗忘方法产生不同重构特征：LUNAR精度高但召回低（精确拒绝信号），DPO/NPO召回高但精度低（广泛抑制）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了拒绝式遗忘中的"目标泄露"（target leakage）漏洞：答案抑制与目标隐藏是两种独立的安 全属性。遗忘干预在被遗忘目标周围留下可观察的行为足迹，使得遗忘提示本身可被恢复。该结果促使重新思考遗忘评估标准——仅衡量答案抑制的传统指标无法预测目标可发现性。有效的防御需要实现"行为不可区分性"（behavioural indistinguishability），使得外部观察者无法从模型输出判断特定输入是否属于遗忘区域。本文将此类问题定位为更广义的"引导目标发现"（steering-target discovery）攻击的实例。

Abstract: Recent unlearning methods (e.g. NPO, DPO, LUNAR) make use of refusal alignment to suppress forgotten data. However, it has been shown that refusal responses might leave traces of unlearning, and recent attacks have been able to successfully recover some of the unlearned knowledge. In this paper, we uncover a new vulnerability. Existing attacks typically assume that the forgotten prompts are already known to the adversary and focus on recovering their answers. However, we show that the forgotten prompts themselves can be extracted by using the retained data and black-box access to the model. Our attack, Targeted Active Search (TAS), first identifies the forgotten entities by constructing canonical templates and entity pool, and selectively querying the model using the most informative template-entity pair under a limited query budget. Once the entities are identified, TAS instantiates prompt templates with those entities to probe the unlearned model and reconstruct the forgotten prompts. Experiments across three unlearning methods with three datasets and three LLMs shows that TAS recovers the forgotten entity with $100\%$ accuracy and reconstructs up to $95\%$ of forgotten prompts, all while using up to $99.7\%$ fewer queries than naive probing.

</details>


### [158] [Resolution-Aware Experimental Design under Partial Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03686)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Resolution-Aware Experimental Design (RAED)，一种在部分可识别性条件下选择实验的框架，通过控制错误排除的同时最小化期望非空结构候选集大小。论文证明了信息增益与结构分辨率之间的精确分离，发展了带有限样本校正的学习实现，并在地下流动基准测试中展示了与EIG的真实分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验设计基于期望信息增益(EIG)选择实验，但在部分可识别性场景下，持续的干扰不确定性会使同一观测具有不同的结构含义。作者指出存在"跨干扰结构混叠"现象——某些实验在每个固定干扰状态下完美区分结构族，但任何干扰盲的终端规则都无法安全地将观测映射到唯一的结构结论。这使得基于信息的设计目标与真正需要的"可控错误排除下最小残留不确定性"目标产生分歧。

Method: RAED的核心方法包括：(1) 形式化决策问题——在假排除容忍度α和干扰尾水平δ下，选择使期望候选集大小J最小化的实验；(2) 精确的循环构造证明跨干扰混叠分离（Theorem 4.1），展示信息增益、结构EIG、平均贝叶斯分类和干扰边际信息性都可以偏好同一实验，而RAED严格偏好另一个；(3) 通过共同garbling的Blackwell单调性（Proposition 4.3）证明RAED在真正的复合信息性下保持期望排序；(4) 基于得分的候选集规则学习，具有nuisance平均和正尾校正的嵌套有限样本验证（Theorem 5.1）；(5) 稀有尾样本复杂度障碍刻画（Proposition 5.2）。

Result: 在四个基准测试中：(1) Watt本体论研究表明相同INJ5脉冲/衰减测试在K=3,6,6,12四种结构目标下均被选中，J值从1.42到1.60；(2) 受限感知下，WCA显示RAED与EIG在16个感知单元中有7个清洁分歧，RAED选择的实验在多数情况下产生更小的J（降幅0.10-0.39）；(3) GWAE-Fluvial显示尾部保护改变了所选物理实验（从E01对称银行到E07分级速率对），平均RAED的J=1.05但M1/M2最差5%风险为0.434/0.876，而尾RAED的J=1.79但风险降至0.014/0.003；(4) 甲烷氧化基准在5%假排除容忍度下，三种结构族的校准上界分别为0.048、0.038、0.048，实现95%联合置信度的有限样本人口保证。

Conclusion: RAED将实验设计从"获取多少信息"重新框架为"在干扰不确定性下能可靠排除哪些结构解释"。论文证明跨干扰混叠是该框架与信息增益产生分歧的精确机制，而共同garbling结果保留了真正的Blackwell信息性下的排序。有限样本校准使目标在受限得分模型下可用，但罕见尾保护需要Ω(log(1/β)/αδ)个独立干扰状态。基准测试表明实验设计不仅是信息获取问题，还需要明确权衡可控错误排除与可接受残留歧义之间的取舍。

Abstract: Experimental design is commonly framed as choosing the experiment expected to provide the most information. Under partial identifiability however, persistent nuisance uncertainty can make the same observation carry different structural meanings. We introduce Resolution-Aware Experimental Design (RAED), which selects an experiment by the smallest expected nonempty structural candidate set achievable subject to false-exclusion control. We prove an exact cross-nuisance aliasing separation: an experiment can be preferred by structural and full-latent information gain, average classification, and nuisance-marginalized informativeness while having arbitrarily poorer valid structural resolution. RAED nevertheless preserves the expected ordering under a genuine composite Blackwell comparison. To make this criterion operational, we develop a learned score-based implementation with finite-sample nuisance-average and positive-tail calibration, and characterize a rare-tail sample-complexity obstruction. Under constrained sensing, two subsurface-flow benchmarks exhibit genuine RAED--expected-information-gain (EIG) experiment-selection disagreements, with the clearest and largest held-out resolution differences in WCA. In a fluvial benchmark, tail protection changes the selected physical experiment and replaces hard-region false exclusions primarily with explicit ambiguity. In a mechanistic methane-oxidation benchmark, a prospectively specified 5\% false-exclusion tolerance also yields a nontrivial finite-sample population guarantee for tail-sensitive nuisance risk, with 95\% joint confidence across all three structural families.

</details>


### [159] [Federated Causal Discovery via Regression-Directed Cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03705)
*Pablo Torrijos,Fabio Stella,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedRCD 家族算法，将基于高阶累积量的 LiNGAM 因果发现方法适配到联邦学习环境（支持水平、垂直、混合划分），通过四阶非对称性得分与稳定的 OLS 压缩系数解决了现有 FedISHC 在对称噪声下因三阶累积量分母趋于零而失效的问题。同时揭示了一个反直觉发现：整个累积量家族（包括集中式基线）在实际有限样本下并非按总体非对称性排序，而是按 DAG 沿有向路径诱导的"方差阶梯"排序——这正是 varsortability 在累积量方法中的对应物，边际标准化会使所有累积量方法崩溃至近随机排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现（特别是 LiNGAM 框架）在医疗等领域应用价值大，但数据稀缺且受 GDPR 等隐私法规约束，跨机构集中数据不可行。联邦学习提供了平衡隐私与发现精度的途径。然而 DirectLiNGAM 依赖非参数独立性检验难以直接联邦化；现有基于三阶累积量的 FedISHC 在对称噪声下失效（κ₃ 在分母趋于零）。更深层的问题是：在实际有限样本下，累积量类方法真正读取的信号是什么？

Method: 1) 利用高阶累积量张量的多线性、可加性（对独立样本群精确可加）特性，设计单轮通信协议同时支持水平、垂直、混合联邦划分及精确联邦反学习；2) 提出 FedRCD 三变体：FedRCD-NI（无压缩，单次行和排序，O(p²) 服务端开销）、FedRCD-Def（服务端代数压缩 OLS，O(p³)，单轮）、FedRCD-X（客户端 OLS 残差压缩，每轮重新计算累积量，p-1 轮）；3) 核心识别使用四阶非对称性得分 τ⁽⁴⁾，压缩系数替换为方差有界远离零的 β̂ = Σ̂_js/Σ̂_ss；4) 提出 Ancestral Accuracy（AncAcc）指标，基于 DAG 传递闭包评估排序质量；5) 通过边际标准化（将 v 从 0.953 降至 0.5）作为诊断工具揭示方差阶梯效应。

Result: 在 Erdős-Rényi ER-2 DAG（11 种噪声族）与 8 个 bnlearn 真实拓扑上的实验表明：FedRCD-X 在对称与偏态噪声下最接近集中式 DirectLiNGAM；FedRCD-NI 略低；FedISHC 因三阶分母问题显著落后；FedRCD-Def 受代数噪声累积限制居中。三变体水平、垂直、混合联邦结果完全一致（联邦不变性）。最关键发现：边际标准化使所有累积量方法（含集中式 HC、HC-LiNGAM）的 AncAcc 跌至 0.30–0.47（低于 0.5 随机基线，意味着排序被反转），而 DirectLiNGAM 保持 0.996–0.998 不变。这证实整个累积量家族在 N≈数千样本下实际读取的是 DAG 深度诱导的方差阶梯信号，而非总体非对称性。

Conclusion: FedRCD 家族在算法层面成功解决了 FedISHC 的对称噪声失效问题：四阶得分 + 稳定 OLS 系数同时修复了识别与压缩两个环节。FedRCD-NI 和 FedRCD-X 分别是 HC-LiNGAM 和 HC 的精确联邦对应物；单轮变体支持任意粒度的精确联邦反学习。然而更深层的诊断表明累积量方法存在根本性的尺度依赖性：其性能依赖于"后代节点从祖先积累方差"这一结构性质（方差分层），属于 Reisach 等人 (2021) 为基于 MSE 的连续优化方法所识别的尺度依赖范畴。这意味着累积量方法在结构满足方差积累假设时表现良好，但与尺度不变的 DirectLiNGAM 相比缺乏鲁棒性。未来工作包括：确定方差分层何时充分、达到总体非对称性所需的样本量、以及对传输张量施加差分隐私。

Abstract: In this paper we study linear non-Gaussian acyclic models (LiNGAM) when used in federated environments. These causal models allow one to go beyond Markov equivalence. However, in many domains data are scarce, and increasing the sample size by centralising data from different clients is not advisable due to regulations such as the GDPR. The federated environment offers an attractive option to balance privacy and causal discovery accuracy. Unfortunately, the standard centralised estimator in the LiNGAM setting, i.e., DirectLiNGAM, cannot be straightforwardly federated. Higher-order cumulant tensors offer a way around this obstacle: they depend only on the joint distribution of the variables involved and add exactly across independent sample groups, so a single communication round suffices in horizontal, vertical, and hybrid partitions. However, FedISHC, i.e., the current federated method along these lines, breaks down under near-symmetric noise. To overcome the above limitation, we introduce the FedRCD family of causal discovery algorithms, and investigate three variants that trade off communication rounds against algebraic noise; two of them are exact federated counterparts of the centralised high-order cumulant (HC) and HC-LiNGAM algorithms, and the single-round variants further effectively support exact unlearning at any granularity, from a single observation to a whole client. Numerical experiments show that at sample sizes typical of real deployments, the entire cumulant-based federated family does not actually rank variables by the population asymmetry that the scores encode at zero. It ranks them by a variance ladder induced by the DAG along its directed paths, the cumulant counterpart of varsortability. Marginal standardisation collapses every cumulant method to near-random ordering, while scale-invariant DirectLiNGAM, not federable under this protocol, is unaffected.

</details>


### [160] [Projected Riemannian Gradient Descent for the Bures-Wasserstein Barycenter: Dimension-Independent Linear Convergence at Unit Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03762)
*A. Afham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Bures–Wasserstein (BW) 重心问题及不变量矩阵投影问题，提出了一种投影黎曼梯度下降算法，在单位步长下实现了与维度无关的线性收敛，速率为 $(1-\kappa^{-3/2})$，并改进了已有小步长方法的 $\kappa$ 依赖性。核心贡献是一条投影引理：将正定矩阵的特征值裁剪至 $[\alpha,\beta]$ 即为其在 BW 度量下向良好条件集 $[\alpha I, \beta I]$ 的非扩张投影，该投影几乎无额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单位步长 BW 黎曼梯度下降（实践中常用的固定点迭代）虽然收敛迅速，但其收敛性分析存在两难：单位步长的保证在最坏情况下对维度呈指数依赖（$\kappa^d$），而与维度无关的保证则需要小步长（$\eta = O(1/\kappa)$），迭代复杂度高达 $O(\kappa^{5/2}\log(1/\epsilon))$，牺牲了实际运行速度。本文旨在打破这一两难局面。

Method: 提出 Projected BW-GD 算法：在单位步长黎曼梯度下降的每一步之后，叠加一个向良好条件集 $[\alpha I, \beta I]$ 的精确 BW 度量投影。技术核心是证明特征值裁剪操作 clip$_{\alpha,\beta}$ 即为该集合上的非扩张（1-李普希茨）BW 度量投影（投影引理）。在方根流形 GL(d) 上分析时，利用 Euclidean 强凸性通过提升引理得到与维度无关的 PL 不等式，并结合单位步长的下降引理（方根流形上的方差恒等式）得到线性收敛速率。投影操作复用了下一步迭代所需的特征分解，因此几乎无额外开销。对于不变量矩阵投影问题，利用不变量子流形的全测地性将已有固定点算法统一处理。

Result: 主定理（Theorem 10）证明了 Projected BW-GD 以速率 $(1-\kappa^{-3/2})$ 实现与维度无关的线性收敛，迭代复杂度为 $\kappa^{3/2}\log(1/\epsilon)$。对于重心问题，使用精细化条件数 $\kappa' \leq \kappa$ 可进一步改进。利用精细化区间 $[\alpha'I, \beta'I]$ 的投影亦可获得收敛性。此外，还给出了一个基于步长的后验终止准则（Corollary 12）。数值实验表明，在大多数实际实例中，投影操作从不激活，但作为休眠的安全保障提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入投影操作，在不损失单位步长实际速度的前提下，获得了与维度无关的线性收敛理论保证，并在 $\kappa$ 依赖性上优于已有的小步长结果。分析基于平坦的方根流形，避免了广义测地线凸性的复杂工具。不变量子流形的全测地性使得分析可直接推广到不变量矩阵投影问题。开放的挑战包括：证明从典型初始化出发未投影迭代天然保持 $[\alpha I, \beta I]$，以及缩小理论保证与实际观测速率之间的差距。

Abstract: The computation of the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) barycenter of an ensemble of positive definite matrices arises throughout machine learning, optimal transport, and quantum information. Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) at unit step size -- the fixed-point iteration used in practice -- converges rapidly, yet existing analyses present a dichotomy: unit-step guarantees carry worst-case exponential dependence on the dimension, while dimension-independent guarantees require small step sizes that forfeit the empirical speed. We resolve this dichotomy, not by improving the guarantees for unit-step RGD, but by proposing a Projected RGD algorithm that achieves dimension-independent linear convergence at unit step size. The achieved rate, $(1 - \kappa^{-3/2})$, where $\kappa$ is the condition number of the ensemble, also polynomially improves on the best small-step guarantee ($\kappa^{3/2}$ versus $\kappa^{5/2}$ iteration complexity). The crux is a novel Projection Lemma: clipping the eigenvalues of a positive matrix to an interval $[\alpha, \beta]$ is the closed-form, non-expansive (1-Lipschitz) BW-metric projection onto the set $\{S : \alpha I \leq S \leq \beta I\}$ -- a statement which, unlike its known one-sided counterpart, does not follow from convexity. The projection is moreover free: it reuses an eigendecomposition the next iteration must perform in any case, so the projected and unprojected iterations cost the same per step. The same analysis covers the invariant matrix projection problem of Brahmachari et al. (2025), whose fixed-point algorithm we identify as unit-step RGD on a totally geodesic submanifold, thereby extending the dimension-independent guarantee to that setting verbatim.

</details>


### [161] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCast v2，一种基于机器学习的12小时云量预报模型。该模型先在欧洲区域再分析数据(CERRA)上预训练学习云演变动力学，再通过条件流匹配(CFM)适配到卫星观测初始场，结合NWP大气强迫场生成预报。相较于前代CloudCast v1，MAE降低10%，并在3-6小时后FSS更优，能在1-12小时范围内同时保持卫星初始细节和大气动力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短临外推方法（1-2小时有效）在3小时后因云生消而失效，而NWP模式在初始化时无法准确表征卫星观测的云场。如何将观测初始化的机器学习预报从传统的1-3小时临近预报(short-range nowcasting)扩展到12小时，同时保留卫星云场的空间细节，是核心挑战。

Method: 采用四阶段训练流程：(1) 基于Swin Transformer的U形视觉Transformer架构；(2) 在CERRA再分析数据上预训练（一阶MSE→自回归6小时滚动）；(3) 在NWCSAF卫星有效云量上确定性微调（直接预测12小时，使用BCE+Soft-FSS损失）；(4) 采用clean-target参数化的条件流匹配(CFM)进行生成式适配，将噪声在观测/NWP条件约束下变换为云量预报。研究区域为斯堪的纳维亚5km网格，验证使用2025年独立测试集（2855个初始化时刻），对比CloudCast v1、MEPS NWP基线及持久性基准，使用MAE、ACC、MSESS、FSS等指标评估。

Result: CloudCast v2在1-12小时范围内的MAE较CloudCast v1降低10%；在3-6小时后（取决于云量类别）FSS超越v1；ACC和MSESS均优于v1和MEPS；在云量剧烈变化子集（前20%动态案例）上，相较拉格朗日持久性基线（光流平流）保持正技巧，并在96.3%的高变化案例中于t+5时优于v1。但CFM带来权衡：像素级误差（MAE）略有升高，但邻域空间一致性(FSS)显著改善；个别个例显示CFM可能过早生成锐利云结构（时效错误）。推理时间约90秒（NVIDIA H100）。

Conclusion: 观测初始化的机器学习模型在NWP大气强迫驱动下，可将云量预报技巧延伸至12小时，弥合临近预报与短期预报之间的鸿沟。CloudCast v2是对NWP的补充而非替代，以极小计算开销融合最新卫星观测与NWP大气场。未来工作包括：将CFM发展为概率/集合预报、扩展至多年度多区域、亚小时级预报、以及更优的大气强迫变量选择。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [162] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OBER+框架，作为已部署机构达成度计算平台的扩展，引入"5R循环"（Report-Reflect-Recommend-Redesign-Reassess），将达成度计算与基于证据的改进决策全程绑定，并通过句子嵌入与认知水平比较检测课程学习成果（CLO）陈述的修订，确保跨授课周期的达成度序列在变更前后具有可比性。在两门真实课程的应用中，揭示了三种当前实践无法暴露的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在成果导向教育（OBE）实践中，机构虽能常规计算课程学习成果达成度，但从"测得的差距"到"评估后的改进行动"这一关键环节仍依赖委员会人工审议，缺少计算化、可追溯的机制。同时，现有达成度计算通常假设连续授课中的同一CLO编号指向同一学习内容，但当成果陈述被实质性修订时，该假设即告失效，导致趋势判断、改进决策与效果归因全部建立在不可比的量之上。课程分析文献亦明确指出"工具如何影响决策与结果"方面证据缺失。

Method: 1. **5R循环**：Report积累每次授课达成度；Reflect基于三授课窗口产生告警（单次低于目标）与标记（最近三次中至少两次未达标），并以CAA四档量表（High/Medium/Low/Very Low）评定严重度；Recommend记录决策（含标准、创新、开放三类实践及证据分级）；Redesign维护形式与检测两类变更日志；Reassess计算缺口闭合率。2. **达成度计算**：按"组件→CLO→课程→PLO"四级链式平均。3. **成果连续性检验**：用spaCy en_core_web_md的300维词向量计算相邻授课CLO陈述的余弦相似度，结合Bloom分类法首动词集合判断修订类型（Unchanged/Replacement/Level change/Paraphrase，τ=0.90）；另设编号对齐检查（式13），仅基于相似度排序，无需阈值与标签。4. **验证方法**：以平台公布数据按文档化规则重算并对比，并测试按标记分配矩阵加权能否复现。

Result: 在CS F351（计算理论基础）与CS F459（计算机视觉）两门课程的实时记录上获得三项结果：（1）CS F351全部五个CLO在2024-25至2025-26年间被实质性重定义，学科内容在不同CLO编号间迁移（编号5最接近前一授课的编号4，相似度0.955 vs 0.933），并新增一项，朴素读法将显示该课从65.0%骤降至40.3%。（2）平台公布的10项达成度中有6项与其文档化规则不符，差异最高达8.6个百分点，按标记分配矩阵加权则能精确复现全部数值——已识别为计算缺陷并向机构报告。（3）15对陈述中5对完全相同；其余10对中6对的最相似旧版CLO编号不同，意味着按编号索引的达成度序列并不跟随其报告的学科内容。

Conclusion: OBER+提供了一个面向成果导向报告的可计算设计，仅需每次授课的达成度单一输入即可实施，且不重新计算达成度本身（这一特性使其在平台存在计算缺陷时仍能正确运行）。研究证明：（a）跨授课的达成度序列在两端CLO陈述被比较前不可作为趋势解读；（b）该比较可计算化且成本极低；（c）省略此比较的机构可能基于未测量的变动采取行动。框架已开源部署（oberplus.streamlit.app），任何按授课报告达成度的平台均可采纳。局限包括样本量小、真实陈述对未标注、阈值基于构造集设定、flag/严重度/缺口闭合仅在构造记录上演示。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results. Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [163] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种“固定时间地标、单次会话一个表示”的建模框架：仅使用比赛或训练开始至10/20/30/40/50/60分钟的信息，为每个运动员-会话构建表示，并继续以当天是否与伤病报告相关这一会话级标签为目标，避免将同一天粗粒度标签错误解释为逐分钟风险。数据来自2020年SoccerMon，覆盖48名精英女子足球运动员的3,743个运动员-会话，其中22个伤病相关会话仅来自5名运动员，且全部集中于Team A。主要的CUM+DYN逻辑回归结果显示，各地标ROC-AUC为0.367–0.607、PR-AUC为0.0080–0.0150，运动员聚类自助法区间很宽；20–30分钟的表观表现较高，但40–60分钟低于或接近类别占比基线。PRE特征在部分地标给出更高点估计，但结果不确定；Random Forest和XGBoost未形成一致优势。总体而言，该研究证明了方法框架的合理性，却没有建立可靠、可部署的伤病预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐分钟监测数据具有很高的时间分辨率，但伤病监督通常只提供运动员与日期，甚至来自运动员提交的伤病报告，没有精确的伤情发生分钟。若把一个伤病相关会话的标签复制给所有分钟，会无依据地制造逐分钟监督信息，暗示已知的伤病发生时点，并可能催生不现实的事中预警解释。论文因此要解决预测变量与目标变量时间粒度不匹配的问题，在不夸大监督信息的前提下，研究截至某个固定会话时刻的信息能否区分当天与伤病相关的运动员-会话。

Method: 研究使用2020年SoccerMon分钟级多模态数据。预处理得到380,193条分钟记录、251个团队-日期暴露单元、3,743个运动员-会话，涉及48名精英女子足球运动员；其中22个伤病相关会话来自5名运动员，全部位于Team A。模型最终使用Team A的2,259个会话和27名运动员。分别在10、20、30、40、50和60分钟构建表示，且每个运动员-会话在每个地标仅出现一次。PRE包含14个训练前/情境特征；CUM包含30个传感器累积统计；DYN包含33个动态、滚动和当前分钟特征。评估采用五折运动员不相交验证，确保同一运动员的会话不会同时进入训练和测试，并使用逻辑回归为主要模型。随机森林和XGBoost在相同数据、外层折和CUM+DYN特征上作为基准比较。预处理参数仅由外层训练集估计，测试预测经汇总后通过1,000次运动员聚类自助法计算区间。稳健性分析包括固定2,104个60分钟可评估会话的共同队列、100种阴性运动员折分配、按运动员等权的描述性指标，以及留一阳性运动员分析。

Result: 主要CUM+DYN逻辑回归的ROC-AUC在10、20、30、40、50、60分钟依次为0.499、0.557、0.607、0.428、0.402和0.367；95%运动员聚类自助区间均较宽，并与随机水平0.5大量重叠。PR-AUC依次为0.0107、0.0150、0.0129、0.0088、0.0079和0.0080；相应的PR基线约为0.0097–0.0105，因此10–30分钟仅略高于基线，40–60分钟低于或接近基线。固定共同队列后，30分钟ROC-AUC仍为0.597，60分钟仍为0.367，支持非单调的时序模式并非完全由各地标样本构成变化造成。100种阴性运动员分配得到的中位ROC-AUC为0.498、0.579、0.609、0.419、0.409和0.351，方向相对稳定。按运动员等权时，20分钟ROC-AUC升至0.746，40分钟升至0.618，说明估计量不同会显著改变结果幅度。PRE及包含PRE的特征组合在多个地标点估计更高，例如PRE+DYN在20分钟ROC-AUC为0.784、PR-AUC为0.0526；但置信区间宽，且与CUM+DYN的配对比较均包含零，不能据此认定稳定优越。随机森林和XGBoost只在特定地标优于逻辑回归，未形成跨地标一致优势。五个探索性特征在留一阳性运动员分析中保持同号，但该分析使用了各运动员自身阴性会话作为参考，不属于留出预测的特征重要性验证。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出并透明评估了一种与分析单位一致的固定地标框架：在缺少伤情发生时间时，只使用地标前已观测的信息，同时始终保留会话级目标。研究发现，在该小阳性样本队列中，区分能力随地标、特征表示和汇总估计量而变化；共同队列和多种折分配下的定性时序模式具有一定稳健性，但总体证据弱且不确定。论文不支持定位伤病发生分钟、逐分钟前瞻风险、临床最佳干预时点、校准后的临床风险概率或部署级预警。PRE特征可能含有互补信息，但因高缺失率及部分时间信息不明确而不能视为可靠优势。模型的泛化、临床效度及因果解释均未建立。未来需要包含更多阳性运动员、医疗确认结局、精确伤病发生时点及外部团队验证的数据，才能评估真正的风险预测或实时部署价值。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated. This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown. Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision. A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available. Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks. Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [164] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从有序Bernoulli字核出发，通过反整数等值面推导出关于Re z=1/2的共轭对称垂直几何，并建立二次型坐标Q(z)=1/4+u²的整数量子化。将临界线零点纵坐标代入该坐标后，利用周期第一Bernoulli函数得到精确的整数-残差分解，并通过圆化得到残差相位Z_k。文章进一步将整数分解提升到素数-指数空间，并引入复指数s的提升，使Dirichlet原子m^(-s)同时组织Dirichlet级数和Euler乘积两种zeta装配。论文明确声称不证明黎曼猜想，仅提供代数组织框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是从一个与zeta函数无关的出发点——有序Bernoulli字核的有序似然——出发，独立地构造出与黎曼临界线Re s=1/2相关的共轭对称几何。这一动机与传统的从zeta函数或其解析延拓入手的方法形成对比，旨在建立一种"原始的"几何坐标，然后将其与zeta零点数据、整数分解、Dirichlet级数和Euler乘积等多个经典数学对象联系起来，以提供一个统一的代数和组织框架。

Method: 论文的方法分为五个层次的精确代数构造：(1) 从核f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^{n-k}出发，二进制反整数等值面2^(-n)唯一选定p=1/2，补全保持复延拓1-z=z̄产生共轭对称垂直线z=1/2±iu；(2) 二次型坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u²具有精确最小值Q_min=1/4和逆映射，整数限制产生离散量子化族；(3) 临界线零点ρ_k=1/2+iγ_k代入得L_k=1/4+γ_k²，通过精确整数-残差分解L_k=N_k+δ_k及周期第一Bernoulli函数δ_k=B̃_1(γ_k²+3/4)，圆化得Z_k=exp(2πiδ_k)；(4) 素数-指数向量空间c_00(P)中唯一分解将整数分为单素数、纯素数幂和多素数复合三类，对数泛函L实现仿射-对数折叠；(5) 引入复指数s的提升F(z,s,k)=z^k(1-z)^{s-k}，求解F=m^(-s)使Dirichlet原子m^(-s)同时成为Dirichlet枚举面和Euler生成面的公共接口。所有精确恒等式、经典桥梁、数值控制和开放条件Weyl统计被显式分离。

Result: 论文获得的主要精确结果包括：(1) 二进制等值面唯一选择实锚点p=1/2（命题2.1-2.2）；(2) 补全保持复延拓强制Re z=1/2的共轭对称垂直几何（命题3.1-3.4）；(3) 二次型坐标具有锐最小值1/4和精确逆映射，整数限制实现算术量子化（公式12-14）；(4) 临界线零点产生的水平L_k具有精确的周期Bernoulli残差分解（公式17-18）；(5) 圆化残差相位Z_k=ie^{2πiγ_k²}与整数壳N_k构成无损对（公式21）；(6) 素数-指数空间分类和仿射-对数折叠保持唯一分解性（命题6.2-6.4，推论6.5）；(7) 复指数s的提升实现公共Dirichlet原子（命题7.1，公式27-29）；(8) 同一原子m^(-s)组织Dirichlet枚举面（公式31）和Euler生成面（公式32-33）。数值方面：前40个零点对应5个素数壳（5/40=12.5%，与PNT预测≈4.83基本一致），前200个残差的KS检验p值0.3661（不拒绝均匀），δ与ω(N)、Ω(N)的相关系数均较弱（|corr|≤0.13），初始控制为负。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是一个五层对齐的代数和组织框架，而非黎曼猜想的证明：第一层从Bernoulli核出发得到共轭对称垂直几何；第二层将整数按算术角色分类；第三层通过二次型坐标提供带精确反演的离散量子化；第四层得到精确的整数-周期Bernoulli残差分解和圆化相位，同时明确区分局部B̃_1角色与Euler-Maclaurin中高级Bernoulli数的角色；第五层通过复指数s提升使m^(-s)成为Dirichlet枚举和Euler乘积的公共接口。论文保持精确恒等式、经典理论、有限数值控制和开放条件Weyl统计问题的显式分离，提出开放问题：条件圆矩W_h(A;K)是否在某些整数壳算术类A中产生非平凡傅里叶信号？前200个零点的初步控制为负。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [165] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向移动网络站点能源低效识别的无监督、相对同类站点的表示学习方法。作者扩展最小失真嵌入（MDE），将结构相似性与局部能耗偏差共同编码到低维空间：对结构相近但能耗异常偏高的站点施加排斥，使其偏离同类邻域；再以相对邻域距离与邻域内部离散度之比构造异常分数。实验基于5372个真实站点数据生成5000个合成站点，并注入四类低效模式。方法在1%—99%的污染率下均优于常规无监督异常检测器；在10%污染率下ROC-AUC为0.9105、Precision@10%为0.5540。嵌入还能提升Isolation Forest、GMM和自编码器的检测性能，并通过伪标签蒸馏为轻量级分类器提供可部署的筛选信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络站点存在制冷控制器故障、空闲射频设备、寄生辅助负载等长期低效问题，但真实数据没有可靠标签，且历史能耗可能已将长期低效吸收为“正常基线”。绝对能耗或全局异常检测还会把站点结构、规模、厂商、共享状态和业务负载等差异误判为低效。因此，论文将低效重新定义为：站点能耗相对于结构与运营特征相近的同组站点明显不一致，从而避免依赖历史基线或固定阈值。

Method: 方法以站点结构属性定义“应当与谁比较”，以能耗定义“比较是否一致”。流程包括：1）从结构特征构建kNN图；2）在同厂商、同共享状态和同类桅杆的邻居中计算局部能耗基线；3）计算站点能耗与基线的对数比值；4）将结构边权重按邻居异常程度降低，异常足够大时使边权变为负，从而在MDE中形成吸引—排斥机制；5）采样非相邻站点作为固定负权边，进一步分离非相似站点；6）求解能量感知MDE目标；7）以站点到结构邻居的平均距离除以邻居内部平均成对距离，得到归一化位移分数。分数越高，越可能是低效候选。方法还可按固定异常比例生成伪标签，训练逻辑回归、随机森林或XGBoost学生模型。

Result: 在合成数据的污染率扫描中，论文方法在1%至99%的所有采样污染率下均取得最高ROC-AUC：低污染率时为0.84，99%污染率时仍为0.71；常规无监督基线整体接近或不超过约0.60。10%污染率下，所提分数ROC-AUC为0.9105、PR-AUC为0.5578、Precision@10%为0.5540；Isolation Forest、GMM和自编码器迁移到该嵌入后，ROC-AUC分别由0.5685、0.5774和0.7242提升至0.9055、0.8972和0.8891。与可部署的监督残差模型相比，RF在低污染率时平均表现更好，但污染率超过20%后方法反超；99%污染率时两者ROC-AUC分别为0.714和0.665。伪标签蒸馏中，RF和XGBoost学生模型测试Precision@10%均为0.5867，高于教师模型的0.5667，但测试ROC-AUC降至0.8906和0.8768。论文还报告了真实现场核查案例，包括错误控制配置导致两台空调持续运行等问题。

Conclusion: 论文证明，能源低效更适合被建模为结构相似站点之间的相对能耗不一致，而不是绝对能耗异常或基于历史数据的残差。能量感知MDE通过将结构同质性和能耗偏差同时写入表示目标，产生了更有利于异常检测的邻域几何结构，并在高污染、未标注场景下比残差方法更稳健。其主要价值是形成站点现场检查的优先级排序，并可通过伪标签蒸馏为轻量级分类器服务。局限在于定量结论主要来自受控合成注入，缺少真实低效标签和纵向时间信息；一次性调参可能无法适应季节变化、配置漂移和未知污染率；罕见结构配置也可能缺乏可靠同类邻居。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何视角（统计流形上的近端策略改进）的离线强化学习actor更新统一框架，将TD3+BC、ReBRAC、IQL/AWAC等经典方法解释为流形梯度流的单步隐式Euler离散（SPI），并提出多步近端策略改进（MPI）——一种即插即用的细化机制，通过重新中心化的连续近端步骤实现对数据集支持之外的受控策略改进，同时保持每步的近端正则化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临分布偏移的核心挑战：一方面策略更新需保持接近数据集支持以维持价值估计可靠性，另一方面有意义的改进又需超越行为分布。现有方法（行为锚定如TD3+BC、ReBRAC；优势加权如IQL、AWAC；评论家保守化如CQL、EDAC）通过不同程度的约束缓解分布偏移，但actor侧存在稳定性与改进不足的根本矛盾——过度锚定导致欠改进，约束不足则在分布偏移下不稳定。作者提出一个自然的关键问题：能否设计一种原则性的策略更新机制，在保持稳定性的同时实现对数据集支持之外的受控改进？

Method: 方法包含三个层次：(1) 将策略族建模为赋予度量几何（Fisher-Rao或Wasserstein-2）的统计流形M，actor目标被解释为由评论家定义能量泛函诱导的流形梯度流的单步近端更新（SPI）。证明TD3+BC/ReBRAC对应Dirac极限下Wasserstein-2几何的SPI，IQL/AWAC局部对应Fisher-Rao几何的SPI（通过KL散度局部近似）。(2) 提出MPI：在每次训练迭代内从基础策略π_base出发，执行K个重新中心化的连续近端步骤，每步解子问题prox(ℰ_s|π_{i-1})(π)，等价于对同一能量泛函的K步隐式Euler细化离散，总"流时间"为Στ_i。(3) 实证实现：对角高斯策略使用闭式W2距离，高维或一般Wasserstein情形使用Sinkhorn迭代作为正则化近似。由于Fisher-Rao在近确定性区域会出现均值更新以Σ_base为预条件子、方差收缩等结构性问题，实践中对IQL也采用Wasserstein-2几何。算法作为可插拔细化阶段，在每步保持基础算法的评论家更新不变。
