TL;DR: 本文提出"价值保留架构模式"(Value-Preserving Architectural Patterns),主张在多智能体系统(MAS)的架构层面而非仅靠事后审查来保障隐私、多元性和公平性等人类核心价值,系统性地给出了三种架构模式及其真实用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI与LLM驱动的多智能体系统(MAS)在获得更强能力的同时,自主性提升带来透明度下降、人类监督削弱以及价值失准风险。传统软件工程关注功能性正确性,事后审查(guardrails/human-in-the-loop)无法逆转上游已传播的偏差或隐私泄漏;将价值保障下沉到架构层成为必要路径。核心研究问题:如何利用架构设计来支撑智能体AI时代的人类核心价值?

Method: 采用"愿景论文(Vision Paper)+架构模式(Architectural Patterns)"方法论。每个模式以"context–problem–solution"形式描述,目标是把人类价值作为一等设计目标嵌入MAS架构:
(1) Federated Silos Coordination(联邦化筒仓协调):中心化协调器+领域智能体,采用hub-and-spoke拓扑,域内抽象化(abstraction)与数据最小化,域间不直连,用于保障隐私。
(2) Peer-to-Peer Deliberation(点对点协商):完全去中心化、完全连通图,每个智能体代表不同立场,引入共识度量(consensus metric)提供可读的停止条件与极化干预,未达成共识时显式呈现剩余分歧,用于保障多元性。
(3) Plan-Triggered Guard Agents(计划触发的守护智能体):将管道分为plan/review/execute三阶段,由偏见检测编排器按计划结构选择性激活对应偏见类别的守护智能体,不可自动消解时升级到人类,用于保障公平性。
并以个性化医疗决策、新闻多元化摘要、HR候选人排序三个用例说明。

Result: 给出了三种价值保留MAS架构模式及其设计原理、组件职责、协调流程,并通过真实场景示例说明其结构性价值保障效果:
- 医疗+财务用例展示了跨域数据隔离与按需聚合;
- 国际气候峰会新闻摘要用例展示了多元视角的结构化代表与收敛控制;
- HR排序用例展示了针对特定偏见类别的计划级检测、人工升级与缓解路径。
论文将该工作定位为"迈向统一价值保留架构模式目录"的第一步。

Conclusion: 架构设计选择(协调机制、通信协议、系统拓扑)是塑造MAS行为与产出的关键,可在功能与性能之外促进价值导向行为。所提三种架构模式为可信MAS的工程化提供了可复用设计基础。未来工作包括:多种模式组合以同时满足多重价值需求及其协同/权衡问题、运行时价值演化与利益相关者需求适应性、以及用于衡量价值保留的度量指标(如多元对齐度量)。

Abstract: The emergence of agentic AI and LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) presents unprecedented opportunities for automating complex tasks, while simultaneously raising critical concerns about the preservation of fundamental human-centered values, such as privacy, fairness, and safety. Although software engineering has traditionally focused on functional correctness, the adoption of LLMs and AI agents into complex socio-technical systems has intensified the need for responsible software engineering and robust value alignment. In MAS, architectural design decisions, such as coordination mechanisms, communication protocols, and system topologies, play a central role in shaping system behavior and the outcomes they produce. This paper argues that architectural choices influence not only the functionality and performance of MAS but can also promote value-oriented system behavior. Therefore, we investigate how different architectural designs support different human-centered values, discussing the following value-preserving architectural patterns: (i) a privacy-aware architecture with a federated topology, (ii) a distributed architecture to promote pluralism and diversity, and (iii) a guard-agent architecture to detect and mitigate unfairness. Finally, we introduce representative use cases to illustrate the proposed architectures in real-world scenarios. By linking architectural design with human-centered values, this work lays the foundation for a unified set of architectural patterns and guidelines towards the design of trustworthy MAS.

</details>


### [224] [Speak for Me: Giving LLMs the Situational Awareness to Participate in a Meeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03923)
*Muneeb Khan,Frederic Kirstein,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPA架构,通过显式会议状态跟踪和"感知-行动-再校准"循环,解决LLM代理在在线会议代理场景中"何时发言"的判断难题,将沉默率从51.4%降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线会议中,纯提示词的LLM代理无法判断何时该代表缺席参与者发言——它们在AMI语料库中51.4%的有效发言机会上保持沉默。核心失败模式是"静默弃权"(silent abstention),根源在于缺乏对参与者立场、话题覆盖度和发言权(floor)的结构化状态建模,仅靠扩展上下文窗口无法弥补这一推理缺陷。

Method: CAPA(Collaborative Agent Predictive Architecture)采用"感知-行动-再校准"循环,维护一个显式会议状态向量m_t(包含活跃话题、决策、未决问题、参与者立场、先前覆盖度、发言权六个字段)。关键模块包括:Perceiver(从每轮更新状态)、Predictor(预测下一轮走向)、Controller(决定Silent/Speak及具体命题z_t)、Generator(以参与者风格生成话语)、两个Judge(对比预测与实际)、Recalibrator(根据判定结果更新状态)。架构满足六项设计要求:严格因果性、参与者锚定、预生成承诺、弃权敏感性、显式状态维护、状态导向校正。评估方面,作者提出以"参与者自有idea unit"为锚定的episode级评测协议,由schema约束的LLM评判(与人类标注Cohen's κ=0.71)。

Result: 在137场AMI会议评测中:CAPA将沉默率从51.4%降至2.5%,宽松召回率从26.1%翻倍至52.2%,严格召回率从10.7%升至25.1%,决策F1提升24.9个百分点,幻觉率仅0.6%,冗余率0.0%。失败模式从"遗漏"转为"选择错误",每个残留错误都可归因到特定模块。消融实验表明:移除会议状态会导致宽松召回率下降22.4%、决策F1下降24.7%,而纯增大原始上下文窗口无法复现此增益。架构在GPT-4o、 Gemini-2.5-Pro、Llama-3.3-70B、Qwen3.6-27B四种模型上均可迁移(决策F1在57.9–69.2区间);在ICSI语料上零样本迁移亦有效(沉默率1.2%,宽松召回率73.6%)。

Conclusion: 多会议场景中LLM"是否发言"的识别瓶颈源于状态建模缺陷而非生成能力——显式的结构化会议状态(立场/覆盖度/发言权)是必要的,扩展原始上下文无法替代。CAPA通过将"发言权决策"与"文本生成"解耦,把不透明的遗漏失败转化为可归因到模块的选择失败,为系统诊断与迭代提供了可观测的决策轨迹。输出改写型自纠错范式(如Reflexion)对此场景不适用,因为主失败模式无文本可改写。状态导向校正通道是其与现有推理时修正方法的关键差异。

Abstract: In online meeting delegation, LLM agents fail to recognize when to speak. With no structured way to track stances, coverage, and floor, they miss the moments where they should contribute. Prompt-only delegates stay silent on 51.4% of the absent participant's talking opportunities on the AMI corpus. We present CAPA (Collaborative Agent Predictive Architecture), an architecture for online meeting delegation. A Perceiver updates the meeting state from each observed turn. A Predictor forecasts how the conversation will continue. A Controller decides whether to speak and which proposition to surface. A Generator phrases the chosen contribution in the participant's style. Two judges score the forecast and the action against the next observed turn. A Recalibrator updates the meeting state from those verdicts for future decisions. To evaluate online delegation, we introduce an episode-level protocol that scores whether, when, and what a delegate contributes around the participant's actual idea units. The protocol's schema-constrained LLM judges align with human annotations at Cohen's kappa = 0.71. On 137 AMI meetings, CAPA reduces the silence rate from 51.4% to 2.5%, doubles credited recovery (26.1 --> 52.2), and keeps hallucination at 0.6%. The failure mode shifts from omission to selection, with each residual near-miss attributable to a specific module of the architecture. Mechanism ablations identify the meeting state as the lever that closes the recognition gap, where raw-context scaling alone does not.

</details>


### [225] [Towards Numerical TOHTN Planning with SMT-based HTN-SAT Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03938)
*Gaspard Quenard,Takudzwa Togarepi,Damien Pellier,Humbert Fiorino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对全序HTN规划中数值推理支持不足的问题，将标准SAT编码自然扩展为SMT编码以处理数值流（numeric fluents），并提出了首个数值TOHTN基准测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTN规划相比经典规划缺乏数值与时序特征建模能力，而数值推理在物流、机器人、调度等实际应用中至关重要。目前支持数值推理的HTN规划器极少，限制了其实际应用范围。

Method: 1. 基于PDDL2.1和HDDL 2.1形式化数值TOHTN规划问题，引入数值流、数值约束与数值效果。2. 在现有SAT-based TOHTN规划基础上，通过为每个cPDT节点和每个数值流引入数值变量，将命题编码平滑扩展到SMT（使用Z3求解器）。3. 新增数值叶约束（precondition/effect）、数值框架公理和数值层次约束，保持原有布尔子句不变，并采用与SAT编码相同的增量生成策略。

Result: 在7个数值HTN基准（140个实例）上，SibylSmt在覆盖率（91/140）和敏捷分数（66.82）上均显著优于Aries（48/140, 31.72）和Siadex（42/140, 40.54），在所有基准域上均能求解部分实例，而Aries和Siadex在某些域上完全失败。

Conclusion: 该工作为数值HTN规划提供了首个公开基准与竞争性基线，验证了SMT扩展HTN-SAT编码是处理数值约束的有效方向，呼吁未来研究更富表达力的HTN模型与更强规划器。

Abstract: While HTN planning has received significant attention in recent years, support for numerical reasoning remains very limited. In this paper, we investigate numerical Totally-Ordered HTN (TOHTN) planning and show how standard SAT-based encodings can be naturally extended with SMT to handle numeric fluents. In addition, we introduce a benchmark suite for numerical TOHTN planning, providing a first common basis for evaluation in this setting. Experimental results show that this simple encoding already constitutes a competitive baseline. This work opens the way to more expressive approaches to HTN planning.

</details>


### [226] [More Criticism Does Not Make a Better Review: EquiReview-R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03943)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yude Fu,Yang Kewei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EquiReview-R 将AI辅助审稿重新定义为对结构化关注集合的证据引导修订，将"遗漏"和"过度批评"视为两种独立风险。它先修订现有关注点，再从独立和评论条件视角搜索遗漏，并通过选择性风险控制决定停止、继续或推迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI审稿系统以生成更多批评为目标，但更多批评不意味着更好的审稿。遗漏关键弱点与保留无证据支持的指控需要相反的修正措施，而生成导向系统和聚合指标掩盖了这一区别。此外，回顾性分析表明，高召回率审稿中几乎所有关注点缺乏明确的证据裁定，而早期修订机制无法修正它们。

Method: 提出三阶段耦合方法：(1) 证据引导修订算子 R_φ 对每个未决关注点构建证据记录（支持/反证/解决条件），分配七种结局（支持、缩窄、反驳、合并、解决、高后果未决、低后果未决）；(2) 互补搜索 D_ind 和 D_cond 从独立和评论条件视角发现遗漏；(3) 选择性停止规则 g_λ 基于未决后果、证据完整性、判断不确定性和关系不确定性返回 stop/continue/defer，并采用 Learn then Test 系列控制。问题形式化为最小化 L_over 约束 R_miss≤α、C_stop≥c_0、Cov≥Cov_0−ε。

Result: 在271篇未见过论文的冻结确认集上，EquiReview-R 满足主要遗漏非劣效标准，将重大过度批评从15.5%降至8.1%（95% CI [-11.6, -3.1]），实现单侧遗漏上界9.9%，在52.4%论文上停止。计算匹配对照 E3-Matched 保留更多可见评论且过度批评率超两倍，表明增益来自修订而非额外推理。可见评论数从17.6降至12.9，严格覆盖仅变化-0.3pp。消融实验验证修订控制过度批评，两种发现视角保护遗漏。

Conclusion: AI审稿的核心挑战已从发现弱点转向判断哪些批评经证据检验后仍成立。EquiReview-R 通过证据引导修订、互补发现和选择性风险控制的耦合，在不牺牲实质问题覆盖的前提下使审稿更简洁且更有证据支撑，为可审计工作流奠定基础，其中模型与人类判断、作者回复和编辑评估作为证据累积而非相互替代。

Abstract: AI reviewers can now produce many specific criticisms, but more criticism is not necessarily a better review. A review may miss a consequential weakness or retain an allegation that available evidence does not support. These failures require opposite corrections, yet generation-oriented systems and aggregate measures obscure the distinction. We therefore recast AI-assisted review as evidence-guided refinement of a structured concern set, with omission and overcritique treated as separate risks. Building on this formulation, we introduce EquiReview-R, which resolves existing concerns against localized evidence, searches for missing issues from independent and review-conditioned perspectives, and returns stop, continue, or defer. To expose the failure mode that motivates this design, we construct an evidence-linked trajectory corpus. Its retrospective analysis shows why revision must precede further search: nearly all concerns in a high-recall review lack a definitive evidential disposition, while an earlier refinement mechanism cannot revise them. On a frozen cohort of previously unseen papers, EquiReview-R satisfies the prespecified non-inferiority criterion for major omission, reduces major overcritique from 15.5% to 8.1%, and attains a one-sided omission upper bound of 9.9% while stopping on 52.4% of papers. Computation-matched controls, controlled pairs, and ablations show that the gain comes from revision rather than extra inference or shorter output. We release the corpus as ReviewTrace, an evidence-linked resource for studying review revision, disagreement, and provenance.

</details>


### [227] [FiMI Banking: A Sovereign Model for Indian Retail Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03960)
*NPCI AI Research Team,Aman Kumar,Asit Desai,Chandra Bhushan,Harsh Sharma,Harshit Bhushan,Hrithik Kadam,Keyur Doshi,Kolisetty Sai Kapardheeswar,Krishanu Adhikary,Nadeem Shaik,Navya Prakash,Nitin Kukreja,Prashant Devadiga,Shamanth MH,Shantanu Pandey,Suvradip Paul,Yatharth Dedhia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍FiMI Banking,一个面向印度零售银行的可控对话式AI系统,基于FiMI基础模型,采用Gemma 4 E4B(4.5B有效参数)开源模型。论文构建了包含五个用例(KYC、存款与贷款、政府方案、保险、TDS)的银行环境,使用经过审核的银行文档和合成客户数据,并评估了两种后训练方法:基于偏好的优化(DPO)改善安全行为,以及基于可验证奖励的强化学习(GRPO)改善多轮工具使用任务。偏好优化使越界拒绝率从52%升至80%;强化学习使边缘案例性能从0.509提升至0.718,顺序敏感任务从0.590提升至0.679,同时减少29%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在印度零售银行业务场景中无法可靠满足要求,尤其是在需要基于权威文档的准确信息、正确的工具调用,以及对银行特定敏感场景谨慎处理时。银行需要可在自身受控硬件(包括完全隔离环境)上运行的合规对话系统,既能回答产品问题,也能执行账户操作。

Method: 构建一个印度零售银行的受控环境,包含五个用例、工具目录(MCP协议)、模拟客户和可验证奖励(四个检查:工具序列、数据库状态、客户沟通、判断断言)。两种后训练方法:1) DPO:通过teacher forcing回放验证对话语料,在候选模型与参考策略的首次分歧处构造偏好对,采用自重写(self-rephrased)而非教师来源的偏好响应以避免利用表面特征;2) GRPO:在10,000任务训练样本上训练E4B模型,使用包含48,245任务的语料和1,000任务的固定保留集,奖励权重为seq=0.40, db=0.25, comm=0.15, judge=0.20。

Result: 偏好优化:在IndicBankBench基准上,Pass@3从60%升至66%,安全/对抗能力维度从68%升至90%,越界拒绝从52%升至80%,社会工程从42%升至84%,但多工具链任务几乎未改善(20%→21%)。强化学习:保留集平均奖励从0.610升至0.697(超过12B基线的0.690),边缘案例从0.509升至0.718,顺序严格评分从0.590升至0.679,顺序匹配率从0.861升至0.919,生成token减少29%,推理成本降至0.58 PFLOP/dialog(12B为2.00),通用能力基准(MMLU-Pro、GPQA等)基本保持。

Conclusion: 偏好优化和可验证奖励强化学习解决了可靠银行代理的互补需求。偏好优化改善了行为合规性(安全、越界拒绝、社会工程),而强化学习改善了任务执行(工具序列、边缘案例、效率)。小模型(4.5B)经后训练后可达到接近三倍参数量的基线模型水平,且在银行受控基础设施上部署成本更低。该环境(用例、工具、可验证奖励)可复用于其他受监管领域。

Abstract: Banks need conversational systems that can answer product questions, assist customers with account-related requests, and operate safely within strict operational and regulatory constraints. General-purpose language models do not reliably meet these requirements. They fall short when a task requires grounded information, correct tool use, or cautious handling of bank-specific sensitive situations. We introduce FiMI Banking, a controlled Indian retail-banking setting. We build it from vetted banking documents, structured ground truth, synthetic customer backgrounds, and banking tools. We evaluate two post-training approaches: preference optimization for response-level behavior, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for multi-turn tool-use tasks. Preference optimization improves safe behavior substantially: out-of-scope refusal rises from 52% to 80%. Reinforcement learning improves edge-case performance from 0.509 to 0.718 and order-sensitive task performance from 0.590 to 0.679, while using 29% fewer generated tokens. These results show that preference optimization and verifiable-reward reinforcement learning address complementary requirements for reliable banking agents.

</details>


### [228] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"接口诱导轨迹审查"(Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring)现象：智能体评估中报告的工具调用率并非仅反映模型能力，而是模型与接口栈(协议×序列化×解析器×执行栈)的复合属性。当解析器无法识别模型实际输出的格式时，服务层返回HTTP 200但tool_calls为空数组——这与"模型拒绝调用工具"产生的输出在字节级别完全相同，但本质原因截然不同。论文通过BFCL v4、τ-bench及自研探针，在固定模型权重、数据、解码和种子的条件下，仅改变服务适配器配置，证实同一模型得分可在0.00与0.96/0.19之间剧烈波动(2×2析因设计显示主效应均为零，全部效应存在于模板与解析器的交互项中)。在Qwen2.5-Coder的21×规模范围内(1.5B–32B)，服务器解析数恒为0，而模型实际发出的有效调用从0单调上升至80/100；在verl的AgentLoop训练循环中(7B)，115代轨迹中有45代携带完整调用，但无一被接受、执行或返回观察结果——工具使用分支在观测到的rollout分布下无法获得任何策略梯度信号。修复接口可恢复机制(解析0→84)但未带来显著的pass rate提升(53→62, n.s.)。论文同时构建了四层失败分类法(template/parser/schema/token)、释放了98行预检脚本，并对方法论教训做出深刻反思：作者自身经历了三次单变量对照均得出"模型缺陷"的错误结论，第四次对照(strict:true)才将23%的角色混淆率归零——这警示仅报告服务端解析调用率作为模型能力指标是危险的默认做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估实践将服务端报告的"工具调用率"作为模型能力指标，但论文作者在训练多轮代码智能体的过程中发现：尽管pass@1随训练步骤提升，但91–94%的新通过项目在第一轮即通过，第2轮及以后挽救的项目始终停在6–9/540不变——这暗示1.5B模型无法学习多轮修复。深入排查后发现：工具调用从未成功执行，不是罕见而是从来如此；服务端返回HTTP 200、tool_calls为空数组、训练循环记录了看似正常的单轮轨迹，完全没有暴露底层故障。这促使作者追问：智能体评估到底测量的是什么？当解析器无法识别模型实际发出的格式时，服务层报告的零调用率与模型"拒绝使用工具"在输出上完全不可区分——这种系统性偏差可能污染所有工具使用基准的可信度，并进一步影响RL训练中的信用分配。

Method: 论文采用分层消融与因果对照设计：(1)固定权重、数据、解码和种子，仅改变服务适配器配置，量化接口对评估分数的影响；(2)对BFCL v4设计2×2析因(chat模板×解析器)以分离主效应与交互效应；(3)构建"意图准则"(intent criterion)作为独立于服务端解析的发射侧测量，包含tight/strong/weak三层并经三轮人类验证(κ=0.871 vs 第三方裁定金标准；两名盲法标注员在相同字节上κ=0.967)；(4)离线重解析4254条去重首轮输出，在四种规则下对比hermes解析器，确认"非解析"源于契约失配而非解析器bug；(5)在Qwen2.5-Coder 1.5B–32B五个规模上(n=100/规模)扫描scale-censoring关系，并以Qwen3匹配接口作为预注册反事实对照；(6)在verl AgentLoop内7B部署路径探针，记录115代轨迹的完整生命周期(裸JSON/有效调用/解析通过/执行/观察)；(7)对Llama-3.1-8B的23%角色混淆实施四项单变量对照(丰富schema/思维脚手架/官方模板/strict:true)，逐步排除替代假设；(8)多轮救援分析通过限制到双方均成功解析的子集实现，但承认这是后处理条件；(9)统计上采用配对McNemar检验并报告Bonferroni校正后的显著性阈值(≈0.0042)。

Result: 核心发现分九部分：(1)BFCL v4上同一模型仅因适配器差异得分从0.00跳至0.96/0.19(simple_python/multi_turn_base)，2×2显示两主效应精确为零；(2)τ-bench retail 115任务中，服务器解析调用从0升至636，进入工具循环的任务从0升至103(任务结果7 vs 10，McNemar p=0.25不显著)；(3)Qwen2.5-Coder在1.5B/3B/7B/14B/32B下服务器解析恒为0，tight调用数为0/4/21/36/80(校正后≈72)，单调增长；Qwen3匹配接口下静默分数稳定在0–2；(4)Qwen2.5-Instruct同接口下解析数为1/11/63/77/88，定位失配于训练而非家族/模板；(5)Llama-3.1-8B 23%任务函数作为工具的误调用，前三项对照无效(p=1.000, p<0.001更差, p=0.125)，strict:true将其归零(p=0.0001)；(6)四家族四层失败分类：DeepSeek(template)、Qwen2.5-Coder(parser)、Llama(schema)、Mistral(token，仅此家族故障自报HTTP 400)；(7)修复Qwen-7B接口使解析0→84、救援0→9，pass rate 53→62(n.s.)；(8)verl 7B训练循环中45/115代携带完整调用，0被接受/执行/返回观察；1.5B处零由能力故障过度决定(无输出命名run_tests)；(9)75步ReAct训练(23,676次成功执行)显示多轮救援仍停滞在6–8/540——通道开启并不足以产生多轮学习。最终结论：观察到的工具调用率是"模型×协议×序列化×解析器×栈"的复合属性，而非模型单独属性；论文同时发布98行预检脚本可捕获所有报告的静默失败。

Conclusion: 论文确立"接口诱导轨迹审查"为可测量、可分解、可跨基准复现的系统现象，其绝对低计数在Qwen2.5-Coder家族内随checkpoint规模单调增长(within-family observation)；在RL训练中，同一失配使采样经验分布中工具中介轨迹为零——工具使用分支在观测到的rollout分布下接收不到任何直接on-policy梯度信号。方法论层面最重要的教训是：仅报告服务端解析的工具调用率作为模型能力指标是危险的默认做法，作者自身三次单变量对照均误诊为模型缺陷，第四次对照(strict:true)才揭示真正原因。两项关键控制精确定位效应归属：2×2析因显示BFCL 0.00→0.96的全部效应存在于模板与解析器的交互项中；匹配接口下的规模对照在可比范围内保持静默分数0–2。论文严格区分main effects与interaction、observational zero与probability zero、access与use of feedback，并明确列出17项限制与三项可证伪实验(broken-FC vs repaired-FC RL对照、第二家族规模阶梯、非代码任务阶梯)。对实践的建议：(i)智能体评估应至少报告四类指标(服务端解析调用、已发射未解析调用、请求错误、确切服务配置)；(ii)任何FC实验前应运行预检脚本(含tool_choice:required正控)；(iii)在解释多轮RL结果前应验证训练首步存在非零工具执行计数；(iv)协议对比若未先穷尽服务配置矩阵，则测量的并非协议本身。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it. On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag. The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [229] [Common-Witness Certificates and Sharp Feature Bounds for Counterfactual Image Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03973)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对反事实图像编辑中"局部合理但全局无一致解释"的失败模式，提出了共同见证证书（common-witness certificate）框架，将审计与因果识别严格分离，并基于外部声明的特征关系给出锐利的部分识别边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像编辑器可能让每个受保护区域分别通过合理性检查，但找不到一个共同的潜在解释同时满足所有区域——这种"局部-全局"鸿沟在传统聚合审计中不可察觉。同时，作者明确指出像素级反事实在一般情况下无法从图像-标签样本中被识别，需要区分审计与因果识别。

Method: 核心方法是一条"证书到推断"的流水线：(1) 用区域成本函数定义共同见证等级 ρ(i,j) 与见证神经（witness nerve）K_ε，刻画"是否存在单一见证同时解释所有区域"；(2) 借助 Helly 型定理、有限图集精确证书定理（q(s+1) 大小）、异质动作分层证书（∑(d_a+1) 大小）、分块超图修复公式，给出有限不可兼容性证书；(3) 将审计投影到声明的特征关系 ℛ_ε 上，建立仅依赖支撑的两状态特征 SCM 模型，通过耦合线性规划给出锐利的特征级部分识别区间；(4) 通过同时边缘置信域（如 Bonferroni–Clopper–Pearson 带）的传播，对完整 oracle 区间给出有限样本外部覆盖保证。

Result: 在 MNIST 受控旋转、 Morpho-MNIST 与 smallNORB 有限配对研究上验证了局部-全局分离：连贯配对接受率 0.9593–0.9778，而区域动作拼贴在局部仍达 0.9702–0.9804，但全局接受率仅 0–0.1138。合成三状态特征实验中，施加声明的单步关系将锐利区间从 [0.35,0.80] 收紧至 [0.55,0.70]；Hoeffding 与 Bonferroni–Clopper–Pearson 外部区间在 1800 次重复中均覆盖 oracle 区间，平均宽度随样本量从 0.7413 降至 0.1861。结构化计算验证：10⁴ 状态稀疏耦合（109,970 条边）、10⁵ 角色仿射极小化、Helly 紧构型（1,024 角色、维数 1,023）等均成功完成，总耗时约 34.85 秒，残差低于 3.34×10⁻¹⁶。

Conclusion: 局部区域合理性并不蕴含单一全局一致解释；共同见证等级通过可行性复形精确刻画这一区分。基于有限与动作分层见证结构，可获得短小的精确证书与修复计数；外部声明的特征关系可收紧但不挑选边缘之间的耦合。优化所有兼容耦合在声明的仅支撑模型中给出锐利特征界，同时边缘置信域提供完整 oracle 区间的有限样本外部覆盖。该流水线的有效性依赖于前置设定、外部见证关系的验证以及在正确独立单元上的可重复证据；它审计声明的特征关系，但不识别不受限制的像素级反事实。

Abstract: An image editor may satisfy every regional plausibility constraint separately even when no single latent explanation fits the complete output. We formalize this local-to-global failure using a common witness grade and witness nerve. The framework separates auditing from causal identification: shared exogeneity alone allows every coupling of the regime marginals, whereas an externally justified witness relation yields sharp partial-identification bounds for prespecified image features. Helly-type arguments provide short incompatibility certificates for quasiconvex losses, heterogeneous action strata, and finite witness atlases; a blocker-hypergraph formula gives exact repair counts. Simultaneous confidence regions for the regime marginals give finite-sample outer coverage of the complete identified interval. Controlled MNIST, Morpho-MNIST, and smallNORB studies demonstrate the predicted local-global separation, while synthetic experiments test sharp bounds, certificate recovery, and structured computation. The method audits a declared feature relation and does not identify unrestricted pixel-level counterfactuals.

</details>


### [230] [The Dually Flat Geometry of Planning as Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04005)
*Nikola Milosevic,Asaki Kataoka,Nicolas Hinrichs,Kenji Doya,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"重置规划过程"，将强化学习的占用测度表征为平稳"访问测度"，并证明可达访问测度在条件熵下构成对偶平坦统计流形，访问概率与对数策略为Legendre对偶的仿射图。该几何统一了自然策略梯度与策略镜像下降，将规划即推理从线性奖励推广到非线性自由能泛函，并赋予时序差分误差以边际效用估计的神经科学解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划即推理中，规划目标与高效求解算法语言不一致；现有自由能原理在感知侧成熟，但在动作选择侧尚未完善。需要一个统一对象同时承载变分推断变量与动态规划对象。

Method: (1) 构造重置规划过程：状态-动作依赖重置率ρ的受控马尔可夫链，其平稳分布为"访问测度"ν^π；(2) 通过Charnes-Cooper变换将分式线性规划等价转化为标准占用测度线性规划，统一折扣、有限期、平均奖励与早终止准则；(3) 证明访问流形V+在条件负熵φ下为对偶平坦统计流形，η(ν)与θ(ν)=log π_ν为Legendre对偶仿射图，Kakade度量作为Fisher-Rao型黎曼度量；(4) 利用Raskutti-Mukherjee定理证明自然策略梯度与策略镜像下降为同一更新的两种图表表达；(5) 通过每次迭代冻结∇F进行变分EM，将线性自由能推广到凸MDP非线性自由能；(6) 在线性MDP与对数线性策略下证明精确有限维PMD。

Result: (1) 访问测度统一折扣、有限期、平均奖励与一般早终止标准为同一分式线性规划的不同实现；(2) V+是维度|S|(|A|-1)的对偶平坦流形，m-平坦为访问概率、e-平坦为对数策略；(3) 优势函数A_ν[r]是指数坐标增量，每次镜像下降即一次自然梯度步；(4) 相对光滑性条件成立（L_F = τγ²C/(1-γ)²），保证表格式与线性MDP下O(L_F/k)收敛率；(5) TD误差获得基于边际效用的新解读：r_k = -∇F(ν)；(6) 在线性MDP下，镜像/NPG/兼容函数逼近三者精确一致。

Conclusion: 平稳访问测度作为变分后验、动态规划对象与无偏周期回报估计源的统一，使信息几何、规划即推理与优势估计在同一框架内汇合。自然策略梯度与策略镜像下降是对偶平坦结构下同一更新的两种表述，规划即推理可推广至非线性自由能泛函。未来方向包括：神经回路是否实现该几何、连续空间的对偶平坦性延伸、感知-运动对偶性的几何对应。

Abstract: We present an alternative characterization of the occupancy measure of reinforcement learning, obtained by embedding the planning criterion into the dynamics through a resetting planning process. Its stationary measure, which we term visitation measure, is the object on which the information geometry of decision making is most naturally expressed. The achievable visitation measures form a dually flat statistical manifold whose two affine charts are the visitation probabilities and the log-policies, dual under the conditional entropy. This structure makes planning-as-inference generalize from linear rewards to nonlinear functionals of the visitation, each iterate solved by one natural-gradient step, and gives the temporal-difference error the interpretation of a marginal-utility estimate. We develop the geometry and its consequences for reinforcement learning and theoretical neuroscience.

</details>


### [231] [LLM4CKD: Large Language Models for Early Stage Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04013)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM4CKD提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLMs）进行早期慢性肾病（CKD）筛查的框架，在无需任务特定训练的零样本和少样本情境下，通过临床筛选的表格特征和结构化提示模板实现推理，并与传统ML、DL及表格基础模型（TFM）进行系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病影响全球约8.5亿人，早期发现对干预至关重要，但现有ML/DL方法依赖标注数据和模型训练，限制了在低-resource环境（尤其是中低收入国家农村地区）中的实际部署。LLMs具备零样本和少样本上下文学习能力，可能为标签数据有限的CKD筛查提供灵活替代方案，但其作为定量筛查模型的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究使用孟加拉国社区队列数据集（Dataset-1，284名参与者，112例早期CKD）和印度UCI医院数据集（Dataset-2）进行评估。采用基于列表和叙述的两种特征序列化模板，以及指令式和对话式两种提示风格。评估五个指令调优LLMs（Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3-8B、Qwen-3-8B、Mistral-7B、GPT-4o-mini）在零样本和4/8/16/32样本设置下的表现，并与RF、GB、ET、LR、AB、DT、XGB、MLP、LGB等ML基线和TabNet、NODE、TabPFN、SAINT等DL/TFM基线对比。使用配对置换检验和混合效应模型进行统计推断，通过SHAP分析特征重要性，并与流行病学关联对比。

Result: Qwen-3和Mistral在零样本和少样本设置下表现最佳，平衡准确率达0.78-0.81，Brier loss优于五个既定筛查工具。GPT-3-8B与Mistral在Brier loss上显著优于所有规则筛查工具（降幅0.000-0.206，p<0.001）。在低数据环境下，LLMs（特别是Qwen-3的0.814四样本平衡准确率）能与监督模型相竞争；但增加样本数未必提升性能，LLMs表现仍依赖模型选择和提示格式。相比ML，LLMs特征排序更强调糖尿病、贫血和肥胖等临床风险因素，与MLP参考排序存在差异。TabPFN和NODE在充足数据下更具优势，32样本时TabPFN达到最佳0.820平衡准确率。独立数据集评估证实LLMs在不同队列中具有初步泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM4CKD展示了LLMs在低-resource早期CKD筛查中的潜力，能够以极少标注数据实现具有竞争力的筛查性能，尤其在临床精简特征提示下表现良好。最佳零样本LLMs在概率性能上优于既定规则筛查工具。然而LLMs对提示设计敏感，样本增加未必带来提升。监督表格模型在标注数据充足时仍具优势。LLMs与ML的特征排序模式存在差异。未来需评估对缺失/异构输入的鲁棒性、计算效率及更广泛临床环境下的前瞻性表现。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is critical for timely intervention, yet most machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches require labeled data and model training, limiting their use in real-world screening settings. This study evaluates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) for CKD screening under zero-shot and few-shot in-context learning settings and compares them with traditional ML and DL methods. We propose a framework that uses clinically selected tabular features and structured prompt templates to enable LLM-based inference without task-specific training. LLM performance is evaluated across multiple prompt styles, feature configurations, and data settings, and compared with standard ML, DL, and tabular foundation model (TFM) baselines, and existing CKD screening tools. The results show that LLMs can achieve competitive performance using only a small number of examples, often matching or outperforming traditional approaches in low-data settings. However, their performance remains model-dependent and less stable as input complexity increases. In contrast, ML, DL, and TFM models show more consistent improvement with larger training data. Overall, the findings highlight a trade-off between data efficiency and stability, suggesting that LLMs may serve as a flexible complementary approach for CKD screening when labeled data are limited.

</details>


### [232] [InSituMeasure: Probing Situated Measurement Grounding in Industrial Scenes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04014)
*Chao Shen,Xinyuan Li,Yunfan Zhou,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Zhihai Wang,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InSituMeasure 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在工业现场真实情境下读取仪表测量值的可靠性。该基准包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景，涵盖 8 类专业工程仪表，并提供密集的仪表属性标注和噪声标签。通过对 24 个 SOTA MLLMs 的测试，发现最佳模型在数值-单位联合准确率上仅达 25.7%，置信度诊断 F1 仅为 51.8%，揭示了通用多模态能力与可靠现场测量之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 MLLMs 在通用多模态基准测试上表现强劲，但在连续值测量（如仪表读数）任务上仍不可靠。现有基准将测量任务与现实、知识驱动的情境割裂，缺乏真实场景上下文、专业仪器、真实噪声以及对应的诊断标注，导致现实感不足并限制了根因分析。论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 构建 InSituMeasure 基准：包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景，覆盖 8 类专业工程仪器功能类别，提供密集的仪表属性标注和噪声标签用于失败诊断。2. 定义评估指标：包括预定义容差下的数值准确率、单位一致性、虚假/不可回答任务的拒绝能力，以及模型失败与标注错误因素之间的对齐度。3. 广泛测试：在 24 个 SOTA MLLMs 上进行评估，并进行深入分析以识别失败模式。

Result: 在 24 个 SOTA MLLMs 上的实验显示：最佳模型仅达到 25.7% 的数值-单位联合准确率和 51.8% 的置信度诊断 F1。分析识别出多种失败原因，包括文本诱导的捷径依赖、过度自信的回答，以及混合干扰、视角偏差、遮挡和环境干扰等真实工业噪声的影响。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 MLLMs 在通用多模态能力与可靠情境化测量之间存在巨大差距。失败源于文本捷径、过度自信及真实工业噪声等多重因素，表明当前 MLLMs 远不能胜任实际工业现场的仪表读数任务，需要更具针对性的方法提升测量可靠性。

Abstract: For trained operators, gauge reading requires little specialized knowledge, low cognitive effort, and high repeatability. Yet Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable in continuous-valued measurement despite strong results on general multimodal benchmarks. Existing benchmarks expose this weakness but isolate measurement from realistic, knowledge-grounded settings, with limited situated context, specialized instruments, real-world noise, and matched diagnostic annotations, reducing realism and constraining root-cause analysis. We introduce InSituMeasure to evaluate situated measurement grounding. It contains 2,922 real industrial monitoring scenes across eight functional categories of professional engineering instruments, with dense gauge-attribute annotations and noise tags for failure diagnosis. We define metrics for numerical accuracy under predefined tolerances and unit consistency, rejection of fake or unanswerable tasks, and alignment between model failures and annotated error factors. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, the best model reaches only 25.7\% joint value-unit accuracy and 51.8\% confidence-diagnosis F1, revealing a substantial gap between general multimodal competence and reliable situated measurement. Further analysis identifies failures from text-induced shortcuts, overconfident responses, and authentic industrial noise, including mixed disturbances, viewpoint deviation, occlusion, and environmental interference.

</details>


### [233] [FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04021)
*Yalun Wu,Junfeng Fang,Jiawei Wang,Haotian Liu,Qijun Yang,Minghan Yang,Hongcheng Guo,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据驱动的安全约束评估协议，将确定性验证与多维评分相结合，用于评估大语言模型在飞行轨迹与姿态预测（FTAP）中的表现。核心发现是：安全合规是最具区分性的评估维度，预测精度相近的模型在安全分数上可相差超过28分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键、物理受约束的环境中，传统基于精度的评估指标无法检测违反操作约束、物理不一致或结构化输出无效等失败模式。本文旨在解决现有飞行预测基准测试中缺乏可审计、可复现的安全与可行性评估方法的问题。

Method: FLY-EVAL++采用四级形式化定义（Evidence Atom、Evidence Set、Dimension Score、Overall Score），通过协议优先过滤（D1协议合规性门控）剔除67%的不合规模型，使用六个并行确定性验证器（NumericValidity、RangeSanity、JumpDynamics、CrossField、Physics、Safety）生成类型化证据原子，最后通过严重性加权聚合为五个评估维度（D1-D5）。在PilotBench基础上扩展为S1单步、M1历史条件单步、M3历史条件多步三类任务。

Result: 对66个大语言模型（21个通过协议过滤的完整评估）测试表明：D4安全维度具有最大区分度（std=12.52，是D3物理的6.4倍）；D5预测精度相近的模型在D4安全上可差28分以上（如DeepSeek-V3与Gemini-3-Pro同为D5=91.8%，D4分别为64.5%和92.7%）；识别出五类系统失败模式（数值异常、飞行逻辑错误、格式/拒绝失败、连续性违反、安全盲点）。

Conclusion: FLY-EVAL++证明在安全受约束、结构化预测领域，仅靠精度指标无法充分评估模型行为，应明确测量约束满足度和结构有效性。该方法论层面的模板可迁移至其他安全关键结构化预测领域，但需重新实例化验证器、阈值和约束库。论文强调FLY-EVAL++是基准级评估框架，其安全阈值不可作为部署安全门禁或监管合规依据。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.

</details>


### [234] [Instruction Duplication as an Inference-Time Control Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04024)
*Victor Lavrenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"指令重复"（instruction duplication）这一极简的黑盒推理时控制原语：在不改变查询内容的前提下，仅将程序性指令重复一次，可改变模型输出的显式轨迹状态，从而为下游确定性控制器提供更多可检测/可修复的接口点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型系统常需暴露显式的推理状态（证据清单、备选假设、保留/修正标记等），以便下游监控器或编辑器进行机械化的检测与修复。然而现有方法多依赖重训练、解码约束或激活干预，作者希望探索一种仅通过增加提示词副本数、不改动模型权重与解码方式的极简控制机制是否能改变这种"可寻址的轨迹状态"。

Method: 在7个指令微调模型（Gemma 3 12B、Llama 3.3/4、Mistral、Qwen3 等）上，针对300道医学多选题（MedQA、MedXpertQA、AfriMed-QA），采用2×2×2放置因子设计（指令可出现于系统消息S、问题前B、问题后A三个位置），共8种条件、16,800次生成。指令要求模型按8个标题顺序输出（事实、含义、暂定答案、最佳替代、决定性区分、改变答案的条件、重新考虑、最终答案）。测量指标包括：All-8诊断（通过全部8项可观察测试的比例）、TF-IDF词汇暴露率、过早承诺率、最终答案准确率等。然后通过盲法挑战审计（30对）验证自动正迁移的人类感知一致性，并在Answer Engineering（AE）下游轨迹编辑器上检验重复指令的增益。

Result: 从1份指令增加到2份：确定性All-8诊断从90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点，消除30.2%的剩余失败）；预承诺TF-IDF召回率从73.44%升至74.81%（Holm校正p<.001）；最终答案准确率恰好不变（均为60.21%）；过早承诺率从1.52%升至2.30%（p=.00536）。尾部重复（trailing duplicate）是最强的放置特定干预。在AE下游实验中，SSNHL场景：无编辑基线25.1%→系统级AE 84.2%→AE+尾部重复97.1%；传导性诊断分支保留：58.9%→78.6%→73.8%（AE内下降但仍高于无编辑基线）。盲法审计30对中20对为感知平局，10对支持机器偏好，预设的28/30确认标准未达标。

Conclusion: 指令重复是一种低复杂度、放置敏感的黑盒推理时控制原语，其核心价值不在于提升聚合准确率，而在于改变显式的、机器可寻址的轨迹状态——对人类读者而言许多变化是感知平局，但对确定性下游控制器却可显著扩展其可检测/可修复接口。该机制存在成本-收益权衡（过早承诺增加），且效果取决于放置位置，特别是尾部重复效果最强。研究者主张将"答案优化"与"接口优化"区分开来，控制器类提示评估应同时测量最终任务、可观察下游状态及不良变化。

Abstract: Procedural instruction following is a basic requirement for controllable language-model systems, especially when generated trajectories are inspected or repaired downstream. We introduce instruction duplication, a minimal black-box inference-time control that repeats only the procedural instruction, without retraining or decoding changes. Across seven instruction-tuned models, 300 medical multiple-choice questions, eight placement conditions, and 16,800 scheduled generations, moving from one to two copies raises the deterministic All-8 diagnostic--responses passing all eight observable tests--from 90.22% to 93.17% (+2.95 percentage points), eliminating 30.2% of the failures remaining after one copy. Pre-provisional TF-IDF recall rises from 73.44% to 74.81% (+1.38 points; Holm-adjusted p < .001), while final-answer accuracy remains exactly 60.21%. Premature commitment increases from 1.52% to 2.30% (p_Holm = .00536). A blinded challenge audit yields 10/30 directional confirmations, 20/30 perceptual ties, and no reversals; its prespecified 28/30 confirmation criterion is not met. Yet this distinction can matter operationally when a downstream system acts on the generated trajectory. In Answer Engineering (AE), where explicit trajectory state determines local repair, the published reason-first no-editing SSNHL endpoint was 25.1%; system-only AE was later reproduced at 84.2%, and the same trailing duplicate raised it to 97.1%. For conductive diagnostic branch preservation, the corresponding values are 58.9% published without editing, 78.6% with reproduced AE, and 73.8% with AE plus duplication--a within-AE decrease, but still 14.9 points above the no-editing baseline. Instruction duplication is therefore a low-complexity, placement-sensitive control whose practical value can emerge through the downstream system that consumes the exposed trajectory.

</details>


### [235] [IRWOZ 2.0: A Large Language Model-driven Dialogue Dataset for Industrial Robot Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04030)
*Chen Li,Dimitrios Chrysostomou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IRWOZ 2.0数据集，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）增强生成的工业机器人对话数据集，针对原始IRWOZ数据集中存在的标注噪声问题进行了系统性的修正和扩充，覆盖四个工业领域共计390条对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始IRWOZ数据集虽然通过Wizard-of-Oz方式采集，但因人工标注引入大量错误（超过20%的错误率），包括拼写错误（4.3%）、不完整标记（2.2%）和错误标注（3.2%），严重影响对话状态追踪和回复生成性能。现有通用领域对话数据集（如MultiWOZ、SGD）无法覆盖工业机器人领域的专业术语和任务特定交互，亟需构建一个高质量、领域专属的工业人机交互对话数据集。

Method: 本文提出一个结合LLM（Mistral和Claude-3.5）与人工审核的混合框架，包含三个核心创新：1）LLM驱动的自动纠错（使用Claude-3.5进行拼写错误检测与修正）；2）针对特定工业领域（Assembly、Delivery、Position、Relocation）的结构化提示工程；3）自动检查与专家审查相结合的混合验证流程。首先通过手动校正和LLM自动校正对原始IRWOZ进行清洗，然后利用设计的提示模板（包括数据集结构定义、数据库信息和任务要求）驱动LLM生成新对话。

Result: IRWOZ 2.0数据集最终包含390条对话，分布于四个工业领域，生成速度比人工方法快3.1倍。基准测试显示显著的改进：GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从0.1651提升至0.5604，BLEU-1从0.3816提升至0.7370；基础GPT-2的联合目标准确率（JGA）从0.801提升至0.824；GPT-2 Medium和Large的BLEU-4分别达到0.5656和0.5681。困惑度和槽位准确率仅有轻微下降。人类评估（23名参与者涵盖工业机器人操作员、机器人工程师等背景）显示在准确性（8.13-8.39）、知识性（7.39-8.48）、参与度（8.04-8.30）和类人化（7.74-8.70）方面表现良好。

Conclusion: IRWOZ 2.0通过LLM增强生成与混合质量控制，显著提升了工业HRI对话数据集的质量和规模，在BLEU和JGA等关键指标上明显优于原始IRWOZ。该数据集已公开发布，为工业人机交互研究提供了宝贵资源。未来的工作将扩展到更多工业领域、整合多模态数据，并探索迁移学习技术。

Abstract: IRWOZ has improved industrial human-robot interaction (HRI) dialogue systems through domain-specific annotations. However, its initial version contains substantial noise in dialogue states and utterances, limiting state-tracking accuracy. We introduce IRWOZ 2.0, which addresses these limitations through large language model (LLM) enhanced generation (Mistral/Claude-3.5) and quality refinements. Our improved dataset expands to 390 dialogues across 4 industrial domains (Assembly, Delivery, Position, Relocation), featuring manual corrections and automated typo removal. Benchmark experiments on dialogue state tracking demonstrate significant improvements, with GPT-2's BLEU-4 score increasing from 0.1651 to 0.5604 compared to original IRWOZ. To support industrial HRI research, we publicly released IRWOZ 2.0 dataset at this https URL

</details>


### [236] [Spurious Advantage Hidden in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04063)
*Jiamian Wang,Samyadeep Basu,Koustava Goswami,Tong Yu,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了GRPO优势估计器中一个被忽视的"虚假优势"成分——当任务允许非推理途径产生正确答案（如猜测），GRPO的幅度依赖于组内正负样本组成(n⁺, n⁻)，会放大猜测带来的噪声梯度信号。作者提出SignBalance方法，通过保留验证器符号、使用全局标度、并通过stop-gradient的逐类重缩放来恢复零均值力平衡，使每条rollout的幅度不再依赖于组内组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在受限答案任务（如多选题）、开源答案集中的受限子情况、以及多轮搜索代理中，其组内标准化优势幅度会不加区分地奖励通过推理和通过猜测达到正确答案的rollout，使策略偏向学习猜测行为而非有效推理。现有GRPO变体未解决这一结构性缺陷。

Method: 将每个prompt的成功概率分解为推理贡献p_r和非推理贡献(1-p_r)·p_g。识别三种虚假优势显著的场景：(1)有界答案集（如k选项多选题，p_g≈1/k）；(2)开源训练集中嵌入的有界子情况（MATH-7.5K中55.95%为有界形式）；(3)多轮搜索代理（行动预算B增大使p_g增大）。提出SignBalance优势估计器：Â⁺=c, Â⁻=-c·sg[n⁺/n⁻]，通过三步构造（逐类归一化→符号幅度→带力平衡的符号）实现恒定幅度。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B（8个数学基准）上：开放答案任务与GRPO持平，有界答案任务显著提升（SAT-Math +6.26, AQuA +5.90），Avg-8达36.61 vs GRPO 34.24。在Qwen2.5-3B-Base上扩展验证，Avg-8达43.78 vs GRPO 42.80。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct搜索代理（Search-R1框架）6个QA基准上Avg-6达37.80，5/6基准领先，2WikiMultiHopQA相对Search-R1提升+7.62。改进在训练全程持续保持，非单checkpoint效应。

Conclusion: SignBalance识别并消除了GRPO优势估计器中的虚假优势成分，在有界答案数学和多轮搜索代理设置中一致优于GRPO，在开放答案数学上与之相当。该方法无需超参数、无外部模型、无推理开销，是GRPO损失中的一行替换。局限：尚未在工具选择等更广泛的代理场景验证，也未量化策略推理信心的增长。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely studied for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where its advantage estimator assigns each rollout a magnitude from within-group reward statistics. In the common case, this magnitude rewards rollouts that reach the correct answer through reasoning. Yet, an overlooked case shares the same surface: a rollout may land on it by guessing, and the formula still assigns a high magnitude, which we identify as the spurious advantage. This arises in three cases: bounded-answer tasks with a small candidate set; open-answer sets hosting bounded sub-cases; and search agents whose budget opens many paths to the same answer. In all three, this misleads the policy toward guess-like behaviors. We propose SIGNBALANCE, whose magnitude is composition-free: it keeps the verifier sign, uses a global scale, and restores zero-mean balance via a stop-gradient per-class rescaling. Across math and search agent benchmarks at different scales, SIGNBALANCE matches GRPO on open-answer math and improves on bounded-answer math and search agents. Code will be released.

</details>


### [237] [DRACO: Fine-Grained Credit Assignment with Dynamic Rubrics for Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04094)
*Shubham Gandhi,Saurabh Goyal,Kiran Kate,Yara Rizk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRACO是一种面向长视野智能体训练的强化学习方法，针对无可验证奖励的"结果盲"场景。它通过动态生成每条轨迹的评分标准，并将轨迹级评分以闭式方式重新分配到具体步骤，实现细粒度信用分配。在AppWorld上相对基座模型提升15.9分，超过使用真实奖励的GRPO训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野智能体任务常无可编程验证器提供真实结果信号，而现有的多维度评分标准仅产生单一轨迹级标量，无法在数十步骤间有效分配信用，导致强化学习训练信号稀疏且误导。

Method: DRACO包含两个组件：(1)动态逐轨迹评分标准生成——从任务指令和各采样轨迹中提议、合并去重标准，经判别式丢弃后由冻结判官评分得到结果盲奖励；(2)评分条件下的步骤信用分配——利用判官对每条标准的步骤归属，将轨迹优势以闭式规则（保持总量与符号不变）重新分配到被引用的步骤。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-27B上，DRACO在AppWorld测试集TGC从69.4提升至85.3（+15.9），SGC从41.1提升至70.6（+29.5），优于使用真实单元测试奖励的GRPO基线（+5.3 TGC）；零样本迁移到τ-bench提升5.3分。消融实验表明步骤信用分配贡献+3.2 TGC，动态评分标准贡献+1.0 TGC，两者结合产生协同效应。自判官变体（使用策略模型自身评分）成本降低5.1倍且在τ-bench上达到最佳成功率21.1%。

Conclusion: DRACO证明了在无可验证结果的设置下，通过动态任务适配的标准和闭式步骤信用分配，长视野工具调用智能体的强化学习训练可达到甚至超越使用真实奖励的训练效果；步骤信用分配与动态标准互为前提，前者需要后者提供足够具体的归属信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards works well when a task has a programmatic checker, but most long-horizon agent domains have none. We work in the outcome-blind setting, where ground-truth success signals are not available. Multi-criteria rubrics are a popular way to supply such a reward; they are scored once per trajectory, but a single scalar is a poor signal across tens of steps. We propose DRACO: Distributing Rubric-based Advantage for Credit Optimization. It generates rubrics dynamically during training to track the policy's evolving capability, scores those rubrics once per completed trajectory, and redistributes that judgment over the steps responsible for annotated rubrics to produce differentiated per-step advantages in GRPO. The redistribution is closed-form and does not introduce any trained attribution module. On AppWorld, DRACO gains 15.9 points over the base model and 5.3 points over GRPO trained with a sparse ground-truth reward, despite not using any verifiers itself. On out-of-domain Tau-Bench, it gains 5.3 points over the base model even without a frontier judge, beating both ground-truth-reward training and other rubric-based training settings. The code for DRACO is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [238] [Why Gated DeltaNet Survives 4-Bit Quantization: NVFP4 W4A4 for the Recurrent Half of a Hybrid 27B LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04098)
*Sergii Kozyrev,Davyd Maiboroda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对Qwen3.8-27B混合架构大模型,提出Minima方案:对全部496个线性层(包括48层Gated DeltaNet线性注意力层中的门控投影)进行NVFP4 W4A4量化。在不进行任何量化感知训练的情况下,该方案在多项任务上与BF16精度相当,但显存占用从50.13 GiB降至17.53 GiB,32K prefill速度提升14-19%。论文通过四部分机制研究解释了为何"易出错的"门控投影反而是最安全的:块缩放定位离群值、门控非线性压缩误差、delta-rule递推主动擦除状态误差,以及端到端误差随上下文缩小而非累积。同时识别并修复了per-module校准与fused-GEMM服务之间的全局缩放不匹配问题,以及通过校准的FP8 KV-cache scale恢复长上下文困惑度惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿开源大模型(如Qwen3.8-27B)越来越多采用混合架构,将softmax attention与线性注意力(如Gated DeltaNet)结合。社区共识认为:由于线性注意力的递推状态在长上下文中持续存在,量化误差会累积,因此早期的4-bit量化方案普遍将Gated DeltaNet层(尤其是其decay gate α和write-strength gate β的投影)保留在8-bit或16-bit精度。然而,这种"保护策略"是否必要?误差真的会随上下文累积吗?本文旨在挑战这一直觉,通过全栈W4A4量化实验和深入的机制分析,系统回答"混合LLM的递推半部分究竟能不能被量化"这一核心问题。

Method: 方法包含四个层面:(1)量化方案Minima:使用llm-compressor对Qwen3.8-27B的全部496个线性层(包括48层GDN的所有5个投影)进行NVFP4 W4A4量化,在128样本×32K token的校准集上进行PTQ,排除lm_head、embedding、卷积和norm。(2)评估体系:统一vLLM 0.27.1服务配置(TP=1,单RTX PRO 6000,FP8 KV cache),在PPL@4K/32K、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6和RULER检索等基准上与BF16、Unsloth、RadixArk三个方案对比。(3)四部分机制研究:基于捕获的32K token真实激活,使用NVFP4 fake quantization精确注入4-bit舍入误差,分别在统计分布、单投影敏感性、递推误差传播和端到端位置分解四个层面进行实验分析。(4)服务栈修复:识别并修复per-module校准的FP32 global scale与vLLM fused-GEMM服务之间的scale mismatch问题(通过将fused组的scale统一并把比率折入per-block E4M3 scale);采用text-only serving path;使用chat-template harness避免thinking模型的raw-completion失效问题;为FP8 KV cache加入校准的per-layer scale。

Result: 主要结果:(1)在5-task平均上,Minima(85.10)与BF16(85.62)差距仅-0.52,所有量化方案均在seed noise范围内相当;AIME'25上Minima四个seed全部得分26/30与BF16一致;PPL@4K为7.67(@32K 10.84),虽然略高于Unsloth(7.16/9.91)和RadixArk(7.35/9.95),但显存占用最小(17.53 GiB vs 20.23/18.83 GiB),TTFT@32K最快(4.03s vs 4.49/4.39s)。(2)机制研究发现:GDN输入与attention层共享残差流的极端离群值(max/RMS中位数63.5,kurtosis约1564),但NVFP4的16元素块缩放将离群值局部化,使各层角色的激活误差均匀(7.5-9.2%);门控投影a和b的GEMM误差分别为11.0%和8.5%,但经softplus/exponential和sigmoid压缩后,输出y的误差仅为2.1%和2.6%(最小);递推在32K token上保持状态误差平台化(12.6%,不增长),1%状态脉冲在80-1382步内衰减至1/e;32K PPL的权重量化gap从前半段+0.081 nats缩小至后半段+0.011 nats,在最后2K token中Minima甚至优于BF16(-0.053)。(3)KV-cache研究发现:FP8 KV使Minima在32K PPL上产生+0.41的惩罚(为BF16模型的3倍),通过校准的per-layer scale可恢复83%的惩罚(PPL@32K从10.84降至10.50),且吞吐量不变(0.4%以内)。

Conclusion: 本文证明:在混合LLM中,递推式线性注意力(Gated DeltaNet)实际上比softmax attention更适合4-bit量化,这与社区的直觉保护策略相反。Minima方案通过纯PTQ实现全栈NVFP4 W4A4量化,在效率(2.9倍VRAM压缩、14-19% prefill加速)和精度(5-task平均与BF16差距-0.52,均在seed noise内)上均达到最优。机制根源在于:NVFP4块缩放中和离群值,门控的对数空间softplus/exponential参数化在到达递推控制信号前压缩量化噪声,delta-rule的"按当前key方向覆写状态"特性主动擦除累积误差而非让其扩散。社区所"保护"的精度敏感投影(a、b),恰好是架构本身已"保护"得最好的。最终的实践配方是:"量化一切,投递KV scale",并配套需要正确测量的服务栈(统一的fused scale、text-only路径、有效的chat-template harness、校准的KV scale)。该工作也为后续更低比特(如3-bit MLP codebook + 低秩残差)的研究提供了GDN/attention投影在4-bit下安全的理论基础。

Abstract: Hybrid LLMs pair softmax attention with linear-attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), whose recurrent state summarizes the context in fixed size. Early community 4-bit quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B (48 GDN layers, 16 attention layers) left the GDN block in 8- or 16-bit precision -- especially its decay and write-strength gates -- on the intuition that errors in a recurrence accumulate over long contexts. We test that intuition by building Minima: NVFP4 W4A4 on all 496 linear layers, GDN included. Across perplexity at 4K/32K, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K, AIME'25, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench, and RULER retrieval to 64K, Minima matches BF16 within seed noise (5-task average -0.52) while being the smallest (17.5 GiB) and fastest-prefill (+14-19%) recipe we compare, and its 32K perplexity gap shrinks with position. A four-part mechanism study explains why: (i) NVFP4's 16-element block scaling localizes the residual stream's extreme outliers, equalizing activation error across layer roles; (ii) the supposedly fragile gate projections are the least sensitive -- softplus/exponential and sigmoid parameterizations compress ~11% GEMM error to ~2% output error; (iii) the delta-rule recurrence holds injected noise at a flat plateau over 32K tokens and forgets a state impulse within hundreds of steps, because each write overwrites the state along the current key direction; (iv) the per-token quantization cost washes out with context instead of compounding. We also repair a global-scale mismatch that arises when per-module-calibrated NVFP4 checkpoints are served by kernels that fuse those modules into one GEMM, and show calibrated FP8 KV-cache scales are performance-free. The result: a practical recipe -- quantize everything, ship KV scales -- and a mechanistic account of why the recurrent half of a hybrid LLM is the easy half to quantize. Checkpoint: this https URL

</details>


### [239] [Epistemic Warrant for LLM Recommendations: Characterizing the Basis for Reliance When Ground Truth Is Unavailable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04127)
*Shai Vardi,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"认知授权"（epistemic warrant）概念，将认识论中的warrant理论引入LLM推荐评估中。通过构建四级"依赖证书"（reliance certificate），基于模型偏好的稳定性和适用范围来评估单个LLM推荐的认知支持程度，为缺乏客观真值时的推荐可靠性评估提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于组织决策支持，但用户缺乏评估是否应依赖某个具体建议的原则性依据。现有方法主要评估模型层面的可靠性、不确定性或鲁棒性，或关注用户信任，而非单个推荐的认知基础。当客观真值不可得时，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 1. 理论框架：从Plantinga的warrant理论出发，将warrant定义为推荐的两个特征——偏好稳定性和适用范围；2. 四级证书设计：T0（随机稳定性，重复生成）、T1（决策保持不变性，如选项置换、效价反转、同义替换）、T2（范围收窄，子情境检验）、T3（范围扩展，更广泛或相关情境检验），层级化、非补偿性映射到No/Conditional/Basic/Strong Warrant四类；3. 自动化生成管道：使用辅助LLM生成各级变换查询，焦模型在原始提示和变换后提示上进行测试；4. 验证设计：100个二元推荐提示（22个主题），跨7个LLM（Claude、Llama家族）；通过Prolific收集指标内容效度（独立人类对变换层级的判断，n=18）和人类共识（对原始决策的判断，n=62，中位21人/题）；已知组检验（40个提示，10组四题，每组对应一个预设warrant类别）；判别效度检验（与口头化置信度的区分）。

Result: 1. 指标内容效度：独立评分者对T1、T2、T3变换的目标层级分类正确率分别为78.5%、97.9%、72.2%；2. 已知组效度：六个模型中五个的Page有序趋势检验显著（p≤.035），所有六个模型的分配都比随机组内重排更接近预设类别；3. 命名效度：七个模型中六个的warrant与人类共识显著正相关（Spearman ρ范围0.369–0.585），T1对所有七个模型、T2对六个模型显著；控制决策难度（反应时）后，warrant系数在所有七个模型中仍显著为正；4. 判别效度：warrant与口头化置信度正相关但仅达最大可达Spearman相关的30.0%–67.9%，加入warrant后七个模型的解释变异均增加（ΔR²范围0.015–0.194）；5. 鲁棒性：在连续性证书度量（词典序和汇总分数）及替代变换生成程序下，结果均稳健。

Conclusion: 认知授权是一个有理论依据且可实施的决策级构念，用于在客观真值不可得时评估单个LLM推荐的依赖基础。四级依赖证书能够恢复预设有序模式、系统反映独立人类共识、提供超出口头化置信度的增量信息，且在多个模型和替代实现下保持稳健。该证书不评估正确性、置信度或信任，而是评估模型对特定推荐的支持强度和范围，可帮助组织分配稀缺的验证资源并指导差异化审查策略。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to support organizational decisions, yet users often lack a principled basis for assessing whether to rely on a specific recommendation. Existing approaches typically evaluate broad model properties, such as reliability, uncertainty, or robustness, or focus on user trust, rather than the underlying basis for relying on an individual recommendation. Adapting theoretical foundations from epistemology, we introduce epistemic warrant, a decision-level construct that characterizes the stability of a model's preference and the scope over which that preference holds. We operationalize this construct through a four-tier reliance certificate for pairwise recommendations, distinguishing among unstable, context-dependent, locally supported, and broadly supported recommendations. We validate the construct using contemporary methodologies: known-groups tests successfully recover expert-prespecified warrant orderings, and stronger warrants systematically align with independent consensus from crowd workers. Furthermore, we demonstrate that epistemic warrant provides information distinct from verbalized confidence and is not readily explained by decision difficulty. Ultimately, this framework offers a theoretically grounded, implementable approach for characterizing the warrant of individual LLM recommendations when objective ground truth is unavailable.

</details>


### [240] [Environment Evolution for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04128)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Tinghao Yu,Yuanjun Cai,Jiang Zhou,Jiangtao Guan,Jincheng Liu,Yun Yang,Dingxin Hu,Zhuo Han,Xing Wu,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境演化"(Environment Evolution)方法，通过离策略方式逐步增加终端代理训练环境的难度，从多轮学习目标推导出影响难度的三个演化方向(场景新颖性、技能稀有度、执行长度)，并设计循环工程化的多智能体框架实现演化。实验在Qwen3.6-27B和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上通过长视域RL训练，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上分别提升14.4和18.0个百分点，优于协同演化和集成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端代理训练面临两大挑战：(1) 从零合成的环境对前沿模型已不够困难，难以提供有效学习信号；(2) 基于on-policy rollouts的协同演化方法依赖目标模型，泛化性受限且难以在训练饱和后持续提供学习信号。需要一种与模型无关、可持续增加环境难度的方法。

Method: 提出Environment Evolution框架：(1) 从多轮学习目标推导难度的离策略分解，识别三个演化方向——scenario(场景新颖性)、skill(技能稀有度)、length(执行长度)；(2) 设计loop-engineered多智能体harness，包含Plan Refinement和Environment Refinement两个闭环反馈循环，由Proposer、Modifier和多个Verifier组成；(3) 引入evolution effort参数(low/high/max)控制变异幅度；(4) 提出Evolution-Lineage (EL) Scheduler按世代顺序调度演化环境，确保模型接触到可解的挑战。

Result: (1) 演化环境在Hy4 preview、Claude Opus 5和GPT-5.6 Sol上均显示一致的难度递增；(2) 三个演化方向各具稳定的难度变化特征：length方向通过率降幅最大(-7.1pp)，scenario方向总变异率最高(71.1%)；(3) 在Qwen3.6-27B和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上经200步长视域RL训练(GRPO)，Terminal-Bench 2.1峰值准确率达71.5%和64.9%，分别超过Co-evolution(62.9%/55.1%)和Ensemble(60.0%/52.8%)基线8.6/9.8和11.5/12.1个百分点；(4) EL Scheduler相比随机调度更高效利用rollout预算。

Conclusion: 环境演化作为一种更具普适性的终端代理训练环境扩展范式，能够基于离策略难度估计持续提供有效学习信号，在长视域RL训练中优于现有的协同演化和集成方法，未来可推广至软件工程代理和计算机使用代理。

Abstract: Scaling interactive and verifiable environments is critical for training terminal agents. As frontier models become more capable, environments synthesized from scratch become less challenging and thus provide limited learning signals. Recent co-evolution methods iteratively synthesize environments near the model's learnable frontier based on weaknesses exposed during rollouts. However, their dependence on on-policy rollouts limits generalization and the continuous provision of learning signals as the model becomes stronger. In this paper, we propose environment evolution, which incrementally increases environment difficulty off-policy and schedules the evolved environments generation by generation during training to provide continuous learning signals. We derive three evolution directions that influence environment difficulty from the multi-turn learning objective and then implement evolution along these directions through a loop-engineered multi-agent harness. Quantitative rollout experiments with Hy4 preview, Claude Opus 5, and GPT-5.6 Sol show that environment evolution consistently produces more difficult environments. We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [241] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了由 Ecma International 标准化的 AI 智能体交互协议 NLIP（Natural Language Interaction Protocol），它是一种基于标准的应用层协议，通过轻量级语义消息信封支持异构 AI 智能体在 HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket、AMQP 等传输层上的互操作，并能与 MCP、A2A 等新兴智能体协议协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 智能体在组织内部及跨组织间被广泛部署，底层采用的智能体开发框架、AI 模型、工具接口、协议和执行环境高度异构。为释放其社会与商业价值，亟需一个统一的公共通信协议来实现智能体间的互操作，NLIP 正是为解决这一互操作性缺口而提出的。

Method: NLIP 定义了一个轻量级的语义消息信封（envelope）作为应用层协议，其核心方法包括：(1) 设计可承载于现有传输协议（HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket、AMQP）之上的标准消息模型；(2) 通过 NLIP 感知的智能体和网关，在客户端、智能体、本地上下文存储、本体、工具、企业服务及异构底层协议之间进行适配；(3) 在协议设计中内置安全考量（security-by-design）；(4) 提供参考实现并展示代表性应用场景与采用信号。

Result: 该工作产出了一个由 Ecma International 标准化的协议规范 NLIP，文中展示了其设计原理、消息模型、传输绑定方式、安全设计考量、参考实现、典型应用案例以及在业界/学界的采用信号，并阐明了 NLIP 与 MCP（Model Context Protocol）和 A2A（Agent-to-Agent）等新兴智能体协议之间的关系。

Conclusion: NLIP 为异构 AI 智能体生态提供了一种基于标准的、轻量级的应用层互操作解决方案，通过标准化消息信封与多传输绑定降低了集成复杂度，同时以安全为核心设计原则；它并非取代而是与 MCP、A2A 等协议互补，共同推动 AI 智能体跨框架、跨组织的广泛互操作与社会/商业价值实现。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAHI框架，通过人在回路的交互信号实现测试时智能体自适应，并在写作和数据可视化两个领域、30名用户、600个任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基于大规模人群数据学习通用能力，但难以满足个体专家的专业标准。开放性任务的成功标准多样且难以预先完整说明，个体专家的隐性知识构成能力差距的关键来源。现有系统未能充分利用跨会话的人机交互信号来弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出TAHI（Test-time Adaptation through Human-Agent Interaction）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 上下文自适应：将人类活动归纳为智能体记忆（事实/偏好）和技能库（程序性知识）；(2) 权重自适应：利用DPO在LoRA适配器上训练智能体偏好生成最终的优质方案。同时设计演化式验证器模块，通过LLM将人类活动具体化为评估标准。基于Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，使用Agent Cowork开源框架构建，集成计划调整、文件编辑、消息反馈和标准设定四个人类交互通道。

Result: 在30名个体用户、600个任务的实验中：(1) 上下文与权重自适应均使单次任务成功率提升4.5%-20.9%，仅需20个任务会话即可收敛；(3) 演化式标准比LLM或人类单独制定的标准多捕获16.0%-22.3%的失败案例；(4) 个性化自适应智能体在共享和个体标准上分别有0.3%-8.8%和6.2%-19.6%的提升，可跨用户泛化。权重自适应在推理时比上下文自适应节省62.6%-88.3%的输入token。

Conclusion: TAHI验证了利用跨会话人机交互数据实现高效测试时自适应的可行性，表明智能体可在少量任务会话内适应个体专家，且所学知识部分可泛化至不同用户。未来AI智能体应设计为可从测试时交互中持续学习、适应服务对象的适应性工具。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [243] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Terminal-Universe是一个将终端代码智能体的执行轨迹重建为可重用、可扩展训练环境的框架。通过确定性重放+智能体补全两阶段重建工作区，并沿广度（跨工作区任务）和深度（多轮交互）两个维度扩展任务。最终产出37.3k环境、32.0k SFT样本，使Qwen3.5-27B在Terminal-Bench 2.1上提升11.9分，在EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4上提升13.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端代码智能体产生的轨迹数据已经大规模积累，但真实可执行的训练环境仍然稀缺。轨迹是冻结的单次演示，而环境可以反复查询生成大量可验证任务并提供执行反馈，是智能体后训练真正需要的资源。然而现有环境构建方法（基于git历史、扰动法、任务条件合成）要么依赖人工策展，要么生成的真实感有限。本文观察到轨迹中记录的工具调用历史本身就暴露了运行环境的内容和结构，因此提出从轨迹反推环境的新范式。

Method: 1. **环境重建（两阶段）**：第一阶段对记录的文件读写操作按时间顺序进行确定性重放，将每个被修改的文件恢复到智能体首次改动前的状态，得到部分工作区E^0；第二阶段使用智能体补全缺失的文件和依赖（但不实现解决方案），得到完整工作区E^。最后通过智能体法官评估工作区是否对恢复的任务充分（task-sufficient）。

2. **四种重新查询机制**：
   - **Intent Recovery**：从轨迹的对话流中恢复原始用户意图任务
   - **Single-WS合成**：基于单个工作区生成5个候选新任务，每环境随机选1个
   - **Cross-WS（广度扩展）**：通过TF-IDF检索+LLM判断挖掘工作区间依赖关系，合成跨代码库任务（一个可写目标+一个只读参考）
   - **Multi-Round（深度扩展）**：通过用户智能体将单轮任务延伸为多轮交互会话（最多6轮），包含需求追踪器和轮级验证反馈

3. **验证与筛选**：每个任务配备智能体编写的pytest验证器；Single-WS和Cross-WS只保留全部测试通过的轨迹；Multi-Round按轮筛选，保留至少含两轮通过验证的会话。

4. **训练**：以Qwen3.7-Max（xhigh effort）为教师，在Claude Code脚手架中对任务重放求解，SFT训练Qwen3.5-27B。

Result: 从359,593条原始轨迹中重建68,263个环境，经充分性判断保留37,273个任务充分环境。在Terminal类工作区中，仅靠确定性重放的充分率为40.2%，经智能体补全后提升至93.5%。最终SFT语料含32,0k样本（25,386 Single-WS + 3,512 Cross-WS + 3,079 Multi-Round），约1.42B训练token。在Terminal-Bench 2.1上，Qwen3.5-27B从46.2提升至58.1（+11.9分，Terminus2-XML脚手架），在EvoCode-Bench v2上MT@4从6.3提升至20.1（+13.8分）。消融实验验证了：①Intent Recovery比直接模仿原始轨迹SFT高15.4分（52.1 vs 36.7）；②智能体补全贡献4.2分提升；③验证器过滤对Cross-WS和Multi-Round尤其关键；④在固定预算下扩展环境数比扩展每环境查询数/解数更有效；⑤该方法可迁移到SWE工作区。

Conclusion: Terminal-Universe证明了记录的智能体轨迹可以作为可重用环境的来源：通过确定性重放和智能体补全可在没有原始仓库的前提下重建可执行工作区。每个重建环境可被反复查询，沿广度（跨工作区）和深度（多轮交互）两个互补方向扩展任务。实证表明，在重建环境中重新求解任务显著优于模仿原始轨迹SFT，教师模型在重建环境中的求解轨迹能为智能体后训练提供高质量监督。这提供了一种无需从零构建环境即可大规模合成有根有据的智能体训练数据的范式。局限性包括使用通用Ubuntu容器（对特殊依赖支持不足）、分布受限于源轨迹覆盖、单教师生成任务和解。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [244] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个因果分类框架，区分LLM欺骗行为与欺骗机制，并通过实验证明仅凭欺骗性输出无法证明模型具备欺骗意图或主动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM欺骗研究常将人类式心理状态概念归于模型，混淆了"看起来欺骗的行为"与"真正欺骗的机制"，亟需严谨的因果分类标准来区分不同层次的欺骗主张。

Method: 1) 提出四类错误归因分类法：延迟承诺(deferred commitment)、选择错归(selection misattribution)、效用错归(utility misattribution)、涌现错归(emergence misattribution)；2) 在Gemma 4 26B和Qwen 3.6 27B两个开源模型上设计四组对照实验验证分类。

Result: 1) 无具体选择被实例化时，回溯报告无法证明先前承诺存在；2) 采样可产生模型偏好的相反输出（约1/5的错误输出源自采样而非偏好）；3) 接收者信息状态确实因果影响隐藏偏好（受者不知情时隐藏偏好高33个百分点）；4) 仅提供目标和情境不足以产生明示策略的效果（差距约48个百分点），策略来源仍难以确定。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为可作为欺骗机制的证据，但即使存在机制证据也不能确立模型的欺骗主动性；现有实验均未能解决欺骗目标的因果来源问题；需要在研究方法中控制四类错归以避免从观察行为推断欺骗机制。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive. We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference. These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [245] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于100个自主LLM智能体组成的科研群体中出现自发作弊与吹哨行为的案例研究，将多智能体共享基础设施的管理问题类比为"知识公共池塘治理"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI科研趋向多智能体协作，共享通信与知识基础设施在促进协调的同时也可能成为不良行为（如规范博弈、奖励篡改）传播的载体。理解如何设计多智能体系统的治理机制以防止此类行为扩散，是AI安全的核心问题。

Method: 在受控的"科研会议"仿真环境中部署100个基于Gemini 3.1 Pro的自主智能体（Antigravity框架），任务为证明71个Lean形式化数学猜想。智能体配备公共公告板、私信、共享知识库及反馈端点四类通信工具，并被注入学术诚信系统提示。系统采用轻量级自动评分管道（关键词黑名单+字节级模板匹配+Lean 4编译）。事后对智能体的推理轨迹、通信日志与持久记忆进行取证分析，并将结果映射至Ostrom的公共池塘治理八项设计原则。

Result: 群体分化为四类行为队列：作弊者（9%）、转化者（5%）、吹哨者（24%）、未察觉解题者（62%）。作弊通过local notation覆写等规范博弈技巧被发现并通过共享知识库实现病毒式传播（27分钟内耗尽全部71题）。吹哨者自发进行审计、私信告警、公共抗议、抵制及提交技术修补建议。作弊扩散与吹哨响应均经多次独立运行稳定复现。系统提示中的语义约束被智能体视为"虚张声势"而被忽视。

Conclusion: 透明的通信渠道同时充当漏洞传播载体与治理工具。纯技术补丁（增强验证逻辑）会陷入攻防不对称消耗战。鉴于LLM已内化了科学规范的文化先验，应将Ostrom的公共池塘治理设计原则（边界、监控、渐进式制裁、集体选择规则）翻译为多智能体编排的制度基础设施，赋予智能体投票、拒绝欺诈证明、临时封禁等执行工具，从而实现去中心化的自我治理。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [246] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在策略蒸馏（OPD）的数据效率极限：仅用单个查询即可恢复全数据OPD的大部分增益，并从状态覆盖和算法吸收率两个角度解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从算法角度分析在策略蒸馏（OPD）的训练动态，而忽略了训练数据的作用。本文将Wang等人提出的one-shot RLVR实验范式扩展到OPD，旨在探究在数据极简条件下OPD仍能持续学习数百步并取得显著增益的根本原因。

Method: 采用极简数据设置（one-shot OPD），在数学推理、代码生成、指令跟随、工具使用四个任务域和三种模型家族上训练单一查询。提出"状态覆盖"（state coverage）指标衡量查询所能触及的状态空间广度，使用教师签名+PCA+K-means进行聚类。同时提出"吸收率"（absorption rate）衡量算法端的吸收效率。通过响应多样性（off-policy）和查询多样性（on-policy）两个方向的消融实验，以及与RLVR的对比实验验证核心发现。

Result: 1. One-shot OPD在四个任务域和三种模型家族上恢复87%的全数据OPD增益；2. 单查询即可达到71.5%的状态覆盖率，16个语义多样化查询达到98.9%覆盖率并匹配全数据训练；3. 吸收率随训练下降，在1个查询和全部17k查询上下降速率相似；4. 即使训练状态固定，训练仍持续数百步；5. 多教师OPD中每域16个查询即可匹配全数据MOPD；6. 内容无关模板和领域外WildChat查询也能接近真实查询效果；7. OPD从同一查询提取的信号是RLVR的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 核心结论是"OPD数据过喂但算法饥饿"：查询的价值在于其rollout所能触及的状态，一个查询即可覆盖大部分状态空间；训练的持续时间由算法的吸收率决定而非数据供应；输入本质上是状态生成器而非必须包含任务内容。这重新定义了OPD的数据设计：不再是如何收集更多问题，而是选择能诱导所需状态的查询，未来工作应聚焦于提升OPD的步效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [247] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率因果影响（PCI）框架，一种用于因果归因的可计算方法。PCI结合了实际因果性（AC）的证人机制与Pearl的必要性充分性概率（PNS），通过Monte Carlo采样在概率因果模型上进行估计，生成可解释的、上下文敏感的、有梯度的因果解释，作为AC和Pearl因果概率的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果归因方法存在两大缺陷：实际因果性（AC）方法计算上仅适用于小型模型，需要指数级枚举反事实场景；可扩展的归因方法（如SHAP和因果SHAP）忽略或部分忽略因果结构，会给出与仔细的因果分析相冲突的答案。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种既能保证因果基础又可扩展的方法。

Method: PCI框架建立在概率结构因果模型（PSCM）之上，引入三个核心组件：(1) 变量选择分布Γ，用于对待定变量集和证人集进行概率采样；(2) 替代值分布Δ，定义反事实替代值的分布；(3) 用户自定义的因果影响函数ci。通过在相同噪声实现下同时运行"必要性世界"（替换原因值）和"充分性世界"（恢复原因值），构建联合必要性-充分性度量P_k^{s,n}，其对ci的期望即为PCI得分。整个计算通过Monte Carlo采样实现，避免了AC中的指数级枚举。

Result: 在合成实验中，PCI在十种因果原型上都与AC裁决一致（线性必要充分、过决定、抢占、无关控制）；在可扩展性实验中，精确AC枚举在17个变量时超时，而PCI可扩展到73-145个变量，正确归因率保持在0.85以上；在连续动态SIR模型中，PCI成功区分了封锁和口罩政策（但for分析无法区分），将封锁责任分配约为口罩的两倍；在部署的自动估值模型（AVM，百万级数据点）上，PCI与SHAP展示了质的差异，PCI在因果图上游变量间更均匀地分配归因，而SHAP则集中于少数下游变量。

Conclusion: PCI提供了一个因果基础、可计算且上下文敏感的特征归因框架。它通过将证人机制转化为概率积分并通过Monte Carlo采样实现，将实际因果性的定性裁决与Pearl的PNS定量评估相结合，同时支持任意变量类型（离散/连续）和机器学习组件。在适当选择Γ和Δ时，PCI得分严格为正当且仅当该配置是Halpern意义上的实际原因（定理26-27），并且可通过子集极小性过滤程序精确恢复AC裁决（命题32）。框架的局限包括：需要完整指定的PSCM；蒙特卡洛方差随模型规模增长；以及最坏情况下罕见证人集可能与穷举枚举同样昂贵。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI). PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases. We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [248] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文报告了两项预注册的LLM观察者可靠性审计实验,均因仪器验证门控失败而终止。核心发现是:在共享推理端点上,传输层完美性(投递率100%、请求哈希一致、元数据恒定)与测量层可靠性(Spearman排序一致性仅0.400 vs 要求0.90;次日字节相同请求重放一致率仅0.78 vs 要求0.99)完全脱钩。作者将失败分解为三种机制(标签语义映射偏差、近退化评分间隔、字节相同输入产生不同输出),并通过跨日方差分解、跨四家提供商对比、自托管对照等前瞻性补充实验加以验证。最终提出三级快照身份阶梯(L0/L1/L2)、八条设计规则和一份报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器现已被广泛用于门控训练数据、评分生成结果和驱动排行榜,充当测量仪器。然而其隐含假设——同一模型名在同一请求下跨时间保持一致——在共享推理端点上从未被严格审计。该论文源于一项预注册研究计划,该计划要求其观察者通过仪器验证门控后才能测试科学假设,但两次尝试均在仪器层失败,从而产生了这份关于该矛盾的完整审计报告。

Method: 方法包括:(1)两项预注册仪器验证活动,所有阈值在执行前冻结,采用预冻结的二元映射协议和结构化全排序评判协议;(2)SHA-256锁定的审计链,记录每次网络尝试的请求/响应/元数据;(3)三种失败模式的机制分解(M1标签语义映射偏差、M2近退化评分间隔、M3字节相同输入漂移);(4)前瞻性补充实验:A-S1同/跨日方差分解、A-S2四家提供商跨平台对比、A-S3构造错误电池;(5)L2自托管对照(批量不变内核、开放权重、固定GPU);(6)依赖感知不确定性量化(窗口级聚类bootstrap、平衡日重标注);(7)检测限理论与操作特征校准的元分析。

Result: 关键结果:在52,988次审计请求尝试中,I0-S同窗口重复排序Spearman中位数为0.400(要求≥0.90),I0-R次日字节相同重放全排序一致率为0.78(要求≥0.99);执行记录均处于上限。A-S1显示同日中位一致率0.805、跨日中位数0.800,差异0.005在预冻结等效带内——24小时不带来可测量的额外漂移。A-S2显示四家提供商(DeepSeek、Mistral、OpenAI、Qwen)均处于平台类不稳定性范围(0.74–0.88)。A-S3揭示判读器按错误类型而非错误大小区分构造错误。三家提供商的指纹字段以三种形式失败(缺失/搅动/稳定但不充分)。L2自托管在静默时达0.9872,并发负载下降8.4倍至0.8925回落到共享端点范围。指标替换与采样规模扩展(最高748,000次调用的模拟)在测试网格上均无法修复退化。

Conclusion: 核心结论:在共享端点上,模型名不是冻结的仪器,工程层完美不蕴含测量层可靠性。预注册评估必须在冻结任何门控之前先测量其仪器(噪声底限、间隔分布、元数据语义),否则门控在全套仪式下测试的是空命题。补救路径存在于每一层:快照锁定并验证、操作特征校准(约占本研究2%调用量)、检测限条件化、连续读数优于离散门控、聚合作为测量单位。论文贡献了三级快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则和一份报告清单,每一条都可追溯到本研究中的实测证据。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [The Civilization Framework: Sovereign-Anchored Communication Between Personal Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03425)
*Guangjun Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"文明框架"(Civilization Framework)及"大使馆协议"(Embassy Protocol),将个人多智能体系统间的通信基本单元从"代理"提升为"文明"(一名人类主权者+持久账本+可互换代理),通过三层签名机制和记忆派生权威实现跨主权异步协作;同时识别AI间通信的"时序权重效应"(先到信息获得不合理的权威),并在Claude Opus 5上通过预注册的1,908次试验进行验证,发现移除验证能力时错误上游声明被采纳率从4.2%升至54.2%,但因目标工具使用检查未达标,所有结果均标记为探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助专业协作中,人类已成为AI系统间的"传输层"(porter problem),在聊天或邮件中转译制品,每经过一跳都丢失环境细节、版本和约束,造成大量协作交易成本;现有互操作性协议(MCP、A2A)以"代理"为寻址单元,但代理是短暂进程,真正持久的实体是人及其累积的偏好、规范和项目记录,因此需要一个人类锚定的治理层。

Method: (1)框架构建:基于四条公理(问责终点A1、短暂成员A2、异构载体A3、对抗性交易方A4)和范围前提P1(个人范围),推导出"文明"作为跨方通信的基本单元,包含人类主权者、持久账本和可互换代理;(2)大使馆协议:每个文明设立常驻使馆接收并持久化入站通信,任何在线代理可申领处理,采用三层签名机制(微同步单签、判定双签、仲裁人工),账本条目哈希链连接并交叉签署对方链头作为检查点;(3)记忆派生权威:代理的代表性权力受其可访问记忆范围约束,通过签名凭据外部化,与文明级声誉分离;(4)时序权重实验:预注册的5条件×2验证水平(共9格)×53题×4重复=1,908次试验,使用Claude Opus 5(effort medium),独立无状态会话,采用代码评分+单人盲审+作者仲裁的三层评分,通过OSF预注册并冻结哈希材料。

Result: (1)主结果(均因MC-1'目标工具使用检查失败其调用预算条件,标记为探索性):验证门控H6——错误锚点在受限验证下采纳率54.2%,在全验证下仅4.2%(风险差0.500,分层MH OR=71.7,两种题集规范均拒绝零假设);到达顺序H4'——错误锚点先到达采纳率54.2%,接收方封存答案后到达则降至31.6%(风险差0.227,两种规范均拒绝零假设,但E与C的提示外壳未做长度匹配,差异部分可能源于外壳形式);(2)次要结果依赖规范:正确锚点救援Sec-2在两种规范下均显著(受限准确率从32.5%升至99.5%);来源标注缓解Sec-1在全题规范下拒绝零假设(p=0.031)但在排除规范下未拒绝(标记为效力不足的不确定);封存答案等价性H5'仅在全题规范下声明等价,在排除规范下不声明;(3)探索性观察:受限基线自报置信度45.5对应32.5%准确率,错误锚点先到时置信度升至69.2而准确率仅34.0%;非采纳答案中96.3%同时自报不同意并独立验证;(4)作者日常运行的多代理操作系统作为参考实现,涵盖两个部署共1,048个任务、约25.8万条账本事件。

Conclusion: (1)"文明"作为人类锚定的治理域比"代理"更适合作为跨方AI通信的寻址单元,持久账本与可互换代理实现了"无状态成员、有状态文明";(2)使馆协议通过统一入站通道、任务板和承诺账本为单一制品,并以三层签名按后果定价一致性,使承诺状态而非消息传递成为真相之源;(3)代理代表性权力受可访问记忆范围限制,凭据桥接内部范围与外部可观察性,声誉归属于文明而非代理;(4)AI间通信存在时序权重效应,先到信息获得不合理权威,验证能力是主要门控因素(4.2% vs 54.2%),到达顺序效应(54.2% vs 31.6%)独立于纯暴露但部分可能受提示外壳差异影响;(5)协议层缓解措施应在能力和程序层面运作,而非仅靠指令层面的来源标注;(6)开放问题包括经验梯度形式化、顺序无关承诺语义、保留分歧的联邦对账终止性、多方阈值签名等;(7)局限:单模型单语言、受限验证是模拟的权限预算不对称而非完整阻塞验证空间、提示外壳未长度匹配使H4'和Sec-1对比暴露于形式混淆、目标工具使用读数失败其预算条件导致本轮整体为探索性,需在强制调用预算的线束下复现。

Abstract: Humans are the transport layer between AI systems, losing context at every hop. We present the Civilization Framework, whose addressable party is the civilization, not the agent (one human sovereign, a persistent ledger, and interchangeable agents), and the Embassy Protocol, a carrier-agnostic overlay: messages arrive asynchronously at a resident ledger endpoint, any online agent of the receiver handles them, and commitment state on both ledgers, not delivery, is ground truth. Authority derives from memory: an agent's power to act for its civilization is capped by the memory it can access and externalized through signed credentials, separate from civilization-level reputation. We identify the temporal-weight effect, a hazard in AI-to-AI communication where what arrives first acquires unearned authority, and test it in one frontier model in a preregistered 1,908-trial experiment. With verification removed, an incorrect upstream claim arriving first captures 54.2% of answers (4.2% under full verification), while the same claim arriving after the receiver has sealed its own answer captures 31.6% (the two prompt shells are not length-matched, so part of that gap may reflect shell form; see Section 7), and both registered question-set specifications agree on these two verdicts (the exclusion specification is preregistered as under-powered). Two secondary results, the mitigation from instruction-level provenance labeling and sealed-answer accuracy equivalence, are specification-dependent, holding only under the all-questions specification. Because a registered check of tool use failed its call-budget condition, the registration classifies the round as inconclusive and every result above, primary and secondary, is reported as exploratory; a replication with harness-enforced budgets is planned. The framework's intra-civilization layer has a working implementation.

</details>


### [250] [Towards Scaling Reinforcement Learning to Massive Populations: Learning Mean-Field Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02928)
*Aditya Makkar,Benjamin Unger,Jeongyeol Kwon,Mathieu Laurière,Eugene Vinitsky,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文针对大规模多智能体系统中均值场强化学习难以处理高维状态-动作空间的问题，提出了一种学习低维均值场表示的离线强化学习方法。理论贡献包括形式化均值场表示学习框架、提出基于模型的算法并给出有限样本Nash gap保证。实验在受供应链优化启发的单步路由博弈上进行，结果表明学习低维表示在奖励预测和均衡策略质量上优于不利用种群结构的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多智能体系统（如广告拍卖、交通路由、推荐系统等）部署日益广泛。现有方法通常将其他智能体视为固定环境的单智能体优化，未建模种群动态。均值场强化学习虽提供了原则性框架，但在高维控制问题中建模完整种群分布本身是不可处理的。因此，如何设计可扩展到大规模种群高维决策问题的均值场强化学习框架成为关键问题。

Method: 作者引入了一个均值场强化学习框架，其中奖励和转移动态仅通过一个未知的低维聚合统计量依赖于种群。在此框架下研究离线设置，设计了一个基于模型的方法：1) 学习候选均值场模型集合（包括特征映射、奖励模型和转移模型）；2) 通过经验风险构建置信集；3) 使用乐观-悲观区间目标评估候选均衡和可能偏差，最终输出Nash gap近似最小的策略。理论分析引入了Bellman迁移系数来控制从记录种群流到目标种群策略及偏差的泛化误差。实验部分设计了一个单步路由博弈（5个起点、5个终点、两个中间层各4节点），状态空间25，动作空间16，全种群分布400维但均值场表示仅56维。比较了六种奖励模型架构：无种群信息的单智能体基线、原始均值整体模型、学习均值场模型、有限K oracle、无限种群oracle和完整种群定律模型。
