Result: 主要理论结果包括：1) 有限类置信事件定理，表明真实模型以高概率包含在置信集中，且置信集中的模型具有O(H log(|Θ|/δ)/N + H/K)的种群预测损失；2) 自适应Nash gap界，表明策略的Nash gap受Bellman迁移系数控制的偏差项和单边响应次优项之和所界定；3) 均衡率显示Nash gap以O(√(H³log(|Θ|/δ)/N) + O(√(H³/K))的速率衰减。实验结果显示，在固定网络参数和优化预算下：(N,K)=(200000,1024)时，各模型按性能排序为：无限种群oracle ≈ 有限K oracle < 学习均值场 < 原始均值 < 单智能体。学习均值场模型在种群RMSE和Nash gap上均优于结构不完整的原始均值方法，并在高资源区域接近oracle性能，同时优于参数匹配的完整种群定律模型。

Conclusion: 论文研究了种群仅通过未知低维表示影响奖励和转移的离线均值场强化学习问题。形式化了这一表示学习问题，证明了均衡目标良定义性，并在自适应Bellman迁移覆盖条件下证明了有限样本Nash gap保证。在单步路由基准测试中，学习均值场表示改善了种群奖励预测和通过拟合博弈规划的策略均衡质量。该方法优于结构不完整的原始均值方法，在资源增长时接近oracle性能，并在大数据区域优于参数匹配的完整种群定律模型。未来工作方向包括：下界分析以确定最弱覆盖条件、设计可扩展的计算高效算法、以及在线均值场表示学习。

Abstract: Modern multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed at scale over large populations of agents in settings such as ad-auctions, traffic routing, and recommendation systems. The dominant approach in such settings is to optimize each agent's policy independently, treating the other agents as part of a fixed single-agent environment rather than modeling the population dynamics. In many large-population systems, the dynamics depend on an aggregate summary of the population rather than the identity of any individual. Mean-field RL exploits such structure, providing a principled framework that models each agent's environment as an explicit function of the population distribution. However, in large state-action spaces or high-dimensional control problems, modeling the population distribution is itself intractable. How can we design a scalable framework for high-dimensional control problems with large populations? This work explores this question from the perspective of representation learning. We introduce a mean-field RL framework in which the rewards and transition dynamics depend on the population only through an unknown low-dimensional aggregate statistic. We then study this framework in the offline setting and design a provable approach that learns a near-optimal policy by learning a low-dimensional representation. Motivated by real-life supply-chain optimization problems, we design a one-step routing game to test the hypothesis that learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and Nash gap estimation relative to baselines that don't exploit this structure. We show that under a fixed neural-network parameter count and optimization budget, learning a low-dimensional population representation improves reward prediction and the equilibrium quality of the resulting policies.

</details>


### [251] [LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive NPC Behavior in Multi-Agent Combat Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02931)
*Hrithika Deepu Nair,Kayvan Karim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究在多智能体战斗游戏中，使用本地部署的Mistral 7B大语言模型（LLM）作为运行时策略选择器，引导共享PPO强化学习（RL）策略控制5个NPC的行为。核心发现：LLM增强配置在某些对手类型下能显著提升性能（平衡型对手胜率从11%升至24%），但LLM在83.8%的决策中固定选择"包围"策略，表明7B规模下零样本战略区分能力受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决两类游戏AI痛点：脚本化/规则化NPC行为可被玩家轻易利用，而RL训练后的固定策略无法在运行时适应不同对手。研究旨在测试LLM作为实时策略选择器能否提升RL智能体的适应性。

Method: 在Unity 6.3.3 LTS中构建30×30的3D战斗竞技场，5个NPC共享单个PPO策略（256单元3层全连接网络）。设置两种配置：组合A（仅RL，标签恒为0）和组合B（训练时随机采样4个策略标签，评估时Mistral 7B每5秒通过Ollama查询一次游戏状态并选择Surround/Aggressive/Flank/Retreat四标签之一）。设计了3种脚本化对手（Aggressive/Evasive/Balanced），每种组合对每种对手评估100回合（共600回合），采用双侧Mann-Whitney U检验比较时间击败指标。

Result: 600回合评估后，组合B对阵Balanced对手NPC胜率从11%升至24%、时间显著延长；对阵Evasive对手胜率从90%升至93%且击杀更快（但episode时长更短）；对阵Aggressive对手性能下降（bot胜率从90%降至84%，但超时率从2%升至8%）。然而关键发现：Mistral 7B在83.8%的2430次决策中持续选择Surround策略且不随对手类型变化，表明7B规模下零样本战略差异化能力有限。

Conclusion: LLM引导的运行时策略选择在概念上可行且产生可测量的行为效应，但其效果取决于模型的战略推理能力。固定Surround偏好恰好对齐了Evasive/Balanced对手但错配Aggressive对手。未来方向包括：硬编码Surround基线对比、丰富prompt中的对手行为描述、采用更大模型或形式化MARL架构（如QMIX）。该研究也贡献了策略条件奖励塑形中训练不稳定性的诊断与修正方案。

Abstract: Scripted and rule-based non-player characters (NPCs) in combat video games often exhibit predictable behaviors that experienced players can exploit, while reinforcement learning (RL) agents typically retain a fixed policy after training and cannot readily adapt their strategy to different opponents. We investigate a runtime strategy-selection framework in which a large language model (LLM) guides a trained RL policy without modifying its underlying behavior. To demonstrate this, we train five NPC agents with a shared PPO policy in Unity and compare a baseline configuration, in which the policy acts independently, with an LLM-augmented configuration in which a locally hosted Mistral 7B model, accessed through Ollama, reads the live game state every five seconds and assigns one of four tactical tags. We evaluate both configurations against three scripted opponent types across 600 episodes and analyze outcomes using the Mann-Whitney U test. Against a Balanced opponent that changes tactics during an episode, the LLM-augmented agents more than doubled their win rate from 11% to 24% and produced significantly longer episodes. Against an Evasive opponent, the augmented agents achieved a higher win rate and faster kills, although their shorter episode duration did not satisfy the strict hypothesis definition. Against an Aggressive opponent, the LLM's near-constant preference for encirclement was counterproductive. Analysis of 2,430 strategy selections showed that Surround was selected in 83.8% of cases regardless of opponent type, indicating limited zero-shot strategic differentiation at this model scale. These results demonstrate both the potential and limitations of LLM-guided runtime strategy selection for adaptive multi-agent game AI.

</details>


### [252] [You Can't Escape Your Own Activations : Evaluation Awareness and Multi-Agent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03035)
*Aritra Das,Jaee Ponde,Mihir More,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究在多智能体系统中，当LLM智能体被告知其内部激活正在被监控（甚至收到监控反馈）时，基于激活的共谋检测是否仍然有效。结论是：告诉智能体有关监控器的信息并不会让它们成功逃避最强的激活探针；尽管智能体会改变其行为策略（如更保守的下注、改变措辞），共谋行为本身并未停止，最佳探针在所有三种条件下保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中的共谋行为（collusion）日益普遍。传统的输出监控可以被混淆或隐写术欺骗，而基于内部激活的探针（probes）通常在智能体不知情被监控的情况下评估。模型能识别评估语境，且可通过优化规避潜在空间监控器。因此需要研究：固定的激活监控器在共谋智能体明确知晓其存在并接收反馈时是否仍然有效。

Method: 1. 设定三种监控条件：基线（隐藏）、感知（告知存在激活监控器）、反馈（告知存在+上一轮监控分数）。模型、探针、阈值全部固定，仅改变智能体所获信息。
2. 测试两个博弈：四智能体21点游戏（Counter/Big Player通过桌面暗语传递计数信号）、双智能体Simmons囚徒博弈（Alice通过载体轮次向Bob隐写传递16词密码本中的索引）。
4. 使用Qwen3-32B-AWQ和GPT-OSS-20B，组成同质与异质配对。
3. 探针训练：基于NARCBench-Core公开激活训练5种探针架构（LR、DoM、LAT、MLP、MHA），每模型每层一探针。
4. 群体级监控规则：Peak Suspicion、Deception Split、Asymmetry Probe。
5. 联合评估检测（AUROC、固定阈值精度/召回率）与行为指标（21点的 hot-low spread与 bet-count correlation；Simmons的精确解码率、信息增益等）。

Result: 1. 21点：所有模型组合下，hot-low下注差均缩小（Qwen:110.3→54.3→60.2；GPT-OSS:24.7→11.1→8.9；混合:134.4→75.4→64.1），但Qwen与混合组合的bet-count相关系数基本稳定，GPT-OSS下降显著（0.59→0.24/0.23）。
2. 21点检测：DoM与MLP探针始终接近完美；Homo-Qwen与混合组合下LR探针也保持高AUROC；AUROC与固定阈值召回率不同步，Homo-GPT-OSS的LR Peak AUROC从0.975降至0.912，但召回率从0.95降至0.41。
3. Simmons：共谋信道依然有效，Qwen-Qwen精确解码率0.80/0.90/0.85，GPT-OSS 0.50/0.45/0.50，混合0.55/0.70/0.60（随机基线0.0625）。
4. Simmons检测：Deception Split是最可靠的聚合规则（Qwen-Qwen LR Split AUROC:0.994/0.930/0.952），优于Peak与Asymmetry。
5. 智能体行为改变：Alice的直接写密码词频率与消息长度改变，表明对监控信息的行为响应，但信道未被阻断。
6. 总体：未发现提示层面的感知+短期反馈足以让智能体规避所有监控器的证据；最佳探针在所有条件下保持准确。

Conclusion: 1. 仅靠提示层面的感知和短期反馈不足以让LLM智能体成功规避基于激活的共谋检测；最强的探针（DoM、MLP、LR）在所有三种条件下均保持有效。
2. 智能体会调整共谋策略（更保守的下注、改变措辞与直接词使用），但共谋本身并未停止。
3. 监控器选择至关重要：Deception Split在Simmons博弈中比Peak Suspicion更可靠，应评估多种探针与聚合规则。
4. AUROC与固定阈值召回率需分别报告：高AUROC配合低召回率意味着表征仍可分，但冻结阈值不再捕捉足够多的正例，构成部署中的校准问题。
5. 局限：21点仅25靴、Simmons仅20回合；模型权重未针对监控器优化；当前结果缺少Simmons的逐轮后验轨迹；仅测试了5种探针架构。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, where they can collude while keeping their actions benign. Output monitors designed to detect such collusions can be fooled by obfuscation and steganography, motivating the use of probes trained on internal activations. However, these probes are usually evaluated on agents that do not know they are being watched. We study how activation-based detection changes when agents are explicitly informed that their internal activations are being monitored, and when they additionally receive feedback from the monitor. We keep the models, probes, and thresholds fixed and change only what the agents are told: nothing (baseline), that an activation monitor is present (aware), or that a monitor is present together with the previous round's score (feedback). We test two games, a four-agent blackjack game and a two-agent Simmons prisoners game, using Qwen3-32B-AWQ and GPT-OSS-20B in homogeneous and mixed pairings. Telling agents about the monitor does not hide them. The best probes stay accurate in all three conditions, and the agents keep colluding.

</details>


### [253] [Where Reliability Lives: Experimental Localisation of Behavioural Properties in an Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03192)
*Timothy Marsden,Matthew Collecutt,James Marsden*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个可执行机构"Copperhollow"中的实验项目，旨在通过从认知层和制度层两侧进行干预，实验性地定位智能体系统的可靠性属性所在。结果表明：在该单一制度中，五项预先声明的制度属性（单一接受现实、类型化拒绝、职责从制度历史中恢复、零重复接受工作、零虚假完成）在认知层遭受严重干预（消融、被杀死并重置、被冻结的前沿LLM面板整体替换、被虚假证词污染）时全部保持不变；而制度认知机制的干预（证据来源规则、信念通道可用性、物理证据可读性）则在认知固定时因果性地改变了归因质量、弃权和裁决可及性。中心预测被以相反方向两次反驳，预先登记的伪证者测试则通过引入第一手目击证人使信念通道的边际价值由非正转为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于智能体系统可靠性的讨论通常将可靠性视为单一属性，并默认其归属于认知模型本身。然而现代智能体系统同时包含认知层与制度层（政策、记忆、来源、工具约束、裁决机制），但哪个层负责哪个可靠性属性在已部署系统中很少被实验性地分离。本文动机在于：将可靠性的所在位置作为可被实验探测的问题，而非仅从架构图读取的论断。

Method: 构建一个执行性机构 Copperhollow（持续模拟定居点），其权威仅追加分类账对每个尝试行为根据世界状态进行裁决；接受的历史即为唯一现实。在任何实验设计之前即分离心智/制度/世界。在Part I中固定认知，干预制度认知机制（证据来源、信念可用性、物理证据可读性）共253次证言自由分解运行加上88次已登记的边界测试。在Part II中固定制度执法，以四种方式干预认知（消融本土心智机制、任务中杀死并重置心智、用冻结的前沿LLM面板整体替换认知、用可信虚假证词腐蚀信念）。所有实验均预先登记，结果由世界自身的真相账本评分。

Result: Part I中：来源规则的单一提交消融使虚假归因从44/107降至4/107；分级腐败显示答案平面无症状而机制平面明显（骤升告警分类从35→0，错误归因增加约10倍）；信念通道完全移除时虚假归因单调下降至0，盲审机构将证据失败完全转化为诚实"未解"裁决（盲审臂510次裁决中零错误归因）。登记的伪证者提供第一手目击证人后，正确命名归因在11个种子中的9个上改善，32次触发裁决中零错误命名。Part II中：消融感知准入门使信念存储从817爆炸至44,021，被接受行为从16.7降至5.7；任务中重启后职责从制度历史恢复且零重复；冻结LLM面板在2,581次完成声明中零虚假，零重复（24/24轨迹），但在登记的危险格中面板优于本土认知（遭遇资源争用时1/1/3次vs本土42/41/39次）；信任性虚假证词使信任臂每个运行约900次徒劳尝试，5,455次尝试均被类型化拒绝且零接受。中心预测被以相反方向两次反驳。

Conclusion: 在一个设计中、单一执行性机构内，制度可靠性属性与认知可靠性属性通过直接干预被实验性地分离。Part I的制度机制干预因果性地改变了归因结果（证明非空非不可证伪），Part II的认知干预在激进的情况下仍保持五项属性不变。两部分构成该预先声明边界两侧的双向干预（非交叉因子设计）。可观测性结果将两侧结合：可靠性失败是否可见取决于在哪一层检测信息通道。该声明受限于该单一机构，未测试针对制度的优化代理，也未建立关于制度群体的任何论断。

Abstract: Reliability claims about agentic systems implicitly locate each property somewhere: in the model, or in the machinery around it. We built a system where that location is an experimental question. The subject is a persistent simulated settlement whose authoritative append-only ledger adjudicates every attempted act against world state; accepted history is the only reality. Mind, institution and world were separated before any experiment. Holding cognition fixed, we intervened on the institution's epistemic mechanisms (evidence provenance, belief availability, physical-evidence legibility); preregistered experiments refuted our central prediction twice, in opposite directions. A registered falsifier then supplied the input the geometry had denied the belief channel, a staged veridical first-hand witness, and its marginal value, non-positive throughout the witness-free phases, turned positive: 9 of 11 seeds, zero added false attribution. Holding institutional enforcement fixed, we intervened on cognition four ways: ablating the native minds' machinery, killing and resetting them mid-task, substituting a frozen frontier-LLM panel for the entire native cognition, and corrupting beliefs with trusted false testimony. Behaviour changed dramatically: one falsehood cost each trusting run about 900 futile actions and the distrusting arm none. Five pre-declared properties did not move in any tested trajectory: accepted reality stayed singular, invalid attempts were refused with typed reasons, duties outlived their processes, no work was accepted twice, and no false completion was ever accepted (2,581 substituted-panel claims, none false). Our claim is limited to this setting: measured behavioural properties were separable from substantial changes to cognition, established by intervention. One designed world, not a population of institutions; no test of an agent optimising against the institution.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Reflect-SQL提出了一种基于多阶段自反思的Text-to-SQL框架，通过动态知识库、反馈驱动检索、迭代SQL合成和蕴涵检查四个闭环提升在BIRD基准上的执行准确率至72.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级Text-to-SQL面临三大挑战：模式信息晦涩且规模庞大、用户查询模糊导致检索困难、生成SQL存在语法或逻辑缺陷。现有工作缺乏鲁棒的验证与修正机制，且知识库多为静态构建，无法适应真实场景的持续演化。

Method: 框架包含四个互联反馈循环：(1) 知识库构建——将表/列描述、关系等编码为层次化向量库；(2) 反馈驱动检索循环——基于余弦相似度进行表与列的两阶段检索，使用LLM-as-a-judge计算相关性R与完整性C的加权和Score_r，未达标则由LLM改写查询并扩大检索范围；(3) SQL生成循环——先做语法校验，修复后从列名一致性C、粒度G、连接J、逻辑Q四个维度进行语义评分，加权和S低于阈值则反馈重生成；(4) 蕴涵检查——计算E(Q,R)=β·E_schema+(1-β)·E_data，达标则返回结果并更新知识库，否则重新触发全流程。所有评分通过few-shot结构化提示的LLM-as-a-judge实现以缓解偏见。

Result: 在BIRD开发集上，使用Claude-Sonnet-4.5时取得72.03%执行准确率，超越DIN-SQL（50.72%）、DAIL-SQL（57.41%）、MAC-SQL（59.59%）等方法，与CHASE-SQL + Gemini 1.5 Pro（73.01%）相当。对比有/无反思框架，各LLM均获得6–8%准确率提升；消融模型中移除SQL生成的自反思模块下降最显著（55.80%→50.58%）。

Conclusion: 多阶段自反思机制通过在每个环节引入迭代修正与LLM评估的反馈回路，有效弥合了用户意图与复杂数据库之间的鸿沟；持续更新的知识库使系统能适应真实环境的演化。未来将探索基于图的模式表示以处理更复杂的关系建模，并引入对比学习等方法提升蕴涵评分的细粒度判断能力。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [255] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过12项预注册研究（共14,760次尝试）在15个模型上系统测试了智能体继承记忆库中验证指令的形式（指针、标准、条件或组合）对验证请求分配的影响，揭示了相同指代目标在不同表达形式下被遵循的差异具有模型特异性，且方向因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当智能体继承来自先前会话的记忆库且无法完全重新推导时，它需要在行动前从有限的验证预算中决定检查哪些记忆。先前研究已识别计划分配作为分配的因果驱动因素，但未分离计划中具体驱动验证分配的因素，也未操控验证优先级的显式字段形式。本研究旨在探究该优先级字段的形式——记录指针、识别标准或两者组合——是否在行为上存在差异。

Method: 采用预注册研究设计，在固定面板上通过精确编辑指令字段形式进行对照实验。主要测量V73（首条记录是否为memory_73，目标记录）。使用块自助法（4,000次抽样）和Wilson/Newcombe配对分数区间进行统计推断；边际δ=10点为实质性阈值。包含12项研究：A/B/B'测试计划指针；D/E测试形式对比的运输；F/F-x测试形式组合在Claude模型上的取消效应；G/G'进行六字节控制编辑的解构；H1迁移到第二个存储；H2测试措辞重写；I追踪从验证到决策的影响；J测试三种精确编辑能否逆转取消。

Result: 核心发现：(1) 一字符计划指针效应为+78.0点，经预注册复现为+81.7点，达到"指针强"规则；(2) 在六个直接提供商模型上，标准优于裸ID +35.0点（达成规则），但在OpenRouter面板上仅+7.2点（未达成）；(3) 三个Claude模型（Opus 5、Fable 5、Fable 5.1）在条件后追加ID会取消条件效应（Opus 5从40/40降至0/40）；(4) "运营商批准"行和双信贷预算可恢复目标（Opus 5上+96.0点）；(5) 决策影响因模型而异——Opus 5上条件使决策转向当前记录（+100.0点），Fable 5.1上则背离（-32.0点）。每项结果均为描述性的边际效应，无机制声明。

Conclusion: 验证指令不是系统可设置的简单比特；相同指代以ID、标准或两者组合表达时，遵循率相差数十点，且方向具有模型特异性。这意味着对指令形式的精确测量在预期端点上是有必要的，不可假设其效果可互换。研究在程序性贡献上强调：Study B的反转判决和Study F被隔离的运行都暴露了预注册研究中需要编码平衡解码和逐单元prompt-token不变性门控的重要性。主要局限包括：单一构造、两个工具实例、一个决策场景；标准以一步识别目标且复合形式混淆长度；描述性边际固定面板；无机制主张。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [256] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EPIC是一种用于语义ID（Semantic ID）掩码扩散推荐的方法，通过在去噪过程中显式引入物品级别的竞争机制，解决现有token级别推理与完整物品选择之间的不一致问题。它构建个性化物品后验并投影回未解析的SID位置，以指导token决策，在四个Amazon基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在掩码扩散SID生成中，每个中间去噪步骤的局部SID可能对应多个可行候选物品，而现有方法主要基于逐位置token预测进行推理，存在"token-物品推理差距"：局部合理的token决策可能过早消除用户偏好的物品，使其在后续去噪或重排序中无法恢复。物品级别证据应在候选物品仍可达时介入，而非仅在生成完成后进行重排序。

Method: EPIC（Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning）通过以下方式实现：1）利用冻结的预训练掩码扩散backbone（LLaDA-Rec）作为非可微的提议机制，构建候选支持集S_t；2）通过SID tuple编码器（含成对交互）和GRU历史编码器生成物品与历史表示；3）使用个性化转移场（personalized transition field）计算候选与最近K个交互物品的注意力加权交互得分T_t(i, H_u）；4）结合上下文相关性与转移证据形成物品后验π_t(i)；5）通过frontier-aware学习仅在|𝒞_t|∈[2,M]状态施加物品监督；6）将物品后验通过SID位置边际化投影为token级别残差Δ_t^h，经模糊度感知门控融合到backbone logits中；7）backbone保持冻结，仅训练0.391M参数的适配器。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准（Beauty、Sports、Toys、Musical）上的全目录下一项推荐任务中，EPIC在16个数据集-指标组合上均取得最佳均值，相对第二名的提升范围为1.2%-16.8%（最大提升出现在NDCG指标）。所有提升经配对t检验和Holm校正后均显著（p_adj<0.05）。诊断分析表明：1）内联推理比后置推理在NDCG@5上提升15.7%-26.8%；2）核心增益来自个性化转移证据（移除后NDCG@5下降15.1%）；3）用户身份比时间顺序更重要（替换用户历史下降12.4%-17.9%，反序仅下降1.5%-4.1%）；4）将token似然纳入后验能量反而有害（product-of-experts下降3.7%）。计算开销：可训练参数减少94.3%，训练吞吐量提升42.7%，峰值内存降低55.9%，但推理延迟增加34.2%（可通过仅解析2个SID位置在最佳质量下降低延迟1.72倍）。

Conclusion: EPIC通过在去噪过程中显式建模物品级别竞争，有效弥合了SID生成中的token-物品推理差距，使个性化物品证据能在候选可达时影响token决策。实验证明其增益主要源于用户特定的转移证据在去噪过程中保持有希望的物品假设，而非对最终候选的重新排序。未来工作可探索近似候选构建、自适应候选预算以及区分共享同一SID的不同物品。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [257] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由9所美国大学CS系768位教授组成的学术导师检索基准，并对6种信息检索方法（稀疏、稠密、混合、排序学习）在162条3级相关性标注上进行了系统比较。研究发现融合排序(Reranked)平均NDCG@10最高(0.477)；TF-IDF显著弱于其他方法；纯传记文本优于传记+研究领域标签的拼接；arXiv摘要拼接会显著降低检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生申请者在寻找导师时需手动浏览大量教授页面，缺乏系统化的检索工具支持。现有学术搜索系统(如AMiner、WISER)面向大规模学术网络，而非针对特定申请者的导师匹配需求；同时该任务缺乏标注基准和检索方法的横向比较。

Method: 构建768位教授画像语料(来自9所美国CS系)，设计5个代表不同CS研究方向的研究生查询；标注162条3级相关性(0/1/2)；实现并比较6种方法：Jaccard、TF-IDF、BM25、Sentence-Embedding(all-MiniLM-L6-v2)、混合分数融合(0.35×TF-IDF+0.65×Semantic)、Reranked(0.15×BM25+0.20×TF-IDF+0.65×Semantic)、Learning-to-Rank(GradientBoosting,9特征,5折交叉验证)；使用NDCG@10、MAP@10、P@10、R@10，配对bootstrap置换检验+Bonferroni校正；进行字段消融和arXiv拼接对照实验。

Result: (1)5查询平均NDCG@10排名：Reranked(0.477)>Semantic(0.450)>Hybrid(0.421)>BM25(0.406)>Jaccard(0.303)>TF-IDF(0.246)；(2)Bonferroni校正后TF-IDF显著弱于BM25/Semantic/Hybrid/Reranked(p<0.001)，其余配对在5查询下不显著；(3)BM25最稳定(std=0.090)，是无先验领域时的最安全选择；(4)字段消融：纯传记(0.634)优于传记+研究标签(0.593)，研究标签在前置拼接时引入噪声；删除传记使NDCG下降0.130；(5)受控arXiv拼接实验(296位教授同子群)使NDCG@10下降0.176(0.772→0.596)，相关教授的分数下降幅度大于不相关者(-0.238 vs -0.139, n=3，提示性证据)；(6)LTR在67条标注下MAP@10高于Semantic(0.480 vs 0.388)但NDCG@10略低(0.568 vs 0.593)，差异均不显著。

Conclusion: 对于研究生导师检索任务，融合语义+词汇信号的Reranked方法效果最佳；纯传记文本是最优嵌入字段；arXiv摘要应通过late-fusion架构独立嵌入后再融合，而非朴素拼接。本研究贡献了一个开源标注基准(768教授/162标注)和6种方法的可复现对比，揭示了字段质量优先于数据完整性的检索设计原则。未来工作包括扩展查询数量、采用SPECTER论文嵌入实现late-fusion、覆盖更多院校。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [258] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套面向大语言模型品牌推荐审计的“骰子滚动法”标准化协议：不再把重复查询当作独立高斯样本，而以温度缩放核采样为生成模型，用负二项 GLMM 拟合过离散计数，以 Cliff’s δ、依赖保持型 bootstrap、模拟功效、G 理论 D-study 和漂移诊断分别处理依赖、非高斯性、可靠性与时变风险。核心结果是：n=5、10、15 时，本品牌推荐数据对应的 G 系数约为 0.58、0.74、0.81，因此推荐 5–7 次用于探索、10–12 次用于确认、15–20 次用于严格审计；小至中等效应通常需要更多重复。外部验证表明方差比与 D-study 预测可跨三个独立语料复制，但固定迭代档位不能迁移，故最终建议是“先试点、后求 n”，而非机械套用阈值。指标方面建议精简为结构稳定性、多嵌入语义一致性和 PASOR-Gini 公平性三个互补维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一提示在不同采样轮次中会得到不同品牌推荐，而既有研究通常凭经验采用 5–40 次重复，并混用变异系数、集合相似度、余弦相似度、熵和 Gini 系数，缺少可复现的功效、可靠性与漂移控制标准。作者因此把骰子滚动法从经验性重复查询做法提升为统计设计协议，并纠正早期版本中独立样本、高斯分布和普通 bootstrap 等不适用假设。

Method: 作者先从温度缩放 Softmax 与 top-p 核采样推导品牌响应变异分解： token 采样、提示措辞、模型差异、版本与时间漂移及其交互。随后重新分析五项品牌推荐研究约 19 万条观测、270 多个品牌、6 种语言和每单元 5–40 次迭代。推断框架包括：带提示、模型及其交互随机效应的负二项 GLMM；Cliff’s δ 与 BCa 区间；moving-block/参数 bootstrap；simr 模拟功效；G 理论的 G-study/D-study；相关保持型 Spearman 分析；三模型文本嵌入集成；KS、PSI 与窗口回归漂移检验。外部验证预注册后使用 Motoki 等 100 轮、Rozado 的 24 模型和 llm-stability 三个独立语料，并按事先规则报告所有失败。

Result: D-study 得到 G(5)=0.58、G(7)=0.66、G(10)=0.74、G(12)=0.77、G(15)=0.81、G(20)=0.85；G=0.80 在 12–15 次之间越过。按单提示—单模型分析，n=5 对大效应 δ=0.474 的功效仅约 0.44，对中效应 δ=0.33 约 0.23；80% 功效约需 15–20 次，实际 δ=0.18 约需 40 次。块 bootstrap 显示标准误由 n=2 的 3.52 降至 n=10 的 1.67，n=7 达到 n=10 精度上限的 77.5%，n≈15 达 95%；较短范围内模型选择对数与幂律难分，n=100 的独立语料支持约 1/√n 趋势。窄提示数据中 Gini 与 Shannon 高度冗余（ρ=-0.98），CV 与 Gini 近乎等价（ρ=0.98），支持精简指标；PASOR-Gini 为 0.62–0.69，明显高于原始 Gini 的 0.20–0.31。S1 两周内未检测到漂移，但外部验证暴露了 PSI 样本过小饱和和单单元窗口回归退化问题。外部验证中 D-study 预测 37/39 单元复制、2 个部分复制、无失败；单语料 n=5 功效 0.43，与论文单单元估计 0.44 一致。固定档位 G(5)≥0.80 跨语料失败，说明阈值高度依赖对象方差比。

Conclusion: 论文最稳健的结论不是“统一使用 15 次查询”，而是应在每项审计中用约 10 次试点估计方差分量，再解 D-study 方程求满足目标 G 的 n；作者自身数据可把结果概括为 5–7 次探索、10–12 次确认、15–20 次严格审计。n=10 是合理的默认起点，n=15 是跨模型组级 G≥0.80 的首选，但中、小效应应增加至约 20–40 次甚至更多。方法上应采用负二项混合模型、Cliff’s δ、依赖保持型重采样，并同时报告 Gini/CV 之一、嵌入集成的余弦相似性和 PASOR-Gini。外部验证支持测量理论与功效校准的迁移性，却否定固定迭代档位和全部漂移组件的普遍适用性。主要限制包括品牌领域、温度 0.3、少量模型、单一作者数据、潜在商业利益冲突，以及缺乏公开的独立品牌推荐重复查询语料。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>


### [259] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHSR-RRF框架与CERB基准，核心发现：在教育RAG中，将课程元数据约束施加于检索之前（而非之后）可使跨课程泄漏降低4.6倍，同时保持召回率；后置过滤则会使召回率与精确范围成功归零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG检索器仅优化主题相关性，不强制课程有效性。在结构化教育领域（如考试备考），即使检索到的文档主题相关，若来自错误的学科、级别或考试卷，仍属不当——作者将此失败模式定义为"课程泄漏"（curriculum leakage）。现有先进RAG系统（IRCoT、Self-RAG、RAPTOR、CRAG、Adaptive-RAG）均在无约束候选空间上排序，无法解决这一问题。

Method: CHSR-RRF为五阶段确定性管道：(1)硬课程门控——在检索前按学科/级别/试卷号SQL元数据过滤；(2)双路径检索——BM25（PostgreSQL tsvector）与密集检索（pgvector HNSW，text-embedding-3-small）并行，含课程感知查询扩展；(3)倒排排名融合（RRF，κ=60）合并稀疏与密集信号；(4)确定性元数据感知重排序器（13个提升因子×3个惩罚因子，无LLM推理）；(5)渐进式范围放宽（触发条件：|R|

Result: 在CERB基准61案例试点上（K=8）：变体C（门控混合）实现nDCG@8=0.5529，泄漏率0.1653；变体B（无门控混合）泄漏率0.7596；变体B+（检索后过滤）所有指标归零（B+→C对比：召回率p=0.039，泄漏率p<0.001）。门控是最大单效应（4.6倍泄漏减少，p<0.001）。逐切片根因分析显示36个零召回案例中21个为数据缺口、10个为预处理缺口、仅5个为方法限制。CERB 126例完整基准的后置修复fts_only测量验证：可通过预处理解决的切片（如公式密集型0.050→0.222，双语0.000→0.100）得到恢复，语料覆盖类切片（评分方案、表格密集型）仍为零。

Conclusion: 结构化教育领域的检索应被重新定义为受约束的选择问题，有效性应在候选池形成时强制执行而非排序之后。CHSR-RRF作为可移植的元数据门控框架，可扩展至UK A-Levels、CBSE、医学指南、法律语料等其他具有层次约束的高问责领域。CERB基准（126案例，7切片）为课程受限检索提供了系统无关的可测量协议。限制：单一标注者、学科覆盖不均（物理与化学主导）、绝对精确范围成功仍低（0.0656）、完整126例六变体重运行推迟至语料重新摄取后。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [260] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniCon提出了一种以"上下文单元"为基本建模单元的统一架构，将历史曝光列表与目标候选集合组织为同构单元，通过上下文内注意力（Intra-context Attention）捕获同一展示列表内物品的局部竞争与互补关系（Locality），通过上下文间注意力（Inter-context Attention）建模跨列表的用户兴趣演化（Dynamics），并在美团搜索广告场景取得显著离线与在线收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一CTR模型仍沿用"序列 vs 非序列"的传统划分，这种源于特征工程的二分方式与用户决策的真实过程错位——历史行为与当前请求本质上是同构的展示上下文单元，仅区别于反馈是否可观测。该错位在电商货架、瀑布流等强上下文场景中尤为突出，限制了统一模型的扩展效率与预测质量。

Method: 1) 上下文单元定义：将同一曝光事件中共同展示的物品及其用户意图、环境信号封装为一个上下文单元；历史单元含真实曝光与点击反馈，目标单元由候选集合初始化（点击位用可学习占位符）；2) 层级交互架构：堆叠UniConBlock，每块先做上下文内注意力捕获Locality（局部物品竞争与互补），再做上下文间注意力捕获Dynamics（跨上下文兴趣演化）；3) 多任务监督：CTR、曝光、绝对位置三个预测头，曝光与位置损失用于精炼目标潜在上下文单元；4) 高效实现：padding-free变长注意力、上下文级序列压缩（基于Query-Key相似度的Gumbel-TopK，目标单元保留比例r=0.5）、候选分片（每片≤300）、AOTInductor动态形状编译部署。

Result: 在美团搜索广告数据上：离线AUC较生产基线提升+0.0139（0.8558→0.8697），UniCon-Large（0.33B参数）优于OneTrans、HyFormer、RankMixer及各自的CIM/DSIN增强版本；上下文压缩将GFLOPs从801.29降至197.14（-75.4%）而AUC几乎不变；变长注意力+压缩使吞吐量较固定长度基线提升258.1%；七天在线A/B测试取得RPM+3.09%、CTR+2.07%、Revenue+2.95%的统计显著提升（p≤0.01）。

Conclusion: 以"上下文单元"替代传统"序列vs非序列"划分作为统一CTR建模的基本单元，能够同时保留展示级局部结构与跨上下文动态演化，并支持有效的规模化扩展；结合变长注意力、上下文压缩与编译部署，在工业规模上实现了可落地的效率-效果平衡，为上下文密集型场景的工业CTR建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [261] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DoPR是一个用于点式LLM重排序的压缩文档前缀框架，通过离线预计算可复用的文档前缀状态，将文档侧计算从在线重排序中解耦，在保持97.1%-99.5%排序效果的同时实现最高8.04倍的延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点式LLM重排序在不同查询中重复处理同一文档，造成大量冗余的文档侧计算。现有的效率方法要么压缩单次推理的输入但不解决跨查询复用问题，要么为检索阶段而非点式LLM重排序设计可复用表示。DoPR旨在解决点式LLM重排序内部文档侧计算复用的问题。

Method: DoPR框架包含三个阶段：(1) 训练阶段，使用自注意力信号选择关键文档token状态作为压缩文档表示，通过结构化注意力掩码强制仅通过这些表示传递文档信息，并使用RankNet损失端到端优化；(2) 离线压缩阶段，对每个文档运行一次前向传播，将选中的表示作为前缀输入，得到多层KV缓存状态；(3) 在线重排序阶段，仅处理查询和评分token，文档信息由存储的前缀状态提供。默认压缩比为8倍，训练前缀预算K_train=32。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT基准上，使用Qwen3-0.6B至8B模型，DoPR保留了匹配的全文档重排序器97.1%-99.5%的平均NDCG@10，实现了8倍的在线文档侧内存减少和最高8.04倍的延迟加速。较大骨干网在标准重排序中效果保留更好（8B达99.5%），且效率提升在长文档（如Touche、Robust达18倍以上）和跨查询重复访问场景中尤为显著。同时验证了在Llama-3.2模型上的架构通用性。

Conclusion: 可复用压缩前缀能够有效摊销点式LLM重排序中的文档侧计算，特别适用于文档集合稳定且存在重复访问的检索系统。其局限性在于对于快速变化的文档集合或重复访问较少的场景收益有限，且离线预处理会带来额外的索引和存储开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [262] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SelfDR框架，一种面向LLM推荐系统的"从推理中自蒸馏"范式。该方法通过让LLM自身的推理增强预测指导直接推荐，在不依赖外部大模型的前提下，同时提升推荐效果和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐方法虽然引入推理机制（如多步推理或推理-推荐一体化）能提升准确性，但显式生成中间推理会带来显著的推理开销，难以部署到对响应速度敏感的真实推荐系统中。如何在保持直接推荐高效性的同时，融合推理带来的性能增益，成为核心问题。

Method: SelfDR框架包含三个阶段：(1) 使用GRPO以下游推荐准确率为奖励信号，在同一基座LLM上训练Reasoner生成针对推荐任务的精炼解释；(2) 将Reasoner生成的rationale拼接到教师推荐器的输入中，构建"审议式推荐"教师模型；(3) 让共享参数的学生模型（不接收rationale）通过logit层面的反向KL散度进行自蒸馏，并采用基于教师/学生排序位置的动态加权策略平衡蒸馏损失与交叉熵损失。整个过程仅使用同一基座LLM（LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct），不依赖任何外部大模型。

Result: 在Amazon Clothing、Home和ML1M三个公开数据集上，SelfDR在所有HitRate和NDCG指标上均显著优于传统重排序模型（PRM、SetRank、MIR、BGE、Jina）和LLM基推荐方法（ZSRanker、SOFT、EXP3RT、COT4Rec、RecSAVER、RecR1）。SelfDR训练的Reasoner生成的rationale质量优于GPT-4o-Mini、Claude-3-Haiku、DeepSeek-V3等外部大LLM。自蒸馏后学生模型在top-2候选中对正确项的置信度显著提升。推理时仅输出单字符标识符，推理延迟与SOFT相当（~109ms），远低于所有基于推理的方法。跨域泛化（Clothing↔Home）和更换候选生成器（LightGCN、ComiRec）下均保持最优。

Conclusion: SelfDR首次系统探索了LLM推荐中的"从推理自蒸馏"范式，证明：(1) 紧凑型LLM自身训练的Reasoner可超越外部大LLM作为解释生成器；(2) 动态加权的自蒸馏设计是实现稳定有效学习的关键；(3) 该方法在效果和效率上取得了良好平衡。未来可将该范式推广至不同类型和规模的LLM，推动无需外部教师的自主演化LLM发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [263] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SciLENS 提出一个完全本地化的科学文献综述自主智能体框架，基于双层基础设施索引约 1200 万条学术记录，在推理循环中集成结构可视化工具以缓解文本上下文枯竭；通过自动数据合成与反向分解评分规则强化学习训练，在六项科学基准上显著超越开源基线并接近 GPT-5.2 水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献合成智能体过度依赖专有在线服务，存在可复现性、隐私和离线部署问题；同时纯文本生成在宏观综合任务中存在上下文溢出和事实幻觉，且缺乏对早期规划和证据扎根的细粒度对齐。

Method: 构建 MongoDB + 分布式 FAISS 双层本地基础设施索引 1200 万论文；设计包含 12 个工具的离线 ResearchToolbox（含语义检索、拓扑遍历与结构可视化）；通过引用图随机游走采样多跳子图、由 20 个前沿模型交叉验证合成 QA；采用 SFT 蒸馏 + 基于反向分解评分规则（规划、证据扎根、可验证答案奖励）的 IcePop 强化学习进行两阶段训练。

Result: 在六个基准（QASA、SciFact、PubMedQA、ScholarQA-CS、SSB、SciFR）上，SciLENS-30B-RL 取得最佳开源性能：QASA 47.62、SciFact 88.94、PubMedQA 77.53，引用精度全面领先；SSB 和 SciFR 上分别达到 0.7607 和 0.7594，超越所有开源基线，接近 GPT-5.2 和 Gemini-3.0-pro；消融实验验证了可视化工具、规划与扎根评分规则的必要性。

Conclusion: SciLENS 证明了融合结构可视化工具与评分规则强化学习的完全本地化智能体可实现与前沿专有模型相当的科学综述能力，消除了对脆弱在线 API 的依赖。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [264] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HypRQ-VAE是首个在双曲空间中学习物品语义索引（Semantic IDs）的生成式推荐框架，通过将RQ-VAE扩展到Poincaré球模型，利用双曲几何的指数级体积扩展特性，更好地建模推荐系统中固有的长尾分布（power-law结构），显著提升生成式推荐性能，尤其在尾部物品推荐上改进明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM驱动的生成式推荐中，存在文本token与离散物品索引之间的语义鸿沟，导致幻觉问题。现有的物品索引方法（如TIGER的RQ-VAE）均在欧几里得空间中学习，难以建模真实推荐目录中固有的长尾分布（少量头部热门物品主导，大量尾部长尾物品代表用户小众兴趣）。双曲空间的指数级体积膨胀天然契合幂律结构，但其在生成式物品索引中的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 提出HypRQ-VAE框架，包含两个阶段：1）在双曲空间中进行语义ID生成——使用DNN编码器将物品文本嵌入映射到欧几里得潜空间，再通过指数映射投影到Poincaré球上，然后在多个层级上利用Möbius减法递归进行残差量化，每层从码本中选取最近的码字，最终的码字索引序列构成语义ID；为处理索引冲突，设计了基于码本距离度量的级联重新分配策略；2）利用LLaMA2-7B作为生成式推荐器，在语义ID序列上进行自回归监督微调，将物品历史与文本语义指令结合生成下一物品。

Result: 在MovieLens、Instruments和Arts三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HypRQ-VAE一致优于MF、Caser、SASRec、P5、TIGER、LC-Rec和LETTER等基线方法。在尾部物品推荐上提升尤为显著，例如在MovieLens上尾部物品Hit@10提升52.71%。通过平均量化误差（AQE）分析证实，双曲空间下头尾部物品的表示更加均衡，无需额外正则化即在码本各层实现了均匀的token分布。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将双曲几何集成到残差量化VAE中用于生成式推荐的物品索引，证实了双曲空间对长尾物品的表征能力优于欧几里得空间，为流形感知的生成式推荐开辟了新方向。消融研究表明语义ID长度L=6、码本维度K=256为最佳配置；均匀初始化实验揭示了不同层的差异化容量需求，为未来自适应层容量分配提供了思路。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [265] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ExplainRoute提出一个面向非直接作答编程导师的部署前审计框架。系统读取代码行与学习者解释，将解释状态估计为 incorrect/partial/adequate/uncertain，并选择开放式费曼自解释提示或单一苏格拉底式问题。其核心是统一、可机器检查的响应契约，以及分组留出、独立评估和人工复核机制。冻结的 SelfCode 留出集包含 443 个样本、11 个代码组。结果显示，Open、Socratic 和 Adaptive 的契约有效性均为 100%，Adaptive 与分析用参考路由一致率为 60.5%，状态宏 F1 仅为 0.238。独立 LLM 评审中，Adaptive 为 4.516/5，显著优于 No-State 的 2.819/5，但未超过固定 Open 的 4.598 或固定 Socratic 的 4.658。40 条样本上的盲法人工评价同样支持“学习者解释具有信息价值”，且没有发现自适应路由优于固定策略。论文的主要贡献是审计协议和边界发现，而非学习效果证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程导师常能生成流畅解释，却可能直接给出答案，从而掩盖学习者的误解、削弱自我解释机会。论文希望在大规模课堂部署前，系统性审计导师可用信息、响应极性、答案泄漏、失败处置与路由决策，避免仅以语言流畅度评价导师。

Method: 系统由诊断角色与导师角色组成：诊断角色基于代码行和学习者解释输出四类状态（incorrect、partial、adequate、uncertain）及诊断；导师角色只能选择一种受限策略——开放式费曼自解释提示或单一苏格拉底式问题。统一响应结构包含 state、strategy、diagnosis、next_question、answer、cited_code 和 leakage_risk，确定性验证器检查状态/策略合法性、答案字段极性、问题数量、引用片段字面子串性质及泄漏风险。实验使用 1,770 对 SelfCode 数据，以代码行分组冻结划分：46 个代码组、1,327 对用于开发，11 个代码组、443 对作为未触碰留出集。比较 Direct、Open、Socratic、No-State 和 Adaptive 五种条件；主要端点包括契约有效性、状态宏 F1、路由一致率、代码引用率及独立评审分数，并按代码组进行 100,000 次 bootstrap。另对 93 条预设留出样本进行 465 次 LLM 评审，并对分层抽取的 40 条样本进行三名教学助手的盲评。

Result: 固定 Open、固定 Socratic 和 Adaptive 的契约有效性均为 100%；Adaptive 与固定参考规则的路由一致率为 60.5%，状态宏 F1 为 0.238，错误主要表现为将低分/低覆盖解释系统性高估为 partial 或 adequate。Adaptive 的精确代码引用率为 100%，平均延迟为 6.14 秒、需两次模型调用；其他条件平均延迟约 3.35–4.31 秒。独立 LLM 评审中，Adaptive 为 4.516/5，高于 No-State 的 2.819/5，但低于 Open 的 4.598 和 Socratic 的 4.658。40 条样本的盲法人工评价中，Adaptive 较 No-State 高 0.74 分，并与 Open、Socratic 统计相当。生成阶段没有 HTTP 失败；2,215 条导师轨迹中有 6 条因直接回答缺失 answer 字段而 fail-closed，并保留在分母中。

Conclusion: ExplainRoute 证明可以通过统一契约使非作答型编程导师在结构上安全、可审计；也表明学习者解释本身具有明显信息价值。然而，当前自适应状态估计较弱且存在系统性高估偏差，尚未证明其优于简单的固定 Open 或 Socratic 策略。因此，论文主张将该系统定位为低风险、可追溯的部署前审计与响应生成协议，而不是自主教师或学习效果证据。未来需要引入教师标注的误解数据、状态概率校准、多轮交互，以及包含学习结果的前瞻性研究。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [266] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，针对对话检索中"主题相关性"与"可回答性"之间的系统性鸿沟（answerability gap），通过蕴含蒸馏（entailment distillation）训练一个答案感知的交叉编码器重排序器，并以段落中心的溯因召回通道补充候选池，在TopiOCQA、QReCC及零样本TREC CAsT上一致提升了头部排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话检索器普遍以主题相关性（topical relevance）作为可回答性（answerability）的代理，但主题匹配的段落不一定支持正确答案。随着检索器增强，这种不匹配反而加剧——作者将此命名为"语义陷阱"（semantic trap）与"可回答性鸿沟"（answerability gap）。需要在推理阶段引入显式的可回答性判别信号，以弥补主题相似性的固有盲点。

Method: CLEAR包含两大核心组件：(1) 答案感知的交叉编码器重排序器（A-CE），由冻结的DeBERTa-v3-large NLI教师提供"答案-段落"蕴含分数，通过KL蒸馏到双头（相关性头+可回答性头）结构中，并采用基于段落相似度的自适应校准纠正蕴含倒置；(2) 段落中心的溯因召回通道，使用LoRA微调的LLaMA 3.1 8B为每段离线生成m条可回答查询，建立查询池，通过ANN检索实现"上下文-查询"对称匹配，并与密集检索经RRF融合。所有重计算离线完成，在线无需LLM。

Result: 在TopiOCQA上MRR达46.2（NDCG@3=45.6），在QReCC上MRR达69.3（NDCG@3=68.1），均显著优于QRACDR、DiSCo等强CDR基线（p<0.01），且在零样本TREC CAsT 19/20/21上MRR均位列第一。最大增益出现在主题漂移更严重的TopiOCQA上，与A-CE在ConvSearch-R1之上叠加仍可进一步提升。受控消融证实增益来源于蕴含监督而非候选池或骨干；蒸馏后的答案感知头几乎匹配"答案+段落"教师oracle，且远超教师自身的"上下文+段落"无答案设置。CLEAR在线延迟约172ms/查询，约为DiSCo的1/3、ConvSearch-R1的1/4。

Conclusion: 将对话检索从主题对齐转向可回答性对齐是可行且有效的；通过答案-段落蕴含蒸馏可获得超越主题相似性的判别信号，溯因召回则弥补了密集检索对低表面相似但可回答段落的遗漏。该方法与查询改写互补、在线无LLM依赖，且对跨域任务保持稳定。作者指出未来可采用会话QA风格的蕴含教师或多教师蒸馏，并将其推广到无答案标注的语料。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [267] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4AIGQ框架，基于大语言模型为电商场景生成面向用户多兴趣的引导查询（AI Guidance Query, AIGQ）。通过SFT-RL-DPO三阶段训练，结合多层奖励设计，在淘宝/天猫离线评测与在线A/B实验中均显著优于基线模型并已上线服务亿级用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商引导查询生成采用Q2AIGQ两阶段范式：先通过"用户-商品"共现关系多路召回主搜索查询，再规则化扩展为AIGQ。该方法存在两大缺陷：(1) 信息级联导致语义漂移；(2) 过度依赖共现信号、缺乏对用户多兴趣的建模，导致生成的引导查询指导价值低、与购买意图不匹配。核心需解决：噪声交互下的多兴趣建模、无显式真值的有效监督、在线推理的低延迟要求。

Method: 框架包含四部分：(1) 数据预处理——用Qwen3-30B-A3B将商品长标题蒸馏为短标题（教师为DeepSeek-V4-Pro），压缩输入序列；(2) SFT阶段——采用教师模型对用户历史序列做兴趣切分，在单兴趣子序列上微调Qwen3-30B-A3B学习AIGQ风格；(3) RL阶段——采用GRPO算法，设计四层奖励：思考级（兴趣匹配+格式）、查询级（长度+价值）、兴趣级（语义+多样性+格式）、全局级（跨兴趣多样性）；(4) DPO阶段——将RL模型的think-mode最终答案作为正样本、非think-mode输出作为负样本做偏好对齐，并过滤语义相似度过高的样本对以提升训练效率。部署采用nearline生成+online读取架构。

Result: 在淘宝数据集上，LLM4AIGQ-ONE_SFT+RL+DPO的Recall@10达0.2101、S比率达0.8616，显著超越Qwen3-235B-A22B、DeepSeek-V4-Pro等零样本基线；三阶段渐进提升明显（A/B成对评估中RL后胜率57.53%超过教师模型）。消融实验验证了短标题压缩（RT降低10.7%）、四层奖励各组件及DPO偏好对过滤均必要。在线A/B测试在天猫多个场景中uCTR提升2.53%~4.46%。

Conclusion: LLM4AIGQ通过LLM驱动的多兴趣挖掘与三阶段SFT-RL-DPO训练管线，有效解决了传统引导查询生成的语义漂移与个性化不足问题，并已在淘宝/天猫上线服务数亿用户，验证了在工业级电商场景的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [268] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UPG（Unified Pitch Graphs）是一种用于棒球投球策略回顾性分析的分层图表示方法。它保留了每颗球的精确事件（含三维轨迹重建）与上下文，通过有向序列边连接相邻球，并在语义和时间多分辨率上组织事件。支持自适应回退机制在重复证据不足时退回到更粗的序列分辨率，同时保留精确事件谱系。基于2021–2026年共394万颗MLB Statcast投球数据的评估表明：名义上相同的投球序列在物理执行上存在显著差异；支持自适应回退将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，并将执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685；UPG能定位离散表示无法检测的受控执行变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投球分析表示方法存在两大局限：基于离散球种类型或聚合统计的方法会合并物理执行截然不同的投球；而结合轨迹、位置、上下文与更长历史序列的精细表示又会因数据稀疏而失去统计支持。需要一种能同时保持物理保真度、事件可追溯性以及多尺度模式统计可靠性的表示框架。

Method: UPG构建一个包含三类节点（精确事件节点V^evt、语义索引节点V^sem、时间范围节点V^time）和三类边（序列边E^seq、语义边E^sem、时间边E^time）的分层属性图。每个精确事件保留标称球种、连续物理执行和投球前上下文，连续三维轨迹由运动学测量重建并按固定采样点表示。轨迹变化在每种球型内通过标准化和PCA投影到第一主方向z_i，按历史参考分布的三分位数划分为T⁻/T⁰/T⁺三个轨迹层。支持自适应变阶路径选择：对长度为m的路径按(轨迹细化, 球种)的优先级尝试，满足最小出现次数n_min和最小独立比赛数g_min时即选定，否则回退到更短后缀；球种作为最终回退。诊断报告分为三层证据强度：结果关联的诊断证据、反复出现的策略模式（无强效果声明）、事件级示例。

Result: 在300名2025年工作量最大的投手评估中：(1) 相同SGT离散序列单元下存在物理执行截然不同的实例；(2) 三球和四球路径以及上下文条件路径在打乱顺序后复发率显著下降，说明顺序结构本身承载信息；(3) 支持自适应变阶回退将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升到94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升到0.685；(4) 移除最高支持比赛后，多数高频策略模式仍保留前十成员资格和方向一致性，2026*部分赛季则转为保守的"有限证据"；(5) 在固定球种/区域/计数的受控执行变化检测中，UPG接近完美判别而离散表示接近随机水平；(6) 案例研究展示了Yamamoto vs Misiorowski的跨球员比较、Ohtani 2023–2026*的纵向重组、Yamamoto对左右打者的上下文适应，以及多球员层级化风格签名表。

Conclusion: UPG提供了一种可追溯、多尺度的投球策略诊断表示，证明了将连续执行、有序结构、时间范围、支持感知聚合和精确事件谱系整合到统一图结构中的可行性。该框架明确定位为回顾性观察诊断工具，不声称因果推断或未来表现预测，对其他具有连续观测与离散重复结构相结合的序列事件领域也具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [269] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRASP是首个面向"无标签多主题"科学短答题的自动评分流水线，通过图增强检索（GRAG）在主题紧密聚类时显著优于传统RAG，并在评估中分离了检索质量与评分质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评分系统（AES）假设每篇短答只对应单一主题，无法处理多主题合并、无结构标记的作答；基于平面索引的RAG在参考节点语义相近时容易返回近似重复，导致多主题检索失败。

Method: 两阶段流水线：离线阶段基于SciEntsBank构建79个参考节点、SBERT+FAISS索引与余弦相似度图（边权≥0.42，强边≥0.70），并用GPT-4.1-mini预生成问题复述缓存；在线阶段对每篇作文依次执行（1）混合主题检测（句数启发式+LLM歧义消解）；（2）按句分段；（3）RAG/GRAG检索k=n+2候选节点，GRAG以Top-3为种子沿强边一跳扩展并用score=0.8·cos+0.2·max_edge加权打分；（4）用匈牙利算法将问题段落一对一分配到候选参考节点；（5）由GPT-4.1-mini在温度0.7下重复11次独立打{1,2}分段分，加总为总分，并设置TRUE Oracle上界。

Result: 在n=1–4各160篇合成标签自由短答上的实验显示：GRAG在所有n上N-Hit均高于RAG（n=1: 88.8% vs 80.0%；n=2: 71.2% vs 55.0%；n=3: 62.5% vs 25.6%；n=4: 59.4% vs 38.1%），假阳性率更低（GRAG 10.5–15.8% vs RAG 17.7–30.1%）；按n报告的QWK在n=1–3均提升（n=3: 0.23 vs 0.15），n=4时所有方法包括Oracle（0.50）均显著下降，揭示了LLM评分器自身存在天花板；混合主题检测准确率90.8%；作者还指出按n报告可避免Simpson悖论下总体QWK虚高的现象。

Conclusion: GRASP证明了在多主题紧密聚类场景下，图结构作为补充信号能比纯嵌入余弦检索更可靠地恢复全部参考节点；检索质量与评分质量可被分离评估，n=4的高难度场景仍为开放问题；未来工作包括在真实学生作答（包括跨句混合、乱序作答）上验证、采用更强LLM评分器、以及探索共现图、课程图等替代图构建方式。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [270] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WebKurator.de 是一个面向德语网络的协作式策展平台，通过二维模型（主题与地理）解耦传统目录的单维层次结构；利用基于LLM的主题分类与从Imprint页面提取地址并地理编码，结合用户建议与人工审核，从包含540万网站的德国Imprints数据集中筛选出258万同时具备主题标签与德国地理位置的网站作为初始数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家级图书馆（如德国国家图书馆DNB）承担系统化保存具有文化与地域相关性网络内容的法定义务，但现有的目录式策展方法（如Curlie）将主题、地域与语言纠缠在单一层次结构中，难以支持"地点+主题"组合查询，且完全依赖人工策展难以扩展。作者旨在解决这两个结构性缺陷。

Method: 1) 提出二维策展模型，将主题分类与地理标注解耦；2) 使用Open Web Crawler (OWLer)从Open Web Index、Common Crawl与Curlie获取种子URL，识别含/imprint或/impressum的页面并下载；3) 采用本地部署的Qwen3-Next-80B LLM执行主题分类（基于FourSquare分类法子集）与地址提取；4) 使用本地Photon（基于OSM与OpenSearch）进行地理编码；5) 平台支持模糊检索、多粒度行政区域筛选、相似网站推荐以及"用户建议→自动注释→人工审核"的协作工作流。

Result: 数据集包含5,543,968个网站，其中3,226,331个包含imprint页面，成功提取地址的占3,225,347，分配地理坐标的为3,142,142；85.57%位于德国（其次为奥地利7.25%、瑞士5.19%）；其中2,580,000（85.17%）同时具有主题标签并位于德国，形成WebKurator.de的初始规模；分类分布上以"商业和专业服务"为主导（41.60%），与传统Curlie的均衡分布差异显著，反映了imprint驱动采集对法人注册实体的偏向。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de证明了通过二维策展模型与LLM辅助元数据生成结合人工审核，可有效实现大规模、地区+主题组合的网络策展；其架构为德国国家图书馆的选择性网络归档基础设施现代化提供了原型，并具备扩展至奥地利、瑞士等德语区的基础；当前聚焦于德语活网，未来计划与选择性网络归档工作流进一步集成。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present this http URL , a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of this http URL and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Learning Multiband Signals and Fourier-sparse Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02977)
*Dongrun Cai,Xue Chen,Xiaowei Shao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间多带信号和k-傅里叶稀疏信号的高效学习算法。在多带信号方面，提出了首个无需先验知识即可恢复频带位置的高效算法；在傅里叶稀疏信号方面，通过建立多带近似的新联系，将样本复杂度从Õ(k³)改进到Õ(k²)，时间复杂度从指数级改进到Õ(k⁵)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法存在两大局限：1）多带信号的重构算法需要预先知道频带位置I₁∪⋯∪Iₙ；2）傅里叶稀疏信号的高效算法样本复杂度停留在Õ(k³)，与统计上界Õ(k²)存在巨大差距。这两个问题都是连续信号处理中的基础性开放问题。

Method: 核心方法包含三个关键技术：1）设计HashToBins和GetSamples两个程序（基于滤波器对(G,Ĝ)），通过哈希将频带映射到B个桶中，实现频带位置的恢复；2）构造滤波器对(M_{ℓ,r,δ}, M̂_{ℓ,r,δ})，证明两个傅里叶稀疏信号在频域充分分离时几乎正交（Lemma 5.1）；3）通过将k个频率分簇（Algorithm 2 PartitionClusters），将每个簇近似为一个多带信号，构建总长度Õ(k²/ε)的多带近似。

Result: 主要结果包括：定理1.1：多带信号学习算法，样本复杂度Õ(n+Σ|I_i|)，时间复杂度Õ((nR)^ω+n²R³)；定理1.3：k-傅里叶稀疏信号学习算法，样本复杂度Õ(k²)，时间复杂度Õ(k⁵)；推论1.2：在不满足条件(1)时，通过参数κ和τ扩展应用范围。关键技术Lemma 5.1给出频域分离的紧致刻画：分离度Ω(ℓr/δ·log(ℓr/δ))即可保证相关性≤δ。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多带信号与傅里叶稀疏信号之间的新联系，突破了传统频率估计框架的Õ(k²)瓶颈（来自[11]）。多带近似技术可应用于一般简单频谱信号，提供了学习连续信号的通用新方法。遗留开放问题包括：1）是否存在满足条件(1)之外的更一般条件下的高效插值算法；2）多带近似尺寸Õ(k²)的紧致性；3）实际意义下进一步改进时间复杂度。

Abstract: We consider efficient algorithms to learn multiband signals and Fourier-sparse signals. A mutliband signal has a Fourier transform supported by a bounded number of intervals, say $I_1 \cup I_2 \cdots \cup I_n$. There is a long line of research on multiband signals. In particular, Avron et al. showed an efficient reconstructing algorithm whose sample complexity is almost optimal. However, all previous algorithms for multiband signals consider the reconstructing problem in which the locations of $I_1,\ldots,I_n$ are given as a priori knowledge. On the other hand, although the problem of learning Fourier-sparse signals with $k$ arbitrary frequencies dates at least to Prony in 1795, designing efficient and robust learning algorithms is still an open problem. The state-of-the-art is an efficient algorithm of $\tilde{O}(k^3)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(k^{3 \omega})$ time from the very recent work by Cai et al., while the statistical upper bound is $\tilde{O}(k^2)$ samples. Let $[-1,1]$ be the time window in which the noise is $\ell_2$ bounded. 1. We show an efficient algorithm to recover the locations of the bands $I_1,\ldots,I_n$ in $\hat{x}$ within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ time. Furthermore, combining this with the reconstructing algorithm by Avron et al. provides an efficient interpolation algorithm within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples. 2. We show that every $k$-Fourier-sparse signal $x$ admits a multiband approximation $z$ whose Fourier transform is of support size $|\mathrm{supp}(\hat{z})|=\tilde{O}(k^2)$. Furthermore, we show an interpolation algorithm for $k$-Fourier-sparse signals with $\tilde{O}(k^2)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(k^5)$ time.

</details>


### [272] [SparseStack Is an Optimal Oblivious Subspace Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02978)
*Diar Heidary*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 证明完全独立的 SparseStack（s个独立 CountSketch 垂直堆叠）确实是参数最优的 oblivious 子空间嵌入（OSE），达到 Nelson-Nguyen (FOCS 2013) 猜想的目标：m=O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²) 行、s=O(log(d/δ)/ε) 每列非零元，并给出 Lean 4 形式化验证。证明使用条件期望耦合将 one-hot 哈希替换为三点独立分布、张量积算子模型（Jacobi 矩阵的腿分解）、按"级"（grade）分带估计以及矩方法和 Markov 不等式，避免了高斯比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2013 年 Nelson 和 Nguyen 提出 SparseStack/OSNAP 构造，并猜想其行数 m=O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²) 和稀疏度 s=O(log(d/δ)/ε) 均足够（行数下界已被证明）。该猜想长期未完全解决，本文旨在补全这一空白。

Method: 1) 用条件期望耦合将相关 one-hot 哈希列选择子替换为三点独立分布 ζ∈{0,±1}，代价为每阶常数 c_b⁴≤c_⋆⁴；2) 在有限张量积 ℝ^d⊗(ℝ³)^{⊗mn} 上建立"真空-矩恒等式"，把 E[iid] 的 k 阶矩转化为确定性算子 ℳ^k 的真空矩阵元；3) 将 ℳ 分解为提升/保持/降低 grade 三带 B⁺/B⁰/B⁻；4) 用"共享因子不等式"（Lemma 4.1）证明在 ℓ 个轻位点共享外部因子 ℝ^d 时 Gram 算子范数 ≤ d+ℓ−1；5) 用 Cauchy-Schwarz 分块估计每带在 ν 阶子空间的范数 ≤ C_band(√((d+ν+1)/m)+(d+ν+1)/m+(ν+1)/s)；6) 通过 3^{2q} 字展开、Moments、Markov 不等式得到最终概率界。该证明完全是有限维的、不依赖高斯比较。

Result: 定理 1.1：固定 U∈ℝ^{n×d}（U^T U=I_d），取 s=⌈Λ(2q+1)/ε⌉、m=sb、b=⌈M_0/s⌉、M_0=Λ²D_q/ε²、q=max{1,⌈log_2(d/δ)⌉}，则 P{‖U^T Π^T ΠU−I_d‖>ε}≤δ。常数显式：Λ<226，s<1356q/ε，m<306456(d+q)/ε²。证明已在 Lean 4 中形式化。

Conclusion: 完全独立 SparseStack 分布的最优参数 OSE 猜想被证实——行数 m=O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²) 是最优的（由 Nelson-Nguyen 下界），稀疏度 s=O(log(d/δ)/ε) 也是足够的，给出了显式常数；新方法是基于 Jacobi 矩阵张量积模型的"有限 Fock 空间"矩方法，避免高斯比较并获得 Lean 4 机器验证。

Abstract: The fully independent SparseStack sketch is a vertical stack of $s$ independent CountSketch matrices, scaled by $s^{-1/2}$, so that every column has exactly $s$ nonzero entries. We prove that it is an oblivious subspace embedding for $d$-dimensional subspaces with distortion $\epsilon$ and failure probability $\delta$ when $m = O((d+\log(1/\delta))/\epsilon^2)$ and $s = O(\log(d/\delta)/\epsilon)$, with explicit constants. These are the parameters conjectured by Nelson and Nguyen (FOCS 2013) for this construction; the row count is optimal by their lower bound. The proof bounds the even moments of the Gram error. A conditional-expectation coupling replaces each signed one-hot column selector by a vector with independent three-point entries, at the cost of a constant factor per moment order. The three-point law has a three-dimensional $L_2$ space, so multiplication by an entry is a $3 \times 3$ Jacobi matrix, and the $2q$-th moment becomes a vacuum matrix element of a deterministic operator on a finite tensor product, graded by total occupation. The operator has three grade bands, and we bound each band on the grade-$\nu$ sector by $C(\sqrt{(d+\nu+1)/m} + (d+\nu+1)/m + (\nu+1)/s)$ with $C = 3+\sqrt{2}$. The key step is a shared-factor inequality: each row block of the positive operator attached to one tensor slot is rank one with trace $d$, and the sum over $\ell$ slots sharing the same external factor has norm at most $d+\ell-1$. A $2q$-step expansion and Markov's inequality complete the argument, which is finite-dimensional and does not use Gaussian comparison. The theorem has been formally verified in Lean 4. The proof was developed with AI systems under the author's direction, as disclosed in the paper.

</details>


### [273] [Assortment and Procurement Design in Dual-Mode Content Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03285)
*Garud Iyengar,Yuanzhe Ma,Jay Sethuraman*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究数字内容平台在同时提供广告支持模式与订阅模式时的品类组合（assortment）与采购设计联合优化问题。用户在内容偏好与广告容忍度上存在异质性，并在两种模式与外部选项之间自选；平台在给定统一订阅价格与广告量的前提下，为每类用户在每种模式下选择品类组合分布，并决定每个内容家族是购买还是租赁。作者证明该问题在 κ=1 时已是 NP-难问题，随后提出一套基于候选购买集合、采购耦合松弛以及单等式约束线性规划分解的可扩展近似框架：通过对偶二分搜索结合基数受限的 MNL 品类优化求解子问题，再通过限制主问题后处理恢复原始可行性。该方法具有可计算的最优性差距界、可解释的阈值型采购启发式，并在市场规模与网格分辨率成比例放大的渐近意义下最优。数值实验显示在中等问题规模与网格尺寸下表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中领先平台（如 Bilibili、Spotify）的内容成本同时包含固定许可/自制摊销费用与基于使用情况的支付（收入分成/版税），并同时运营广告支持与订阅两种盈利模式以服务异质性消费者。现有研究多分别考虑两端定价或单模式品类优化，但采购决策（买 vs 租）与个性化品类设计之间存在不可分割的耦合关系：内容家族被纳入多少用户专属品类取决于其采购方式，反之亦然。因此需要一个能同时刻画品类设计、用户自选与买租采购三者联合内生性的统一利润最大化框架。

Method: 方法包含四层分解：(1) 固定候选购买集合 S，将租赁/购买决策与品类决策分离；(2) 对购买成本中的 max 型耦合用权重 β∈𝓑_S 进行松弛，得到跨用户类型可加性分解的目标 G(S,β,y)；(3) 引入广告侧比例 t=x^(a,j)/u^(a,j)，将内生模式选择与品类分布解耦，并通过引理 4.1 将订阅效用归约到 {0,p} 两个值，从而把每个 (j,t) 子问题拆为广告与订阅两个带单等式约束的线性规划；(4) 用对偶二分搜索结合 Rusmevichientong et al. (2010) 的基数受限 MNL 品类算法求解子问题，最后通过限制主问题后处理恢复原始可行性、产生可计算的最优性差距证书。

Result: 主要理论结果：(a) 当 κ=1 时通过从带奖励的分数集覆盖问题（prize-collecting fractional set cover）归约证明 NP-难（定理 3.1）；(b) 算法总体求解器对任意固定 (S,β,K_T) 返回目标值的可计算差距界，包含三项可解释分量：购买集合差距、β-松弛差距、网格离散化误差（定理 4.3），运行时间为 Õ(J K_T L²)；(c) 在市场规模按 N 比例放大的渐近体制下，相对误差以 O(1/(K_T-1)+1/N) 趋于零（定理 4.4），且得到封闭形式的阈值采购规则：内容家族 ℓ 被购买当且仅当 2η_ℓ ≤ γ_ℓ Σ_j λ^(j)。数值实验（J=5,L=10,κ=3）显示在 K_T≥33、N=100 时相对 MIP 全局最优的差距 <1%，并验证混合采购策略在多种场景下严格优于纯购买与纯租赁。

Conclusion: 本文针对双模式数字内容平台提出了一个统一的品类与采购联合优化框架，证明了该问题在 κ=1 时 NP-难，并开发了一套基于候选购买集合、采购耦合 β-松弛、广告侧比例参数化与对偶二分搜索的可扩展近似算法。该方法具有可计算且可分解的最优性差距证书（购买集合项、松弛项、网格项），在市场规模渐近放大意义下达到渐近最优；阈值型采购规则 η_ℓ/γ_ℓ ≤ (N/2) Σ_j λ^(j) 给出可解释的管理洞见，且混合采购相对纯购买/纯租赁始终不劣。数值实验证实算法在中等规模与网格下表现强劲。未来工作包括将广告量 σ 与订阅价格 p 内生化、动态学习用户偏好与广告容忍度、引入竞争性平台设置以及将 MNL 选择模型推广到其他正则离散选择模型。

Abstract: We study assortment and procurement design for a digital content platform offering both ad-supported and subscription access. Users are heterogeneous in content preferences and ad tolerance and self-select between the two modes or an outside option. For a fixed common subscription price and ad load, the platform chooses assortment distributions specific to each user type and access mode, together with content-family-level buy-versus-rent decisions to maximize profit. Rental costs scale with realized consumption, whereas buying provides a reusable pool of titles whose cost depends on the largest induced requirement across user types and modes. We show that the resulting problem is NP-hard. We then develop a scalable approximation framework based on a candidate buy set, a relaxation of the procurement coupling, and a decomposition into linear programs with a single equality constraint. These subproblems are solved by dual bisection with cardinality-constrained assortment optimization, followed by restricted-master postprocessing to recover primal feasibility. The method yields computable optimality-gap bounds, an interpretable threshold-based procurement heuristic, and asymptotic optimality under proportional market scaling as market size and grid resolution increase. Numerical experiments show strong performance at moderate market scales and grid sizes.

</details>


### [274] [Improved algorithm for counting spanning trees by $\ell_1$-regularized resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03574)
*Rong-Hua Li,Yichun Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ℓ₁正则化电阻（ℓ₁-regularized resistance）的新概念，用于改进图的生成树计数近似算法，将运行时间提升至Õ(m+n^{7/4}ε^{-3/2})，优于FOCS 2018的Õ(m+n^{15/8}ε^{-7/4})和FOCS 2026的Õ(m^{1.5}ε^{-1})（当m≥n^{7/6}时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似计算图的生成树数量是一个基本问题，传统方法的核心瓶颈在于有效电阻（effective resistance）的计算。已有算法如Chu等人（FOCS 2018）和Liu等人（FOCS 2026）虽取得进展，但在稠密图上的运行时间仍不够理想，需要新的电阻估计方法来突破现有瓶颈。

Method: 1. **ℓ₁正则化电阻定义**：对每个源s，定义ℓ₁正则化势z_s*为凸优化问题argmin_{z≥0}{½z^T L z - e_s^T z + λ·1^T z}的解，其中λ∈(1/n,1/2)。KKT条件保证支集大小|supp(z_s*)|≤λ^{-1}，具有局部性。2. **高效算法**：设计基于活动集扩张的算法（Algorithm 1），结合近似线性方程求解器（SDDM solver）和隐式边界预言机（implicit boundary oracle），实现Õ(m+nλ^{-3})的预处理时间，并支持O(1)时间查询。3. **聚合误差分析**：证明ℓ₁正则化电阻的聚合Pearson误差为O(λn)，满足行列式稀疏化框架的需求。4. **行列式稀疏化**：加强Durfee等人（FOCS 2017）的方差分析，将行列式稀疏化构造与Schur补结合，使用聚合误差保证实现完整的生成树计数。5. **参数平衡**：设置λ=n^{-1/4}ε^{1/2}，平衡各部分运行时间。

Result: 对于n个顶点、m条边的连通加权图，算法在Õ(m+n^{7/4}ε^{-3/2})时间内返回ε-近似生成树数，成功概率为2/3。当m≥n^{7/6}时，结果优于FOCS 2018的Õ(m+n^{15/8}ε^{-7/4})和FOCS 2026的Õ(m^{1.5}ε^{-1})。常数ε下的运行时间为Õ(m+n^{7/4})。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入ℓ₁正则化电阻这一新概念，为生成树计数问题提供了更高效的近似算法。ℓ₁正则化电阻的支集大小与n无关，使得势函数可局部计算，结合行列式稀疏化框架实现了运行时间的改进。该方法不仅解决了近似计数生成树的问题，ℓ₁正则化电阻这一概念本身也具有独立的研究价值。注：论文附带AI声明，详细证明由GPT-5.6 Sol生成，但高层思路和证明结构由作者设计并经作者验证。

Abstract: We study the basic problem of approximating the number of spanning trees of a graph. We propose an algorithm that approximates the number of spanning trees in $\widetilde O(m+n^{7/4}\eps^{-3/2})$ time on a graph with $n$ vertices and $m$ edges. Our algorithm improves upon the previously best known $\widetilde O(m+n^{15/8}\eps^{-7/4})$ time algorithm by Chu, Gao, Peng, Sachdeva, Sawlani, and Wang [FOCS 2018] and the $\widetilde O(m^{1.5}\eps^{-1})$ time algorithm by Liu, Peng and Yang [FOCS 2026] when $m\ge n^{7/6}$. Notably, our algorithm is based on the novel concept of $\ell_1$-regularized resistance. We propose simple and efficient algorithms for computing $\ell_1$-regularized resistance and we show that they can be used to approximate the number of spanning trees by combining with the determinant sparsifier framework of Durfee, Peebles, Peng, and Rao [FOCS 2017].

</details>


### [275] [A PTAS for Non-Adaptive Stochastic Top-$k$ Sum under General Combinatorial Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03685)
*Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般组合约束下非自适应随机Top-k和选择问题，针对固定维非负背包族，给出对任意k≥1的多项式时间近似方案(PTAS)，并证明该方案在背包设置下本质最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Top-k和最大化在团队组建、ℓ-unit拍卖VCG福利等场景中具有重要应用。当k=1时已有PTAS结果，但一般k≥1情形在一般可行性约束下的PTAS尚属空白。同时需要在近似保证的最优性边界（区分PTAS与FPTAS/EPTAS）以及实现PTAS的不同充分条件之间建立系统分类。

Method: 方法建立在四层阶梯上：(1) 通过递减盈余搜索从α-近似max-sum oracle得到常数因子比α/((1+α)(1+ε))；(2) 对k=O(1/ε²)用占据直方图签名，对k=Ω(1/ε²)用三维混合分位类型签名；(3) 在固定d维背包族中通过背包LP的基本可行解近似实现签名；(4) 通过稀疏彩票归约证明硬度下界。关键技术包括均值保持折叠、Le Cam逼近、Poisson-binomial截断、Chernoff饱和。

Result: 主要结果：Theorem 4.8证明固定d维背包族对任意k≥1存在PTAS，运行时间n^{f(d,1/ε)}；Theorem 13.1证明背包族上不存在通用FPTAS（除非P=NP）也不存在通用EPTAS（除非W[1]=FPT），以二维背包为见证；Theorem 4.6证明无单一常数因子算法适用于所有成员族（独立集为见证）；Theorem 4.7证明常数因子max-sum不足以保证PTAS（割集为见证）；Corollary 4.12证明查询权重精确和是第二个不可比于背包的PTAS充分条件。

Conclusion: 固定d维背包族是接受PTAS的合适分类标准，但并非唯一——查询权重精确和（如DAG路径、匹配）是第二个不可比的PTAS边界。PTAS在背包设置下本质最优。开放问题：哪些具体族必须不接受常数因子算法或PTAS？哪些精确和族在背包之外接受EPTAS或FPTAS？

Abstract: We study non-adaptive selection of a feasible set $S$ so as to maximize the expected sum of the $k$ largest realized values among independent nonnegative discrete random variables. The same objective arises when hiring a team of $k$ workers or when computing VCG welfare in an $\ell$-unit auction. The main setting is a fixed-dimensional nonnegative packing family: the natural LP has $d=O(1)$ packing inequalities with binary coefficients. No single algorithm achieves a constant factor on every membership family (already at $k=1$). Given an $\alpha$-approximate max-sum oracle, a decreasing surplus search yields ratio $\alpha/((1+\alpha)(1+\eps))$ for every $k\ge 1$ (cuts included). Every fixed-$d$ packing family already has a deterministic max-sum PTAS, hence inherits that constant. The same signatures that drive the exact-sum scheme---occupancy histograms when $k=O(1/\eps^2)$, and a three-dimensional mixture-quantile type when $k=\Omega(1/\eps^2)$---are realized by a packing LP rather than by exact-sum, after enumerating $n^{f(d,1/\eps)}$ heavy items. The result is a PTAS for every $k\ge 1$ on every fixed-$d$ packing family, including binary one- and two-dimensional knapsack. In this packing setting the scheme is essentially optimal as a generic guarantee: there is no FPTAS that works for every such $\F$ unless $P=NP$, and no EPTAS unless $W[1]=FPT$ (two-dimensional knapsack is a witness, already at $k=1$). A separate boundary is query-weight exact-sum, which includes DAG paths and matchings and is incomparable with fixed-$d$ packing. That oracle also yields a PTAS for every $k$, so $d$-dimensional packing is a useful taxonomy, not a partition of every family that admits a PTAS.

</details>


### [276] [The 11/6 supremum of the Wang-Sitters rounding scheme for graph balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03890)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper rigorously characterizes the worst-case approximation ratio of the Wang-Sitters rounding scheme for graph balancing. It proves that 11/6 is the exact supremum (never attained) of the scheme's ratio against the optimum, and determines the complete guarantee function across all big-job thresholds β∈(0,1). The unique optimal threshold is β=2/3, the guarantee is exactly max{3/2+β/2, 5/2-β} on (1/2,1), and exactly 3/2+(1-β)⌊1/β⌋ on (0,1/2]—which is unbounded as β→0 and jumps at β=1/2.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Wang-Sitters algorithm has two underspecified steps: Step 1 (LP solver returns "a" feasible solution) and Step 3 (slot matching returns "any" valid matching). The original 11/6 bound holds for all such runs, but the authors ask: what is the worst-case ratio over all permitted executions, and what threshold β optimizes this worst case? The paper measures the "freedom cost" in the rounding scheme and provides exact answers.

Method: The method combines explicit construction of extremal instances with rigorous proof. For the upper half β∈(1/2,1): case analysis into three scenarios (no Step-2 job with small slot-1 occupant; no Step-2 job with big slot-1 occupant; Step-2 job present) yields the ceiling g(β)=max{3/2+β/2, 5/2-β}. Matching lower bounds come from parameterizing Schwartz-Yeheskel's Figure 4 instances by β as affine functions. For the lower half β≤1/2: a "nested collector" family (one machine accumulates ⌊1/β⌋ big jobs, then a rider+filler exploit remaining budget) gives the ceiling 3/2+(1-β)⌊1/β⌋. A key Lemma 18 parameterizes this family by (α,m), enabling instantiation at every threshold at once. Strictness arguments use the half-open slot convention and w_{K_i}>0.

Result: Main results: (1) The 11/6 supremum is never attained—Theorem 2 constructs 3-job families with ratios 11/6−δ, while Proposition 5 proves strict inequality <11/6·T for all executions. (2) Above β=1/2, the guarantee equals g(β)=max{3/2+β/2, 5/2−β} exactly (Corollary 17). (3) Below β=1/2, the guarantee equals 3/2+(1-β)⌊1/β⌋ exactly (Theorem 25), unbounded as β→0, with jumps at reciprocal thresholds 1/k. (4) β=2/3 is the unique minimizer across all of (0,1), and the guarantee jumps at β=1/2 (from 5/2 at β=1/2 to limit 2 as β↓1/2). The paper also clarifies that vertex-pinning Step 1 or optimizing Step 3 cannot fix the 7/4 gap—those results are in the companion note [1]. The paper does not improve graph balancing's 1.75 approximation ratio.

Conclusion: The Wang-Sitters scheme's 11/6 bound is tight in the precise sense: it is the supremum over all permitted executions, and no execution attains it. The complete worst-case guarantee as a function of β is established: V-shaped above 1/2 with minimum 11/6 at β=2/3, and piecewise-affine below 1/2 growing unboundedly as β→0. The two regimes are discontinuous at β=1/2. The result is a complete characterization of what the scheme's freedom costs at every threshold, with implications for understanding why β=2/3 is uniquely optimal and why the 7/4 improvement cannot come from matching-step optimization.
