By bridging the gap between local workspace and remote resources, JuPyLive offers a seamless experience for scaling local resource-intensive workflows with minimal user intervention, thus further democratizing the usage of HPC clusters among the interdisciplinary researchers.

</details>


### [304] [Every Kernel Is a Join: Automatic Multi-GPU Parallelism for AI Computations in Einsummable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03905)
*Zhimin Ding,Chen-Kuan Liao,Chima Adiole,Brianna Barrow,Fangzhou Du,Yu Hsiao,Ge Huang,Yicheng Jin,Ismail Syed,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Einsummable是一个自动多GPU并行化系统，通过将每个AI操作建模为张量关系上的关系连接-聚合（join-aggregation），自动将PyTorch风格的AI计算分布到多GPU服务器上，无需程序员编写设备分配、分片注解或通信操作。其通过join-agg specs枚举分解方案，通过动态规划优化器选择通信成本最低的方案，并通过拓扑感知的exchange programs合成专用通信模式，完全替代NCCL等固定集合通信原语。在8卡A100上运行LLaMA transformer block时，几何平均运行时间为8.97ms，优于PyTorch的13.65ms和vLLM的14.87ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU服务器上分布AI计算是AI系统的核心问题，但手工分配设备、分片注解和通信操作既繁琐又容易出错。现有自动并行化系统（如FlexFlow、GSPMD、Alpa）只能从固定命名策略菜单中选择，限制了其发现新颖分解方案的能力（如3D矩阵乘法、DeepSpeed-Ulysses等）。需要一个能自动探索整个分解空间并针对硬件拓扑合成最优通信的系统。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1)每个抽象操作符导出"join-agg specs"，描述其可能的张量关系分解方式，产生恰好p个函数规范；(2)使用动态规划算法Opt(Op, T)选择整个计算的分解，最小化通信成本的代理函数（仅计算浮点数传输量），Cost = 重分区成本 + 连接成本 + 聚合成本；(3)使用ExProg（交换程序）域特定语言，通过硬件拓扑模拟器指导，合成每个分解操作的专用通信和聚合模式（多播、relay、聚合推下拉、源替换等转换），编译为CUDA图执行。系统自动恢复经典3D矩阵乘法算法和DeepSpeed-Ulysses方案。

Result: 实验结果（NVIDIA DGX A100，8卡40GB，FP16）：(1)Transformer工作负载上几何平均8.97ms vs PyTorch 13.65ms vs vLLM 14.87ms vs JAX相近；128K单序列推理143ms vs PyTorch 506ms（快3.5倍）；(2)矩阵链A-E上与JAX和PyTorch基本持平（6%以内），都达到约6倍加速；(3)随机计划选择对transformer致命（中位数22.8ms，比优化器差2.5倍），贪心选择在小数差异内；(4)在DGX V100混合cube-mesh拓扑上优化exchange program比naive快5.6%（transformer），1.41倍和1.32倍（链A和B）；(5)通信成本代理与实测运行时间的Pearson相关性为0.47-0.92（10个设置中8个高于0.72）。

Conclusion: Einsummable证明了将AI计算的每个操作建模为张量关系上的关系连接-聚合，配合cost-based逻辑优化和拓扑感知的物理优化（exchange programs合成），可以在多GPU服务器上实现优于手工调优PyTorch/vLLM的自动并行化。核心贡献：(1)张量关系上的关系框架统一数据/张量/序列等并行策略；(2)join-agg specs机制让操作符暴露分解；(3)基于动态规划的逻辑优化器；(4)exchange programs替代固定集合通信库。系统能自动发现人工设计的方案（3D matmul、DeepSpeed-Ulysses），代表数据库理念（Volcano exchange operator、cost-based optimization）在AI工作负载中的回归。

Abstract: Distributing an AI computation across the GPUs of a multi-GPU server is one of the central problems in systems-for-AI. We present Einsummable, a prototype system that accepts a PyTorch-like description of an AI computation and automatically distributes it across a multi-GPU server, with no device assignments, sharding annotations, or communication operations written by the programmer. Einsummable models every operation as a relational join followed by an aggregation over tensor relations, in which the tuples contain sub-tensors. Each operation exposes its possible decompositions through what we call "join-agg specs". An optimizer then selects decompositions across the whole computation to minimize a communication-cost proxy. Because it searches decompositions rather than a menu of named strategies, Einsummable discovers plans that mesh-based auto-parallelizers cannot. Each decomposed operation is implemented by synthesizing an exchange program, which is a topology-aware generalization of Volcano's exchange operator. Einsummable invokes no canned collectives: all communication and aggregation is special-purpose, derived at compile time. Despite being fully automatic, Einsummable can outperform custom-designed implementations. For example, on LLaMA transformer blocks on an eight-GPU A100 server, Einsummable achieves a geometric-mean runtime of 8.97 ms, versus 13.80 ms for hand-tuned PyTorch and 15.90 ms for vLLM.

</details>


### [305] [Barnacle: Adaptive Multi-Leader Scheduling for DAG-Based Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03978)
*Zeno De Angeli,Alexandru Ianov Vitanov,Philipp Jovanovic,Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino,Pasindu Tennage,Igor Zablotchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Barnacle 是一种针对基于 DAG 的拜占庭共识协议的运行时自适应多领导者调度插件，通过监测已提交 DAG 上的直接决策率，采用类似 TCP 拥塞控制的 AIMD（加性增/乘性减）策略动态调整每轮领导者数量，在无需额外消息或密码学操作的前提下，在健康网络中实现比单领导者低 6–13% 的延迟，在网络退化时与单领导者持平并显著低于高静态配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 DAG 的共识协议（如 Mysticeti）理论上支持多领导者配置以降低排队延迟，但生产部署仍采用单领导者，原因在于头阻塞（head-of-line blocking）：慢速领导者阻塞流水线，且领导者越多风险越大。任何静态领导者数量在某些网络条件下都是次优的。

Method: Barnacle 是一个通用插件，通过抽象接口（A1–A4）接入 DAG 协议。每隔 Interval 轮（如 50 轮），以已提交领导者为枢轴（pivot），在其因果历史的窗口中统计由直接决策规则（direct decision rule）提交的槽位比例（直接决策率）。若比率 ≥ 阈值（如 0.96），则每轮领导者数 +1（上限 maxLeaders）；否则按 2^backoff 减少（≥1），并递增 backoff。机制无需额外协议消息或密码学计算，完全确定性。安全性与活性证明对任意确定性更新规则均成立，并已在 Lean 4 中机器化验证。

Result: 在 n=10 验证者的模拟中，对四个基协议（Mysticeti 3f+1、Blue Bottle 5f+1、Nemo-Nemo 2c+1、Orcaella 5f+3c+1）测试：健康网络中 Barnacle 比单领导者延迟低 6–13%（与五 leaders 持平）；网络退化时迅速回退到单领导者，与单 leader 延迟持平，比静态五 leaders 低 35–56%；恢复阶段约 15 秒回升到最高领导者数。退化期全程不出现停顿。

Conclusion: Barnacle 是首个在运行时自适应 DAG 共识领导者数量的机制，作为确定性、消息无关的插件可在不同故障模型下保持安全性与活性，并在所有网络条件下匹配最佳静态配置。已与 Sui 团队合作集成进 Sui 区块链。未来工作包括反事实领导者评估跳到最优计数，以及扩展到 DAG 之外的多提议者协议。

Abstract: In DAG-based consensus, all validators propose blocks concurrently, and designated leader blocks drive transaction commit. Having multiple leader slots per round cuts queuing latency, yet production deployments run a single leader because of head-of-line blocking: a slow leader stalls the pipeline for at least one leader timeout, and for several waves when its slot must wait for the fallback indirect decision rule. This risk grows with the leader count. We introduce Barnacle, an add-on that adapts the leader count at run time. Every interval, it measures on the agreed committed DAG the fraction of slots decided as commit by the direct rule, and drives the leader count with additive increase, multiplicative decrease. The measurement requires no extra messages and no cryptography, and is deterministic. Barnacle is generic over DAG protocols; we instantiate it on four protocols spanning the Byzantine (3f + 1, 5f + 1), crash-only (2c + 1), and mixed (5f + 3c + 1) fault models, with proven safety and liveness. Results show Barnacle matches the best static leader count in every regime: in a healthy network its latency is 6-13% lower than a single leader's, and under degradation it matches a single leader while remaining 35-56% below a static high count. We are currently collaborating with the Sui team to integrate Barnacle into the Sui blockchain.

</details>


### [306] [Para-Pipe: Exploiting Hierarchical Operator Parallelism of ML Computational Graphs on SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04168)
*Yujie Zhang,Huiying Lan,Ehsan Aghapour,Zhiyuan Ning,Peng Zan,Weidong Shao,Anuj Pathania,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Para-Pipe是一个针对异构SoC的分层映射框架，通过在流水线架构中集成阶段内和阶段间算子并行性，为具有密集算子并行的现代神经网络在吞吐量、延迟和能效之间提供Pareto最优的权衡方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘深度学习应用日益复杂，主流ML框架在异构SoC上往往仅使用单一高性能处理单元顺序执行，未能充分利用多核并行能力。传统流水线方法虽能提升吞吐量但牺牲了延迟，而纯算子并行方法虽能降低延迟却损害了吞吐量，二者难以兼得。此外，现代神经网络（如InceptionNet的Inception模块、Transformer的多头注意力）天然具有丰富的算子并行性，但现有调度方法未能有效利用这一特性。同时，异构SoC上的能效优化对嵌入式部署至关重要。因此，需要一种能同时协调流水线与并行执行、平衡多目标的统一映射框架。

Method: Para-Pipe采用两级分层映射架构：(1) **Pipeline Mapper（流水线映射）**：通过图划分算法（Algorithm 1）将ML计算图分割为拓扑有序的子图（每个子图呈线性链或fan-in结构），然后枚举1到m个流水线阶段配置，并为每个阶段分配非重叠的处理器组合和分区点；(2) **Operator Mapper（算子映射）**：提出两种基于整数线性规划（ILP）的并行映射算法——粗粒度方法以分支为映射单元（最小化同步开销）、细粒度方法以单个算子为单元（最大化并行利用率，通过big-M技术约束处理器可用性）；(3) **成本估算与策略选择**：基于离线剖析的计算成本（cpc）和通信成本（cmc），结合性能（延迟=各阶段时间之和；吞吐量=最长阶段时间倒数）和能效（动态功耗+静态功耗模型，frames/joule），筛选Pareto最优配置。运行时基于ARM Compute Library实现，支持Amlogic A311D SoC（big.LITTLE CPU + GPU）和BST A1000 SoC（NPU + 2个DSP）。

Result: 在Amlogic A311D SoC和BST A1000 SoC上对6个现代模型（GoogLeNet、Inception-v3/v4、Inception-ResNet-v2、PETR-based、BEVFormer-based）进行评估：(1) **延迟-吞吐权衡**：纯流水线（pipe-only）相对纯并行（para-only）吞吐量高但延迟增加113.8%；纯并行吞吐量低26.6%但延迟最优；混合策略hybrid-L相比pipe-only降低延迟36.0%（仅损失12.4%吞吐），hybrid-T降低延迟26.8%（仅损失7.3%吞吐）；(2) **基线对比**：para-only相比HEFT&CPOP平均延迟降低10.9%（Amlogic）/15.5%（BST），吞吐提升12.5%/18.8%；(3) **能效**：Amlogic上hybrid-T相对pipe-only提升11.0%，相对para-only提升23.3%；(4) **成本估算精度**：延迟RMSPE 15.33%、吞吐15.25%、能效6.40%，能可靠确定相对性能排名；(5) **映射时间**：大多数模型细粒度ILP求解<5分钟，PETR最复杂子图（169算子）约6小时；(6) **细粒度开销**：CPU+GPU并发时同步开销21.7%，加入small CPU后降至4.7%。

Conclusion: Para-Pipe通过分层算子并行性有效解决了异构SoC上现代神经网络推理的延迟-吞吐权衡问题，相比纯流水线策略能效提升11.0%、相比纯并行策略提升23.3%，同时生成多个Pareto最优配置以满足不同应用需求。该框架通过图感知的流水线与并行执行融合，降低了处理器间通信开销，并基于子图划分的高效ILP求解策略保证了大模型的映射可扩展性。

Abstract: As edge-based deep learning applications become more complex, optimizing performance on heterogeneous System-on-Chips (SoCs) presents unique challenges. Traditional pipelining techniques distributing the computation across different on-chip processing units, while effective for throughput, do not address the latency demands posed by modern neural networks with complex interdependencies and extensive operator parallelism. There is a potential in leveraging operator parallelism to enable concurrent execution across multiple processing units, thereby reducing inference latency. However, prioritizing pipelining or parallel execution often necessitates a compromise, where optimizing one performance metric adversely impacts the other. This paper introduces Para-Pipe, a hierarchical mapping framework that integrates intra- and inter-stage operator parallelism within a pipelined architecture. Para-Pipe navigates the trade-off between throughput and latency by selectively fine-tuning parallelism levels within and across pipeline stages. This strategy can significantly reduce inter-processor communication overhead, significantly improving energy efficiency. Our evaluation demonstrates that Para-Pipe generates multiple Pareto-optimal configurations, achieving a balance between throughput and latency on an Amlogic SoC equipped with ARM this http URL CPUs and GPU, as well as the Black Sesame Technology SoC featuring a deep learning accelerator and two DSPs. More importantly, throughput-optimized configurations under Para-Pipe on Amlogic SoC show an average energy efficiency improvement of 11.0% over purely pipelined strategies and 23.3% relative to non-pipelined parallel execution.

</details>
